           *_IYABO QUARTER'S NEAR OMUPO WORD_*




"Sauyin rayuwa kan nuna maka alamun ka hankalta, amma ina mamakin mutane da basa gane azancin duniya, duniya tamkar waina ce dake bisa kasko takan iya juya mutum a duk sanda taso, shiyasa ko yaushe nake fatan Duniya ta muku juyin alkairi, domin bana fatan abinda zai shiga tsakanin ku har ya samu damar rabaku tamkar jinina haka nake jinka *_NAMEER_*  so nake ku kasance tsintsiya maɗauri ɗaya har ƙarshen numfashinku,
 *_HISHAM NAMEER_*  ba iya Amini yake a wajen ka ba tamkar ɗan uwa na jini haka yake tunda a hannuna ya tashi tun bayan mutuwar mahaifansa, Soyayyar ku da abotarku tun ta yarinta ne, duk da a zuciyata bana jin akwai abinda zai iya rabaku saboda tsantsar ƙaunar dake tsakanin ku, amma wasiyya da nasiha ta dole ce a gareku, abotarku tana da ƙarfin da tafi jini tasiri, nasan dole ne kuyi nasara akan maƙiyanku ina matuƙar alfahari daku ina muku wannan nasihar ce saboda ita mutuwa bata da notice haka kuma bata ƙwanƙwasawa bawa ƙofa, zan iya komawa ga mahallicina a ko wani lokaci."
    Kallon Nameer Hisham yayi tare da sakin numfashi yace.
    "Abba Ina mantawa da cewa Nameer Abokina ne, kallon jinana kawai nake masa, yana kyautata min Abba, yakan yaƙi da duk wata damuwar da yaga ta tunkaro ne, karka manta Abba, Ammi ta kasance ta bamu tarbiyya iri ɗaya, ta bamu abinci a baki tare, ta share mana hawaye tare, to ta yaya zan ɗauke sa iya Aboki kawai? ya wuce wannan matsayin Abba, domin ƙaunar da nake masa ta kai ga zan iya mallaka masa komai a rayuwata domin kuwa yafi min komai mahimmanci ina matukar alfahari da Nameer Abba shine jigon rayuwata na basa duk wata yardata."
    "Abba Hisham fa ɗan uwana ne ka daina shakku akanmu domin kuwa zamuyi ƙargo har abada, igiyar Amintar mu tana da ƙarfin da bazata taɓa tsinkewa ba ni da Ɗan uwana Hisham mutu ka raba Abba, zamu bawa maƙiya kunya ka kwantar da hankalinka a kanmu Abba."
    "Hmmm! Ni ma shaida ce Abban Hisham domin kuwa yarana basu taɓa ko da cacar baki bane bare faɗa, lokacin Office na ƙurewa kuyi maza kar ƙananan ma'aikata su gaji da jiranku Allah ya muku albarka ya tsare min gabanku da bayan ku."
  Murmushi Hisham yayi tare da cewa.
   "Ameen Ammin mu, mun gode sosai sai mun dawo."
   "Sai mun dawo Ammi."
Shima Nameer yayi Maganar cikin fara'a, da adawo lafiya Ammi da Abba suka amsa, kafin ko wannensu ya ɗauki jakar aikinsa suka fita tare, Murmushi Ammi tayi kafin ta mayar da kallonta ga Abba tace.
   "Abban Hisham, shaƙuwa da ƙaunar junan da suke yiwa junansu har yayi yawa, ina jin Nameer a jikina tamkar ni na haifesa, Abban Hisham, mutane sai saka maka albarka suke bisa ɗaura Nameer a matsayin shugaban Companyn ka *_ HISHAM RICE MILL LIMITED_* madadin ka bawa Ɗanka Hisham ka kyauta sosai na tabbata kuma zai tsare maka dukiyar ka da mutuncin Companynka, Allah ya jiƙan iyayen Nameer mutane ne na ƙwarai da suka san haƙƙin makwabtaka."
    Numfashi Abba ya saki yace.
    "Na lura yafi Hisham nutsuwa da sanin abinda yake zaifi mai da hankali ga cigaban Company shiyasa na basa dan haka ki daina gode min abinda ya kamata nayi, Nameer shima ɗana ne."
    "Hakane to Allah yayi jagora, bara naje na bawa TUTU key ɗin store ta ɗauko coffee na kitchen ya ƙare."
    "Okay kina iya tafiya."
Abba yayi Maganar yana maida glass ɗinsa idanunsa tare da ɗaukar jarida ya cigaba da karantawa itama Ammi bedroom ɗin su Nihal ta nufa.



    Tafe suke a ƙas sun fito daga ofishin Jaridar Aminci, suka hango cincirindon mutane, a gefen hanya, duban Basmah tayi tace.
    "Basmah meke faruwa a can.?"
    "Kaji wata tambayar rainin wayo, mu fito tare sannan ki tambayeni aljana ce ni ko boka da zansan abinda ke faruwa a wajen."
    "To sarkin ɓakar magana, ni bara na ƙarasa wajen naga abinda ke faruwa."
  Kanta Basmah ta girgiza tare da cewa.
    "Kina da matsala *JIDDA* baki san meke faruwa a waje ba ki kai kanki saboda kawai kina ƴar jarida mai tarar kasada ko ke gaki mai son ɗauko rahoto."
   "Bani da lokacin ki idan zaki iya zuwa ki biyoni idan bazaki iya ba, mu haɗu a gida."
   Tayi Maganar tana saka kai tabar wajen, bakinta Basmah ta taɓe tare da bin bayanta, ko da Jidda ta isa wajen a cike yake babu masika tsinke jama'ar wajen ta dinga ketawa har ta samu ta shiga tsakiyar wajen da ƙyar, wata ƴar matashiyar budurwa ce baje ƙasa, da alamu bata motsi, yayin da jama'ar dake Wajen suka zuba mata idanu babu wanda yayi yunkurin taimakon ta, jikinta duk ya kuje da alamun yaguni da sauri Jidda ta sunkuya a kanta tana furta.
    "Subahanallah! Meya faru da ita, kun zuba mata idanu bazaku kaita asibiti ba idan ta mutu a wajen nan fa.?"
  Wani daga cikin wadanda ke tsaye a wajen ne yace.
   "Ta yaya zamu sanya hannu a jikinta bayan muna kallo aka wurgota daga mota, mun sanya hannun mu a cikin masifa kike so, idan taimakon ta kike son kiyi to ki fara kiran Police tukunna."
   Ɗago idanunta Jidda tayi a zuciye tace.
    "Amma dai Allah wadarai wallahi kuna kallon mace wurge gefen titi ku gagara taimaka mata, idan ƙanwarku ce zaku zuba mata idanu haka ba tare da taimako ba, mtsss! Wai kuma har kana da bakin bani shawara, ku kauce ku bani ni zan rungumi kasadar idan yaso masifar da kuke gudu ni ta kasheni."
   Tayi Maganar tana miƙewa tare da kwaɗawa Basmah kira, duk kaucewa gefe sukayi suka bata hanya babu wanda ya ce komai a cikin taron, Basmah ƙarasowa tayi ta tsaya a gabanta tare da cewa.
    "Gani kiran me kike min.?"
    "Taimakon ta zamuyi Basmah domin kuwa tana buƙatar taimako ta zubar da jini da yawa Sannan bata motsi zata iya rasa rayuwarta muddun muka tafi muka barta a hannun wadannan marassa imanin."
    "Hmmm! Jidda ba rashin imani yasa basu taimaketa ba, karki musu mummunar fahimta domin kuwa rayuwa ta lalace, a sanadin taimako sai ka shiga cikin wata masifar banga laifin su ba, ko da ma suna da laifi to laifin ɗaya ne na rashin kiran jami'an tsaro da wuri, karmu taɓa wannan yarinyar idan har taimakonta zamuyi to police ya kamata mu kira kamar yadda wannan bawan Allah ya faɗa miki."
    "Kinga Basmah bazan tsaya har sai an kira Police sunzo ba! Kafin a kaita asibiti rayuwarta zamu ceta bama bukatar jira idan zaki taimaka to idan bazaki taimaka ba kija baya zan iya ba tare da taimakon ki ba."
   Tayi Maganar cikin masifa tana fita sarari da kallo Basmah ta bita domin kuwa ko yaushe bata gajiya da mamakin taurin kai da kafiya irin na Jidda ba, sam ba'a juyata inda ta sanya kanta nan kawai takeyi tana kollonta ta taro adaidaita, da ita da mai adaidaitan suka kama yarinyar suka sanya ciki kafin Jidda ta shiga ta tallafota jikinta, Basmah kanta ta kaɗa tare da shiga cikin adaidaitan, harara Jidda ta watsa mata tare da cewa.
    "Nayi zaton ai imaninki ya gushe ashe har yanzu da saura."
  Tayi Maganar dai-dai mai adaidaitan yana ja.
     "Bazaki fahimce ni bane, yanzu da kika ɗauketa kina zaton asibiti zasu amsheta ba tare da Police bane.?"
    "Bazasu amsheta ba na sani, shiyasa ma na turawa Ahmad text cewa kinyi accident muna  IBUNKU OLU CLINIC wataƙil ya rigamu isa kinga police ɗin ma aishi zai jiramu."
    "What! Jidda meye kikayi haka."
    "Ceton rayuwar mace ƴar Uwar mu shi nayi ko akwai laifine ayin hakan."
    "Ƙwarai kuwa akwai babban laifi ma, akan me zakiyi ƙarya dani ki faɗa masa gaskiya mana zaki wani ɗaura min mungun abu."
    "Koma dai mai zaki faɗa kiyi haƙuri ni ne na miki laifi amma kin sani muddun na sanar dashi gaskiyar abinda ke faruwa ba zuwa zaiyi ba."
    Tsuka Basmah taja cikin ɓacin rai tace.
     "Dole ki bani haƙuri ai tunda kin cuceni."
   Tayi Maganar dai-dai suna shigowa asibitin, shima Ahmad dai-dai yayi parking ɗin motarsa ya fito a gigice turus ya tsaya ganin  Basmah sun rungumo wata, wajen su ya ƙarisa da sauri yace.
    "Jidda ya haka kince Basmah tayi accident kuma naga kin rungumo wata ga Basmah tsaye lafiya.?"
      "Ahmad bamu lokacin Doguwar magana dan Allah ka sanya hannu a duba wannan baiwar Allah, kafin mu maka bayani, a gefen titi muka tsinceta cikin wannan halin."
    Ahmad zaiyi Magana Basmah ta katsesa da cewa.
     "Kar ka mata gardama domin kuwa kasan mayya ce ita, ka sanya hannun."
     Numfashi Ahmad ya saki kafin likitoci suka taho da keken marassa lafiya aka turata, ganin Ahmad da kayan Police a jikinsa, bayansu yabi ya cike dukkan wata takadda rashin sanin sunanta yasa ba'a rubuta sunanta ba kafin sukayi emargency da ita.
    Bayan an shiga da ita ne Ahmad yayi kan Jidda da faɗa na ɗaukar wannan kasadar, ita dai bata kulasa ba, suna nan tsaye har doctor ya fito duban Ahmad yayi yace su samesa a Office bayansa sukabi tare da zama doctor ya dubi Ahmad yace.
     "Wannan marar lafiya da kuka kawo, gaskiya ta zubar da jini da yawa, muna da jinin da yayi dai-dai da nata kasancewar tana neman jini cikin gaggawa yasa bamu muku magana ba muka sanya mata da alamu faɗowa tayi daga sama ko dai something haka ta kuje sosai amma dai Alhamdulillah! Babu matsalar buguwar kai, mun mata allurar bacci sannan akwai zazzaɓi a jikinta zata iya farkawa ko wani lokaci zakuje receiption ku biya kuɗin jini, ga kuma takaddar magani zaku saya."
   Hannu Ahmad ya sanya tare da amsa yace.
    "Thank Doctor."
Tashi yayi ya fita bayansa suka biyo, Jidda ce tace.
     "Akwai kudi a account ɗina ni zan biya wannan taimakon da kayi da jikinka ma kaɗai ya wadatar na gode sosai."
    "Hmmm! Jidda kece zaki biya kudin kika ce, Karki manta ɗawainiyar mahaifiyarki da ta ƙaninki kuɗin hayar gidan ku duk a kanki suke, dan haka idan ba'a ƙara miki ba baza'a rageki ba, ni zanje na biya da aljihu na."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki tana ganin darajar Ahmad sosai musamman yacce yake tausayawa rayuwarta.
    "Hakane Ahmad na gode sosai, yanzu yaya za'ayi da kwana a wajen yarinyar kaga fa Ummana ba lallai ta amince na kwana ba infact ma bazata yarda ba."?
    "Karki damu Jidda, zan kira A'isha sai tazo su kwana zuwa gobe ta dawo hayyacinta sai musan ahalinta, kuna iya tafiya zanje na gama komai kafin Aisha tazo ni sai na wuce."
   Murmushi Jidda ta yi tace.
    "Na gode Ahmad Allah ya maka abinda kayiwa wannan yarinyar sai goben."
  Tayi Maganar tana yin gaba, Basmah duban Ahmad tayi tare da sakin murmushi tace.
    "My Dear ina matukar girmama Soyayyar mu, kai Mutum ne na Musamman a rayuwata, ina sonka matukar Soyayya."
    "Hmmm! Basmah idan ban kyautata miki ba waye zan kyautatawa, kinfi ƙarfin komai a gareni Basmah dan haka ki tafi kuje huta zan shigo anjuma."
    "Shikenan farin ciki na bye."
   Tayi Maganar cike da soyayya tana bin bayan Jidda.



            💫💫💫💫💫💫



 "Na rantse da Allah sai naga bayan zuri'ar Iyabo bazan taɓa gushewa ba sai naga ƙasƙantarsa da tozarta sa, koda kuwa zanyi yawo tsirara, nasan zuwa yanzu saƙona ya fara ƙwanƙwasa musu ƙofa, sai na sanya su a cikin *_JALALAR_* da sai sun roƙi mutuwa da kansu, *TUNDE* muddun kai jinina ne to tabbas bazaka gushe ba har sai ka ɗauki bashin gabar da ke tsakanin mahaifinka *Babawale* da *Iyabo*, kadda ka saurara musu nima kuma bazan saurara musu ba, na sanya mutanena a cikin Companyn su suna sanya min ido akan dukkan  abinda zaije ya dawo acikin Companyn to yana tafin hannuna dan haka karka saurara."
   *Mama Bisola* tayi Maganar cike da tsantsar wutar fansar dake ruruwa a cikin zuciyarta da tsanar familyn Iyabo.
    "Mamabisola karki manta cewa babu mutanen dana tsana a wannan duniyar sama da su Iyabo ba kuma zan gushe ba har sai naga bayansu, daga ƴaƴansa zan fara da wannan tsintaccen yaron da ya ijiye ya basa yardarsa fiye da matar sa da ɗansa, wallahi Mamabisola sai na sanya su kuka a tsakiyar titi."
    "Haka nake so Tunde kaje kana tare da Sa'a."
    "Na gode Mamabisola, zan wuce wajen Bola."
   "Ka dawo lafiya."
Mamabisola tayi Maganar cike da ƙaunar Tunde, yana fita  Wemimo dake gefe zaune ta dubi Mamabisola cikin harshen yarbanci tace
    "Mamabisola, ina mamakin yadda Iyabo ya bawa wannan dan Hausan Amana sunansa Nameer ko, meyasa ya basa shugaban Kamfanin sa bayan ga Hisham ɗansa.?"
    "Hmmm! Wamimo ke kanki hakan zai baki mamaki har yanzu ban gano wani ƙwaƙƙwaran dalili da yasa yayi hakan ba, duk da cewa yaron abokin ɗansa ne, amma ni na sani sai yaci amanarsa domin kuwa Hausa babu amana."
    "Hakane Mamabisola, amma Mamabisola, gani nayi kinfi MamaIyabo yawan yara naki yaran Uku ne, ita kuma MamaIyabo nata yaron ɗaya ne Iyabo amma har kikayi sake dukiyar MamaIyabo ya yaɗu ku naku ya lalace, meyasa Mamabisola, meyasa kuka sake haka."?
    "Hmmm! Wamimo bazaki fahimta ba, amma maigidan mu shine wanda ya cuce mu ni da yarana, shi ya bawa Iyabo ƙyautar Companyn sa na Auduga, wanda bai shiga cikin gado ba, wanda shine yanzu iyabo ya gyarasa ya maida shi  Hisham rice mill limited, wanda a yanda Ademola yake sanar dani a ko wani wata suna samun kuɗi sama da 50m ni kuma gani ni da ƴaƴana muna zaune bama samun rabin abinda suke samu, dan haka Wamimo na ɗaura aniyar tozarta rayuwar Iyabo da iyalinsa, wacce hanya ce zaki iya taimaka min."?
   Numfashi Wamimo ta saki tare da cewa.
   "Huuuu! Mamabisola, ni kuwa nake da hanyar taimaka miki, zamuje wajen sarkin aljanu domin ya tarwatsa dukiyar su rasa komai ya kike gani zamuje."?
    Girgiza kanta Mamabisola tayi tace.
    "No Wamimo, bazai yiwu zuwa wajen Sarkin Aljanu yanzu ba domin kuwa mai gidan mu *ABDULWASI'U* yayi kafi a jikin yaransa idan mukayi tsafi yanzu zai iya dawowa kanmu, da badan wannan kafin ba, da na juma da hallakar da Iyabo, sai dai taimako ɗaya zaki min ki shiga ki fita wajen sarakan tsafi ki samo min makarin kafi idan har aka samu to tabbas zan hallaka Iyabo da tsafi."
    "Za kuma a samu tabbas ni ne nake tabbatar miki da wannan, zan fara bibiyar Sarakan tsafi kuma sai na kawo wannan makarin."
    "Idan kika min haka Wamimo bazan taɓa mantawa da ke ba, kije ki bincika ni kuma zan cigaba da jifansu da makaman sharri."
    "Baki da matsala Mamabisola, ni yanzu zan shige sai gobe zan fara nawa aikin."
    "Na gode Wamimo muje na rakaki."
   Mamabisola tayi maganar tana tashi har bakin get ta raka Wamimo kafin ta koma cikin gida.

  A bakin ƙofar gidan su Jidda suka rabu da Basmah kasancewar gidan su Basmah yana gaba da nasu Jidda, sallama sukayi da nufin sai gobe zasu haɗu, kafin Basmah ta shige ita kuma Jidda ta shiga cikin gida, Umma ta samu zaune a tsakar gidan gefenta kuma AbdulRahman ne yana mata wanke wanke zama tayi tana cewa.
     "Wash Umma na gaji sosai yau."
    Murmushi Umma tayi kafin tace.
    "Ai dole ki gaji jiddatul kair tun safe fa kika fice, kece sai yanzu gabannin magaruba, meya tsayar dake ne haka ke da kika saba dawowa 4:00pm.?"
   "Hmmm wallahi Umma wani matsala ne ya faru a hanyar mu ta dawowa, wata yarinya ce aka bigeta da mota to shine muka taimaka ni da Basmah aka kaita asibiti yanzun ma daga asibitin muka dawo shiyasa nayi dare."
   "Subahanallah! Amma dai haɗarin da sauƙi ko.?"
    "Ehh Umma da sauƙi sosai ma, amma dai mun baro asibitin bata dawo hankalinta ba, amma likita yace zata farka lafiya."
    Gyaɗa kanta Umma tayi tace.
   "To masha Allah, Allah ya bata lafiya, ki cigaba da taimako Jiddatul kair insha Allah kina tafe da gagarumar nasara a rayuwarki, nakan jin daɗi harma nayi alfahari idan naji kin taimakawa wani, Allah ya miki albarka ya rabaki da sharrin maƙiya."
    "Ameen Umma."
"Adda Jidda sannu da dawowa."
    "Yauwa abdulrahman kana taya Umma aiki ne, magaruba ta gabato, ka tashi kaje kayi alwala ka tafi masallaci bara naje na ijiye mayafi na sai nazo na ƙarisa wanke wanken."
    "To Adda."
Yayi Maganar yana tsame hannunsa daga ruwan wanke wanken, ya ɗauki buta Jidda tashi tayi taje ta ijiye mayafinta da jakarta kafin ta dawo ta idda wanke wanken, ita ta tuƙawa Umma tuwon kafin aka kira sallah sukayi alwala sukayi sallah bayan sun idar ne ta ɗauki Alqur'anin ta ta hau karatu bata idda karatun ba har sai da tayi sallar isha'i"
   "Jiddatul kair tashi kije ki sako mana tuwo muci, naga alamar Abdu bazai shigo yanzu ba."
   "Ina ga sun tsaya karatu ne, bara naje na ɗauko."
  Tayi Maganar tana tashi taje ta ɗauko musu tuwan masara miyar ɗanyar kuɓewa, zama tayi ta ijiye tuwon tare da Bismillah suka fara cin abincin babu wanda yayi magana har suka kammala cin abincin suka wanke hannu sannan Jidda ta fitar da kwanukan ta dawo."
     "Umma wai nikam dama haka yaruba suke basu da kirki."
   "Jiddatul kair meyasa kika min wannan tambayar wani abin suka miki na rashin kyautatawa.?"
    "A'a Umma wacce nace miki an bigeta da mota mun kaita asibiti ita suka gwadawa rashin imani domin kuwa Umma Yarinyar ta juma sosai wurge tsakiyar titi cikin halin rayuwa ko mutuwa sun cika a kanta Umma amma babu wanda yayi tunanin taimaka mata sai ma idanu suka zuba mata tamkar tv suna kallon ta hakan ya ƙona min rai sosai Umma."
   "Jidda a gaskiya Bayarben mutum, bashi da daɗi ta wani fannin amma ta wani fannin kuma yafi kowa daɗin mu'amala, domin kuwa ba duka aka taru aka zamo ɗaya ba, basu da matsala ta fannin mu'amala domin kuwa suna kyautatawa talakan da suka fisa ƙarfi, nayi zama cikin su sosai na fahimci ɗabiun su suna da zumunci da kyautatawa ga maƙwabci, amma nayi mamaki da basu taimaki wannan Yarinyar ba."
   "To dai gashi basu taimaketa ba, ni har na fara ma jin haushin wannan yaren Yaruba basu da im............
    "A'a jiddatul kair kul kika ƙarisa, ko kin manta Mahaifiyata Yaruba ce kike ƙoƙarin aibata su.?"
    "Ban manta ba Umma sanin hakan ma yasa ni ƙwarin gwiwwa da hujjar rashin imani irin na Yaruba domin kuwa na sani da Hausawa ne ko Fullo ko Kanuri bazasu taɓa yasar da ɗansu ba komai girman munin halinsa, na sani Umma da mahaifinki yana raye bazaki tagayyara haka ba, Umma nasan zakiji zafin maganganuna domin kuwa uwa ba abar wasa bane sai taki mahaifiyar bata da tausayi."
    Idanun Umma cikowa da hawaye sukayi tabbas maganganun ƴarta akan mahaifiyarta gaskiya ne sai dai har cikin ranta tana jin zafi da ciwo idan taji an aibata mata uwa bata taɓa zaton rayuwa na juyawa mutum baya haka ba sai a tsakanin waɗannan shekaru 18 da suka gabata numfashi ta saki kafin ta cewa Jidda.
   "Jiddatul kair ki tashi ki bani waje."
   "Amma Umma y.......
      "Ki tashiii ki bani waje nace miki! Bana son maganar banza babu ruwanki tsakanina da mahaifiyata koma me ta min a rayuwa uwata ce koda mahaukaciya ce ina alfahari da ita, koda kuwa kasheni tayi ta dawo dani ina sonta zuciyata kuma bazata jurewa munanan maganganu akanta ba, ki kiyaye muddun bakya son fushina ya sauka a kanki."

"Kiyi haƙuri Umma Allah ya huci zuciyarki, banyi zaton maganar zata ɓata miki rai haka ba, ke uwace gareni fushinki masifa ce a gareni ki yafe min insha Allah bazan kuma wata magana marar daɗi akan Mama ba."
  Umma ba tare da ta dubeta ba tace.
    "Naji na kuma yafe miki Allah ya muku albarka."
   "Ameen Umma na gode."
  Tayi Maganar dai-dai Abdu na shigowa gefensu ya zauna, Jidda ce ta dubesa tare da cewa.
    "Abdullahi sai yanzu ka dawo daga sallar."
   "Na tsaya Liman ya ɗaura min karatu ne shiyasa na juma, ina abinci na."?
   "Abincin ka yana madafa."
  Ta basa amsar tana miƙewa ta shige ɗakinsu, Abdu Umma ya kalla yace.
    "Umma kamar wani abu yana damun Adda naga kamar ranta babu daɗi."?
  Numfashi Umma ta saki tace.
    "Yanayin rayuwa ne Abdu ba ko wani lokaci bane bawa yake cikin farin ciki, kaje ka ɗauki abincin ka."
   "To Umma."
 Yayi Maganar yana miƙewa yaje ya ɗauki tuwon nasa kafin ya shiga ɗakin da Jidda ta shiga tana zaune tsakiyar katifa tayi shuru cikin tunani da damuwar rayuwa sallamar sa shine ya dakatar da ita daga tunanin da ta tafi, ta amsa sallamar zama yayi yana sanya hannunsa cikin aljihun sa ya zaro dubu biyar ya miƙa mata.
   "Gashi Adda."
Kallon kuɗin tayi ba tare da ta amsa ba tace.
   "Gashi kamar yaya kuɗin waye.?"
   "Uncle Abdulra'uf ne ya bani yace na kawo miki ko kina da wata buƙata kiyi amfani dasu."
   "Abdulra'uf ya baka ka kawo min, na roƙesa kuɗi ne ko kuwa ya ganni cikin tozartacciyar rayuwar da har zan fara roƙo, banyi wannan lalacewar ba Allah ya min ni'ima da har ya bani damar karatu na samu aikin yi, ka mayar masa da kuɗinsa kace bana buƙata."
   "Amma Adda gani nayi kamar babu kyau mai da hannun kyauta baya, uncle Abdulra'uf fa sonki yake Adda ba laifi bane idan ya miki kyauta sanin kanki ne yana tausayawa halin da muke ciki, kefa macece komai kece kike mana namiji ma yaya ya ƙare da nauyin gida bare kuma mace dole ne a tausaya miki Adda gashi ni bana da ƙarfin da zan iya neman kuɗi a yanzu bani da lafiyar ɓargon jiki sannan ga ƙafata janta nake hmmm! Adda...........
    "Ya isa haka Abdullahi, ka daina min wannan Maganar mace ma mutum ce kamar kowa duk abinda Namiji zaiyi zamu iya dan haka ka daina jin damuwa a kaina, kaje ka mayar masa da kuɗinsa kamar yadda na umarce ka, bana buƙatar abinda zai fito daga garesa na yafe zan iya ɗaukar nauyin rayuwar mu sannan ka sani idan shi Abdulra'uf yace maka yana sona to tabbas yayi kuskure domin kuwa bazanyi kuskuren da mahaifiyata tayi ba, na amincewa Yaruba dukkan su macuta ne basa da imani."
    "Amma Adda ya kamata ki duba wann.........
  Cikin sauri ta ɗaga masa hannu.
   "Ka tashi ka fita min daga ɗaki Abdullahi idan ba so kake na ɓata maka rai ba."
  Ganin ranta ya ɓaci yasa Abdullahi tashi ya fice da kwanon tuwonsa yana mai da kuɗin aljihun sa.
  Tsuka Jidda taja tare da jawo bargo ta kwanta tana rufe jikinta.





      Washegari da safe, Tutu da kanta ta musu breakfast ita da Peju kukun gidan su, a daining table suka jera abincin kafin Tutu ta shige bedroom ɗinta tabar Peju tana kimtsa kitchen ɗin Ummi ta turawa saƙon sun kammala breakfast ɗin kafin ta shige tollet wanka.

       A hankali ya buɗe idanunsa yana duban agogon bangon dake manne a bangon ɗakin idanunsa ya lumshe tare da duban Nameer da ke bacci har yanzu bashi da niyyar tashi, ƙafafunsa ya ziro ƙasa tare da sanya takalmin dake gefen bed ɗin, ya sauƙa, towel ya ɗauka tare da shigewa tollet, ya juma sosai kafin ya fito ƙugunsa ɗaure da towel ya rataye ɗaya kuma a kafaɗarsa da alamu wanka yayi, a dressing mirror  dake gefen bed ɗin ya zauna tare da sanya hannu ya kaɗowa Nameer ruwan gashin sa a fuskarsa yana sanya towel ɗin ya hau tsame ruwan kansa, cikin azama Nameer ya buɗe idanunsa, jin sauƙar abu kamar ruwa a fuskar sa, a kan Hisham ya sauƙe idanunsa kanne masa ido Hisham yayi tare da nuna masa agogo da hannu yace.
   "Raise your eyes and look at 11:00 am this weekend Abby is home, breakfast on time is a must."
  Numfashi  Nameer ya saki tare da jawo ƙaramin filon dake tsakiyar su ya jefi Hisham dashi yana cewa.
   "Saboda yau weekend ne shine zaka min irin wannan tashin na razanarwa wai Hisham yaushe zakayi hankali ne, ko sai randa kayi Aure ne.?"
     Wani irin kallon off and down Hisham yayiwa Nameer kafin yace.
   "Aure! Ni hmmm! Idan mafarki kake ka tashi kayi sadaka, natsani Soyayya, bare kuma Aure da yake ginshikin Soyayya, dan haka ka daina sani a wannan layin."
  Murmushi Nameer ya saki yana tashi ya jingina da jikin bed ɗin tare da cewa.
   "Ba'a cikawa so baki Hisham, domin kuwa so tamkar iska yake ba'a sanin shigarsa ba'a kuma ganinsa sai dai a jisa a zuciya idan ta ɗauro ma ya taho a makance, sannan baya shawara da zuciya idan zai shiga gareta faɗawa kawai yake, dan haka ba'a cikawa So baki, ta iya yiwa ka faɗa tekun Soyayyar da bazaka taɓa iya fitowa ba komai iya ruwan da kayi."
   "Idan har za'a danganta so da iska to tabbas kuwa za'a iya dakatar da shigarta domin kuwa sai ka ja numfashi kafin iska ya samu damar shiga jikinka kaga kuwa za'a iya ɗaukewa So numfashi dan haka zan iya kare kaina daga faɗawa wahalar SO."
  "To ai kai Hisham kayi mantuwa, ba'a taɓa iya rayuwa babu numfashi muddun zaka ɗauke numfashinka na awa guda to tabbas zaka rasa rayuwarka ta har abada, ba'a gujewa so kamar yadda ba'a gujewa mutuwa duk tserenka dasu wallahi sai sun cimmaka."
    "Musu da kai ai wahala ne Nameer domin kuwa duk inda gaskiya take kauce mata kake muddun a fagen so ne, tamkar malamin Soyayya haka kake, ina tausaya maka domin kana cikin wahala duk da har yanzu bamu ga gwanar taka ba duk wannan lectures ɗin."
  Yayi Maganar yana tashi ya je drowar gajeren wando boxer ya sanya tare da farar vest Nameer ne ya mai da masa amsa da cewa.
   "A fagen so ba'a kuri na tabbatar maka wata rana sai Soyayya tayi wasa da rayuwar ka a gaɓar da bakayi zato ba."
 Kafin Hisham yayi magana Tutu ta turo door ɗin Room ɗin bakinta ɗauke da sallama Hisham amsawa yayi yana cewa.
    "Autar Abba, Abba yayo kira ko."?
    Dariya Tutu tayi tare da cewa.
    "Yeah abinda kuka sani ne ai, yana can yana ta faɗa wai baku fito da wuri ba, yace ku fito yanzun nan daining."
   "Kingan ni nan na gama shiryawa ga dai makararren da zaki faɗawa Aku sarkin magana ni kaga fitata sai ka fito."
  Yayi Maganar yana zarar wayoyinsa tare da sanya takalmi ya fice,  Nameer duban Tutu yayi tare da cewa.
   "Ya akayi kika ƙura min ido tamkar kinga baƙo."
   "Hmmm! Dole ai na ƙura maka ido ko yaushe kai sai kayi let akan komai kana dai kallon Yaya Hisham akan lokaci yake komai nasa baya wasa da lokaci saɓanin kai."
   "Kema fa kinsan dole na makara domin kuwa jiya munja dogon dare, dan haka bai kamata kimin ƙorafi ba, kije kawai ina fitowa nan da 10minute, sannan zamu fita by 12 tunda yau ba ki da school."
  Numfashi Tutu ta saki tare da riƙe kunkuminta da kyau tace.
    "Bazan fita da kai ba yau Yaya Nameer, gajiyar jiya ma bata gama barin jikina ba bare kuma na kara biyeka, sannan ka dinga ɗaga ƙafa gudun fahimtar wani abu ga ahali, kayi maza ka fito kasan Abba baya jurewa jira."
  Tayi Maganar tana fita daga room ɗin numfashi Nameer ya saki tare da tashi ya shiga tollet.



   "Good morning Abba."
"Morning my soon wannan sarkin makaran yauma dai still ko."?
   "Ummm Abba kaima fa kasan Nameer abinda aka saba ne kullum."
  Yayi Maganar yana kallon Ummi tare da cewa.
    "Good morning Ummina."
   "Morning Hisham, da alamu dai yau uba da ɗa sun haɗewa ɗana baki, to kuwa baku isa ba muddun ina zaune anan baku isa kun tsige min yarona ba."
  Dariya Tutu tayi kafin tayi Magana, suka jiyo Muryar Nameer daga step yana cewa.
    "Yeah! Uwata maganin kukana faɗa musu dai ni ai nasan muddun Ummina na kusa babu wanda ya isa tureni."
   "Hmmm! Masu uwa mu kuma ai marayu ne mararsa gatan uwa."
  Hisham yayi magana yana murmushi, Abba shima murmushi ya saki yace.
   "Chaptern ya isa haka, Tutu kiyi seving ɗin mu."
Miƙewa Tutu tayi tare da jawo plas ɗin abincin ta yi seving ɗin kowa, sanin halin Hisham ba komai yake so ba yasa Tutu dubansa tare da cewa.
  "Yaya Hisham me zakaci.?"
    "Zuban chief's."
Kanta ta girgiza tare da sanya masa ta tura masa gabansa, kafin tasa nata, breakfast suka fara cikin soyayya da ƙaunar junansu babu wanda yayi magana har suka gama breakfast ɗin, Hisham ya riga kowa tashi, ya koma tsakiyar falon ya zauna, kafin Abba ya biyo bayansa.
   "Hisham."
Abba ya kira sunan sa ɗago idanunsa Hisham yayi tare da amsawa.
   "Na'am Abba."
"Wannan week ɗin kaje gidan Mamabisola kuwa ka gaishe ta."?
   "Banje ba."
"Meyasa."?
"Haka kawai."
"Hisham bana son sakarci da iya shege, Mamabisola uwa ce gareni bana son kana ɗaukarta tamkar wata bare."
   "Hmmm! Abba ni fa bazanje inda za'a dinga zagina ana kallona banza ba, kaine take dole a gareka bani ba, dan haka ni babu abinda zanje nayi a gidan ta, kawai ka ƙyaleni."
   "Hisham...........
"Dan Allah Abba kabar wannan maganar, please."
  Ya katse Abba cikin ɓacin rai fuskarsa a haɗe domin kuwa ya tsani yaji ana zancen Mamabisola.
    "Hisham katseni zakayi ina magana saboda iya shege, to bara kaji tunda har Mamabisola ta zamo dolena to kuwa kaima ta zamo dolenka, ni da WALE uba ɗaya muke bani da ɗan uwan da ya shigesa, dan haka ya zamo wajibi ka girmama mahaifiyar sa."
   "But Abba........
"Hisham...."
Ummi ta katsesa tana ƙarisowa wajen ta zauna tare da cewa.
   "Hisham bana jin daɗin abinda kake yiwa Mamabisola, karka manta su fa jikinka ne dole ne ka girmama su domin kuwa baka da ɗan uwan daya wucesu, baka ganin yadda Tunde ke girmama mahaifinka ko wani weekend sai ya zo ya gaishe sa cikin girmamawa kuma baya taɓa zuwa hannu haka sai yazo masa da abin arziki sai kaine zaka wulaƙanta nasa Iyayen ka kiyaye, jiya ta kirani ai Mamabisolan tana sanar dani rashin mutuncin daka tafka masa, a Company."
   "Rashin mutunci kuma Hisham ɗin ya yiwa Tunde, Ramata."?
  Abba ya tambayi Ummi cike da mamaki.
   "Ƙwarai kuwa wai yaje Company da takaddunsa zai nemi aiki saboda inda yake aikin babu salary mai kyau, amma yaron nan Hisham sai cewa sakatariya yayi bazai gansa ba, hakan yaɓatawa Mamabisola rai shi kansa Tunde cewa yayi bazai koma ba."
   "Yo ina zai koma yaje ɗan uwansa ya wulaƙanta sa, HISHAM anya kana son farin ciki na kuwa, Nameer!."
 Abba yayi Maganar yana kwaɗawa Nameer kira, Nameer dake zaune a daining shida Tutu ne ya juyo tare da amsawa yana miƙewa ya taho garesu gefen Abba ya zauna yace.
    "Lafiya Abba naga ɓacin rai a fuskar ka.?"
   "Dole ai kaga ɓacin rai a fuskata tunda ku baku da mutunci."
   "Abba me kuma mukayi.?"
   "Dole ai ka tambaye ni wato saboda Tunde yaje Company neman aiki shine bari a wulaƙanta sa ko aƙi ganinsa bare aji meke tafe dashi."
   Numfashi Nameer ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Tunde kuma yaushe yaje Company, wallahi ban sani ba bani da labari."?
    Kallon Hisham daya haɗa rai Abba yayi tare da cewa.
   "Oh ashe kai ɗaya kayi tsiyar ya maka kyau, Nameer zan kira Tunde Monday zaizo company lallai ku gansa kuma ku amshi takaddunsa, ku tabbatar kun basa aiki a gurbin daya kamata wannan umarnina ne."
   "Shikenan Abba insha Allah abinda kace haka za'ayi Allah ya huci zuciyarka."
  Nameer ya yiwa Abba maganar cikin sanyin kalami tare da girmamawa, duban Hisham dake kumbura Abba yayi yace.
     "Ba kumbura zakayi ba, ka fashe, sannan duk uzurin dake gabanka tsakanin yau da gobe ka ijiye sa kaje ka gaishe da Mamabisola idan ba haka ba ni da kaine."
   Hisham shuru yayi ba tare da yace wa Abba komai ba, saima tashi da yayi yabar wajen, Da kallo Nameer ya bisa ganin ransa a ɓace yasa Nameer bin bayansa, kanta Ummi ta girgiza dai-dai Tutu na ƙarisowa ta zauna jikin Ummi.
    "Hmmm! Abban Hisham, ni fa lamarin Hisham da Mamabisola yana bani tsoro, domin kuwa Hisham da gaske baya sonsu a shekaru irin na Hisham ya wuce ace sai an turasa yaje ya gaishe da Mamabisola, amma Abban Hisham menene duk hakan anya kuwa rayuwa zata yiwu a haka babu fahimtar juna tsakanin su, sune fa gatansa bashi da jinin daya wuce su a yau muka mutu dole sune zasu zame masa katanga."
    "Ramata ni kaina wannan lamari na Hisham yana bani tsoro duk da Mamabisola ta taka muhimmiyar rawa wajen cusawa zuciyar Hisham tsanar su, a duk lokacin da zaka nunawa yaro ƙiyayya tun yana ƙaraminsa to tabbas zai tashi da haushinka, Ni da ɗan uwana Wale mun kasance cikin ƙaunar junanmu sai dai ita Mamabisola sam bata son hakan bata ƙaunar shaƙuwa dake tsakanina da Wale dalilin kenan daya sanya tabar gidan mu na gado ta koma gefe, tunda Baba ya mutu Mamabisola ta ɗaura min tsana har yau na kasa fahimtar laifin dana mata sai dai bana fatan hakan ya faru dasu Hisham shida Tunde, wato rashin zumunci da gaba."
    "To Allah ya kyauta ya gyara lamarin, bara naje na gyara Room ɗin ka, ke kuma ki tashi ki tattara daining ɗin."
   Ummi tayi Maganar tana tashi ta haura Room ɗin Abba.
  Abba hannu yasa ya ɗauki jarida.


   Dafe kansa Hisham yayi sosai ransa ya ɓaci, da faɗan da iyayensa suka masa, drowa ya buɗe tare da ɗauko sigari zai kunna, Nameer daya shigo ne yayi saurin sanya hannu ya fisge karan sigarin yana cewa.
   "Wai da gaske bazaka daina shan sigari ba Hisham.?"
    Idanunsa da suka kaɗa sukayi jajur Hisham ya ɗago ya kalli Nameer tare miƙa masa hannu cikin ɓacin rai da kaushin murya yace.
    "Bazan daina ba, miƙon Abina!."
   "Bazan bayar ba, ka ƙwace da ƙarfi."
   "Nameeeer!"
"Hishammmm! Bazan bada ba, ka zauna muyi magana."
  Shima Nameer ya masa maganar cikin tsawa dafe kansa Hisham yayi tare da zama yana kallon Nameer, kafaɗarsa Nameer ya dafa cikin taushin murya da alamun kwantar da hankali ya ce masa.
    "Cool down Hisham, ka nutsu, ba komai ake ɗauka da zafi ba, ka fahimci Abba mana gaskiya yake faɗ.........
    "Nameer! Ya isa bana son jin komai akan wadannan mutanen, ni ba ƴan uwana bane bana ƙaunarsu Nameer, ko dasu ko babu su zan iya rayuwata, meyasa dole sai nayi mu'amala da mutanen da basa ƙaunata Abba ya kasa gane hakan, Nameer ni kai ne ɗan uwana kaine rayuwata ka ishine rayuwa a matsayin jigo, idan kana kusa dani bazan nemi agajin kowa ba, ina jinka tamkar numfashina, akwai ka ƙyaleni bana buƙatar su."
    "Hmmm! Hisham jigo baifi jini ba, ko ka manta da karin maganar Hausawa, jini yafi ruwa kauri, duk yadda zan kai ga ƙaunarka bazan maye gurbin jininka ba, ka yiwa Abba biyayya, dan Allah, kasan Abba bazai cutar da kai ba, ka shirya anjuma muje gidan Mamabisolan."
   "Nameer Meyasa zaka tursasa zuciyata yin abinda bana so, ka sani bana iya musu da kai, tamkar Yayana na gaba dani haka nake kallonka."
   Numfashi Nameer ya saki yace.
   "Na sani Hisham, shiyasa nake son kayiwa Abba biyayya, meyasa baka sanar dani zuwan Tunde Company ba.?"
   "Saboda nasan zaka sauraresa har ka basa aiki shiyasa ban sanar da kai ba, sannan ka sani ina kan bakata baza'a bawa Tunda Tunde aiki a Companyn mu ba, maganar zuwa gidan Mamabisolan kuma tunda ka matsa zanje."
   Yayi Maganar yana saka kai ya kwanta saman bed ɗin, numfashi Nameer ya saki, domin kuwa har cikin ransa baya jin daɗin yadda Hisham ke jin tsanar Mamabisola, tollet ya tashi ya shige yabar Hisham kwance.


*********

 Da shirinta ta fito bayan ta gamawa Umma duk wasu ayyukan ta, a baranda ta samu Umma zaune, kallonta Umma tayi tace.
    "Ina zuwa kika fito cikin shiri bayan yau baki da aiki weekend ne.?"
   Sunkuyawa tayi tare da cewa.
     "Umma zamuje asibiti ne mu dubo wacce na ce miki moto ya bigeta mun kaita asibiti, ni da Basmah zamuje."
   Kanta Umma ta girgiza tace.
   "Ai na manta da zancen ma shaɗaf, to Allah ya tsare hanya ina mata sannu da jiki, amma ki dawo da wuri karki juma."
    "To shikenan Umma."
 Tayi Maganar tana miƙewa ta fice a ƙofar gidan nasu ta ga Abdullahi shida ABDULWASI'U suna zaune wajen shagonsa, kanta ta kawar tare da tsayawa ganin ta tsaya yasa Abdu tashi ya nufota.
    "Ina zakije Adda.?"
"Zanje asibiti ina fatan ka mayarwa da wannan mutumin kuɗinsa.?"
  "Ehh na maida masa kin bawa Umma kuɗin cefene ne.?"
  Dafe kanta Jidda tayi tace.
    "Kash kaga wallahi na manta ita kuma Umma bata tuna min ba."
 Tayi Maganar tana sanya hannu taja zif ɗin jakarta dubu da dari biyar ta bawa Abdu tace ya kaiwa Umma na cefene amsa Abdu yayi tare da mata a dawo lafiya kafin ya shiga cikin gidan ABDULWASI'U cike da so da ƙauna yake kallon  Jidda sai dai bata basa fuskar da zai mata magana ba, tunda yace yana sonta ta daina koda gaishesa, yana kallonta Basmah ta ƙariso da mai adaidaita ta shiga suka bar wajen.
    "Dama nayi hasashen yanzu zaki ƙariso shiyasa ma na fito, jiya na kwana da tunanin halin da wannan yarinyar ta ke Allah dai yasa ta dawo hankalinta."
    Jidda tayi maganar tana duban Basmah, numfashi Basmah ta saki tare da cewa.
   "Ai kuwa mun kwana da damuwa ɗaya, domin nima da ita na kwana a raina."
  Basmah tayi Maganar cike da tausayawa Yarinyar daidai sun iso harabar asibitin IBUNKU OLU CLINIC fita sukayi Jidda ta biya kuɗin kafin suka shiga basu sha wata wahala ba suka hango Aisha ƙanwar Ahmad zaune a bakin room ɗin da aka kwantar da Yarinyar, bayan sunyi sallama Aisha ta amsa Jidda ce tace.
    "Aisha sannu, ya mai jikin dai, ta farfaɗo kuwa, wai ma ya kike zaune a waje."?
   "Jikin nata da sauƙi sosai tama farfaɗo, likita ne a ciki shiyasa na fito waje, Aunty Basmah ina wuni."?
   "Lafiya Aisha sannu da ƙoƙari, Allah ya saka da alheri, amma jikin babu wata matsala tana gane mutane.?"
   "Ba matsalar komai tana gane mutane dan sanda ta farfaɗo naji tana kiran Abioye dana tambayeta waye ita sai tace Auntyn ta ne."
   Girgiza kanta Basmah tayi tace.
   "Okay to alhamdulillah."
Tayi Maganar dai-dai likita na fitowa, shiga sukayi suka sameta zaune jingine jikin gadon asibitin.
   "Sannu baiwar Allah, ya jiki."
  Sukayi maganar kusan a tare da kallon rashin sani take binsu kafin cikin raunanniyar murya ta amsa da.
  "Jiki da sauƙi."
  "Hmmm! Allah ya ƙara afuwa."
  Cewar Jidda kafin Basmah tace.
    "Baki gane mu ba ko, mune waɗanda muka kawo ki  asibitin nan sanda moto ya bigeki, sannu ya jikin dai."?
   Kafin tayi Magana Aisha tace.
   "Sune suka ceci rayuwarki, sai ki gode musu."
   Kanta ta girgiza tare da cewa.
    "Na gode sosai tabbas da badan ku ba da wataƙila labarin mutuwa ake yanzu na gode ƙwarya da kuka ceceni daga hannun wannan azzalumin, da yanzu rayuwata ta lalace, wayyo! Abioye kizo ki koma dani imo state bazan iya rayuwar wannan garin ba."
  Tayi Maganar cikin matsanancin kuka, cike da tausayin ta, Jidda ta zauna a bakin bed ɗin cikin tausayawa ta fara rarrashinta.
   "Ya Isa haka ki daina kukan ba lafiya gareki ba nutsuwa da hotu kike bukata naga duk kina firgice har yanzu, waye azzalumin kin sansa ne sannan su waye iyayenki da alamu kinsan wanda ya bigeki.?"
  Kafin tayi Magana Basmah tayi saurin cewa.
   "Haba Jidda meye haka na jera mata wannan tambayoyin Yarinyar da take gadon asibiti, kwance babu ƙwarin jiki kike aikin jarida akanta, kya bari ai ta samu sauƙi a sallamata kafin ki jero mata wadannan tambayoyin."
   Tsuka Jidda taja tare da cewa.
    "Ƙanwata rabu da ita, naga jikin naki da ƙwari, wanda ya miki wannan aikin yana bukatar a hukunta sa, ki faɗa min waye ne."?
  Kanta Yarinyar ta girgiza idanunta na kuma zubo da hawaye tace.
   "A'a bazan faɗa ba idan na faɗa miki yayi alƙawarin tozarta rayuwata ta yacce zan gwammaci mutuwa fiye da rayuwa, ina tsoro shi ɗan masu ji da mulki ne a ƙasar nan."
  Numfashi Jidda ta saki cikin ƙunar rai tace. 
   "Kalli ƙwayar idanuna ki gani akwai tsoron wani basarake ko mai kuɗi a cikin su, ki kalla da kyau ko akwai rashin amana da ƙwarin gwiwwa a tattare dani, ki kalla da kyau shin zaki iya yarda dani."?
   Idanunta Yarinyar ta saka cikin na Jidda ita dai Basmah tana tsaye tana kallon ikon Allah a wajen Jidda.
   "Ban hango tsoro ko rashin amana a tattare da ke ba, na yarda da kai sai dai shi ɗin hatsabibi ne zai iya cutar da Rayuwarki kema muddun kikayi ƙoƙarin taimakona."
   "Wai shin waye wannan ya tsorata rayuwarki haka na faɗa miki ki yarda dani babu abinda zai samu Rayuwarki sai ma ƴanci da zaki samu muddun kika bani haɗin kai ki faɗa min waye shi sannan kema menene sunanki ya...........
    "Jidda wai mey...........
"Ya isa Basmah ki daina katseni ki barni na kai aya, na ce ki faɗa min waye wanda yaso kasheki waye wannan wanda bai san mutuncin mace ba, wani dabban ne ki faɗa min."
  Jidda tayi maganar cikin tafasar zuciya, a tsorace cikin rawar baki yarinyar tace.  "Na....na......na... Nameer ab..dulzahir, Iyabo masu Companyn *_HISHAM RICE MILL LIMITED_* shi..shi...shi ne yaso yamin fyade NAMEER."
   Wani razana Jidda da Basmah sukayi lokaci guda har Aisha sanda Maganar ta soki zuciyarta Basmah ne tace.
    "Whatttttt! Ke Nameer Abdulzahir Iyabo kike faɗa, Iyabo dai wanda muka sani shahararren attajirin da babu kamar sa a wannan jihar tamu, shi kike faɗa, capp lallai kam babu yadda zakiyi Jidda muddun Nameer Iyabo ne babu abinda zaki iya a kansa."
  Tayi Maganar cikin yanayin mamaki da damuwa domin kuwa zata iya rantsuwa da Allah NAMEER bazai ɗauru ba ba kuma zai kamu ba, murmushi Jidda ta saki na takaici tace.
    "Yaruba Man nawawo, oh Rapping, rapping ɗinki zaiyi, Mema sunansa Nameer ko menene.?"
   Aisha ce tace.
  "Nameer, gaskiya Aunty Basmah ta ke faɗa miki Nameer hukuma ko wata ƙungiya bazasu iya kamasa ba saboda ƙarfin kuɗi da iko na Alhaji Iyabo."
     "Ko waye ubansa sai ya kamu domin kuwa case ɗin fyaɗe, dawamammene akan dukkan wanda ya aikata ko yayi ƙoƙarin aikatawa, komai kuɗi da ikon ubansa sai ya tozarta tunda shi ɗan Akuya ne mai dabbar zuciya."

"Hmmm! Jidda ki tsaya iyakar matsayin ki, karki jawowa kanki da Mahaifiyar ki rigimar da bazaku iya ɗauka ba, Iyabo ba ƙaramin mutum bane a ƙasar nan ya wuce duk tunaninki daga Nigeria har ƙasashen duniya sunansa ya fantsama, dan haka ki riƙe mutuncin ki ki ƙyale wannan maganar."
   Murmushin takaici Jidda ta saki cike da jin haushin maganar  Basmah tace.
    "Kin bani mamaki Basmah ban taɓa zaton bakya kishi da abinda ya samu mace ƴar Uwar ki ba, idan ƙanwarki aka yiwa haka zakice abar Maganar ne, na tabbata ko da mutuwa zakiyi zakice sai kin ƙwato mata ƴancinta, amma da shike ita wannan ba jininki ba ce shine kike cewa abar Maganar, wallahi bazan bar maganar ba koda zan mutu ta sanadiyyar haka sai na ƙwato mata ƴancinta, sai ya fuskanci hukunci komai gata da ikon ubansa Allah yana tare da mai gaskiya nayi imanin bazai bari na tozarta ba zai kareni, na ɗauki wannan kasadar."
    "Hmmm! Jidda kenan yau ni kike faɗawa baƙar magana Saboda kawai na ce miki ki kula, Jidda tunda har kika min kuskuren fahimta a tsawon zamana dake tun yarinta, shekaru ashirin da biyu muna tare ai shekara ashirin da biyu ba kwana ashirin da biyu bane, idan wani yamin abinda kika min yau bazanji haushi ba, sai dai ki sani bazan fasa faɗa miki gaskiya ba saboda ina ƙaunarki bana ƙaunar abinda zai taɓa rayuwarki, amma idan kin zaɓi rayuwa a cikin tsoro da fargaba, bazan iya hanaki tafiyar da rayuwar ki yadda kike so ba amma ki sani bazaki iya faɗa da ahalin Iyabo ba."
   Basmah tayi Maganar cikin ɓacin rai tana saka kai tabar room ɗin, yarinyar ce ta share hawayen ta tare da duban Jidda tace.
   "Gaskiya take faɗa miki bazaki iya ja dasu ba domin kuwa suna da ƙarfin da bamu da kwatansa, ni na barsu da Allah kema dan Allah kibar maganar bazan yafewa kaina ba muddun wani mummunan abu ya sameki a dalilina."
   "Ƙwarai kuwa gaskiya ne Aunty Basmah ta faɗa miki Aunty JIDDA dan Allah mubar wannan maganar mu barsu da Allah shine mai sakayya akan duk wanda aka zalunta dan Allah."
  Aisha tayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki, shuru Jidda tayi kafin zuwa can ta dubi yarinyar tace.
    "Meye sunanki.?"
"Hajara." Ta bata amsa a taƙaice.
   "Okay ki amshi wayar Aisha ki kira wani daga cikin gidan ku, bara naje na dawo."
  Kanta Hajara ta ɗaga kafin Jidda ta tashi ta fice, Basmah ta hau dubawa cikin asibitin babu ita babu alamunta hakanne ya tabbatarwa Jidda Basmah gida ta koma, kanta ta kaɗa tare da ficewa ta tari adaidaita tace ya Kaita police station.
    A bakin station ɗin mai adaidaita ya sauketa ta basa kuɗinsa kafin ta shiga ciki, First Dpo taso gani suka ƙi barinta ta gansa, hakanne yasa ta shigar da ƙarar NAMEER ABDULZAHIR IYABO, a tsorace ɗan sandan ya ɗago ya kalleta tare da cewa.
    "NAMEER kike tuhuma da Case ɗin fyaɗe? Bazamu amshi Case ɗin nan ba."
   Murmushi Jidda ta saki tare da ɗauko id card ɗin ta na aikin jarida ta nuna masa tare da cewa.
   "Dama nasan zaiyi wahala ku bari a shigar da ƙara a kansa, sai kamar yadda ka gani a wannan ID CARD ɗin ni ƴar jarida ce wanda mutanen wannan ƙasar suke riƙe jaridar mu a ko wacce rana dan haka muddun baku amshi wannan ƙaran ba to wallahi zakuji mummunan labari akan wannan Police station ɗin naku, yadda ko wannenki sai ya rasa aikinsa."
  Tayi Maganar tana ja gefe ta tsaya shuru ɗan sandan yayi a tsorace kafin ya miƙe cikin sanyin jiki ya nufi office ɗin Dpo Abbas Marafa, yayi nocking kafin dpo ya basa umarnin shiga, shiga yayi tare da sara masa.
    "Sargent Bala ya akayi."?
    "Sir wata matsala ce take shirin faruwa."
    "Matsala kuma Sargent Bala, ta mecece."?
   "Wata ƴar jarida ce ta shigo yanzu take shigar da ƙara akan NAMEER ABDULZAHIR IYABO."
    "Whatt! Nameer to wani laifi take zarginsa dashi."?
    Dpo yayi Maganar cike da mamaki domin kuwa bai taɓa jin wanda ya kawo ƙorafin ya'yan gidan ba sai yau kowa albarka yake musu da godiya saboda kyautatawarsu ga talakawa musamman NAMEER.
    "Sir akan Case ɗin fyaɗe ne wai ya yi ƙoƙarin yiwa wata yarinya fyaɗe yanzu haka Yarinyar wai tana gadon asibiti kwance tana jinya."
   Dafe kansa Dpo yayi tare da cewa.
    "First time da aka fara kawo case mummuna akan ahalin Iyabo, is okay ka ɗebi jami'an mu kuje asibitin ku fara tabbatar da maganar ta, idan ya tabbata to wajibine aje ayi arresting ɗin NAMEER saboda kace yarinyar ƴar jarida ce gudun ɓatawa wannan station ɗin suna da kuma kare kakin mu ya zamo wajibi mu amshi Case ɗin, kana iya tafiya."
  "Yes Sir."
Sargent Bala yayi Maganar yana ficewa numfashi Dpo ya saki har yanzu bai daina mamaki ba.
    "Allah ya kyauta."
Shine abinda Dpo ya furta ya cigaba da aikinsa.
   Ko da Sargent Bala ya fito harara ya watsawa JIDDA tare da cewa.
     "Ana ganin mutum Allah ya rufa masa asiri za'a fara tunanin ta yadda za'a tona masa, to ta Allah ba taku ba insha Allah sharrinku a kanku zai ƙare."
   Yayi Maganar yana fita ya haɗa jami'ai huɗu ya kuma leƙowa yace.
   "Sai kizo muje asibitin mu tabbatar da ƙaryar taki."
   Bakinta Jidda ta taɓe tare d da bin bayansa asibitin sukaje Sargent Bala da kansa ya yiwa Hajara tambayoyi ta kuma tabbatar da cewa lallai Nameer shine yaso mata fyaɗe, hakan yasa Sargent Bala da Jidda da sauran Police ɗin suka nufi IYABO Estate.
   A bakin get ɗin farko na gidan Soja suka tare su duk da ganin motar Police ne, har sai da suka nuna takaddar ijinin da aka basu, sannan suka barsu suka shige, zuwa get na biyu wanda yake ɓangaren su Nameer, mai gadi shine ya buɗe musu get ɗin suka shiga, kafin suka fito tare da bawa maigadi umarnin ya shiga ya sanar da cewa ƴan sanda suna neman NAMEER, da ganinsu alaramma mai gadi bai yarda dasu ba sai dai ba tare da tsoro ba ya nufi cikin babban falon nasu kasancewar yasan ƴan sanda sun saba zuwa neman alfarma gidan, JIDDA da kallo take bin girma da tsaruwan gidan yanda akayi asarar dukiya wajen ginasa, bakinta ta taɓe ko kaɗan gidan bai burgeta ba.
   A falo mai gadi ya samu Ummi da Tutu, Ummi ce ta dubi mai gadi tace.
    "Alaramma ya akayi akwai abinda kake bukata ne.?"
    "A'a Uwar gida babu abinda nake buƙata Police sukazo wai suna son ganin Oga NAMEER, sai dai Uwar gida a yanda na gansu kamar ba lafiya ba."
  Murmushi Ummi ta saki tace.
    "Meye abun damuwa saboda Police suna bukatar ganin NAMEER sun saba zuwa neman alfarma dan yauma bana rabe abinda ya kawo su kenan, ke Tutu jeki yiwa NAMEER magana yaje ya samesu."
  Girgiza kanta Tutu tayi cike da tsarguwa da rashin yadda tace.
   "No UMMI nima hankalina bai kwanta da yaya Nameer ya fita ya samesu ba gaskiya domin basu saba zuwa wajen sa, wajen Abba suke zuwa kai tsaye, dole dai akwai wata damuwar to amma wacce irin damuwa ce zaisa Police suzo neman yaya NAMEER, Ummi muje mu fara jin meya kawo su tukunna."
   "Ƙwarai kuwa Uwar gida gaskiya Tutu take faɗi domin kuwa naga harda wata yarinya budurwa suka shigo sai famar haɗa rai take da alamu dai babu alkairi a tare da ita."
     Alaramma mai gadi yayi Maganar cikin kulawa, Tutu ce tace.
   "To kinji Ummi muje dai mu duba kafin a kira su yaya NAMEER ɗin."
   "Gamu ko ba'a kira mu ba."
   Sukaji muryar NAMEER ya doki kunnen su gabaki ɗayansu idanu suka ɗaga suka kalle su suna sanye cikin gezna baƙa iri ɗaya sun kafa baƙar hula sosai shigar ta musu kyau tamkar ƴan biyu haka suka fito, Ummi murmushi tayi tace.
    "To gara da kuka zo kuwa, daru nake da wadannan rigimammun, wai Police ne sukazo nemanka, shine ƙanwarka ta ce baza'a kiraka ba sai anje anji abinda ya kawo su wai basu saba zuwa neman alfarma wajenka ba, wajen Abban ku suke zuwa, shi kuma wannan ustazun ƙaryar yace min bai yarda dasu ba wai harda wata mace suka taho, to koma dai menene ai banga abin tsoro ba, domin kuwa marassa gaskiya su ke razana ba masu gaskiya ba, kunji abinda yake faruwa."
   Hisham ne yace.
 "Zancen banza zuwan Police gidan nan kuma har wani abun mamaki ne, ƴan maula ne karnuka, ƙashi kawai suke nema da zarar an jefa musu zasu ƙara gaba, muje NAMEER bani da lokaci."
   Kansa NAMEER ya girgiza kafin yace.
    "Ƴan sanda sune ƙashin bayan al'umma Hisham sam mummunar magana akansu bai dace ba, da babu su bazaka iya tafiya kaje inda kake so cikin kwanciyar hankali ba, dan haka ka dinga faɗar magana mai kyau a kansu."
    "Faɗa masa dai domin kuwa naga alamar Hisham ya tsani Police da yawa, kuje ku samesu kuji abinda yake tafe dasu." Ummi tayi maganar.
   Bakinsa Hisham ya taɓe tare da yin gaba, bayansa NAMEER yabi yana cewa.
    "Muje Alaramma."
 Bayan NAMEER shima yabi suka fita, Tutu miƙewa tayi zata bisu Ummi ta dakatar da ita da cewa.
    "Ina zakije, ba aiki na sanya ki ba, maza shige kije ki cigaba da aikin dana saki.?"
    "Ummi ki bari naje naji tukunna sai na dawo na ƙarisa aikin."
   "Bazan bari ba, bana son shashancin banza ki shige kije kimin abinda na saki."
  Bakinta Tutu ta tura tare da shigewa kitchen.



A bakin motar Police ɗin suka tsaya, Nameer ne ya dubi Sargent Bala tare da sakin murmushi yace.
     "Sargent Bala kaine kuma baka kirani ba, meke faruwa domin kuwa yadda na ganka dole akwai Magana kai ɗaya ka saba nemana yau kuma na ganku ku shida."?
   Shuru Sargent Bala yayi cike da fargaba da tsoron yadda zai sanar da NAMEER abinda ya kawo su, HISHAM ne yace.
    "Kai Magana ake maka ko kurma ne kawai, mtsss ko da yake ba sai kayi magana ba, neman kuɗi ya kawo ku ba."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana sanya hannunsa cikin aljihu ya zaro rafar 500 ya wurga musu tare da cigaba da cewa.
   "Gashi sauri muke bamu da lokacin ɓatawa, muje NAMEER."
   Hisham yayi Maganar cikin gadara da iko gami da wulaƙanci, cikin jin zafin abinda HISHAM yayi Jidda take bin kuɗin da kallo tare da ɗago kanta ta furta.
    "Tirrr! Allah ya wadaran mutumin da zai wulaƙanta ɗan adam akan wasu ababen duniya, himm! Wai ɗan sanda jagoran al'umma ake wulaƙantawa ake jefa masa kuɗi saboda sun lalata mutuncin kakinsu da son abun duniya har ake musu kallon ƙasƙantattun mutane kare ake jefawa abu ba mutum ɗan Adam ba, gashi kuma kun sunkuyar da kanku kun kasa faɗa musu abinda ya kawo ku, to sunce su sauri suke basu da lokacin ɓatawa tunda ku kuma kuna da lokacin jiransu sai mu samu waje mu zauna mu jira har su dawo ko."?
   Jidda tayi maganar tana jefawa Hisham mungun kallo Nameer sai yanzu ya ɗago idanunsa ya kalleta, Hisham kuwa murmushi ya saki zaiyi Magana NAMEER ya ɗaga masa hannu tare da cewa.
    "Sargent Bala ɗago idanunka ka kalleni ka faɗa min meya kawo ku."
   Sai yanzu Sargent Bala ya ɗago kai tare da cewa.
   "Ranka ya daɗe ƙara aka shigar akanka shine Dpo ya mana umarnin muzo muyi arresting ɗinka."
     "Ƙara kuma aka shigar a kaina? Waye ya shigar da ƙara a kaina kuma akan me yake tuhumata.?"
   Sargent Bala Jidda ya nuna da yatsa tare da cewa.
   "Wannan itace ta shigar da ƙorafi akanka, akan Case ɗin fyaɗe, kayi haƙuri ranka ya daɗe muje office sai ka ƙarisa jin Case ɗin."
  Wani irin hasala HISHAM yayi tare da jefawa Jidda kallon tsana domin kuwa tun a maganganun ta na farko ya fuskanci tacacciyar marar kunya ce.
    "Whattttttt! Kai kasan abinda kake faɗa kuwa NAMEER ake tuhuma da Case na fyaɗe, hmmm! Da alamu ku mahaukata ne da kuka debo ƙafa kuka sako wannan Yar tashar a gaba wai kunzo kama Nameer, to bara kuji ko da NAMEER ya aikata wannan case ɗin bazai kamu ba, bare kuma ɗan uwana bazai taɓa aikata wannan ƙazamin aikin ba, mtsss nonsense! Let go NAMEER."
     "Hmmm! Ɗan tasha shi ke gane ɗan tasha, ashe kuwa kaine cikakken ɗan tasha tunda har ka gane ƴar tasha hmmm! Wasu dai ba mutane ba domin kuwa sam basa gane gabas bare kuma yamma."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana taɓe bakinta, Hisham hannu ya ɗaga zai ɗauketa da mari, Nameer yayi saurin riƙe hannunsa yana girgiza masa kai.
    "Nameer meyasa zaka riƙe hannuna akan wannan daƙiƙiyar jaka ƴar matsiyata, waye ita meye ta ke taƙama dashi da har zata tako ƙafarta cikin gidan nan ta taka mu, tabbas kinyi kuskuren da baki taɓa irinsa ba a rayuwar ki, tunda har kika fara tunanin taɓa ɗan uwana to ki fara ƙirga ranakun tashin hankalin ki, duk wanda ya taɓa ɗan uwana na rantse da Allah sai naga ƙarshen sa, wallahi sai na tozarta rayuwarki da ta ahalinki, daga yau kin shiga komar HISHAM ABDULZAHIR IYABO iyayenki ma kwanciyar hankali sai ta gagaresu zaki ga yadda ake tsantsar tozarci ƴar matsiyatan talakawa."

*_Page 7 to 8_*




Murmushi Jidda tayi tare da harɗe hannunta a ƙirji tana yiwa Hisham kallon mahaukaci Nameer ne yace.
   "Ya isa haka Hisham, meye haka meya kawo iyaye cikin wannan maganar, iyaye suna da mahimmancin da taka su mungun haɗari ne a rayuwa, koda basu haifeka idan sun kai girman haifar kamarka to iyayenmu zamu kirasu, karna kuma jin ka zagi iyayenta, please Hisham ka dinga dannar zuciyar ka."
    "Da ka barsa ya cigaba da zagin domin kuwa mahaukacin kare shike haushi, Malam Hisham dan Allah karka fasa tozarta ni, idan kuma ka fasa to baka amsa sunan gidan ku ba, idan kai mahaukaci ne ni kuma likitar mahaukata ne zanyi maganin haukarka, karka fasa."
   Jidda tayi maganar tana jan doguwar tsuka tare da juyawa ga Sargent Bala ta cigaba da cewa.
    "Idan kunga dama ku kamasa idan kunso ku ƙyalesa, ni kuma bazan fasa ƙudiri na a kanku ba ku tuna da wacece ni."
  Tayi Maganar tana juyawa tabar wajen ba tare da ta tsaya taji me Sargent Bala zai ce ba.
   Wani irin hura zuciyar Hisham yake tunda yake a tarihin rayuwarsa babu wanda ya taɓa tsayawa a gabansa ya faɗa masa magana yadda ta masa cikin tsawa mai rikitarwa Hisham ya jawo kwalar Sargent Bala yace.
     "Waye ita! Dame ta dogara waye ya ɗaure mata gindi ƴar uban waye Minister ne ko  shugaban ƙasa ko gomna ka faɗa min na rantse da Allah sai na tozarta ta sai na bambance mata tsakanin aya da tsakuwa, sai na zamo sanadiyyar lalacewar rayuwarta idan ban aikata hakan ba ni ba jinin Iyab..............
   Saurin toshewa Hisham baki NAMEER yayi yana janye hannunsa daga rigar Sargent Bala, cikin taushin murya NAMEER yace.
     "Cool down Hisham ba komai ake ɗagawa hankali ba, kar ƙaramin abu ya tayar maka da hankali ma, kayi haƙuri ka koma ciki ni zanje station ɗin rago shike tsorata marar gaskiya kuwa gumi yake bana da matsala ko jin zafin akan Sargent Bala yazo tafiya dani aikinsa yake ka barsa yayi aikinsa kaje ka sanar da Abba abinda yake faruwa, muje Sargent Bala."
    "Bazai yiwu ba Nameer mutuncika ya wuce ka shiga cikin motar Police, bazai yiwu ba hakan ba mai yiwuwa bane, Sargent Bala kaje ka cewa Dpo ni Hisham nace NAMEER bazai zoba, ku fice ku bar gidan nan ko na sunce muku karnuka suyi maganinku."
   A ɗan tsorace Sargent Bala ya ɗago ya kalli Hisham domin kuwa yasan zai aikata duk abinda ya furta NAMEER ne yace.
    "Sargent Bala kuje zanzo a motar gida ɗan uwana baya son na shiga motarku bazan shiga ba amma zanzo karka sanar da Dpo sakonsa domin kuwa baku da laifi aikinku kuke kuje."
  Kansa Sargent Bala ya ɗaga tare da bawa sauran Police ɗin umarnin su tafi, NAMEER kallon Hisham yayi tare da cewa.
    "Baka cika bibiyar jarida bane shiyasa baka santa ba, tabbas zata iya ɓatawa station ɗin su suna muddun basuyi abinda tace ba, ba ƴar kowa bace face ƴar Jaridar Aminci mataimakiyar shugaban Aminci na santa domin kuwa na juma ina son na kai mata ziyara sai dai kuma gashi ita ta riga kawo min, naji daɗin hakan sosai."
  Cike da rashin fahimta Hisham yace.
    "So whatt! Idan ƴar Jaridar ce, tukunna ma ya akayi kasan wani abu a kanta kana da alaƙa da ita ne.?"
    "Hmmm! Yanzu ba lokacin tambaya bane Hisham, ka koma ciki ka sanar da Abba abinda ke faruwa ni zanje station ɗin sannan karka fasa zuwa gidan Mamabisola kamar yadda muka shirya dan Allah kaje."
   "Ta yaya zan Barka ka shiga cikin station kai ɗaya sam bazan iya barinka ba tunda ka dage sai kaje sai dai mu tafi tare babu gidan Mamabisolan da zanje muje kawai."
   "Bazamu tafi ba tare da ummi tasan abinda ke faruwa ba, kaje ka sanar da ita ta yadda zata fahimta ka tabbatar hankalinta bai tashi ba."
  Kansa Hisham ya girgiza tare da juyawa cikin gidan cikin wani irin mungun yanayi, yana shigewa NAMEER ya buɗe mota da sauri sauri ya shiga tare da jan motar ya fice.


 Falon ya koma cikin sanyin jiki tare da zama gefen Ummi, Ummi cike da mamaki take dubansa tare da cewa.
   "Lafiya Hisham ka shigo haka cikin wani irin yanayi, ina shi NAMEER ɗin yake, kun fasa zuwa gidan Mamabisolan ne.?"
   Ummi ta jefa masa tambayar daidai Tutu na shigowa falon ta zauna gefen su.
    "Tambaya uku Ummi kika min ajere sai dai kafin na baki amsar ki, ina da bukatar wata amsa daga gareki, Ummi meyasa ya sanda basu da amana meyasa basu san mai basu ba, Ummi shin dama muna da maƙiya ne wadanda zasu iya mana mungun ƙazafi, ana niyyar tozarta min ɗan uwana! Kuma wallahi bazan bar hakan ba sai na sanya duk mai hannu cikin wulaƙanta min ɗan uwana cikin JALALAR da bazasu taɓa iya fita ba, wannan yarinyar da ta samu zarrar kai ƙarar ɗan uwana Police station da mungun ƙazafi harma ta tako cikin gidan nan take faɗa min Magana son ranta Hmm Ummi! Sai na hana rayuwarta bacci a yayin da ni zanyi bacci a cikin zuciyarta, na rantse da Allah sai tayi danasanin taɓo ni, ta tsokono rigimar da bazata iya ba, wuta ce wacce zata babbakata ta ɗauko bazan taɓa ƙyaleta ba."
   Tunda ya fara Maganar Ummi take kallonsa cikin rashin fahimta da tashin hankali, tafi kowa sanin ɗanta mutum ne mai zafin zuciya da saurin fushi, bai cika yafiya ba, ba kuma a dannarsa ya dannu numfashi Ummi ta saki cikin yanayin damuwa tace.
     "Hisham yanayin maganganun ka da yadda ka hasala ya ɗaga min hankali domin kuwa hakan na gwada min cewa ƴan sandan da sukazo sun tafi da NAMEER, hmmm! Wani laifi NAMEER ya aikata wanda har zai kai Police su zo su tafi dashi, NAMEER ɗane mafi soyuwa a cikin zuciyata bazan taɓa bari wani mummunan abu ya samesa ba, rayuwata ne shi, bazan bar wani abu ya samesa ba, dan haka sanar dani wani laifi ake zarginsa dashi, nasan sharrin magauta ne domin kuwa kaf ƴaƴana babu mai jawo rigimar da zata tada hankalin mu."
   "Yaya Hisham meya faru da Yaya Nameer dama ni hankalina bai kwanta da zuwan wadannan Police ɗin ba, yanzu ina yaya Nameer ɗin."
   "Me yake faruwa ina daga sama ina jiyo hayaniyar ku, Hisham me nace maka ɗazu ba cewa nayi ka shirya kaje ka gaishe da Mamabisola ba ina shi NAMEER ɗin yake."?
   Suka jiyo Muryar Abba daga saman step yana tsaye, numfashi Hisham ya sauƙe tare da ɗaga kansa yace.
    "Abba ƴan sanda sun kama NAMEER wai suna zarginsa da Case ɗin fyaɗe."
    "Whattttttt!"
 Abba ya furta da ƙarfi Ummi wani irin sarawa kanta yayi itama tace.
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un, Nameer ɗin nawa shine aka yiwa wannan ƙazafin innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un na shiga Uku, Abban Hisham Case ɗin fyaɗe fa babban Case ne."
   "Ƙwarai kuwa babban Case ne, hmm! Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Bana tunanin cewa NAMEER zai aikata wannan laifin, Case ɗin fyaɗe."
   Abba yayi Maganar cikin tashin hankali, yana sauƙowa tsakiyar falon, Hisham runtse idanunsa yayi kafin yace.
    "Abba ka kira a sakar min ɗan uwana, wannan yarinyar da ta masa ƙazafi wallahi Abba bazan ƙyaleta ba."
  Zama Abba yayi yana kallon Ummi da hankalinta yayi mungun tashi har gumi take haɗawa ya cewa Hisham.
    "Wacce yarinya ce takai Case ɗin.?"
   "Abba wata ce naji ma suna cewa ƴar Jarida ce, har ikirari take cewa muddun Police ɗin basu amshi Case ɗin ba sai ta yi sanadiyar aikinsu, na rasa gane wacece ita da me take taƙama ƴar uban waye, kuma kamar tsoronta Police ɗin sukeji, ni dai Abba ka sanya a sako min ɗan uwana."
  Numfashi Abba ya saki yace.
   "Bazai yiwu ba Hisham, a yanzu bani da ikon sanyawa a saki Nameer Case ɗin fyaɗe babban Case ne da duk gatanka baza'a iya fitar da kai ba, idan naje na ce a saki NAMEER tamkar na zubar da mutuncin gidan nan ne domin kuwa duniya ɗauka zatayi ɗana yayi laifi nayi amfani da ƙarfi da dukiyata na sanya an sakesa bare kuma Case da ƴan jarida aciki dan haka mu ƙyale Nameer gaskiyar sa ta fitar dashi."
    A wani irin zabure Ummi ta miƙe tare da cewa.
    "Me kake nufi Abban Hisham! Kana nufin mu bar Nameer ya tozarta a ɓata masa suna ƙarshe ya ƙare rayuwarsa a gidan yari bayan kana da hanyoyin da zaka saka a sakesa, meyasa zamu barsa a wulaƙance, ko dan saboda ba ɗanka na cikinka HISHAM aka kama ba, ina ƙaunar Nameer fiye da yadda nake jin Hisham a cikin raina ji nake tamkar ni na shayar dashi nono bazan taɓa bari ya tozarta ba, Abban Hisham ya zamo wajibi a kanka ka sanya a sako min ɗana."
   Abba kallonta kawai yake tare da miƙewa shima yace.
    "Hmmm! Mata kenan duk girman ku, hankalin ku kaɗan sam baku da dogon lissafi a tunaninki kin fini son Nameer ne, ko da kaso ɗaya a cikin goma na Soyayyar Nameer da nakeji a raina baki ɗauki ɗaya ba, domin kuwa Soyayyar da nake masa yasa na sanya wasiyyar koda bayan raina ne a raba dukiyata biyu a bawa NAMEER kaso ɗaya sauran kaso ɗaya kuma iyalina suyi tarayya a kai ki faɗa min idan ba Soyayya ba me zaisa nayi haka, shin yanzu kina jin kin fini sonsa ne, ki faɗa min."?
     Bakinta Ummi ta toshe tare da silalewa ƙasa ta zauna tana sakin wani irin kuka mai cin rai Hisham kuwa zuciyarsa tafasa take, cikin wani irin yanayi na damuwa ya dubi Abba.
    "Abba kana nufin bazaka sanya a saki ɗan uwana ba, ina bazai yiwu ba Abba ka sake tunani ya zamo dole ɗan uwana ya kwana a gida yau zanje Police station ɗin kafin fito ya zamo wajibi a kanka mu dawo tare da Nameer, ki daina kuka Ummi bazan taɓa bari NAMEER ya wulaƙanta ba zanyi duk yadda zanyi ya fito ko da kuwa babu ƙarfin Abba."
   Yayi Maganar yana saka kai zai bar wajen Abba ne ya kira Sunansa.
   "Kai Hisham ka dakata karkaje ka aikata wani kuskuren."
  Juyowa HISHAM yayi a fusace tare da cewa.
   "Kuskure! Hmmm! Domin gyara wani kuskuren dole sai an tafka wani kuskuren, tunda baka da karfin dawo da Nameer ni zan nemo wannan ƙarfin."
    Yayi Maganar tare da ficewa Abba na kiransa ko sake waiwayowa baiyi ba, Tutu share hawayen fuskarta tayi tare da miƙewa ta haura sama.


 Hisham yana fita Police station ɗin ya nufa abun baƙin ciki da takaici Nameer ya iske a cell yana zaune tamkar wani marar galihu idanunsa ya runtse cike da baƙin ciki, wajen Sargent Bala ya nufa tare da tsayawa a gabansa yace.
   "Sargent Bala waye ita meye sunanta ka bani bayani akanta har adress ɗin ta ina bukata."
   Yayi Maganar yana watsawa Sargent Bala kuɗi, kuɗin Sargent Bala ya ɗauka tare da ɗago kai ya dubi Hisham.
      "Jidda Muhammad Bello, cikakkiyar ƴar Jarida, ƴar unguwar ORA layin igbaja gida mai number 234."
   Numfashi Hisham ya saki tare da kuma cewa.
    "Ina Dpo ina son magana dashi."
    "Dpo yana ciki zaka iya shiga."
  Kansa Hisham ya kaɗa tare da shigewa nocking yayi kafin Dpo ya basa izinin shigowa.
    Shiga Hisham yayi tare da tsayuwa ya harɗe hannunsa a ƙirjinsa Dpo dubansa yayi cike da mutuntawa yace.
    "Hisham Barka da zuwa Bismillah ka zauna mana."
  Kujerar Hisham ya duba tare da takowa ya zauna yana cewa.
    "Barka Dpo kayi mamakin ganina ba."
   "A'a banyi mamaki ba duba da ɗan uwanka yana station ɗin nan, nasan dole zakazo sai dai bansan da wata bukatar ka shigo office ɗina ba.?"
   "Ka san bukatar da ta shigo dani, belin NAMEER nake bukatar ka bani, duk da kunyi kuskuren ijiyesa a inda yafi ƙarfin zama."
    "Sorry Hisham da ace zamu iya bada belin NAMEER to da bazamu je mu kamasa ba, kayi haƙuri bamu da damar sakin NAMEER har sai bincike ya tabbatar cewa bashi da laifin da ake zargin sa, saboda Case ɗin sa mai girma ne."
 Cike da mamaki Hisham ke kallon Dpo tare da cewa.
    "Whatt! Dpo kasan abinda kake faɗa min kuwa, Dpo wannan itace kawai alfarmar da zaka mana a rayuwa karka manta Matsayin da kake kai yanzu silar arzikin mu ne, sannan ka saka a ranka muddun NAMEER ya fito zaka haura wannan matsayin, well idan ma bazaka iya kyauta ba, ka faɗi farashin ni zan biya ko nawa kake bukata, koma ya za'ayi NAMEER ya fita daga wannan kurkukun da kuka jefasa."
   Murmushi Dpo yayi yace.
   "Ka kasa fahimta ta Hisham, tabbas kun min rana domin kuwa ko wani irin matsayi na kai kune sila sai sakin NAMEER kan iya jawowa na rasa aikina gabaki ɗaya, karka manta Case ɗin fyaɗe ake tuhumar sa dashi wanda gata da faɗa aji gami da dukiyarka bazasu taɓa ƙwatarka ba, da fito da NAMEER zai yiwu to da mahaifinku shine mutumin da zai fara kirana, naso ace ina da halin taimakawa NAMEER da sai na fi kowa farin ciki.''
    "Hmm! Okay na fahimta idan kai bazaka iya ba akwai na gaba da kai, sai dai ka sani ka yiwa kanka babbar asara."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana tashi tare da ɗaukar wayarsa ya fice numfashi Dpo ya saki tare da bin ƙofar da kallo ya furta.
    "Ikonku da kuɗinku bazai taɓa fitar da NAMEER ba sai dai gaskiya da tsantsar bincike ya fitarsa, kana da matsala Hisham zafin rai gami da izza sun maka yawa uwa ba ƙasƙantar da Mutum."
  Dpo yayi Maganar yana mai da kansa ga aikin sa.


  Ko da Hisham ya fito bakin cell ɗin ya tsaya cikin ɓacin rai murmushi Nameer ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Wai damuwar me kake ne Hisham, akan wannan ƙaramin lamarin kake damuwa.?"
   "Ƙaramin lamari wannan kake ƙaramin lamari, wankin hula na shirin kaimu ga dare amma kana kiransa ƙaramin lamari, Hmm! Gaskiya ko waye ya shirya wannan tarkon yayi dogon lissafi akanmu, ina zuwa."
 Hisham yayi Maganar yana juyawa zai bar wajen, yaji muryar NAMEER.
    "Ina Ummi da Abba ina fatan hankalin su bai tashi ba."
   "Hmmm! Kaima kasan dole hankalinsu zai tashi."
  Yayi Maganar yana saka kai ya fice daga Police station ɗin motarsa ya shiga yaja karfi babu inda ya nufi sara Ora cikin mintuna kaɗan ya samu damar shiga unguwar baisha wahala ba wajen gane layin igbaja, kasancewar yasan wajen, parking yayi a ƙofar gida mai 234 tare da harɗe hannunwansa.



  *********


 A tsugunne taga Basmah gaban Umma cikin sanyin jiki ta ƙarisa domin kuwa tasan duk yadda akayi Basmah ƙarar ta ta kawo, ƙarisawa tayi tare da zama gefe tana cewa.
     "Umma sannu da hutawa."
   A hasale Umma tace.
      "Sannun gidan ubanki, nace sannun gidan ubanki, Jidda ashe dama mahaukaciya ce ke ban sani ba, so kike ni ki kasheni ki raya iyayen wasu, ina kiranki da sunan Alkairi kullum sai kuma gashi kina niyyar Kawo min masifa ina ke ina faɗa da ahalin Abdulzahir Hmm! Tabbas cikakkiyar mahaukaciya ce ke Jidda."
    Idanunta Jidda ta runtse tare da jin haushin Basmah cikin sanyin murya tace.
    "Umma mahaukaciya kuma, kenan Umma saboda su masu kuɗi ne shine sai mu bari su zalunci talakawa muyi shuru Umma kefa kikace na taimaki duk wanda na gani yana bukatar taimako, muddun ina da halin taimaka masa meyasa zan gani nayi shuru bayan zan iya bata gudunmawa Umm........
   "Ki rufe min baki dan ubanki! Da nace ki taimaki al'umma cewa nayi kisa Rayuwarki a cikin haɗari, ita yarinyar bata da iyayene da zasu kai ƙara sai ke gwali, duk masifar da muka shiga a baya bai isa haka ba Jidda har sai kin ƙara ɗauko min wata masifar so kike bakin ciki ya kasheni da badan wannan Yarinyar Yar albarka Basmah tazo ta sanar dani ba da shikenan sai ki jawo mana masifa, to muddun ni ce uwarki, ki cire hannunki a cikin wannan Case ɗin."
   Kanta ta sunkuyar ƙasa tana sauraren faɗan mahaifiyarta kafin ta ɗago tace.
    "Kiyi haƙuri Umma."
"Ba haƙurinki nake son ji ba, ki fita daga cikin wannan faɗan da babu ruwanki tashi ki bani waje."
   Umma tayi Maganar tana tashi ta shige ciki, ɗago idanunta tayi ta kalli Basmah tare da girgiza kai zatayi Magana sukaji muryar yaro.
   "Assalamu alaikum wai inji wani a waje Jidda ƴar tasha tazo ko shi ya shigo da kansa."
   "Jidda ƴar tasha! To fa a gidan nan kuwa.?"
   Jidda tayi maganar cike da mamaki, Basmah ce ta taɓe bakinta tare da cewa yaron.
     "Jeka tana zuwa."
 "Yaje ina zuwa zakice bakiji sunan daya ambata ba Jidda ƴar tasha, gani nayi gidan nan babu ƴar tasha."
 Shidai yaron bai tsaya ba yayi ficewar sa, Basmah cewa tayi.
    "Idan har lissafina dai-dai ne kinje gidan Iyabo idan har kinje Kuma an kama NAMEER to babu shakka Hisham ne yazo ƙofar gidan nan kuma ya aiko a kiraki da Jidda ƴar tasha."
    Zaro idanunta Jidda tayi tare da furta.
     "Hisham! Kuma a gidan mu.?"

"Yess HISHAM kinyi mamaki ne.?"
    "Ƙwarai to me zai kawosa ƙofar gidan mu it means ma a ina yasan gidan mu waya gwada masa."?
    "Hmmm tambayar rainin hankali, ke ta yaya kika san gidan su, sannan kinfi kowa sanin abinda ya kawosa."
    "Wai ke Basmah meyasa bakya gane Annabi ya faku ne, haba dalla sanin gidan su ai ba ɓoyayyen abu bane saboda mahaifinsa mutum ne wanda ya sanu a ƙasar nan."
  Numfashi Basmah ta saki tare da cewa.
   "Haka zalika kema a matsayinki ta babbar ƴar jaridar Aminci gidanku bazai ɓuya ba kinga kuwa kunyi ɗaya yana da kyau ki daina wannan zancen banzan ki fita kiji da me yazo kafin ya shigo ya ɗagawa Umma hankali dan kin san zai aikata."
    "Mtsss! Zan fita kuwa na koya masa hankali yacce bazai ƙara tunanin kallon ƙofar gidan nan ba tunda mahaukaci ne shi." 
   "Ke kin kirasa mahaukaci ke kuma Umma ta kiraki mahaukaciya to bari muga ke dashi waye mai asalin cutar haukar amma dai ki kula gudun faɗawa ramin da bazaki iya fita ba, munje ni na shige gida."
   Harara Jidda ta watsawa Basmah tace.
  "Wallahi babu ramin da zan faɗa ban fito ba, shi dai wannan ƙaramin tsageran bai isa jefani wani rami ba, ke ko da kuwa da kuskure na faɗa wallahi sai nayi tsalle na fito, ki kuma saka ido kiga yadda zan koyawa rayuwarsa hankali."
   Taɓe bakinta Basmah tayi tace.
    "We'll idan ke baki koya masa ba shi ai zai koya miki."
    "Mungun bakinki ya biki Basmah naga alamar kamar kina ɗaure masa gindi ne."
    "Oho koma dai mai zaki faɗa bazai dameni ba."
   Basmah tayi Maganar tana ficewa bayanta Jidda tabi a fusace.
  Yana tsaye jikin mota idanunsa manne da glass ya harɗe hannunsa, Basmah gefe ta tsaya tana jiran fitowar Jidda babu jumawa kuwa ta fito ko inda Basmah take Jidda bata kalla ba ta nufi Hisham tare da tsayawa a gabansa tace.
    "Ban taɓa ganin Mutum marar daraja ɗan adam ba irinka me kazo yi ƙofar gidan mu."?
   Bakinsa Hisham ya taɓe tare da ɗaga ido ya kalli gidan kafin yace.
    "Ina gidan yake wannan kangon kike kira da gida hmm! Ko da yake zaki kirasa gida saboda tsoho bai mutu yabar komai ba, wannan ɗin ma hayace zaman notice."
   A fusace Jidda tace.
      "Ya isheka haka Malam duk abinda zaka faɗa min ka tsaya iya kaina karka kuma sako iyayena ciki, domin kuwa naga kai rayuwa kake irin ta dabbobi."
   Ɗan murmushi Hisham yayi tare da sanya hannu ya shafa gashin kansa kafin yace.
   "Rayuwa irin ta dabbobi, Hmm! Shi yasa nazo na gwada miki halin dabbobin, ba dai ke ƴar tasha bace har kina da zarrar sawa a kama ɗan uwana yanzu zaki shiga masifar da zaki kasa fita a cikin sa, ba dai kin sokeni a ƙirji ba, to ni idan nazo sokarki ba iya ƙirji zan tsaya ba sai na huda har cikin zuciyarki za kiga yadda ake tashin hankali."
    "Barazana kazo kamin ne naji tsoro naje na sanya a saki wannan ƙaton dabban da baisan darajar Mace ba."
   Dariya sosai Hisham yayi kafin yace.
   "Hisham baya bazarana aikinsa kawai yake aiwatarwa sai dai dama sharaɗina ne kafin a fara yaƙi dole sai na faɗi abinda zai biyo baya, domin kuwa da zarar an fara yaƙin da kanki zaki fara neman sanin me zai faru gobe, we'll ki fara tunanin gobenki domin kuwa zaki shiga baƙar cakwakiya ki fara nazarin rayuwarki inma ki mutu domin rayuwar ahalinki inma ki rayu cikin baƙin ciki da takaicin rayuwa."
   Cikin hasala Jidda tace.
   "Me kake nufi da maganganun ka me zaka yiwa ahalina, rayuwata ne su taɓa su tamkar taɓa rayuwata ne, ka ciresu cikin wannan faɗan domin basu san komai ba, tsakanina da kaine mu kashe kanmu mu biyu."
    "Rayuwarki ne su, ni kuma nawa ahalin da kika saka cikin tashin hankali ba rayuwata bane, Hmm! Lokaci shine zai gwada miki abinda nake nufi ki ijiye a ranki NAMEER dole zai dawo gida inma yau inma gobe koma jibi, tabbas zai dawo daga ranar kuma da kikaji labarin an sakesa to daga wannan ranar lokacin fargaba da tashin hankalin ki zai fara bazan ƙara waiwayowa kanki ba har sai NAMEER ya dawo gida."
  Yayi Maganar yana sakin murmushi tare da shigesa cikin mota yaja da ƙarfi yabar wajen da kallo Jidda ta bisa cikin ƙunar rai ta furta.
   "Banza Dabba sai muga ta yadda NAMEER ɗin zai fito."
   "Hmmm! Ni kam na wuce gida Allah ya tsayar haka."
  Basmah tayi Maganar tana kallon Jidda, hararar Basmah Jidda tayi tare da shigewa ciki.



  **********


"Wai dariyar me kikeyi ne Wamimo.?"
    Gyara zamanta Wamimo tayi tana kuma sakin dariya tace.
    "Hmmm! Mamabisola ai dole nayi dariya wannan abun farin ciki da ya samemu, Hmm! Ɗan riƙon dai ankamasa yana can hannun shegun a rufe."
   "Waye kenan Wamimo ban gane wanda kike faɗi ba."?
   Mamabisola tayi Maganar cike da rashin fahimta, Wamimo dubanta tayi tare da cewa.
   "Au wai har kin manta, yaron da ɗanku Iyabo ya ke riƙe dashi mana Nemeer shi nake nufi."
   "Whatt! Wamimo da gaske kike yana Police station, kar dai tarkon mu ya kama kurciya."?
   Dariya Wamimo tayi tace.
    "Ƙwarai kuwa muɗin na wasa ne an faɗa miki ke dai kawai zuba ido kiga yadda wasan zai kaya."
   Dariya Mamabisola ta saki tare da cewa.
    "Kinga Wamimo tashi ki rakani gidan Iyabo ai dole muje mu masa jaje da kuma bugan ciki, ga mayafina kusa muje."
   Tayi Maganar tana ɗaukar mayafin Wamimo ma tashi tayi suka fice tare.



*********


Zaune yake yayi shuru yana mamakin wannan ƙazafin da aka ƙulla masa a iya saninsa bashi da abokin gabar da zai masa wannan mungun ƙazafin, numfashi ya saki tare da dafe kansa yana kallon ruwan dake gefensa, tsayawa tayi jikin cell cike da tashin hankali idanunta na zubar da hawaye tace.
    "Yaya NAMEER yanzu kaine a nan ƙasƙantaccen wajen, wai me yake faruwa ne yaya Nameer na kasa fahimtar komai waye zai maka wannan ƙazafin, rayuwata ne kai bazan iya juran ganinka cikin matsala ba."?
   Murmushi Nameer yayi yana zuba mata idanu kafin ya miƙe tsaye ya taho jikin cell ɗin.
    "Bani da maƙiyi dan haka bana zargin kowa, bansan dalilin da yasa nazo wajen nan ba, sai dai na ɗauki hakan a matsayin jarabawa ce Ubangiji ya ɗauro min, a matsayina kuma na musulmi dole nayi imani da ƙaddara,Tutu ina da gaskiya dan haka bana tsoron wajen nan ina da yaƙinin gaskiya ta zata fitar dani, na sani kina jin zafin acikin zuciyarki amma kiyi haƙuri ki danne damuwarki insha Allah komai zai wuce, me Abba yace sanda yaji Maganar.?"
   "Yaya Nameer meyasa sai lokacin da yazo daf zaka sanar da Abba Soyayyar mu za'a katse mana numfashi, yaya Nameer ina son kowa ya sheda Soyayyar mu, amma hakan ta kasa faruwa ko yaushe mukayi yunkurin hakan sai matsala ta shigo tsakani, mtsss! Ko yaya Hisham kaƙi bari yasan Soyayyar dake tsakanin mu sai rufaffiyar Soyayya muke wanda bata da alƙibila ina cikin tashin hankali."
   Tayi Maganar cikin kuka mai cin rai bakinsa Nameer ya cije tare da cewa.
   "Tutu tambayar da na miki baki bani amsar ta ba, kin tsaya kina min tambayar da zata ɗaga min hankali a halin da nake ciki yanzu bai kamata ace kina min magana akan goben Soyayyar mu ba, kamata yayi ki mai da hankali wajen ganin ta yaya zan fita daga wannan masifar, kin san ina sonki kuma bazan taɓa juyawa Soyayyar ki baya ba, ko kina shakku akan soyayyata ne ko baki yarda dani bane.?"
    "A'a Yaya Nameer na yarda da kai yarda marar misaltuwa, Kawai ina jin zafin matsalolin da ke shan gaban Soyayyar mu ne, amma na fahimta kayi haƙuri zanyi ƙoƙari naga duk yadda za'ayi ka fita, Abba shima yana can cikin tunani da nazarin mafita kuma nasan yaya Hisham bazai barka cikin wannan halin ba dole zai fitar da kai daga nan ko da babu hannun Abba, Ummi ma tace ka kwantar da hankalinka komai zai daidaita."
   "Hmmm! Ba komai Tutu ki koma gida idan kin koma ki sanar da Ummi cewa karta ɗaga hankalinta ina nan lafiya kuma insha Allah zan fito."
    "Shikenan ka kula min da kanka."
  Kansa Nameer ya kaɗa kafin Tutu ta juya ta bar wajen, bata juma da fita ba Dpo ya fito, cell ɗin aka buɗe masa ya shiga tare da zama Nameer dubansa yayi tare da sakin murmushi.
   "Nameer Abdulzahir Iyabo, har ga Allah banji daɗin zamanka a inda kafi ƙarfin zama ba dole ta kama babu yadda na iya."
    Murmushi Nameer yayi tare da cewa.
    "Karka damu a sake na ke ban takura ba, kasan ai ko wani ɗan adam komai gatansa ƙarshen mutuwa ce, yaje yayi rayuwa a ƙarƙashin ƙasa inda ba tabarma bare filo, ina tunawa da wannan kullum shiyasa bana jin wata damuwa dangane da zamana a nan Malam Dpo."
   "Hakane kayi daban da ɗan uwanka, kai mutumin kirki ne."
   "Ɗan uwana ma mutumin kirki ne fahimtar sa ne kawai bakuyi ba, jeka kai tsaye kan abinda ya shigo da kai nan ka ijiye maganar ɗan uwana a gefe."
   "Hmmm! Okay we'll, Meyasa ka aikatawa yarinya fyaɗe shin baka san illar hakan bane laifi ne da zai iya kaika kurkuku na tsawon shekaru.?"
   Dariya Nameer yayi tare da cewa.
   "Bansan wannan maganar ba, wataƙila wani mai suna Nameer ɗin kuke nema amma wannan Nameer ɗin yasan darajar mace ba zai aikata abinda kuke zarginsa dashi ba meye hujjar ku daya tabbatar cewa ni ne mai laifin.?"
   "Ba'a yiwa Police tambaya cikin tambaya Malam Nameer, ka bani amsar tambayata."
   "Hmmm! Ikon Allah na amsa maka tambayarka kafin jefo tawa tambayar ko a ruɗe kake ne, Malam Dpo kasancewata anan bashi zai baka damar min tsawa ba, karka manta halacci."
   "Hakane bazan manta halacci ba, sai a bangaren bawa ƙasata gudummawa to bazan ɗagawa kowa ƙafa ba, har yanzu bamu gama tabbatar da hujja akanka ba shiyasa ma muka ijiyeka cikin kulawa babu horo."
   "Okay kuje ku cigaba da binciken idan har kun tabbatar ni ne criminal ɗin is okay ku karya ƙasussuwana karku raga min sai dai fa Malam Dpo idan ya tabbata Nameer bashi da laifi ɗan uwana bazai taɓa ɗaga muku ƙafa ba sai dai idan ni ne na dakatar dashi."
  Nameer yayi Maganar yana sakin murmushi shima Dpo murmushin yayi kafin yace.
   "Shikenan hakanma yamin."
  Shima yayi Maganar yana tashi ya fice daga cell ɗin Sargent Dogo yazo ya kulle.
