Tun daga get ɗin gidan ake bawa Mamabisola girmamawa har ta iso get ɗin da zai sadata da part ɗin Iyabo, kallon Wamimo tayi tace.
     "Munzo ki nutsu domin muna bukatar makamin da zamu kuma hurawa Case ɗin wuta kin fahimta.?"
   "Na fahimta kuma zan baki mamaki."
  Murmushi tayi sannan suka shige part ɗin, sallama sukayi Ummi dake zaune tayi tagumi tana share hawaye jefi jefi Abba na gefenta shima yayi shuru Ummi ta amsa sallamar ƙarisowa sukayi Abba na ganin Mamabisola ya miƙe cikin girmamawa.
    "Mama sannu da zuwa."
   Ummi tayi Maganar tana zamewa ƙasa ta zauna kasancewar al'adar su ce idan babba na zaune a sama su suyi ƙasa, murmushi Abba yayi tare da zama gefen ƙafar Mamabisola cikin girmamawa yace.
    "Sannu da zuwa Mama kece tafe ai da kirana kikayi nazo da baki wahala wajen zuwa ba."
    "Babu komai Iyabo, zuwan ai dole ta kama yanzu ina zaune nake jin maganar da ta ɗaga min hankali shiyasa nace banci ta zama ba, dole nazo naji gaskiyar zancen, wai yaron nan da kake riko yayi laifin fyaɗe an kamasa.?"
   Numfashi Abba ya saki tare da jin ƙunar rai wato ma har maganar ta fara zagayawa gari kansa ya girgiza tare da cewa.
   "Ehh Mama da gaske yana hannun hukuma sai dai NAMEER bazai aikata wannan laifin ba, ina tunanin sharri ne kawai amma insha za'a san yadda za'ayi a wankesa mai gaskiya baya taɓa faɗuwa ƙasa Mama."
   "Hmm ni kaina nayi mamaki ace yaro mai hankali da nutsuwa ya aikata wannan mungun aikin, to Allah ya fidda sa, amma dai.........
  Sallamar Hisham itace ta dakatar dasu daga maganar, shi kansa bai kula da Mamabisola ba sai da ya shigo tsakiyar falon Sannan ya ganta idanunsa ya runtse muryar sa a dakile ya risina tare da cewa.
    "Ina yinin ku."
"Da bami wuni ba zaka ganmu dan ƙwal ubanka, dama ai nace zamu haɗu ne komai daɗewa, idan arziki bai haɗamu ba tsiya ta haɗa mu,  wato shine Tunde zaije Company neman aiki kasa a koresa kamar kare kai ga isasshe mai gadara da Companyn ubanka ko? Tunde dai ɗan uwanka ne babu abinda zai goge hakan ka fifita bare sama dashi, ina magana kana wani haɗe fuska kamar wanda na kashe maka uwa, to bara kaji wallahi ka gyara wannan shegen baƙin halin naka."
  Ransa Hisham ya haɗa tare da kawar da kansa gefe yaƙi tanka mata ganin yayi shuru ne Mamabisola ta cigaba da cewa.
   "Ina magana kamin shuru ka maisheni mahaukaciya."
  Miƙewa Hisham yayi tare da cewa.
    "Bani da amsar da zan tanka miki, ƙorafin da kika kawo wajen Abban bai isa ba sai kin biyoni har gida Tunde bai cancanci zama ɗaya daga cikin ma'aikatan mu ba, saboda mu muna ɗaukar masu ƙwazo ne da hazaƙa kuma masu riƙon amana Tunde bashi da ɗaya a cikin wadannan idan kin manta bara na tuna miki, Tunde sata yayi a Companyn da ya baro na makudan kudade, dan haka bama bukatar barawo a Companyn mu."
   Yayi Maganar cikin izza tare da saka kai yabar wajen, Mamabisola wani irin ɓacin rai taji wanda ya sanya har tayi danasanin zuwa domin kuwa da tasan zatazo HISHAM ya faɗa mata magana tabbas da batazo ba, Abba ne ya nisa tare da cewa.
   "Kiyi haƙuri Mama jikan naki ne na rasa yaya zanyi da zafin kansa kullum cikin faɗa nake masa amma tamkar wanda nake ƙara tunzura sa, kiyi haƙuri dan Allah, mun gama magana da Nameer cewa Monday za'a ɗauki Tunde a Company, to sai wannan jarabawar ta shigo amma insha Allah idan komai ya daidaita Tunde zai kawo takaddunsa a basa aiki a gurbin da ya kamata kiyi haƙuri dan Allah Mama."
    ''Hmm! Ba komai Iyabo ni kaina nasan halin Hisham, karka damu yanzu a wacce matsaya ake wani ƙoƙari kake wajen ganin yaron nan Nameer ya kubuta."?
   "Ehh gaskiya har yanzu banyi wani yunkuri ba domin kuwa bani da ƙwarin gwiwwar da zan fara tunkarar matsalar Saboda Case ne mai girman gaske, amma insha nasan dole Hisham zai samo evidence da zamu iya kare sa, insha Allah zuwa anjuma zan kirasa sai muji abinda ya samo insha Allah duk abinda ake ciki zan sanar dake."
    "Duk dai yadda za'ayi ka tabbatar ya kubuta domin kuwa yaron kirki ne mai amana, sannan karka yanke hukunci ba tare da ka sanar min ba."
   Murmushi Abba yayi tare da cewa.
   "INSHA ALLAH babu abinda zanyi ba tare da saninki ba kece uwata a yanzu ya zamo wajibi nabi umarnin ki."
   "Hakane Iyabo shiyasa nake alfahari da kai domin kuwa kaine gatana a yanzu, sannan ka jawa Hisham kunne ya kiyayi faɗa min duk maganar da tazo bakinsa sannan ka tambayesa wani irin laifi muka aikata masa daya tsanemu haka, mu zamu koma."
   Kafin Abba yayi Magana Ummi tace.
    "Babu abinda kuka masa Mama kawai tsiya ce irin tasa insha Allah zan masa magana bazai kuma mayar miki da martani ba kiyi haƙuri."
   Bakinta Mamabisola ta taɓe tare da cewa.
     "Nayi hakuri Hmm! Ramata ina da yaƙinin cewa kece kike kitsawa Hisham duk abinda yake min domin kuwa kema ba ƙaunar uban Tunde kike ba ko an faɗa miki ban gane take takenki ba ne."
     Ɗan zaro idanunta Ummi tayi zatayi Magana Abba yayi saurin katseta tare da cewa.
     "Karkice komai ki shige sama."
   Kanta Ummi ta kaɗa tare da bin umarnin Abba ta haura kafin ya dubi Mamabisola da Wamimo da ta nutsu tana sauraren su ya cigaba da cewa.
    "INSHA ALLAH za'a masa faɗa muje sai drever ya sauƙeku."
  Kanta ta girgiza tare da yin gaba Abba yabi bayansu.




**********
  Misalin goma da rabi na dare Jidda ta yi shiru a ɗaki tana nazarin maganganun HISHAM duk da bata jin zai iya aikata abinda yake faɗa mata, amma zuciyar sa ta girgiza da maganar sa sosai tana son ahalinta fiye da komai a rayuwarta bata jin zata iya jurewa ganinsu cikin tashin hankali, numfashi ta saki tare da ɗaukar wayarta ta kira Aisha ringing ɗaya Aisha ta ɗaga.
    "Kina asibitin ne."
Daga can Aisha tace.
   "A'a na dawo gida tun ɗazu, saboda ƙanwar mahaifiyar Hajara tazo shiyasa na taho."
   "Okay kina da numbern nasu ne ina son zanyi magana da ita hajaran ne."?
  "Kai gaskiya Aunty Jidda babu numbern nasu nan domin kuwa banyi seving ba, nima ina son kiransu naji ya jikin nata na duba wajen calling banga numbern ba, amma dai tahota jikinta da sauƙi sosai harma ta zaga harabar Asibitin."
    "Okay to shikenan ba damuwa da safe naje asibitin mun gode da ƙoƙari Aisha Allah ya biyaki ki gaishe da Ikko da Ahmad."
   "Zasuji na gode Aunty Jidda sai da safe."
  Aisha tayi Maganar tana katse kiran.
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki tare da saka kanta ta kwanta ganin dare yayi har 10:30pm.
    Sai dai me bacci ya gagari idanunta tunani da tsoro duk ya cika mata zuciya tana son ahalinta fiye da komai a rayuwarta su kuma ya'yan masu kuɗin nan ba imani garesu ba su tozarta talaka ba abun mamaki bane, numfashi ta saki tare da tashi ta ɗauro alwala ta hau sallah.


Washegari da safe bayan sunyi karin kumallo ne Umma ta dubi Jidda tare da cewa.
    "Jiddatul kair."
 "Na'am Ummana."
   "Jidda dan Allah na roƙeki ki cire hannunki cikin wannan Case ɗin fyaɗen dan Allah na roƙeki a matsayina ta mahaifiyarki, ki barmu mu zauna lafiya, ina da hawan jini ga suger, bana bukatar tashin hankali, har yau ciwon baƙin cikin mahaifin Abdullahi bai wankewa zuciyata ba, ki barni dashi ma kaɗai ya isheni."
   "Kanta Jidda ta girgiza tare da cewa.
  "Shikenan Umma insha Allah na cire hannuna zanje na janye ƙarar farin cikin ku yafi min komai a rayuwata."
   Tayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki cikin zuciyarta ta fara saka, ita da take da niyyar zuwa asibitin taga yadda hajaran ta kwana sannan ta biya Police station ɗin taji ya ake ciki, amma gashi Umma na ƙoƙarin dakatar da ita, amma koma yaya ne bazan bar wannan Case ya tafi a banza ba, tayi Maganar zucin tana sakin ijiyar zuciya Umma ne tace.
    "Shikenan jiddatul kair Allah ya miki albarka, yau zaki fita ne ko zamu a gidane."?
    "Ehh Umma zan fita yanzu zanje gidan su Basmah na bata haƙuri jiya ta tafi cikin fushi akan wannan case ɗin."
   "Hakan yana da kyau Basmah masoyiyarki ce ta haƙiƙa, idan kinje kya gaishe da babarta ta ko."
   "Insha Allah zataji Umma bara na ɗauki hijabi na na tafi."
  Da adawo lafiya Umma ta mata kafin Jidda ta miƙe ta shiga ciki ta ɗauki hijabinta ta fita, ko da ta fito ABDULWASI'U yana tsaye ƙofar gidan su da niyyar aikawa tazo tsayawa tayi tana dubansa kafin tayi Magana yace.
   "Dama jira nake Abdu ya fito na aikesa yamin kiranki."
  "Okay sai kuma gashi na fito meke faruwa.?"
   Murmushi ABDULWASI'U yayi kafin yace.
   "Kamar kuma naga sauri kike ai."
  Cike da fara gajiya da maganganun sa tace.
   "Eh idan ba damuwa zan iya tafiya idan na dawo na saurari abinda kake da niyyar faɗa ko."
  Tayi Maganar tana saka kai tabarsa tsaye kansa ABDULWASI'U ya girgiza ya rasa meyasa Jidda bata ƙaunarsa duk da kyautatawa da biyayyar da yake yiwa iyayenta, numfashi ya saki ya koma bakin shagonsa.

   Jidda kanta tsaye mai adaidaita ta tare tace masa IBUNKU OLU CLINIC zai kaita can ta nufa, ta shiga mai adaidaitan yaja suka nufi hospital ɗin, bayan ya Kaita ne ta basa kuɗinsa 200 naira kafin ta shiga cikin asibitin darect ɗakin da Hajara take Jidda ta nufa domin kuwa tun jiya hankalinta yaƙi kwanciya ji take tamkar wani abun zai faru da Hajara maganganun HISHAM sun tayar mata da hankali tanajin zai iya komai domin ganin ya kuɓutar da wannan azzalumin ɗan uwan nasa, a tsaye taga wasu mata su biyu wanda bata sansu ba cikin tashin hankali gadon da Hajara ke kwance ta kalla wayam babu kowa cikin sauri tace.
    "Waye ku ina Hajara wacce take kwance a gadon nan jiya, an sallameta ne."?
  Ɗaya daga cikin matan ne ta kalli jidda tare da cewa.
   "Mu ƙannen mahaifiyar Hajara ne, ba sallamar mu akayi ba, Hajara aka nema aka rasa tun jiya cikin dare anyi nema anyin nema kaf cikin asibitin nan da unguwar nan har gida an kira da sauran ƴan uwanmu amma Hajara bata nan hankalin mu yana tashe bamu san ina Hajara take ba."
  Dafe kanta Jidda tayi tare da furta kalmar innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un.
   "What ba'aga Hajara ba to ina ta shiga karfa a saceta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un."

*_Special tnx to my people masoya masu ƙaunata masu bibiyata ako da yaushe, yanzu ma gani tafe muku da sabon salo sabon labari mai tafiya da zamani labarin daya ƙunshi abubuwan ban al'ajabi a cikin sa ku kasance dani domin jin yacce cakwakiyar zata ƙarke, idan kinsan kina da ra'ayi akan wannan labarin to ki fara bibiya tun daga farko kar sai nayi nisa kice min daga farko please._*





*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻





*_PAGE 5_*



Ƙanwar mahaifiyar Hajaran ne wacce suke kira da Baba jummai tace 
  "Ba'a ganta ba, mun rasa ya zamuyi tun daren jiya muke nan cikin tashin hankali abun kamar almara."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki tare da cije bakinta cikin zuciyarta take saƙa cewa.
   (To waye zai sace Hajara garin yaya za'ace da bakinta da hankalinta an sace ta anya kuwa wannan abinda zai yiwu ne, to amma idan da gaske saceta akayi garin yaya hakan ta faru? ko dai Hisham ne, kai ina bazai yiwu ba babu ta yadda Hisham zai iya saceta a cikin wannan asibitin dole yasan idan aka nemeta aka rasa su za'a zarga, to amma fa hatsabibi ne Hisham zai iya aikatawa ta wata fuskar domin ba imani garesa ba, sai dai kuma ba Teddy bace ita bare ya saceta cikin sauƙi, Hmm! Lallai akwai lauje cikin naɗi, bai zamo lallai ace Hisham bane saboda dole yasan su za'a zarga, kai zaima fa iya aikata sacetan yadda yake jin kansa tamkar sarki.)
  Tayi Maganar zucin tana sakin numfashi kafin ta dubi Wacce tayi Maganar tace.
   "To amma garin yaya kuna tare da ita cikin room daya har a ɗauketa baku sani ba, sannan abun mamakin ma ta yaya akayi bakuji motsinta ba a matsayin ku na majiyantan ta dole dai idan har tasan saceta za'ayi tayi ƙoƙarin kururuwa, gaskiya kunyi sakaci.?"
   Baba larai ce wacce suke tare da Baba jummai tace.
       "Ba sakaci mukayi ba, ƴar nan Saboda cikin dare ma misalin 3:00pm na farka har na bata ruwa tasha, tashin mu sallar asuba muka nemeta muka rasa munyi tunanin ko gida ta koma muka kira ummanta muka tambayeta tace batazo ba muka duba cikin asibitin nan gaba ɗaya babu ita hakanne ya sa hankalinmu ya tashi kaf cikin dangi da abokan arziki da aka san zata iya zuwa an tambaya amma bataje can ba tabbas sace ta akayi, maganar bacci kuma shi bacci ai ɓarawo ne bamu da laifi."
  "Hmmm! To an sanar da Police station ne sannan ina shi doctor dake kula da ita, sannan ina mahaifiyarta.?"
   Jidda tayi tambayar cikin sanyin jiki, Baba larai ne ta kuma magana.
   "Doctor ya je station zai sanar da abinda ya faru yanzu ma haka suna can tare da Mahaifiyar Hajaran."
   "Okay wani statin ɗin ne.?"
    "Wanda dai kika kai ƙarar wadanda suka yi ƙoƙarin mata fyaɗen can yace mana zaije tun ɗazu muke jiran dawowarsu muma."
   "Okay muje station ɗin."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana ficewa daga room ɗin bayanta sukabi kafin suka tari adaidaita zuwa Police station ɗin.


 Ko da isar su Police station ɗin sun sami Mahaifiyar Hajara da doctor zaune gaban Dpo daya fito harabar station ɗin cikin cell ɗin kuwa Nameer ne zaune, Jidda inda Nameer ke kulle ta kalla tare da cewa.
   "Dpo wannan ɓatan Hajara akwai lauje cikin naɗi ta yaya za'ace ana wannan case ɗin a nemeta a rasa mutum ce fa ba dabba ba ba kuma mahaukaciya ba, bare ace rashin hankali yasa ta fice, ya kamata a binciki gidan Alhaji Iyabo ina zargin su da sune zasu sace Hajara domin rufe babin wannan Case ɗin."
  Murmushi Nameer dake cikin cell ɗin yayi yana tsura mata idanu ko kaɗan baiji zafin maganar ta ba, Dpo ne yace.
   "Nima zargin da nake kenan, sai dai koma waye yayi wannan aikin zamu ganosa very soon."
  Yayi Maganar yana miƙewa tare da nufar cell ɗin ya dafa ƙarfen ƙofar yana duban Nameer dake zaune yana sauraron su yace.
   "Ina kuka kaita domin nasan tana wajenku."?
  Murmushi Nameer ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Tunda kasan tana wajen mu mai zai hana kaje ka ɗaukota, Abbas Marafa kana zaƙewa da yawa fa ka fara ƙoƙarin fusatar dank dan Allah ka dinga kulawa kana tauna harshen ka wajen min tambaya, ina nan rufe ko kun buɗeni ne, bare naje na saceta."?
  Dpo zaiyi Magana Jidda tayi saurin cewa.
   "Ba'a buɗeka ba amma abokin cin mushenka yana can yana shawagi kamar tsuntsu, zai iya aikata hakan a madadinka saboda imaninsa yayi ƙaranci, zai iya komai domin ganin ya kuɓutar da kai, ɗan uwanka bashi da imani kamar dai Kai ɗin nan."
   "Okay na fahimta Hisham kike nufi ko? let go ku kamasa sai ya muku magana da bakinsa idan shine bazai ɓoye muku ba, ɗan kanfai ne Hisham yana dai-dai da ƙugun kowa, gaskiya kina ƙoƙari wajen shigar da kanki cikin matsalolin da ba naki ba, zakiyi danasani kuma zakiji kunya a duk sanda gaskiya ta bayyana."
   Nameer yayi Maganar cikin murmushi, Jidda bakinta ta taɓe ta kuma cewa.
   "Wacce gaskiyar zan gani bayan wannan, hmm! Akanku dai bazan taɓa jin kunya ba bare kuma nayi wata danasani, a duniya babu wanda na tsani gani a rayuwata sama da kai da ɗan uwanka kuma tabbas sai kun fuskanci hukunci."
  Tayi Maganar a fusace Dpo ne yace.
     "Ba damuwa kuna iya tafiya zamuyi bincike kuma zamu samota nan da zuwa awa ashirin da huɗu, kai kuma Nameer zaka gane kuskuren ka."
  Kanta Jidda ta girgiza mahaifiyar Hajara ne tace.
    "Ranka ya daɗe dan Allah ku rufa min asiri ku nemo min ƴata, ita ɗaya Allah ya bani dan Allah ku rufa asiri bazan iya zure rasata ba."
  Jidda ce ta riƙota tana cewa.
    "Kiyi haƙuri Mama insha Allah Hajara Zata dawo cikin ƙoshin lafiya ki daina kukan kinji.?"
   "Naji Jidda na gode sosai kina ta ƙoƙari akan Hajara tamkar ƴar Uwarki ta jini Allah ya biyaki da mafificin alkairi."
    "Ameeen Mama wannan yiwa kaine muje, Baba larai mu tafi ku hau adaidaita ku mayar da Mama gida insha Allah komai zai daidaita ."
 Tayi Maganar tana riƙe da ita suka fita tare, DOCTOR shima hannu ya bawa Dpo sukayi musabaha kafin ya tafi.






  **********

  Abba ne ya dubi Hisham da yake cin abincin tamkar yana cin magani yace.
  "Hisham kamin shuru tun ɗazu nake magana ni ɗaya ko baka jina ne?."
  Ɗago idanunsa Hisham yayi ya kalli Abba dake riƙe da cup ɗin block tea a hannunsa kafin yace.
    "Ina jinka Abba me kakeso na ce maka Abba akan wannan lamarin kace babu ruwanka mu jira gaskiyar Nameer ta fitar dashi, to kuma meye amfanin sanar da kai wani abu, kabar maganar kawai."
   "Hisham! Hisham! Tambayar ka fa nake kake min zancen banza munyi magana da commissioner jiya ina son sanin wani evidence kake dashi domin kuwa commissioner yace dole sai anje kotu muna bukatar barrister shiyasa nake son ji daga bakinka domin kuwa zaman Nameer cikin cell yana ɗagawa mahaifiyar ku rai dani kaina."
   "Abba na gama tsara komai Nameer zai fito cikin aminci kabar komai kawai a hannuna, sai dai Abba ina bukatar 5million daga gareka ina son buga wasa ne dasu."
   Ummi ne ta ture plat ɗin abincin dake gabanta tace.
   "Wasa Hisham, 5million ne zaka buga wasa dashi saboda baka da hankali, ka dinga hankali da rayuwa Hisham, baza'a baka waɗannan kuɗaɗen ba, sannan ka tashi muje ka kaini naga yarona Nameer."
  Hisham murmushi ya saki yace.
  "Ummi kin sani bana wata aba wasa, wannan shine wasa na na farko da zan buga dan haka Abba kai nake saurare."
   "Hmmm! Hisham kuɗi ba matsala bane, damuwata yanzu...............
    "Nameer ya fito haka zakace."
  Ta katse Maganar Abba tana shigowa gabaki ɗayansu juyawa sukayi suka mai da kallonsu kanta, Ummi ce tayi murmushi, shima Hisham murmushin ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Aunty Halima kece.?"
 "Eh Hisham ni ne."
Tayi Maganar tana zama a daining ɗin tare da cigaba da cewa.
    "Yanzu nake samun labarin abinda ke faruwa da NAMEER shiyasa ma na taho cikin gaggawa, ta yaya za'ace da kuɗin mu da ƙimarmu da darajar mu, muna da ƙarfin iko a ƙasar nan gabaki ɗaya harma da tsallakenta, ace Nameer ya kwana a police station, wannan ƙasƙantar da ahalin mune, Hmmm! Yaya ya zamo wajibi muyi amfani da kuɗin mu da ƙarfin ikon mu NAMEER ya fito, kuma duk wanda ya masa wannan ƙazafin na rantse da Allah bazan ƙyalesa ba sai na hukunta sa."
   "Hmmm! Yes Aunty Halima magana kikayi mai kyau, Amma Abba shi bazai yarda da haka ba, yafi yarda da gaskiyar ce, Aunty Halima a yanzu bama bukatar kashe ko sisi ko neman alfarmar wani akan case ɗin Nameer na gama komai Nameer zai fito gobe."
   "Kamar yaya me kake nufi Hisham."?
   Abba yayi Maganar yana ɗan zaro ido.
    "Ina nufin Nameer zai Fito gobe, Aunty Halima nasan Ummi bazata bar Abba ya bani 5m ba, amma nasan zan iya samu a gareki."
   "Zan baka kuɗin da kake bukata Hisham, zakaji alart, amma ta yaya zaka fitar da Nameer daga cikin wannan haɗarin."?
   "Hanya ce mai sauƙi Aunty Halima, kuma na gama samun hanyar, nasan amfani akayi da wannan yarinyar wajen ganin an tozarta mu shiyasa nima nayi amfani da ita wajen ganin na kuɓutar da ahalina, muje Aunty Halima."
   Hisham yayi Maganar yana ture plat ɗin abincin tare da miƙewa, ummi ce ta dubesa tace.
    "Hisham meka aikatawa ƴar mutane."?
   Aunty Halima ne ta cewa Ummi.
   "Muddun Nameer zai fito to kawai ki sanya idanunki, muje Hisham."
  Itama aunty Halima tayi Maganar tana tashi suka bar wajen ita da Hisham Ummi duban Abba tayi tace.
    "Abban Hisham, kana jin abinda yaron nan yake faɗi kayi shuru karfa ya ƙara jawo mana wata fitinar bamu gama da wannan ba, ga kuma Halima tabi bayansa."
    "Hmmm! To me zance Ramata, nasan dai ko mai Hisham zaiyi bazai cutar da yarinyar ba, sannan na yarda da maganar sa amfani akayi da Yarinyar ki ci abincin ki kawai zanyi magana dashi a nutse."
   "Shikenan Allah ya kare."
  Da ameeen kawai Abba ya amsa.



 Bayan sun fito compound ɗin gidan ne Hisham ya dubi Aunty Halima tare da cewa.
    "Aunty Halima, naje na sace yarinyar da tace Nameer ne yayi ƙoƙarin mata fyaɗe, yanzu haka gareta nake son muje tare da ke."
  "Hisham da gaske ka ɗauko yarinyar, amma garin yaya ka ɗauketa ba'a ganeka ba."?
   "Aunty Halima wannan abu ne mai sauƙi a gareni zan iya ɗaukar ko wacce irin kasada akan Nameer bazan yafewa kaina idan na kasa fitar dashi a wannan halin daya shiga kawai muje."
   Yayi Maganar suna nufar parking space bayansa Aunty Halima tabi suka shiga mota, tunda suka shiga babu wanda ya kuma magana har suka shiga gidan gonar Abba parking sukayi suka fito tare fa shigewa can ciki wani ɗaki Hisham yasa key ya buɗe, Hajara ce zaune tayi shuru jin an buɗe ƙofar ya sata firgita ta ɗago kanta ido huɗu sukayi da Hisham, murmushi ya saki tare da shigowa ya zauna gefenta, Aunty Halima ma shigowa tayi tana kallon Hajara kafin tace.
    "Yanzu wannan yarinyar ne ta yiwa Nameer sharri.?"
    "Itace Aunty Halima."
Hisham yayi Maganar yana kallon Hajara hannu yasa ya ɗauketa da wawan mari wanda sai da taga taurari Uku, da yatsa ya nunata yace.
    "Waye ya biyaki kuɗi domin ki tozarta ahalina ki ɓatawa gidan mu suna waye shi."?
  Fuskarta Hajara ta dafe da shatin yatsun Hisham suka bayyana a kai idanunta kuma sai hawaye yake kanta ta girgiza tace.
   "Babu wanda ya sani ba sharrin na masa ba tabbas yaso min fyaɗe."
  Murmushin gefen baki Hisham ya saki Aunty Halima ne tace.
    "Okay bazaki faɗi ba kenan okay kin zaɓi ki wahala akan ki tonawa wanda ya saki asiri ko, Hisham ka ɗauko belt ka bigeta har sai jini ya fita a jikinta karka ƙyaleta har sai ta faɗa."
   "No Aunty Halima ba dukanta zanyi ba, akwai sistern ta zansa a mata fyaɗen idan yaso sai ashiga kotun da dalili wallahi aunty Halima da gaske nake zansa ayiwa ƴar Uwar ki Zainab fyaɗe muddun baki faɗa min gaskiya yanzu ba kuma na sanya a tozarta mahaifiyarki."
  Wani irin firgita Hajara tayi jikinta na rawa bakinta na karkarwa tace.
   "A'a dan Allah karka min haka karku taɓa Zainab bata san komai ba dan Allah ka rabu da ita."
   "Hmmm! Idan ita bata san komai ba to ke ai kin sani kece kaɗai zaki iya ceton ta ki faɗa min waye ya sanyaki.?"
   "Idan na faɗa maka zaka ƙyale Zainab idan har zaka rabu da ita zan faɗa maka gaskiya."?
   Numfashi Aunty Halima ta saki tare da cewa.
    "Gaskiyar muke bukatar ji muddun mukaji gaskiyar zamu rabu daku."
    "Nameer bai sanni ba bai taɓa ganina a cikin rayuwarsa ba, tabbas kuɗi aka biyani domin na masa sharrin da xa'a ɗauresa na tsawon shekaru."
     Kansa Hisham ya girgiza tare da cewa.
   "Waye ne ya sanyaki.?"
        "Tunde shine ya biyani dubi ɗari biyu domin nayi wannan aikin dan Allah ka rabu da ƴar uwata na tuba bazan kuma ba kayi haƙuri."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki aunty Halima kuwa bakinta ta riƙe cike da mamaki.
    "Tunde! Hmmm! Ni nasan za'a rina wai an sace zanin mahaukaciya, tom riƙe wayar nan na mai da layi na no number ki kira Tunde kar kuma ki nuna masa cewa kina cikin damuwa kuyi Magana dashi."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana dannawa Tunde kira tare da sanya handsfree, ringing ɗaya kuwa ya ɗaga saurin sanya recording.
    "Hello dawa nake magana."
   "Ni ce Hajara Tunde."
 "Hajara! Ina kikaje akace kin ɓata har muna zargin ko Hisham ne ya saceki domin mungun wayo garesa zai iya tunanin mune muka ɗana musu wannan tarkon kina dai lafiya ko.?"
    "Ina lafiya Tunde babu wanda ya sace ni kawai guduwa nayi daga asibitin, ƙarin kuɗi nake bukata wanda ka bani sunyi kaɗan domin kuwa ba ƙaramin kasada na ɗauka ba, idan ba haka ba zan tona asiri."
    "Haba Hajara yanzu 200k ne tayi kaɗan, kinga yanzu kina ina zan zo na ƙara miki 50k hakan yayi.?"
   "Yayi zan sake kiranka anjuma."
  katse kiran Hisham yayi tare da sakin murmushi ya dubi Aunty Halima yace muje.
    "Dan Allah karku tafi ku barni anan tunda na faɗa muku gaskiya ku sakeni."
   Juyowa HISHAM yayi ya kalleta tare da cewa.
    "Kamar yadda na miki alkawari bazan cutar da ƴar Uwarki ba tunda kin faɗa min gaskiya sai dai fa ki sani ke bazan sakeki ba sai kin gane kuskuren ki zaki gane wanda aka biya ki taɓa."
 Yayi Maganar yana saka kai ya fice bayansa Aunty Halima tabi tana dakawa Hajara harara, ko da suka shiga mota Aunty Halima cewa tayi.
     "Meye ribar Tunde idan ya tozarta Nameer.?"
    "Babu wata riba da zasu samu kakarsa ce maybe ta sakasu, shi Abba har yanzu ya kasa fahimtar su waye Mamabisola, kawai ƙiyayyace da hassada take damunsu a rai."
   "To Allah ya kyauta amma dai ya kamata yaji wannan voice ɗin ko.?"
   "A'a Aunty Halima bazai ji ba bana son abinda zai tayar masa da hankali bare ma yadda Abba yake ɗauka duk duniya bashi da gatan daya wuce su, sanin halin da ake ciki kan iya tayar masa da hawan jininsa zan kashe Case ɗin ba tare da su Abba sun san komai ba."
    "Hmmm! Hisham naji daɗi da kayi hakan Allah ya shirya su su kuma, amma daga nan Police station zamuje ko."?
  "A'a gidan ki zan sauƙeki daga nan Police station sai gobe ki turo kuɗin yau gobe nake son zanyi amfani dashi."
   "Okay baka da matsala."
   Har bakin get ɗin gidan ta ya sauƙe ta kafin ya yi shigewarsa yana murmushi domin kuwa shi ya juma da fahimtar Mamabisola ba ƙaunar su take ba shuka take a idanun makwarwa wanda har yanzu ita bata fahimci Hisham yasan abubuwan da take ƙulla musu ba.

*PAGE 13 to 14*




Isar ta cikin gidan Ta samu Umma sunkuye tana tsintar shinkafa, sallama tayi Umma ta amsa tana ɗago kanta.
    "Kin dawo."?
"Eh Umma na dawo bamma samu Basmah ba sai na biya gidan su Zainab."
   "Tana ɗakin ki ma ai tana jiranki, ta juma a ciki da alamu dai kamar tana cikin damuwa na tambaye ta ko akwai abinda yake damunta sai tace babu wai abbanta ne yake asibiti jikinsa ya tashi sai kije ki sameta ki rarrasheta."
   "Subahanallah! Jikin Abban ne kuma ya ƙara tashi bara naje na sameta."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana tashi da sauri ta shige ɗakin nata, tana zaune tayi shuru hawaye na biyo idanunta ko kaɗan bata kula da shigowar Jidda, ta yi nisa sosai a cikin tunani, sai ji tayi ta dafa kafaɗarta.
   "Basmah abun har ya kai ga kuka, jikin Abban babu daɗi ne.?"
   Numfashi Basmah ta saki tana goge hawayen fuskarta tace.
   "Jidda jikin Abbana ba daɗi, dole sai an masa cs ana neman dubu arba'in, mu kuma bamu da ita bamu da wanda zai bamu, ga kuɗin hayar gidan namu ma ya kare suna bukatar Kudi masu gidan, salary na kuma na wannan watan kuɗin makarantar su hafiz aka biya, gacar Mama ta shiga cikin damuwa."
   "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Allahumma ajirni fil musibati! Lallai dole hankalinki ya tashi Basmah, yanzu ina zamu samu wannan kuɗin ko Abba a fara masa cs ɗin 14k ne kawai a cikin account ɗina, bansan kuma ina zamu samu 26k ba, mutanene yanzu basa taimako kowa kansa da iyalansa ya sani, bashi ma ba kowa bane zai baka wani ma yana da shi sai ya gama jin sirrinka yace maka babu, amma yaushe ne za'a masa cs ɗin."
   "Sunce jibi zasu masa, bansan inda zan samu kuɗin nan zuwa jibi ba, shiyasa kikaga hankalina ya tashi Abba bashi yake da shi ba, kaf ƴan uwansa babu wanda zai taimaka masa wallahi, suna da kuɗi sai dai basa taimakon sa, abinda yake sani kuka kenan."
   "Karki damu Basmah akwai Allah muyi addu'a insha Allah, Allah zai kawo hanya, gobe ai muna da aiki idan yaso sai muje mu tambayi accounter bashi zuwa ayi salary idan yaso sai su bamu aron 26k ɗin sai mu haɗa da 14k ɗin wajena in akayi salary sai su cire 10k cikin kuɗina kema su ciri 16k karki damu Allah ya bawa Abba lafiya."
   "Ameeen, amma Jidda bazan amshi kuɗin ki ba, kema kina fama da ɗawainiyar gidan ku, dan haka ki bari muje mu nemi bashin 40k ɗin insha Allah zan biya amma bazan sakaki cikin wahala ba."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki tare da girgiza kanta ta ɓata ranta.
    "Saboda ni Abba bai haifeni ba ko shiyasa bazan masa ɗawainiya ba, idan kika min haka baki min adalci ba, tamkar mahaifina haka nake masa kallo yadda baki isa hanani yiwa Ummana abu ba, haka baki isa ki hanani na yiwa abbanki abu ba, dan haka karki sake cemin komai ki share hawayen ki kukan ya isa haka insha Allah Abba zai samu lafiy."
    "Hmmm! Shikenan Jidda Allah yabar zumunci, amma asibiti kikaje ko nasan karya kika yiwa umma ba gidan mu kikaje ba, wato har yanzu baki fita a cikin wannan sha'anin ba ko Jidda.?"
   "Basmah na faɗa miki bazan fita ba dole sai na taya Hajara kwato ƴancinta yanzu haka maganar da nake da ke an sace Hajara."
    "An sace Hajara, kamar yaya sai kace wata kaza."
   "Da gaske ansace ta kuma ni gaskiya wannan Hisham ɗin shi nake zargi babu wanda zai sace Hajara sai shi Saboda ya kuɓutar da ɗan uwansa."
    "Amma abun da mamaki Jidda Hisham bazai sace Hajara ba domin yasan dole shi za'a zarga, Allah ya bayyana ta, amma dai Jidda ina ƙara faɗa miki ki fita cikin wannan Case ɗin bana son ki jawa kanki matsala."
  "Hmmm! Naji mubar maganar, ki tashi muje mu taya Umma aiki sai mu koma asibitin."
   "Okay."
Basmah tayi Maganar tana tashi suka fita.




************


"Wai Tutu bazaki daina wannan damuwar ba, zafa ki sawa kanki ciwo."
   "Hmmm! Samira dole fa na shiga cikin damuwa, gani nake kamar yaya Nameer baya sona, yaƙi bayyana Soyayyar mu, harta ga yaya Hisham da suke ƙaunar juna yaƙi bayyana masa akwai wani abu tsakanin mu, hankalina yana tashi idan na tuna da hakan zuciyata sai ta dinga tsinkewa ina matukar son yaya Nameer Soyayyar da ban san iyakar ta ba."
    Numfashi Samira ta saki tare da dafa kafaɗar Tutu tace.
   "Tutu ni kaina kullum ina wannan tunanin Soyayyar da kike yiwa yaya Nameer kamar shi baya miki, da Yaya Nameer yana sonki Tutu to wallahi da zai bayyanawa kowa Soyayyar ki, bazai ɓoye ba anya ma kuwa ba da gaske Nameer shi ya yiwa wannan yarinyar fyaɗe ba Tutu."?
    "A'a Samira na rantse da Allah Yaya Nameer bazai aikatawa wata mace fyaɗe ba, domin kuwa Mutum ne mai ƙyanƙyami bare kuma wannan Yar talakawa, da zaiyi lalata da wani to da dani zai fara saboda yadda na sake masa jiki nasan yaya Nameer mutum ne shi mai tsananin sha'awa, amma hakan bazai sa ya zalunci ƴar wata ba, na fi kowa sanin waye shi."
  Bakinta Samira ta taɓe tana yiwa Tutu kallon mahaukaciya kafin tace.
    "Na sani Soyayyar da kike yiwa yaya Nameer bazai taɓa barinki ki faɗi aibunsa ba, wannan Soyayyar ita take cutarki, da ita yake amfani yake cutarki ya kasa bayyanawa duniya yana sonki, soyayyar gaskiya ba'a ɓoye ta, ko da an ɓoyeta ita da kanta take bayyana kanta, ya mayar dake mahaukaciya sai wasa yake da tunaninki, Allah ya ganar da ke Tutu yasa ya aureki."
    "Ba karesa nake ba Samira nasan halin yaya Nameer nasan abinda zai aikata da wanda bazai aikata ba, wallahi bazai yiwa wata fyaɗe ba, ki yarda Samira ba halinsa bane, Hmm! Amma ke ba lallai ki yarda ba, Samira kece kaɗai wacce kika san wannan sirrin nawa muddun asirina ya tonu to kece zan zarga, dan Allah ki ɓoye min sirrina, ki bari har shi ya bayyana Soyayyata."
   "Bazan taɓa tona miki asiri ba Tutu domin kuwa na san menene amana, amma ina baki shawari muddun yaya Nameer ya dawo gida ki tabbatar Soyayyar ku ta bayyana idan ba haka ba zaki tura mota ta tashi ta barki da ƙura."
  Samira tayi Maganar tana miƙewa tare da ɗaukar mayafinta ta kuma cewa.
   "Ni kinga tafiyata Allah ya fiddo miki dashi."
  Kanta Tutu ta girgiza tare da tashi ta raka Samira har bakin get kafin ta dawo a falo ta tarar da wayar Hisham tana ringin hannu tasa ta ɗauka taga Sadiq ya bayyana a screen ɗin wayar bata ɗauki kiran ba ta haura bedroom ɗin su nocking tayi Hisham dake zaune yayi shuru yana saƙa da warwara akan Tunde, yace.
    "Yes come."
  Turo door ɗin tayi ta shigo cikin sanyin jiki gefen Hisham ta zauna sai kuma idanunta ya ciko da hawaye, sanin hawayen fuskarta yasa HISHAM sanya hannu ya share mata hawayen cike da ƙaunar ƴar uwarsa domin kuwa a rayuwarsa ya tsani abinda zai ɗaga mata hankali su biyu kawai Ummi ta haifa bashi da wani yaya ko ƙani itace ƴar uwarsa guda ɗaya tilo.
    "Tutu wai meyasa bakya jin rarrashi ne, ba nace miki Yayanki Nameer zai dawo gobe ba, ko baki yadda dani bane, kin damu akan Nameer sosai Tutu dole kuma ki damu domin kuwa Nameer shine hasken gidan mu, ɗan uwa ne na gari bazan bari wani abu ya cucesa ba zan karesa da jikina da kuma rayuwata."
  Kanta Tutu ta kaɗa tare da miƙa masa wayarsa.
    "Ya Sadiq ne yake kiranka."
   Amsa yayi tare da cewa.
    "Okay kije ki wanke fuskarki."
   "To Yaya."
Tayi Maganar tana tashi tabar wajen kiran Sadiq Hisham yayi Ringing ɗaya ya ɗaga.
    "Malam kazo airport ka ɗaukeni."
    "Whatt airport, serious."
   "Yeah da gaske wallahi ina jiranka ko su Daddy basu san dawowata ba."
    "Shegen gari, okay gani yanzu dama kuwa akwai game gobe."
  Yayi Maganar yana katse kiran tare da tashi ya zari key ɗinsa ya fita.




***********



Motoci huɗu ne sukayi parking a police station ɗin, da sauri Soja ya fito ya buɗe wata baƙar mota Abba ne ya fito hannunsa riƙe da waya shida commissioner bayansa masu tsaron nasa suka bi har cikin Police station, gaba ki ɗaya ƴan sanda miƙewa tsaye sukayi cikin girmamawa, kujera aka ɗaukowa Abba, zama yayi shima commissioner aka kawo masa kujerar ya zauna.
   Commissioner ne yace
    "Ku buɗe Nameer."
Cike da bin umarni Sargent Dogo yazo ya buɗe, miƙewa Nameer yayi ya fito tare da nufar Abba Abba yana ganin Nameer ya miƙe suka rungume juna cikin soyayya da kulawa kafin Abba ya zauna Nameer gefen ƙafar Abba ya jawo kujera ya zauna tare da kwantar da kansa a kafaɗar Abba.
    "Nameer ka takura da zaman nan ko.?"
    "No Abba ban takura ba rayuwa ce ba inda bata kai mutum Abba ina ummina ita kawai nake tunani nasan zata fi kowa damuwa."
    "Hmmm! Umminka ta damu ƙwarai Nameer sai dai ɗan uwanka yana kwantar mata da hankali, munyi magana da commissioner gashi nan yace dole ne sai an kaika kotu, zamu nemi lauyer insha Allah zan tsaya domin ganin ka samu adalci."
    "Abba bani da damuwa da zuwana kotu har yanzu bana jin koda tsinkewar zuciya saboda nasan ban aikata laifin da ake zargina dashi ba, Abba ina tunanin Hisham yayi fushi dani ko tun shekaran jiya daya zo bai kuma dawowa ba."
    "Ka sani Hisham bazai taɓa fushi da kai ba Nameer kasan zuciyar sa bazai iya jure ganinka cikin wannan halin bane sannan ya sanar dani baya son ƙara ganin fuskar Dpo wata ƙila hakan yasa yaƙi zuwa amma yana can yana ƙoƙarin ganin ya kuɓutar da kai."
   "Idan ka koma Abba kace masa ina kewarsa."
   "Shikenan zan isar masa da sakon."
   Commissioner ne ya tambayi Sargent Bala Dpo tare da basa umarnin ya shiga ya sanar dashi sunzo babu ɓata lokaci ya shiga kiran nasa.
    Babu jumawa Dpo ya fito cikin girmamawa ya sarawa commissioner tare da gaishe da Abba shima cikin girmamawa kafin ya zauna, commissioner ne yace.
    "Dpo ya ake ciki game da case ɗin Nameer me kuka tattara ga binciken da kukayi."?
    "Sir har yanzu muna kan bincike bamu kammala ba shiyasa ma bamu kai case ɗin kotu ba tukunna, amma binciken asibitin dai ya nuna cewa an ya kushe ta sosai ma'ana anyi kokawa da ita sannan kayan jikinta duk sun yaga, kuma the Best ma mun samu agogon hannun Nameer a jikinta wanda maybe bai san ya maƙale a jikinta ba domin kuwa doctor sunce a jikinta suka samu sanda aka kawota asibitin wannan agogon dai shine yake tabbatar mana da cewa Nameer shine yayi ƙoƙarin keta mata haddi, sannan ita yarinyar ma ansaceta jiya, sai dai sir Hisham muke zargi da saceta saboda shine kawai zai iya hakan duk da zargi muke, yanzu ma niyyar zuwa wajensa muke sai kukazo."
   Commissioner duban Nameer yayi tare da cewa.
    "Amma har yanzu 2days a wannan binciken kawai kuka tsaya, Dpo ganin agogon Nameer a jikin ta tabbas hujja ce sai dai fa ku sani za'a iya masa sharri saboda mahaifinsa babban mutum ne kuma yana da maƙiya shiyasa nake son kuyi bincike da kyau, dan haka na baka zuwa yau da gobe ka tabbatar ka gama tabbatar da gaskiyar binciken, maganar Hisham kuma kuna iya zuwa kuyi binciken ku duk da ba lallai ace shi ɗin bane."
    "Shikenan Sir insha Allah zanyi yadda kace."
   Numfashi Abba ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Commissioner ina son idan ta tabbata za'a kai Nameer kotu, to karku sanar da Hisham ranar Shari'ar."
   "Meyasa."?
Commissioner yayi tambayar yana kallon Abba.
    "Saboda zai iya ɓata komai."
    "Shikenan, zamu iya tafiya."
    Duban Nameer Abba yayi yace.
    "Zan koma Nameer insha Allah komai zaizo cikin sauƙi dan Allah karka ɗaga hankalinka."
    "Insha Allah Abba ka cewa Ummi ina gaisheta kuma nayi kewarta, Abba Tutu kace injini tayi haƙuri karta sa damuwa a ranta ta dinga cin abinci."
   "Saƙon duk zai isa garesu ka kula Nameer."
  Abba yayi Maganar yana tashi suka fita kafin Sargent Dogo ya mayar da Nameer cikin cell, shuru Nameer yayi yana nazarin maganganun su Abba, cikin ransa yace.
    (Meyasa Hisham ya iya wadannan kwanakin baizo ya ganni ba, me yake shiryawa tabbas Hisham ne ya ɗauke wannan yarinyar domin ganin ya kuɓutar dani to amma ko ta ina Case ɗina a dabaibaye yake ta yaya Hisham zai iya warwarewa, nasan halinsa sarai zai iya aikata komai domin idan ya yanke hukunci baya jin Muryar kowa, lallai na kuɓuta Dpo bazai shaba a wajen Hisham, nasan yanzu yana can yayi target ɗin Jidda zai kuma iya aikata mata duk abinda ya kudurta a ransa sai dai bazan so hakan ba.)
  Ya yi maganar yana sakin numfashi, jingina kansa yayi jikin cell ɗin tare da yin shuru.




**********


"Yau tsawon kwana biyu babu labarin ki ko wayarki na kira baya tafiya."
    Gyara zamanta Wamimo tayi tace.
    "Naje ƙauye ne kinsan na kira yata dake ƙauyen Agunjin, nace idan taji labarin wani shahararren matsafi wanda yake karya kafi ta min magana, to ranar da mukaje gidan Iyabo da ke bayan na koma gida shine sai ta kirani take cewa wani mai tsafi yazo shi, kuma aikinsa yana yi sosai yana karya kafi, to shine fa naje ƙauyen naso dawowa a ranar to layi yayi yawa a wajen matsafin shiyasa na kwana biyu, babu network ne ma a ƙauyen shiyasa ban kiraki ba."
    "Wamimo da gaske kike min wannan Maganar.?"
   Mamabisola ta tambayeta cikin zakuwa.
   "Ƙwarai kuwa na samu ganawa da matsafin na kuma masa bayanin ki, yace dole sai kinzo da kanki ya duba ya gani idan aikin zai yiwu."
   Numfashi Mamabisola ta saki tace.
    "Hmmm! To ki shirya jibi muje Wamimo Allah yasa aikin nan ya yiwu ina son ganin bayan Iyabo ina son ganin dukiyar sa ta tarwatse ina son ganin ya rasa komai a rayuwarsa idan hakan bata kasance ba hankalina bazai taɓa kwanciya ba."
   "Karki damu Mamabisola wannan aikin sunansa ya yiwu domin kuwa ance wannan matsafin bai taɓa aiki bai yiwu ba, indai yayi aiki to tabbas sai aikin yayi, yanzu bara naje kasuwa anjuma zan dawo."
     "Shikenan kije dawon."

*_Page 13 to 14_





Washegari da safe bayan Hisham sun kammala breakfast ne wajajen 11:30am ya ɗauki wayarsa tare da duban Abba yace.
   "Abba zanje Company yau saboda naga yadda abubuwan suke tafiya."
   "Okay hakan yayi, amma baka sanar dani halin da ake ciki ba, ina yarinyar da ka ɗauke ɗin, Dpo ya fara zargin kaine ka saceta kuma yana gaf da tunkaro ka domin tuhumar ka sai ka shiryawa tseratar da kanka."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Nayi mamaki ai da basu zo da wuri ba, nasan dama dole ni zai zarga, karka damu Abba dai-dai nake dashi."
     Ummi ce tace.
  "Hisham wai Meyasa baka tsoron rayuwa ne kai wannan duniyar fa abar tsoro ce Hisham amma kai sam bata baka tsoro, meye ribarka idan ka ɓoye wannan yarinyar, to kaje ka buɗe ƴar mutane muddun kana gudun ɓacin raina idan ba hakaba sai na saɓa maka yau naga shashancin banza."
   "Ummi bazaki fahimta bane amma ni naga ribar da zan ɗauka, kin sani Ummi idan na yanke hukunci *_KO UMARNIN ZUCIYATA BANA BI_* dole sai na aiwatar nake samun sukuni, *_ƊABIAR ƊANKI NE KIN SANI_*."
  "A Wannan taƙin kuwa zan taka maka burki da ƙanin ubanka, kaje ka sau yarinyar nan dai na faɗa maka."
   Duban su Abba yayi yace.
    "Hisham na yarda da kai bana kuma jin zaka bani kunya kaje ka binciki yarinyar da kyau sannan ka sake cikin ƙoshin lafiya ta koma ga iyayenta."
   "Ya haka Abban Hisham, kamar fa ɗaure masa gindi kake akan me zaka basa ƙarfin gwiwwa bayan kasan ɗanka ba ji yake ba, mutumin da ya kashe kansa da girman kai da izza harshi ne Mutum, Allah ya yiwu Nameer albarka shi sam bashi da wannan ɗagawar taka, ta inda ya fika morar Rayuwar Duniya kenan, amma ka zauna kana bin Bayan yaro kuma kasan halin zuciyarsa sarai, wannan ba gata bane."
   Ummi tayi Maganar tana hararar Hisham murmushi Hisham ya saki ganin ummi na neman rikice masa ya sashi saurin fita, Abba murmushi yayi yace.
   "To ai ya fita faɗan sai ya ƙare ko."
  Shima Abba yayi Maganar yana tashi a wajen, bakinta Ummi ta taɓe tana kallon Tutu tace.
    "Ke kuma meye yake damunki kin hana rayuwarki sukuni tunda Nameer ya bar gidan nan,  wai ke bazakiyi tawakkali ba, ko ce miki akayi Nameer ɗin bazai dawo bane, to ki daina wannan damuwar bana so."
   "Ummi gidan ne duk babu daɗi babu yaya Nameer."
    "Insha Allah Yayanki zai dawo mu cigaba da addu'a, sai ki tattare daining ɗin."
     "To Ummi Allah yasa."
Da ameeen Ummi ta Amsa tare da tashi ta haura sama.



 Hisham yana cikin tuƙi yaji shigowar kuɗin da Aunty Halima ta masa alƙawari, wayar ya ɗaga ya kira Sadiq Ringing ɗaya ya ɗauka, daga nan Hisham yace.
    "Mu haɗu Aminci News by 1:00."
   Ya katse kiran Companyn ya shige ya je ya duba abubuwan da ke tafiya, Salisu ya aika BANK ya ciro masa wannan kuɗaɗen ya dawo, ya juma sosai cikin Companyn shine bai baro Companyn ba har sai da yayi Sallah kafin ya kira Sadiq yace su haɗu yanzu gashi nan zuwa, ya zari key ɗinsa ya fice daga Companyn.
   A bakin ofishin Jaridar ya samu Sadiq yana tsaye da alamu saukesa akayi parking Hisham yayi tare da fitowa hannunsa riƙe da baƙar jaka ya nufo Sadiq yana dariya hannu ya basa sukayi musabaha.
   "Kaddai ka juma kana jirana."
    "Ina ban juma ba yanzu Ib ya sauƙeni ya akayi me zamuyi a ciki."?
  "Muje kawai Sadiq kai dai ka min video record akan duk wata tattaunawar da zamuyi a wajen ta, muje amma karka fara ɗaukar har sai na taka maka ƙafa."
  Hisham yayi Maganar tare da yin gaba Sadiq yabi bayansa.


"Jidda dama na sani ba lallai mu samu rancen kuɗin nan ba, Hmm! Bansan ya zanyi ba, Abbana yana cikin matsala ina son na ceci rayuwarsa babu wanda zai masa idan bani ba."
    Cikin kulawa Jidda da alamun damuwa Jidda tace.
    "Hmmm! Basmah akwai Allah kuma zai taimaka karki damu bari mu koma gida insha Allah bazamu rasa abin yi ba."
   "Any................. ..
 "Assalamu alaikum."
Sallamar Hisham ta katse Maganar da Basmah zatayi gabaki ɗaya idanu suka ɗaga suka kalli bakin ƙofar, ganin Hisham yasa Jidda sakin murmushi domin kuwa tasan yauma haukar ce ta kawosa.
   "Ameeen wa'alaiku mussalam."
    Shigowa sukayi Hisham ya harɗe hannunsa tare da tsayawa a gabanta.
    "Bana hana maƙiyi abu biyu wajen zama da kuma ruwan sha."
   Murmushi Hisham ya yi yace.
    "Kina nufin mu zauna kenan."?
   "Kwarai kuwa."
 Zama sukayi Sadiq da Basmah dai kallon ikon Allah suke, Hisham gyaran murya yayi tare da cewa.
      "Hisham ya faɗi domin kuwa ya gagara cire ɗan uwansa daga cell, shiyasa na dawo gareki durƙusawa wada ba gajiyawa bace neman taimako ne."
   "Hmmm! Ashe dama matsiyaciya tana da rana duk da nasan ungulu bata jimar banza meke tafe da kai shin yauma kazo kamin barazanar ce akan wancan dabban ɗan uwan naka, kayi ikrari har yanzu ka kasa cikawa."
   "Hmmm! Ko da zancena na farko ya nuna ba tashin hankali bane ya kawo ni."
  Yayi Maganar yana taka kafar Sadiq.
    "Meya kawo ka to.?"
"Neman alfarma ina son ki cire hannunki a wannan Case ɗin kije Police station ki nuna ke baki san komai ba, infact ma sharri yarinyar take yiwa Nameer, ba kyauta zaki min ba biyanki zanyi manyan kuɗaɗen da zasu saya muku gida abar na haya kiyi tunani ba gaggawa nake ba."
    Kallon Hisham Jidda tayi wato ma ya raina mata wayo ya rasa mafita shine zai bata cin hanci, saboda tsinannen ɗan uwansa ya kuɓuta.
   Za tayi Magana Basmah ta taka ta tare da sunkuyawa dai-dai kunnenta ta mata raɗa.
    "Dama ce mai kyau da zamuyi amfani dashi wajen samun kuɗin da muke nema, ki amshi tayinsa da zummar zakiyi aikin mu amshe kuɗin banza, amma a zahiri baza kiyi aikin ba, ki duba halin da Abbana yake ciki ki cecesa Jidda kimin wannan taimakon, ba wai zakiyi masa aikin bane kuɗin kawai zaki amsa kuma ki ƙi yin aikin Please Jidda."
    Shuru Jidda tayi zuciyarta na kai kawo.
    _Anya kuwa hakan zata yiwu, to amma Basmah tana bukatar taimako, zanso ace na taimaka mata amma ba ta wannan hanyar ba, domin kuwa amsar cin hanci babban laifi ne duk da ba aikin zan masa ba, ko da yake koda na amsa babu wani laifi domin kuwa bazan taɓa take gaskiya ba, gaskiyar Basmah zan amsa na nuna masa bashi da yawo._
   Murmushi Jidda ta saki kafin tace.
    "Amma dai anyi faɗuwar baƙar tasa ko nace faɗuwar guzuma ƴa kwance uwa kwance, ka bada maza ina kurin ina barazanar shin ka fasa tozarta nin ne, hmm an dai ji kunya, ba damuwa nawa ne farashin."
    Murmushi Hisham ya saki yace.
   "5million babu tayi idan sun miki shikenan idan basu miki ba zan koma da kuɗina."
    Murmushi Jidda itama ta saki tace.
    "Ciniki ya faɗa zanje na ƙaryata Hajara, Ɗan uwanka zai fito nan da dare."
       jakar ya ijiye mata tare zazzage duka kuɗaɗen a ƙasa yace.
    "Ga su nan, Sadiq muje."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana miƙewa Sadiq dake ɗaukar dukkan abinda ke faruwa ya tashi suka fice tare, Jidda duban Basmah tayi tace.
      "Anyi banyi kuskure ba kuwa Basmah zuciyata bata kwanta da Hisham ba, anya kuwa babu wani abu a ƙasan zuciyar sa.?"
  Numfashi Basmah ta saki tace.
    "Babu komai Jidda karki wani damu kawai ɗan uwansa yake son ya fitar a cikin matsala, ya gagara ne kawai yaga bashi da wata mafita sai wannan, kuma ni a ganina wannan dama ce mai kyau tunda dama Umma tace ki cire hannunki a cikin wannan Case ɗin kinga kin samu riba biyu kenan, ga kuɗi ga kuma bin umarnin Umma kije kawai ki cire hannunki kalas magana ta ƙare."
    A fusace Jidda ta kalli Basmah tare da cewa.
    "Whatt! Kinsan abinda kike faɗi kuwa na cire hannuna fa kikace, amma sanda kike so na amshi kuɗin me kika cemin ko kin manta na tuna miki, ba cewa kikayi na amshi kuɗin naƙi masa aikin ba, shine yanzu zakiyi ƙoƙarin turani ga hallaka, to wallahi tashi zan naje har gidan su na mai da masa da wanannan kayan tsiyar tasa, domin kuwa bazan taɓa ganin gaskiya na take ta ba, bazai yiwu ba, kai amma nima mahaukaciya ce dana biye miki na amshi kuɗin nan, yanzu dana amshi wannan kuɗin naje gida na cewa Umma ina na samesu, kin san dai sai ta bincike ni ƙarshe ma raina yayi mungun ɓaci kin kaini kin baro Basmah."
  Tayi Maganar cikin ɓacin rai sai yanzu ma taji haushin kanta da ta amshi wannan kuɗin shikenan zai rainata ya dinga mata kallon ƙasƙantacciya mai kwaɗayin abun duniya.
    "Jidda baki fahimce ni bane Abbana yana cikin matsala kin sani sannan zaki cecemu ne idan kin amshi kuɗin kina gani fa cikin office ɗin nan abamu aron kuɗin sunsan za'a cire wannan kuɗin cikin salary namu amma sukace babu kuɗi a ƙasa kuma akwai kuɗin bawai babu ba, to ina zamuje a bamu, muna ganin dama tazo kuma muna bukata sai mu barsa, Jidda muje mu yiwa Umma bayani a nutse zata fahimce mu, sannan.......
  Hannu Jidda ta ɗagawa Basmah tace.
    "Sannan na daure na cire hannuna a cikin Case ɗin ko? To impossible."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana tashi ta fice daga office ɗin nata, numfashi Basmah ta saki tare da dafe kanta tasan halin taurin kan Jidda sarai dama ba lallai ta amince ta cire hannunta a case ɗin ba.




  "Ban taɓa ganin Hisham ya nemi alfarma a wajen wata ba sai yau ban taɓa ganin an faɗawa Hisham baƙaƙen magana yayi shuru ba sai yau, tabbas kana ƙaunar Nameer fiye da kowa a wannan duniyar, sai dai nayi mamakin da ka shanye maganganun wannan yarinyar."
  Murmushi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa
    "Hmmm! Hisham baya jewar banza kamar yadda ta faɗa, ba Nameer yasa naje mata da wannan buƙatar ba domin kuwa ina da hujjar da zai sanya Nameer ya fito yanzu haka Police station zamu wuce tare da Nameer zamu fito."
    "Ban fahimta ba Hisham me yasa to kaje wajenta shin meye manufar, sannan shi kuma wannan video record ɗin da kasa nayi fa."?
    "Ka sanya idanu kayi kallo Sadiq zaka fahimci manufata, wannan shine wasan da nace maka zamu buga kuɗina zai dawo Naira biyar bazaiyi ciwon kai ba, ko da kuwa ta kashe wani abu daga ciki na rantse da Allah dai ta biya."
   "To Allah ya kyauta."
Sadiq yayi Maganar a takaice babu wanda ya sake magana har suka isa Police station ɗin.
      Shigar su cikin station ɗin, Hisham darect cell ya nufa Nameer na zaune ya haɗa kansa da bango yaji muryar Hisham.
    "Hmmm! Wata ƙila ɗan uwana yana cikin damuwa nasan dole izuwa yanzu ka fara gajiya da zaman wajen nan."
   Kansa Nameer ya ɗago ya dubi Hisham kafin yace.
    "Sadiq Barka da zuwa yaushe ka dawo ƙasar.?"
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki domin kuwa ya hango fushi Nameer yake dashi ya kuma san fushin bazai wuce akan rashin dawowarsa station ɗin ba, Sadiq ne ya amsa da.
   "Jiya shigowata sai kuma nake jin labari marar daɗi."
   Kafin Nameer yayi Magana Hisham yace.
    "Kayi haƙuri nasan fushi kake dani akan rashin dawowata, nayi hakan ne saboda bana son ganinka a cikin wannan halin nayi alkawarin bazan dawo station ɗin nan ba har sai da hujjar da zata kuɓutar da kai."
   "Bana fushi da kai Hisham sai dai ina fushi da zuciyar ka, saboda ta cika rashin haƙuri zafinta yayi yawa, nasan Kaine ka sace wannan yarinyar meyasa zakayi haka Hisham kuskure ne babba ka tafka bana son ka shigo haɗari saboda ni, da ka shiga cikin tashin hankali Gara ace na rasa rayuwata."
    "Ina zuwa Nameer Sadiq muje wajen Dpo tukunna."
   Yayi Maganar yana juyawa domin kuwa bashi da amsar da zai bawa Nameer, ko inda su Sargent Bala yake Hisham bai kalla ba ya wuce office ɗin Dpo, babu wanda ya kulasa kasancewar sunsan halinsa nocking yayi Dpo ya basu damar shigowa sosai Dpo yayi mamakin ganin Hisham mutumin da yake da niyyar zuwa garesa sai gashi ya kawo kansa, zama Hisham yayi tare da sakin murmushi yace.
    "Nasan dama dole akwai ranar da ɗan tsakon Police zaiji kunya sai ga kuma ranar tazo, har abada ahalin IYABO masu Nasara ne ko da alfarma ko babu."
  Murmushi Dpo yayi yace.
    "Na fahimta, wani tsuntsun tarkon ka kuma ya kama Hisham Abdulzahir.?"
    "Ba tsuntsu ba dai sai dai tsuntsuwa domin kuwa na farauto tsuntsuwar kuma har na samu kwamishon farauta, ita tsuntsuwar da kuke nema tana gareni."
   "Hajara tana wajenka kenan, ehh tabbas ƴar Jaridar nan tayi gaskiya da tace kaine zaka sace yarinyar, meye ribarka da hakan.?"
   "Hmmm! Ita Zabiyar har ta hasashoni kenan, we'll ribar na kawo maka yanzu idan ka shirya amsa sai na gabatar maka."
  Numfashi Dpo ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Hmmm duk tsuntsun da yaja ruwa ka sani dole sai wannan ruwan ya dakesa, gabatar."
   "Shikenan zan kuwa jawo babban ruwan da zai dake ku amma ni tuni na zillewa wannan ruwan."
  Yayi Maganar yana ɗauko wayarsa a aljihu ya danna recording ɗin da yayi na Hajara ya ijiye, gaba daya shuru sukayi suna sauraron recording ɗin, Sadiq sosai yasha mamaki musamman da yaji wai Tunde ne yasa ayiwa Nameer wannan ƙazafin, Dpo kuwa wani irin gumi ne ya keto masa, domin kuwa duk binciken da sukayi nunawa yake Nameer ne ya aikata bayan recording ɗin ya ƙare ne Hisham ya kashe tare da mayar da wayarsa aljihu ya saki murmushi.
   "Ya kamata ka cire wannan kakin naka domin kuwa sam wannan aikin bai dace da kai ba tunda har ka gagara gano gaskiya, Dpo na faɗa maka shiga komar Hisham ba abu bane mai sauƙi, amma zan kyaleka za kuma mu cigaba da bawa karnuka ƙashi, sai dai fa dole ne a wannan karon kamin wani aiki sannan a rufe babin Case ɗin Nameer gaba ɗaya kamar ba'a taɓa yiba bana bukatar ko Abba yasan da labarin Tunde ne ya haɗa wannan tarkon, sai kuma aikin da nace zaka min, idan ka amince zan ɗaukaka darajar ka ta hanyar samun ƙarin girma."
  Shuru Dpo yayi tare da ɗago idanunsa ya kalli Hisham.

*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻




*Page 17 to 18*




"Hisham ka sani akan gaskiya ta nake aiki, kamar yadda ka buƙata za'a rufe Case ɗin Nameer ba kuma za'a tuhumi Tunde ba kamar yadda ka bukata, sai dai ban san wani aiki zaka bani ba, idan har babu cutarwa a ciki ƙofata a buɗe take."
    Kallon Dpo Hisham yayi kafin ya saki numfashi yace.
    "Yarinyar nan wannan ƴar tashar da ta sanya ku kama Nameer nake son kuje ku kamota ina zaune ina jira ku jefata cell yayin da kuka fitar da Nameer wannan shine aikin da zaka min Dpo."
   "Ban fahimta ba Hisham ta yaya zamu kamata bayan bamu da hujjar yin hakan ka sani doka ce baza'a kama mutum ba har sai da hujjar da take nuna yayi laifin da za'a kamasa ita kuma wannan yarinyar bata da wani laifi."
  "Ita kuwa take da laifi ko sanya bakinta wajen yiwa Nameer ƙazafi wannan ma babban laifi ne kuma idan mun so zamu shigar da ƙara akan hakan kuma dole ku amsa, bar wannan ma tunda nace a rufe wannan babin Case ɗin, ni ina da cikakkiyar hujjar da za'a kamata domin kuwa naje mata da cin hanci kuma ta amsa har Naira miliyan biyar a hanuna da zummar zatazo ta ƙaryata kanta, wannan babban laifi ne a dokan ce sannan hukuncin wanda yayi wannan kora ne daga wajen aikinsa, ta muku furucin cewa zata iya sawa ku rasa aikin ku, to wannan kalmar zata dawo kanta domin kuwa ita zata rasa aikinta."
  Yayi Maganar yana amsar wayar Sadiq ya kunna recording ɗin ya miƙawa Dpo amsa Dpo yayi kafin Hisham ya cigaba da cewa.
    "Wannan shine shaidar gani da ido naje na gwada muku gaskiyar ta ne ta yadda zaku fahimci wacece ita ba tasan ina da hujjar da zan saka a saki Nameer ba, nayi hakanne saboda ku san wacece ita dan haka hukunci yana gareku."
  Numfashi Dpo ya saki yana duban video ɗin murmushi ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Mutum mungun ice, Hisham ko da baka nemi na kama Jidda ba, dole ne a kamata bara a kira Sargent Dogo suje suyi arresting ɗin ta, ina kuka baro ta.?"
    "Tana can ofishin su, sannan a taho min da kuɗina."
   Waya Dpo ya ɗaga ya kira Sargent Dogo babu jumawa kuwa ya shigo umarni ya basa da ya haɗa Police Uku suje suyi arresting ɗin Jidda, a office ɗin su sannan su taho da kuɗin da aka bata."
  Sarawa Dpo Sargent Dogo yayi tare da furta.
   "Yes Sir."
Yayi Maganar yana ficewa, takaddar belin NAMEER Dpo ya bawa Hisham.





***********



Jidda tunda ta fita daga office ɗin take tsaye a waje duk sai taji babu daɗi ta gwammace da bata amshi wannan kuɗin ba, zuciyar ta sai ayyana mata take taje ta mayar masa da kuɗinsa, juyawa tayi zata shiga office ɗin nata da niyyar ɗaukar kuɗin taje ta mayar wa Hisham taji kuwwar motar Police a bakin ofishin Jaridar tasu, juyawa Jidda tayi tare da tsayawa tana nazarin me kuma ya kawo Police wannan office ɗin.
    Sargent Dogo ne suka shigo su Uku basu kula Jidda ba duk da Sargent Dogo ya ganta, darect ofishin shugaban nasu suka shige yana zaune suka shigo, miƙewa Manager yayi cike da mamaki yace.
    "Yallabai lafiya kuwa."?
Sargent Dogo ne yace.
   "Lafiya bata cika kawo mu ba, amma da zaka bamu dama zamu sanar da kai abinda ya kawo mu."
    "Ina sauraronka meke tafe daku."
   Sargent Dogo wayar Sadiq ya miƙawa Manager bayan ya kunna video record ɗin,  nutsuwa Manager yayi yana kallon videon wani irin ɓaci ransa yayi Jidda da suke yiwa kallon mai ƙwazo da gaskiya yau itace take neman ɓatawa ofishin su suna ta rasa inda zatayi almundahana sai a ofishin su, numfashi ya saki cikin ɓacin rai yace.
    "Kuje ku kamata zanzo office ɗin naku yanzu."
   Manager yayi Maganar cikin tashin hankali domin kuwa yasan wannan video ɗin ya fita jaridar su ta gama yawo, fita sukayi daga office ɗin Manager har yanzu Jidda tana tsaye har Basmah ma ta fito Sargent Dogo a gabanta ya tsaya tare da cewa.
    "Munzo mu tafi da ke ne.?"
   Zaro idanunta Jidda tayi zuciyarta ta tsinke tace.
    "Whatt! Ku tafi kuma laifin me na aikata.?"
    "Idan munje zakiji umarni ne aka bamu mu tafi dake office."
  Kanta Jidda ta ɗaga ta kalli Basmah kafin tace.
    "Basmah karki sanar da Umma abinda yake faruwa ko da ban dawo ba."
   Tayi Maganar tana shigewa gabansa Yar sanda mace ce ta rufa mata baya, Basmah shuru tayi cikin tashin hankali wani irin laifi Jidda zatayi wanda har za'a kamata bata shiga harkar kowa ba'a taɓa rabata faɗa da wani ba.
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un!."
  Shine abinda Basmah ta furta cikin tashin hankali marar misaltuwa.




 ***********


Ko da suka isa Police station ɗin Sargent Dogo da kansa yaje ya sanarwa Dpo sun taho da ita, bayan sun gama tattaunawa ne da Hisham tare da alƙawarin da Hisham ya yiwa Dpo na zai sakar masa da Hajara muddun ya rufe masa Jidda na tsawon kwana Uku babu beli, yayi Maganar yana sakin murmushi tare da miƙewa Dpo shima miƙewa yayi suka fito tare, Jidda tana tsaye cikin tashin hankali taga Hisham da Dpo idanunta Jidda ta zaro tare da furta.
   "Kaine!?"
   Dariya Hisham ya yi tunda take ganinsa bata taɓa ganin yayi dariya ba sai dai murmushi sai gashi yanzu yana dariya.
    "Yeah ni ne kinyi mamaki ne ko kin manta Ungulu bata jimar banza kina zaton zaki taka Hisham ne ki taka banza, ba'a taɓa ja da Hisham aci bulus ki shiryawa kare kanki domin kuwa nayi alƙawarin tozarta rayuwarki."
   Nameer a zabure ya miƙe jin furucin Hisham yana mamakin me Jidda tayi aka kawota nan.
    Bakinta Jidda ta cije tare da cewa.
    "Kayi nasarar jefani cikin tarkonka Hisham amma ka sani wannan shine mafi girman kuskure da kayi a rayuwarka."
   Shima murmushi yayi yayin da Dpo da kansa ya buɗe Nameer kusa da ita Hisham ya tsaya daf har yanzu bakinsa na ɗauke da murmushi.
    "Na faɗa miki bazan waiwayo gareki ba har sai NAMEER ya fita daga komar hukuma, idan Banda ke jahilace wawiya marar lissafi, an faɗa miki Hisham zai nemi taimakon ƙaskantacciya irinki ne, bari kiji wannan abin da na miki yanzu ƙofa na ƙwanƙwasa miki ba'a buɗe ƙofar ba tukunna kin sokeni a ƙirji kuma na faɗa miki idan na tashi sukanki a ƙahon zuciyar ki zan sokeki, wannan jifan da na miki mutum biyu zai samu na farko ke da kuma mahaifiyar ki domin kuwa itama sai taji ciwo kamar yadda kika saka tawa Mahaifiyar taji ciwon rufe mata ɗa da kika saka akayi, wasan ya fara daga yanzu mu buga mu gani tsakanin ni da ke waye zai faɗi."
   Wani irin huci Jidda take sosai ranta ya hasala wato tarko Hisham ya ɗana mata da wadannan kuɗaɗen, zatayi Magana Manager ɗinsu ya shigo tare da katseta yana wurga mata takaddu guda biyu Basmah ce a bayansa cikin tashin hankali, idanunta sai zubar da hawaye yake duk abinda ya samu Basmah lallai itace sila.
    "Baki isa kin ɓata mana sunan ofishin mu ba, kin san girman laifin da kika aikata kuwa amsar cin hanci almundahana a office ɗin mu a matsayinki na ƴar jarida da kece zakiyi kira da a daina, wannan takaddun na korarki ne daga aiki mun koreki karki sake doso mana office bama bukatar ki."
    Wasu hawaye ne masu zafi suka zubo daga idanun Jidda babu wanda zai yarda da ita ko mai zata faɗa, shikenan ta rasa aikinta ta rasa damarta ta rasa hanyar da take taimakon ahalinta yaya zasuyi da me zasuci abinci.
   Ɗagowa tayi ta kalli Hisham tare da girgiza kanta tace.
    "Kaji daɗi ko, kaji daɗi ka zuba ruwa a ƙasa ka sunkuya ka shanye amma na rantse kayi danasanin taɓa min aikina."
   Tayi Maganar dai-dai zuwaira na tura ta cikin cell ɗin murmushi HISHAM ya saki Nameer ne ya dubi Dpo tare da cewa.
    "Wai me kuke aikatawa ne haka macece fa ita akan me zaku tozarta ta, wannan ba adalci bane Hisham meyasa Meyasa baka yafiya Dpo ka bani belin ta."
   "Bazai yiwu ba domin kuwa bazataci banza ba, amanar ƙasarta taci dan haka dole a hukuntata, babu ruwanka a cikin wannan Case ɗin Nameer."
    "Da ruwana Hisham bazan zuba idanu ku wulaƙanta mace ba."
  Dpo ne yace.
"Sorry Nameer ko da za'a ba da belinta ba yau ba."
  Numfashi Nameer ya saki tare ficewa a zuciye, hannu Hisham ya bawa Dpo sukayi musabaha, yana amsar kudaden sa kafin suka bi bayan Nameer shi da Sadiq.


  "Manager me kake bukata naga kamar kana da abin cewa?"
  Dpo ya jefa masa tambayar bayan fitar su Hisham.
    "Sunan Jarida ta, karta ɓaci dan Allah ka rufa min asiri kar aji wannan Case ɗin Please Dpo kasan ni bani da laifi, idan wannan video ya fita tabbas zan samu matsala."
   "Hakane Manager baka da laifi gaskiya ne insha Allah babu abinda zai faru zanyi magana da Hisham ɗin insha Allah zai goge video ɗin."
   "Na gode Dpo."
  Manager yayi Maganar yana hararar Jidda.

  Basmah kuwa sosai take kuka hankalinta yayi mungun tashi, Dpo ne ya kalleta tare da cewa ta fice tabar masa wajen cikin tsoro Basmah tabar wajen.
    Jidda tunda ta shiga cell ɗin ta haɗa kai da gwiwwa cikin matsanancin tashin hankali, idan Umma taji wannan labarin tasan hawan jininta sai ya tashi, hawaye ne ya biyo idanunta taja numfashi tare da furta.
    "Hisham! Hisham! Hmmm.!"
  Sai kuma ta daki bango da hannunta zuciyarta na tafasa tana jin wani irin haushi da tsanar Hisham.




************


Basmah a wajen station ɗin ta tsaya tare da kiran Ahmad tace yazo yanzu su haɗu a ƙofar gidan su, kafin ta katse kiran tana tare adaidaita sahu ta hau, yama rigata zuwa a tsaye jikin babur ɗinsa ta samesa, mai adaidaita ta biya kuɗinsa tare isowa wajen Ahmad yana ganinta ya tabbatar ba lafiya ba domin kuwa idanunta sunyi jajur alamun tasha kuka.
    "Basmah lafiya ko jikin Abban ne.?"
    "A'a Ahmad, Ahmad! Nasa Jidda a cikin masifa banyi zaton zata shiga cikin wannan balain ba, mai zan cewa ummanta me zan ce mata innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un!."
   "Basmah ki nutsu ki tsayar da kukan ki faɗa min meke faruwa."
   Cikin shashshekar kuka take sanar da Ahmad abinda ya faru, numfashi Ahmad ya saki tare da furta.
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Ashsha! Basmah garin yaya kika bata wannan gurguwar shawarar, kin san hakan babban laifi ne, a yanzu rayuwa ta sauya ba kowa bane yake yadda yayi taimako saboda zaka taimaki mutum ne sai shi kuma ya jefaka cikin masifa yanzu ina ranar wannan taimakon, yanzu tana can police station ɗin kenan.?"
   "Tana can ina cikin tashin hankali Ahmad bansan wacce irin masifa Jidda zata faɗa ba, nayi kuskure Ahmad ka kuɓutar da Jidda dan Allah."
    "Hmmm! Babban kuskure ma kuwa amma ita kanta Jidda tasan bazaki mata haka saboda ta tozarta ba, na tabbata zata miki kyakkyawar fahimta, yanzu bamu da damar da zamuje muyi belin Jidda dole ne sai ta kwana, gobe zanje Police station, yanzu abinda zai faru muje gidan nasu wajen Umman ni zan fahimtar da ita."
    "Amma Jidda tace bata son Umma tasan tana Police station hankalinta zai tashi tana da hawan jini."
    "Karki damu nasan yadda zan fahimtar da ita."
   Kanta Basmah ta ɗaga kafin suka nufi gidan su Jidda Basmah ce ta fara shiga ta samu Umma zaune cikin barandarta, amsa sallamar Basmah Umma tayi tana cewa.
   "Basmah kece yanzu bakije wajen aikin naku bane yau."?
    "Naje Umma ni da Ahmad zai shigo ku gaisa."
  Murmushi Umma tayi tace.
   "Ai sai kice min keda suruki na ne, kije ki shigo dashi Umma tayi Maganar tana ɗaukar hijabinta ta sanya a jikinta, da sallama Ahmad ya shigo suka gaisa Umma tace ya zauna a tabarma zama Ahmad yayi kansa a sunkuye.
    "Barka da rana Umma fatan mun sameki lafiya."
   "Lafiya lau Amadu ya aikin naka da can gidan naku."
    "Komai lafiya Umma."
  "Masha Allah! Allah ya muku albarka, Basmah yau ke kin tashi da wuri kenan kinbar ƴar Uwar taki a can."?
    Kanta Basmah ta sunkuyar ƙasa idanunta ya ciko da hawaye Ahmad ne yayi saurin cewa.
    "Umma dama ai munzo mu miki bayani dan gane da jiddan, amm Umma dan Allah ki nutsu ki saurare mu karki ɗaga hankalinki."
   Cikin tsinkewar zuciya Umma tace.
    "Amadu wani abunne ya faru da Jidda ko accident tayi ta mutu ne."?
   "A'a Umma ki kwantar da hankalinki babu abinda ya samu Jidda tana lafiya, kawai ƴar hatsaniyar da suka samu da wannan yaron ɗan gidan Iyabo HISHAM shine yayi report ɗinta a police station bayan an gano ƙazafi aka yiwa ɗan uwan nasa shine fa Police suka kamata, amma Umma ba wata matsala ba ce insha Allah gobe zanje nayi belinta, dan yanzu bazai yiwu su bani belinta bane da tuni mun dawo tare da ita amma insha Allah gobe zata dawo sai kuma musan yadda zamuyi mu kashe Case ɗin."
  Umma idanunta ta runtse tare da furta.
   "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Allahumma ajirni fil musibati! Subahanallah! Tana Police station wato da nace ta cire hannunta a case ɗin bata cire ba, sanda ta jawo min masifa, da Jidda taji magana ta da wannan tashin hankalin bai sameta ba abinda nake kiyaye mata kullum kenan bata da gata ta daina shiga cikin rigimar da bata shafeta ba, Amadu yanzu goben zata dawo kana jin kuma case ɗin zai iya mutuwa, kai ɗan sanda ne ka taimaka min Amadu kuje gidan Alhaji Iyabo ku bawa yaron haƙuri idan ta kama ni naje da kaina ne ma sai naje."
    "A'a Umma ba sai kinje ba nima zan iya komai Jidda zata dawo insha Allah ki kwantar da hankalinki, Basmah ki zauna tare da Umma ni zanje asibiti sai na sanar da Mama abinda ke faruwa kar ta jiki shuru ki kula da ita."
   Kanta Basmah ta ɗaga tare da tashi ta raka sa waje tsayawa sukayi Basmah tace.
    "Ahmad baka sanar da Umma cewa ankori Jidda a aiki ba."
   "A'a bai kamata ta sani yanzu ba hankalinta zai tashi fiye da yanzu dan haka karki sanar da ita mu jira har mu kashe case ɗin tukunna ke dai ki kula da ita tunda kinga tana da hawan jini damuwa kuma shine first abinda yake tayar da hawan jini ki tabbatar bata shiga cikin damuwa ba, ni bara naje."
    "Shikenan zanyi abinda kace amma dan Allah gobe kayi belin Jidda da wuri."
    "Insha Allah! Ahmad yayi Maganar yana tafiya komawa ciki Basmah tayi cike da zullumi.

*_Page 17 to 18_*




Ciki Basmah ta koma gefen Umma ta zauna tayi shuru.
    "Basmah da yawan yara suna faɗawa cikin masifa saboda kujewa maganar iyaye da sukeyi a ko yaushe uwa takan nunawa ɗanta hanyar da zai samu nasara ne a rayuwarsa, babbar musibace kin bin maganar iyaye na tabbata da jidda taji maganata to tabbas da bata shiga wannan halin da take ciki yanzu ba hankalina yana tashe."
    "Kiyi haƙuri tabbas Jidda tayi kuskure amma na tabbata ita bata ɗauki hakan akan zai zamo mata matsala ba, ki yafe mata Umma ki yi addu'a Allah ya fitar da ita daga sharrin Hisham."
    "Ameeen Basmah amma duk wanda baiji bari ba zaiji hoho, ina mata addu'a Allah ya tsareta."
   "Ameeen Umma."
 Basmah tayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki.




***********


A parking space sukayi parking kafin suka fito tare dukkan su har Sadiq cikin falon suka shiga Abba da Ummi suna zaune yayin da Tutu ke kitchen tana abincin rana, sallama sukayi Ummi ta ɗago kanta ganin Hisham da Nameer yasa Ummi miƙewa da sauri.
   "Alhamdulillah! Nameer welcome."
   Tayi Maganar cikin farin ciki, shima Nameer cikin dariya ya tafi da gudu ya rungume Ummi.
    "Welcome Ummi nayi missing ɗinki."
   "Nameer kaine alhamdulillah! Allah na gode maka."
   Tayi Maganar tana sakinsa, zama dukkan su sukayi NAMEER ya dubi Abba yace.
      "Masha Allah! ABBA dama na faɗa muku mai gaskiya baya taɓa tozarta ba gashi na dawo ba."
   "Hakane Nameer, Hisham da wani hujja ka cire Nameer daga wannan haɗarin."?
    Murmushi Hisham  ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Tunda ya fito ai zance ya ƙare."
  Hisham yayi Maganar a takaice yana tashi yabar wajen gudun kar Abba ya kuma jefa masa wata tambayar.
   Kasancewar Abba ba mutum bane mai yawan ƙwaƙƙwafi yasa shi basar da maganar cikin farin ciki suke hira har Sadiq.
    Tutu da ke cikin kitchen ne take jiyo tamkar Muryar Nameer, shuru tayi ta kasa kunne tabbas dai Muryar Nameer ɗin take jiyowa, da sauri ta fito tsayawa tayi turus tana kallonsa wani irin farin ciki ne ya lulluɓe mata zuciya, da sauri ta ƙariso kusa dashi.
    "Yaya Nameer hmmn alhamdulillah! Yaya Nameer ka fito Wayyo daɗi."
   Dariya Nameer yayi tare da ɗaga mata kai.
    "Na dawo Tutu kin rame da yawa ko dai fargaba na ne ya ramar da ke haka.?"
    "Yaya Nameer ai dole na rame gidan ne duk babu daɗi babu kai."
   "Gashi ai na dawo bara na haura sama nayi wanka 2days ban samu na watsa ruwa ba."
    "Shikenan sai ka fito."
Tayi Maganar tana komawa kitchen Sadiq duban Nameer yayi yace.
    "Nima gida zan koma zuwa yamma in kun fito zamu haɗu."
   "Okay shikenan na gode Sadiq."
   "Ba komai."
 yayi Maganar yana yiwa Ummi sallama.



 A kwance ya tadda Hisham ya na famar zuƙar sigari hancinsa Nameer ya toshe tare da ƙarisawa da sauri ya wafce sigarin.
   "Haba Hisham haba dan Allah, ka min alƙawarin ka daina shan sigari amma kuma ka karya min alƙawarin Hisham why.?"
  Yayi Maganar yana take sigarin a ƙasa idanunsa da suka kaɗa sukayi jawur Hisham ya ɗago ya kalli Nameer maganganun Jidda suna ƙara faɗo masa yaji zafi da ƙunar maganar.
    _Amma dai anyi faɗuwar baƙar tasa ko nace faɗuwar guzuma ƴa kwance uwa kwance, ka bada maza ina kurin ina barazanar shin ka fasa tozarta ni ne, Hmm andai ji kunya._
    _kaji daɗi ko, kaji daɗi ka zuba ruwa a ƙasa ka sunkuya ka shanye amma na rantse sai kayi danasanin taɓa min aikina_
   Da ƙarfi ya furta.
"Har yanzu bata rusuna ba, har yanzu kuri da baki take min, ni ne take cewa zanyi danasanin taɓa mata aikinta, lallai sai na ƙasƙantar da rayuwarta ƙasƙanci mafi muni, sai na sanya mata tsorona wanda sai ta gwammaci ganin mahaukacin zaki da ganina."
    Tsuka Nameer yaja yana zama bakin bed ɗin.
    "Mtsss aikin banza yanzu duk wannan haukar da jan idon da kake akan Jidda ne, Hisham wai Meyasa kake biyewa ruɗin zuciyar ka ne, yanzu duk wannan tozarcin da ka mata bai isheka ba har akwai wani abinda kake shirin mata wanda yafi wannan muni da aikinta fa ka rabata, Hisham ka rabu da jidda ka dakatar da wannan faɗan haka ya isa."
    "Bai isa ba Nameer! Yanzu ma aka fara! Ka sani idan na yanke hukunci *KO KAINA BANA SAURARA* bazan ƙyaleta ba sai naga ƙarshen rashin kunyarta."
  "Shikenan Hisham karka fasa, ni kuma zan kareta domin kuwa ta shiga wannan Case ɗin ne akan gaskiyarta kuma ni ta burgeni domin kuwa ba ta goyon bayan zalunci, idan yaso idan ka ɗebo masifar ka haɗa harda ni."
   Bakinsa Hisham ya cije tare da cewa.
    "Hmmm! Bata goyon bayan zalunci kuma ta amshi rashawa, Hmm! Na fahimci tun farko baka ga laifinta ba ai ba sai ka sanar min ba, ina jin haushi da takaicin zuciyar ka da bata nuna maka masoyanka, kafi ɗaukar maƙiya a masoya, tirr."
   "Naji amma ka sani Hisham ba'a taɓa cin nasara akan maƙiyi ta hanyar zafin rai da hasala ka sani idan ka jawo maƙiyinka jikinka babu shakka gobe masoyinka zai zamo, HISHAM dan Allah na roƙeka a karo na biyu ka daina shan sigari dan Allah cuta ce gareka ka kiyaye lafiyarka Hisham bana ƙaunar ciwo ya kwantar da kai, domin kuwa sai na fika cutuwa."
    "Naji."
Yayi Maganar a takaice, kansa Nameer ya kaɗa tare da ijiye wayoyinsa ya shiga wanka ya juma sosai a tollet ɗin kafin ya fito ya samu Hisham tuni har yayi bacci Kasancewar zafin da ake na damina yasa Nameer ko mai bai shafawa jikinsa ba turaruka kawai ya fashe jikinsa dasu kafin ya sanya boxer da vest ya kwanta a bayan Hisham, bai juma da kwanciya ba shima bacci ya kwashesa kasancewar gajiya da rashin samun bacci na kwana biyu da baiyi ba, Ummi jinsu shuru ya sata haurowa bedroom ɗin nasu da kanta gaba daya suna bacci murmushi Ummi ta saki cike ƙaunarsu zama tayi a gefen su tana zuba musu idanu.
    "A ko da yaushe na ɗaga idanuna na kalleku sai naji wani farin ciki marar misaltuwa, damuwata sai ta kau, kaine jigona Nameer rashin ka gareni zaizamo macewar farin ciki na, a duk sanda ka shiga damuwa nima ji nake tamkar zuciyata ta fashe kaine haskena Nameer farin cikin gidana, tun kana ƙanƙaninka kake kore duk wata damuwata baka taɓa bari wani ya ɗaga min yatsa sai ka karya wannan yatsar, ina jin farin ciki a duk sanda kake nunawa ɗan uwanka hanyar dai-dai, kaine control ɗin Hisham domin kuwa baka taɓa bari ya aikata ba dai-dai ba, ta yaya zan kasa sonka a cikin raina Nameer bayan kafi kowa ƙaunar ta ni da ƴaƴana, muddun ina numfashi bazan taɓa barin rayuwarka ta shiga cikin matsala ba."
   Ummi tayi Maganar tana sakin murmushi.
    Idanunta ne ya sauƙa akan karan sigarin da Nameer ya ƙwace ya kashe, numfashi ta saki tare da miƙewa ta ƙara musu ƙarfin Ac jin garin da zafi, kafin ta fice domin tasan Hisham ke shan sigari tayi faɗan tayi nasihar amma ya gagara, abun ya zame masa kamar masifa.





************

Da misalin ƙarfe 4 na yamma Tunde ya fito yana jiran su Mamabisola a parking space.

Da shirin su na tafiya ƙauye Mamabisola da Wamimo suka fito duban Tunde Mamabisola tace.
   "Kayi sauri mana ka jamu mu tafi kar dare ya mana a hanya."
    "Ku shiga muje ai na fito nima."
  Yayi Maganar yana shiga motar, suma shiga sukayi har tunde zai ja motar kiran wayar Hisham ya shigo wayarsa.
    "Hisham kuma."?
Ya furta yana kallon Mamabisola.
    "Ka ɗaga kiran muji meke faruwa."
   Kiran ya ɗaga tare da kara wayar a kunnen sa yana danna handsfree.
    "Ina jinka Hisham."
Daga can Hisham daya ke zaune bakin bed Nameer yana kwance har yanzu bai tashi daga baccin ba yace.
    "Ka ƙara buɗe kunnenka da kyau domin kaji abinda zan faɗa maka da kyau."
   "Wai me ya faru ne Hisham."?
    "Kafi kowa sanin abinda ya faru, jifan da kuka yiwa Nameer ya faɗi ƙasa domin kuwa bai samesa ba, wai shin ance maka taɓa ɗan zaki wasa ne, ina neman mutuwa ne, kuma ka nema ka fara tseratar da rayuwarka domin kuwa nayi alƙawarin sai na baka kashinka a hannunka."
   Idanunsa Tunde ya zaro ya kalli Mamabisola kafin yace.
    "Me kake nufi Hisham ban fahimta ba."?
  Murmushi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Zaka fahimta domin kuwa tsuntsuwar da kuka aikowa Nameer tana hannun Hisham sannan inda ka faɗo kayi waya da ita a gabana kuma na naɗi komai, kazo Amar garden ka sameni by 5:00pm idan ba haka ba zan tona maka asiri duniya tasan waye ku kai da wannan kakar taka lallai kazo ka sameni."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana katse kiran bai jira amsar Tunde ba, a gigice Tunde ya kira layin Hisham, switch 📴 ya kashe wayar gaba ɗaya.
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Mama kina jin abinda yake faɗi asirin mu ya tonu ya gane, Hajara ta tona mana asiri, Hmm! Meye abun yi Mama.?"
    Numfashi Mamabisola ta saki cikin tashin hankali ta furta.
   "Lallai idan har IYABO yaji wannan maganar to tabbas naji kunya domin kuwa bai san zagon kasan da nake masa ba, wannan yaron ya zamo min ciwon ido a rayuwata ya zamo wajibi na ɗauki mataki a kansa, abinda za'ayi mu fasa zuwa ƙauyen sai gobe kaje da gaggawa kaji mai zaice ni kuma zansa a min maganinsa."
   "Okay shikenan Mama ku fita daga motar bara naje wajen Oga Wale muyi shawara dashi domin kuwa bazan yarda naje wajen ni ɗaya ba."
   "Shikenan kaje Allah ya baka Nasara."
   Tayi Maganar suna fita daga motar da ƙarfi yaja motar yabar gidan wani irin tashi hankalin Mamabisola yayi, sai kaiwa da komowa take Wamimo ne tace.
     "Mamabisola Akwai matsala wannan yaron shiya kamata a kawar idan ba haka ba shi zai ga bayan mu."
    "Naga alamar haka Wamimo domin kuwa tuya muke muna mantawa da albasa, lallai dole ne Hisham ya mutu."
   "Ƙwarai kuwa mutuwa ce ta dace dashi ki barmin komai a hannuna ni zan nemi wanda zai kashe mana shi cikin sauƙi ba tare da an zargi kowa ba."
    "Hakan yamin duk yadda zanyi domin naga ƙarshen Hisham na rantse da sarkin da ya busa min numfashi bazan taɓa yadda na faɗi warwas ba sai dai muyi mutuwar kasko.
