Mamabisola tayi Maganar cikin hasala da tashin hankali domin kuwa dukiyar Iyabo ba ƙaramin ƙona mata rai yake ba.
    "Haka nake so karki damu burunki sai ya cika, kiyi haƙuri mu koma ciki."
  Kanta Mamabisola ta ɗaga tana huci suka koma cikin falon nata.



 Gudu kawai Tunde yake shararawa a tsakiyar titi tamkar mashayin da yasha giya ya bugu, masu abin hauwa sai kauce masa sukeyi yayin da masu Allah wadarai akansa suna yi, ikon Allah ne kawai ya kawo Tunde shagon Wale parking yayi ya fito a fusace shi kansa Wale ganin Tunde haka sai da ya razana sa.
  Zama Tunde yayi yana huci dubansa Wale yayi yace.
    "Tunde lafiya kuwa meke faruwa.?"
   "Hmmm! Ina kuwa lafiya wale ina cikin tashin hankali da masifa.?"
   "Tashin hankali da masifa kuma Tunde, faɗa min me akayi.?"
   "Wale Hisham ya gano duk shirin mu ya gane mune muke musu zagon ƙasa, meye abun yi yanzu haka yace mu haɗu a Amar garden lallai ko ya tona mana asiri wayar da nayi ranar da Hajara a gabansa nayi."
    "Kaga idan har Hisham ya gano to tabbas bazai ƙyale ka ba, sai ya zamo barazana ga rayuwar ka, Hmm! Tunde kaje ku haɗun duk abinda zai faɗa maka marar daɗi to ka danne zuciyar ka, sannan Hajara taci amanar mu ya zamo wajibi ta mutu domin kuwa barinta babbar hujja ce ga Hisham zamu iya sa ko Nameer ya sanya Hisham ya goge wannan record ɗin saboda wawa Nameer muna nuna masa nadamar mu shikenan mun gama dashi, yaushe yace ku haɗu."?
  Numfashi Tunde ya saki yana dafe kansa yace.
    "By 5:00pm."
"Okay to kaje babu abinda zai maka na cutarwa a yanzu ni na sani."
    "Okay shikenan amma hankalina ya tashi tabbas Hajara ta tafka babban kuskure kuma zata girbi abinda ta shuka, na tsani na buɗe idanuna na kalli Iyalan Iyabo suna cikin walwala, ka duba yanda suka sanu sukayi suna a duniya, ni kuma mahaifina har ya mutu babu wanda ya sanshi, haka IYABO ya barsa ya mutu a wulaƙance Wale bazan taɓa barinsu suyi nasara ba a rayuwar su ko ta halin yaya sai sun ƙasƙanta."
  "Hmmm! A hankali ake dukan ƙarfe har yayi laushi Tunde dan haka a juri zuwa rafi."
    "Bani ruwan sanyi nasha zuciyata hura take."
   Tunde yayi Maganar cikin matsanancin damuwa, tashi Wale yayi yaje ya kawo masa ruwan.






************


Tun biyar saura Nameer ya shirya cikin ƙanana kaya, ya fito Hisham dake zaune a falo shi da su Tutu da Ummi ne ya cewa Nameer.
    "Ina kuma zakaje haka, ka fito da key ɗin mota."?
   "Unguwa zanje."
 Tutu ne tayi carap tace.
      "Yauwa Yaya Nameer sauke ni a gidan su Samira."
    "Okay ɗauko mayafinki ina mota, Ummi bara naje yanzu zan dawo."
   "Allah ya dawo da kai lafiya amma dan Allah ka dawo da wuri tunda ka dawo dai kasan bamu zauna munyi hira ba ko, ina bukatar lokacinku dukkan ku."
    "Ba matsala Ummi insha Allah a gida zanyi sallar magaruba."
    "To Allah ya tsare."
Da ameeen Nameer ya amsa yana fita, bai juma ba tutu ma ta biyosa a baya, da kansa Nameer ya tuka motar suka fita.
    Sunyi nisa da tafiya Nameer yace.
     "Farin ciki na, kinga yadda kika rame kuwa, ashe dai kina yina da yawa."
  Murmushi Tutu ta saki tace.
    "Yaya Nameer ai ba iya rama kawai nayi ba zuciyata ma so tayi ta buga gaskiya dai naga masifa, yauwa Yaya Nameer wai ina maganar mu ta kwana ne, ya kamata zuwa yanzu ace Soyayyar mu ta bayyana amma har yanzu banga alamun hakan a tattare da kai ba, Soyayyar da nake maka yaya Nameer ta wuce misali."
    "Na sani tabbas kina sona fiye da komai a rayuwarki Tutu, sai dai fa ki sani na faɗa miki idan lokaci yayi dole Soyayyar mu zata bayyana har yanzu akwai sauran lokaci tukunna."
   "Wani irin sauran lokaci meka rasa wanda zai hanaka Aure kana da kuɗi to meyasa lokacin baiyi ba.?"
   Tayi Maganar dai-dai sunzo kofar gidan su Samira, parking yayi tare da cewa.
    "Lokacin ne dai baiyi ba, bana son doguwar magana, kije idan na dawo ma karisa Maganar."
  Kanta Tutu ta kaɗa tare da cewa.
    "Shikenan zan jiraka kazo ka ɗaukeni."
    "Okay."
Yayi Maganar Tutu na fita motar ya yiwa key tare da shan kwana yabar layin, darect kofar gidan su Jidda yayi parking, yaro ya hango ya kirasa tare da cewa ya shiga yace wa mamar Jidda tayi bako.
   Yaron ya ɗan juma da shiga kafin suka fito da Basmah har bakin motar Nameer ganin Nameer yasa Basmah razana tare da zaro idanunta a tunaninta yazo ne shima ya ciwa Umma mutunci cikin nauyin baki Basmah tace.
     "Kaine! Kazo ne kaima ka rama abinda Jidda ta maka, Umma tana da hawan jini dan Allah na rokeka, karka ɗaga mata hankali ka dubi darajar mahaifiya ka kalleta a matsayin uwa dan Allah."
   Murmushi Nameer yayi yace.
    "Ko ɗaya ni banzo na ɗagawa wani hankali ba, ina son ganinta zanyi magana da ita."
   "Shikenan muje."
Basmah tayi Maganar tana yin gaba Nameer yabi bayanta, a baranda Basmah ta shinfida masa tabarma kafin ta shiga ta yiwa Umma magana babu jimawa suka fito tare da Umma, a bakin kofa umma ta zauna cikin girmamawa Nameer yace.
   "Barka Umma ina yini."
"Lafiya alhamdulillah! Basmah ta ce min kaine wanda Jidda tasa aka kulle ko, kayi haƙuri ina baka hakuri Jidda yarinya ce, tayi hakanne bisa kuskure duk da bansan meya kawo ka wajena ba amma banga tashin hankali a tattare da kai ba."
   "Hakane Umma ni sam banga laifin Jidda akan kamani da akayi ba, nasan tayi komai ne akan gaskiyarta, nima zuwa nayi na baku haƙuri akan abinda ɗan uwana yasa aka mata ba kulleta bane matsalar aikinta da yasa ta rasa, saboda nasan insha Allah gobe ina zuwa Dpo zai saketa, kiyi haƙuri Umma insha Allah zan samowa Jidda aiki mai kyau wanda yafi wanda ta bari."
   Idanunta Umma ta zaro tace.
    "Bayan kamata da yasa akayi harda aikinta yasa ta rasa yanzu an kori Jidda daga wajen aiki innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Wannan ɗan uwan naka yana da zuciya kuwa a ƙirjinsa, kasan mutum nawa aikin Jidda yake taimakawa kuwa, itace ci da sha na gidan nan, ita komai na makarantar ɗan uwanta suturar da muke sawa a jikinmu duk da aikinta muka dogara meye ribarsa idan ya toshe mana hanyar ci da sha ɗinmu, ashsha wannan yaro bai kyauta ba, amma babu komai bakin da Allah ya tsaga bazai hanasa abinda zai ci ba Allah ya shiryesa."
   "Umma Hisham ba mutumin banza ba ne, zuciyar sa ne tayi yawa amma bayan haka ɗan uwana mutumin kirki ne, zuciyarsa na hasala akaina muddun za'a ɗaga min yatsa to wallahi Hisham bazai iya riƙe kansa ba, na ji daɗi da kika masa addu'ar shiriya, ki ƙara hakuri gobe ƴarki zata dawo kuma insha Allah kamar yadda na faɗa zan sama mata aiki mai kyau."
   "Bakomai Allah ya mana mai kyau kaima Allah ya maka albarka."
  "Ameeen Umma." Nameer yayi Maganar yana ciro kuɗi dubu hamsin a aljihun sa ya ijiye a gaban Umma Basmah dai tana zaune tayi shuru ga damuwa da tunanin abbanta.
    "Ga wannan Umma kwayi wata bukatar da ita."
   Kanta Umma ta girgiza tace.
   "A'a yaro ka barshi bama buƙata muna da komai."
   "Ba'a mai da hannun kyauta baya Umma ni ma ɗanki ne ki amsa kimin addu'a albarkar ku ita muke bukata."
   Wani irin kaunar Nameer Umma taji take taji ya burgeta domin kuwa da alamu yaron yana da dattako da mutunta na gaba dashi murmushi Umma tayi tace.
   "To na gode sosai Allah ya saka da alheri, Allah ya maka albarka."
   "Yauwa Umma haka nake so ameeen na gode ni zan koma."
   "To ka gaishe da mutanen gida."
  Tashi Nameer yayi ya fita cike da farin ciki da ƙaunar Umma matar sam bata da hayaniya ko kaɗan matar kirki tabbas wannan ƴaƴanta sunyi sa'ar samun uwa ta gari.
  Basmah har bakin motarsa ta rakasa.
    "Nameer mun gode sosai nayi zaton kamar yadda Hisham yake kaima haka kake sai gashi kunyi banbanci sosai kai mutumin kirki ne na gode sosai da kulawarka garemu dan Allah ka dakatar da faɗan dake tsakanin Jidda da Hisham."
   "Hmmm! Karki damu Basmah insha Allah komai zaizo ƙarshe, shima Hisham mutumin kirki ne zafin zuciya ne kawai ya masa yawa, wanda yake zaune dashi kullum shi zai faɗi waye Hisham."
  "Hakane mun gode."
Murmushi Nameer yayi dubu talatin ya ɗauka ya bawa Basmah, amsa tayi cikin farin ciki tana godiya tana kallon Nameer yaja motarsa yabar ƙofar gidan, murmushi ta saki maganar Jidda ne ya faɗo mata a rai
    _Insha Allah Basmah Allah zai mana zamu samu kud'in aikin da za'a yiwa Abba."_
   Numfashi ta saki ta koma ciki ta nunawa Umma kuɗin murmushi Umma tayi ta ƙara mata dubu ashirin tace ta haɗa aje a yiwa Abba aiki sosai Basmah taji daɗi, sun juma sosai a wajen suna zancen kirkin Nameer.

                5:14pm



Zaune Hisham yake a Amar garden shida Sadiq, Motar Tunde tayi parking a parking space, Cike da zullumi Tunde ya fito yasan babu inda Hisham yake zama sai wajen coffee nan ya nufa kamar yadda yayi zato kuwa haka ya samu yana wajen, da ke zuciyarsa yayi ya nufi teburin da suke, zama yayi a kujerar da babu komai hannu ya miƙawa Hisham.
     "Ƙazanta ne bawa hannu mai datti hannu mai tsafta, Tunde good job."
   Murmushi Sadiq ya saki yace.
      "Good Tunde gaskiya ka yi bazata da yawa amma abun mamakin Tunde kana zaton zaka wurgawa zaki Dutse ya samesa, mtsss! Kayi kuskure Babba."
    Murmushi Hisham ya saki yace.
   "Maybe ya manta ne, amma yanzu zan tuna masa da Zaki zai yi faɗa, kai ɓarawo ba iya satar ka iya ba kenan harda tuggu, da naso da na tona maka asiri Tunde ta hanyar kunnawa Abba wannan record ɗin, sai dai bazanyi haka domin kuwa bana bukatar ɗagawa Abba hankali a yanzu, amma fa ka sani dole ne wannan video record ɗin yaje ga Abba, Saboda zan sanar dashi ne ta hanyar da hankalinsa bazai tashi ba, saboda yasan waye ku ƙazantar da kuke rufe masa zai bayyana kasan meyasa na kiraka saboda na baka saƙo ka kaiwa wannan tsohuwar kakar taka, kace mata Lulluɓin biri zai yaye ta fara neman hanyar tsira domin kuwa asirinta ya kusa tonuwa."
    Wani irin suya da raɗaɗi zuciyar Tunde ke yi, bakinsa ya buɗe yace.
     "Hish............
Mari Hisham ya ɗauke sa dashi bai dasa aya ba yace.
   "Karka kuskura ka ambaci sunana da wannan ƙazamin bakin naka, ka sani bana bukatar jin Muryar ka a nan, kuma ka tuna da wannan ko wani Aji akwai malaminsa sai dai amma Malaman suna suka tara, wani malamin hoti ho ne baya sanin ana kallon sa, kaje ka faɗawa Tsohuwar kakar ka, malamin zamani yazo ta shirya sauyawa wannan tsohon malamin Aji, ka ce mata komai gudunta bazata kamo gobe ba, dole sai ta jira yazo da kansa, mu gobe ne munfi ƙarfin yau, kowa da burinsa, haka zalika muma da burin mu, ku naku burin shine ganin bayan mu, yayin da mu namu burin shine mu tunbatsa a duniya labarin mu ya zaga ko ina, baku isa  kuma ku toshe wannan hanyar ba dole ku kallemu ku barmu ko baƙin ciki ya zamo ajalinku, ka faɗa mata cewa shuka take a idon makwarwa tar Hisham yake kallonta da idanunsa, tashi ka fece ko na karya maka muƙamiƙinka ɓarawon banza."

*_Page 21 to 22_*




Tunda Tunde ya dawo ya sanar da Mamabisola abinda ke faruwa hankalinta ya kuma tashi damuwarta ta ƙaru sai kaiwa da komowa take ta kasa samun nutsuwa, hankalinta duk a kaɗe ta wacce hanyar zata toshe wannan ɓarakar meyasa Hisham yake son ya shiga rayuwarta ne, numfashi ta saki tare da samun waje ta zauna.
    "Tunde lallai Hisham zai zamo karan da zai tsokale mana ido muddun mukayi wasa."
   "To amma Mama ya kike ganin zamuyi kar fa asirin mu ya tonu shi nake tsoro."
   Girgiza kanta Mamabisola tayi tace.
   "Ka daina jin tsoron yau muji tsoron gobe kamar yadda ya faɗa saboda bamu san da me zata zo mana ba, tunda har Hisham ya fita daga Police station bai tona asiri ba to a wannan wasan ruwa tasha, bazai fasa wannan ƙwan ba, ka bari zansan yadda zamuyi dashi kafin ƙwan yayi wari zamu aikasa shida hujjar gaba daya, yaro ne shi bai san wuta ba sai ya taka."
   "Amma dai Mama karki ja lokaci muna bukatar ɗaukar mataki akan Hisham da gaggawa, saboda matsala ne shi a garemu yasan abinda muke shirya musu."
   "Karka damu tsalle ɗaya ake a faɗa rijiya sai ayi dubu ba'a fito ba dani yake zancen."
  Tashi Tunde yayi yace.
    "Okay shikenan."
Yayi Maganar yana barin falon, da kallo Mamabisola ta bisa cike da ƙaunarsa tanayin komai ne saboda shi.



***********


    Washegari da misalin 12 na rana motar Nameer tayi parking a police station ɗin, har ciki ya shiga cikin Sa'a kuwa ya samu Dpo yana nan waje hannu ya basa sukayi musabaha suna dariya.
    "Ɗan sanda abokin kowa."
  Cewar Nameer murmushi shima Dpo yayi yace.
   "Nameer Abdulzahir jiya akayi belinka yau har kazo belin wata."
  Dariya Nameer yayi yace.
   "Harma ka fuskanci abinda yake tafe dani kenan, to Allah yasa dai za'a baka belin nata amma Dpo ni fa a nawa tunanin Hisham shiya yaudari yarinyar nan ta iya yiwuwa gadar zare ya ƙulla mata,  kasan halin Hisham zai iya aikata komai domin ganin ya tozarta yarinyar nan da zaka min alfarma tunda babu wani na sama da kai da yasan wannan Case kawai a rufe Case ɗin ya zamo baya da file a cikin Police station ɗin nan saboda hakan tamkar ɓata mata suna ne."
   Girgiza kansa Dpo yayi.
   "Impossible a rufe file ɗinta, because an riga da an buɗe zan baka belinta amma dole zamu cigaba da bincike a kanta wannan shine abinda zan iya yi maka."
    Murmushi Nameer ya saki wai Police ne zai cigaba da bincike akan rashawa.
   "Dpo in dai akan rashawa ne bai kamata aji bakin ku ba saboda kune kukafi kowa cin hanci da rashawa a ƙasar nan, sorry amma ka sani dole ne za'a rufe file ɗinta, ni ba Hisham bane komai da lissafi nake yinsa, kasan me da ace Hisham kuka rufe na rantse da Allah bazai kwana ba domin kuwa ni nafi Hisham basira da lissafi nake komai iya basirar Hisham a neman fitar dani wajen commissioner of police kawai ya tsaya amma ka sani akwai baban commissioner Minister governor da sauransu, shi kansa Abba bai so fitar dani bane saboda gudun abinda al'umma gari zasu faɗa akansa sannan yasan ina da gaskiya dole zan kuɓuta bawai dokar banza ta gwamnati bane tasa ban fito ba, amma ka sani a wannan karon file ɗin zai rufu ba kuma za'a ƙara buɗesa ba har abada."
   Murmushi shima Dpo yayi yace.
    "Okay shikenan a rufe file ɗin mu gani na baka nan da 24hr."
   Bakinsa Nameer ya taɓe yana girgiza kansa tare da kallon agogon dake saƙale a hannunsa.
    "No 10minute 24hrs is over, hmmm bara muga."
  Nameer yayi Maganar yana ɗaga waya ministan Jiha ya kira ringing ɗaya ya ɗaga bayan sun gaisa Nameer ke sanar dashi abinda ke faruwa da kuma bukatar sa, kafin suka katse kiran, bai 5minute ba da katse kiran commissioner ya kira Dpo tare da umarnin a rufe Case ɗin Jidda a sallameta, Dpo yaso magana amma commissioner ya hanasa magana numfashi Dpo ya saki yana kallon Nameer shi kuwa Nameer murmushi ya saki tare da miƙewa tsaye yana cewa.
    "Na faɗa maka ni ba Hisham bane da zan dage wajen neman alfarmar ka, dama ɗaya kawai nake bawa Mutum a rayuwarsa da zarar yayi wasa da wannan damar zanbi ta kansa na shige gargadi ne wannan Dpo, nafi Hisham tsauri zafin zuciya kawai ya fini da gaggawa, kuje ku sako yarinyar mutane."
   Cikin ɓacin rai Dpo yaje ya buɗe Jidda dake zaune tana sauraron su a cikin cell, domin kuwa a bakin cell suke tsaye, fitowa Jidda tayi a kwana ɗaya har ta faɗa idanunta kuma sun zurma bata jira Nameer ba ta fito waje tana fitowa Ahmad yana shigowa da babur ɗinsa numfashi Jidda ta sauƙe tare da nufar Ahmad da sauri, tsayawa tayi gaban babur ɗin daya kashe.
   "Ahmad ina Ummana tayi fushi dani ko, Allah yasa bataji labarin na rasa aikina ba, bazata iya jurewa hakan ba, Allah yasa baku faɗa mata ba, Hisham ya cuceni Ahmad ya min mungun illa bazan taɓa kyalesa ba wallahi sai ya girbi abinda ya shuka."
   Gyara tsayuwar sa Ahmad yayi yana kallon Jidda kafin yace.
    "Waye yayi belinki.?"
"Ni ne nan."
   sukaji muryar NAMEER daga bayansu juyowa Ahmad yayi duk wanda yake cikin kwara state kuma yake ganin gidan tv to tabbas yasan Nameer ko da kuwa bai taɓa kallonsa a zahiri ba.
    "Nameer Abdulzahir Iyabo, Barka amma nayi mamaki ace ɗan uwanka shine yasa ta cikin wannan halin amma kai kuma kazo ka fitar da ita duk da itace tayi sanadiyar shigarka cell, hmmm! Sorry da tambayar da zan maka Nameer meyasa kayi belinta."?
    "Bani da amsar tambayar ka saboda nima bansan dalilin ba, Jidda muje na mayar da ke gida saboda na yiwa Mahaifiyar ki alƙawarin cewa zan dawo mata da dake a wannan lokacin."
  Juyowa Jidda tayi cike da mamaki tace.
   "Ummata ka yiwa alƙawarin zaka dawo mata dani gida, kunje gidan mu ne kun gwada mata rashin mutuncin ku, meya kaika gidan mu, Meyasa zakuje ku ɗagawa mahaifiyata hankali meye laifina saboda na nemawa talaka ƴar uwata ƴanci, meye laifin hakan, anfi bukatar a tozarta talaka ne saboda shi talaka bashi da ƴanci meyasa yawancin masu kuɗin mu azzalumai ne marassa iman...........
   Bata ƙarisa ba Nameer ya katseta cikin tsawa mai firgitarwa.
   "Ya isheki haka! Nima ɗin talaka ne kamarki, wataƙila ma ke kin fini wadata tunda har kina da aikin yi a wannan lokacin, Masu kuɗin mu ba duka suka haɗu suka dawo azzalumai ba, akwai na gari a cikin su, domin kuwa ni Alhaji Iyabo yamin sutura a rayuwata, iyayena ƙasƙantattun talakawa ne marassa gata da ƴanci a rayuwa wadanda abinda zasu saka a bakin su ma yake gagararsu, wannan mai kuɗin shine yamin riga da Wanda domin ya suturta rayuwata, karki sokesa Jidda gara ni ki sokeni sunmin hallacin da babu wanda ya taɓa minshi a rayuwata ciki harda jinina, domin kuwa ya ɗaukoni ya sani a jikinsa ya mai dani ɗan da ya haifa a cikin sa dani da Hisham bamu da banbanci a wajensa."
   Gabaki ɗayansu shuru sukayi musamman Jidda da tayi suman tsaye tsabar mamaki da ƙin yadda da abinda kunnuwanta ke jiyo mata, cike da mamakin tace.
   "Kai ba ɗan Abdulzahir Iyabo bane, me kake son faɗa min Nameer.?"
   Kansa Nameer ya ɗaga mata idanunsa na ƙoƙarin ciko da hawaye ya dubi Ahmad yace.
    "Ƙwarai ka ijiye Babur ɗinka muje na baku mamaki Ahmad."
   Mashin ɗin Ahmad ya yiwa key tare da duban Jidda yace.
   ''yana da kyau muje mu saurari labarinsa Jidda domin kuwa na kaɗu sosai da jin Nameer ba ɗan Alhaji Iyabo bane."
   Kanta Jidda ta kaɗa tare da bin bayan Nameer motarsa suka hau ya Nameer ya jasu har unguwar Ile-Ire unguwa ce ta talakawa sosai infact ma kamar ƙauye haka unguwar take yawancin gidajen unguwar irin gidajen lakan nan ne na da can wasu gidajen ma babu gini sai rufin langa-langa, wani dogon layi sukabi mai yawan kwatoci, a ƙofar wani tsohon gida na laka rabinsa duk ya rushe fitowa sukayi Nameer ya musu jagora har cikin gidan, wani ƙaramin dakali da yasha ƙura Nameer ya kaɗe da kansa suka zauna gabaki ɗayansu kallon gidan suke ita Jidda tunda take a rayuwarta bata taɓa ganin tsohon gida lalatacce irin wannan ba.
  Nameer numfashi ya saki tare da dubansu mamaki ya hango ƙarara a idanunsu.
     "Kuna mamakin wannan gidan ko, shine gidan mahaifina a nan aka haifeni anan na fara rayuwata bari na baka labarina yadda zaku fahimci cewa Alhaji Iyabo wato Abbana yamin Rana a rayuwata.





    *_SHEKARU 27 DA SUKA GABATA_*




  Ruwa ake sosai tamkar da bakin ƙwarya garin duk ya narke yayin da Ruwan ke tafiya da iska, zaune nake takure a gefe yayin da ɗakin mu ke yoyo kwanon ɗakin duk ya huje gefen gabar ɗin ɗakin mu yafi ko ina yoyo kwano Mamana tasa take tare ruwan dubanta ta mayar gareni da nake takure tace.
   "Babana yunwa kakeji ko, kayi haƙuri bari Babanka ya shigo muga me zai dawo dashi bani da abinda zan baka Babana kayi haƙuri kaji."
   Kaina na kaɗawa Mamanmu nace.
    "Mama yunwa nakeji sosai gashi Baba yaƙi dawowa, ni dai Mama ko garin rogo ne ki bani zansha ko ba Suger."
   Zama Mamanmu tayi gefena ta kamo hanuna tace min.
   "Babana bani da komai na faɗa maka nima raina baya jin daɗin halin da kake ciki na yunwa, kayi haƙuri kaji insha Allah yanzu baban naka zai dawo."
   "To Mama Allah ya dawo dashi lafiya."
  Nayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki, yunwa kuma tamkar zata kasheni, hanjin cikina kuwa ji nake tamkar sun naɗe, ga feshin ruwa dake shigo mana bayan yoyon da ɗakin keyi kana kallon Mamana a wannan lokacin zaka fahimci tana cikin damuwa.
  Muna nan zaune Baban na ya dawo duk ya jiƙe jikinsa sai karkarwa yake yana layi, idanunsa sunyi ja, babu komai a hannunsa hakanne ya tabbatar min Baban mu bai dawo da komai ba, zuciyata ne ya tsinke domin nasan daƙyar idan ba da yunwa zan kwana ba, duk da nasan iyayena suna bakin ƙoƙarin su wajen ganin ban kwana da yunwar ba, ko da su zasu kwana da ita, zama yayi gefe har yanzu jikinsa rawa yake ko magana baiyi ba, Mama ne tace.
    "Baban Nameer lafiya kuwa, ya akayi ka bari ruwan nan ya dakeka haka, ka tashi ka sauya kayanka ko sanyin zai ragu maka.?"
  Kansa Baba ya girgiza cike da damuwa yace.
   "Ba sanyin bane damuwata Usaina abinda zaku ci shine damuwata kun wuni baku ci ba, bazai yiwu ku kwana baku ciba, ban samu komai ba nayi iya yina amma babu abinda na samu yau babu kasuwar Raken da ƙyar na sai da sanda ɗaya naira 70 kinga itace kawai a hanuna."
   Numfashi Mamana ta saki ta dubi Baban mu tace.
   "Kaga Baban Nameer kayi haƙuri INSHA ALLAH komai zai wuce rayuwar taƙaitacciya ce, kuma kaga ai ita kasuwa dama Sa'a ce idan ba'a samu ba yau gobe za'a samu, idan ruwan ya tsagaita, sai ka fita ka sayowa Nameer garin Hamsin Suger na ashirin, ya samu yasha yunwar ta damesa mu zamu iya haƙuri shi kuma yarone bai san babu ba."
  Kansa Baban mu ya kaɗa yace.
    "To Allah ya iyar mana."
    Ni dai ina zaune ina kallon su, shi kansa Baba kana kallon sa zaka fahimci shima yunwar yakeji amma haka ya danne tasa, kayan jikin nasa ya sauya yazo ya zauna kusa dani yana lallashi na, ko kafin a gama ruwan tuni har bacci ya ɗaukeni, sai ji kawai nayi Mama na tashi na nasha garin ashe har an gama ruwan Baba yaje ya sayo min, haka na tashi nasha garin da Mama ta jiƙa min ita kuwa da Baba haka suka kwana da yunwa.



 Washegari da safe ma da zan tafi makaranta Mama da kanta ta fita taje ta amso min bashin Taliya a wajen iya mai Taliya da Ɗanwake dake bayan layin mu ta samin a plast na tafi dashi makaranta.
   Ko da na dawo daga makaranta na samu Mama ta mana buragandu shi naci nasha ruwa, na fita wasa na, da la'asar lis Hajja Anti ta shigo gidan mu makwabciyar mu ce suna shiri sosai da Mama duk da ƙarancin shekaru na a wannan lokacin ina iya fahimtar wasu abubuwan, tazo ne tana sanar da Mama cewa akwai wanda take musu aiki ta mata magana ta cikiya musu ƴar aiki wanda zata dinga musu wanke wanke da shara kawai, shine taga halin da muke ciki tace ko Mama zataje ta dinga yi sosai hakan ya yiwa Mama daɗi sai dai bata amince mata ba tace sai ta sanar da Babana, akan wannan maganar sukayi sallama da Hajja Anti akan tana sauraran Mama.

  Ko da Baba ya dawo Mama ta sanar dashi ransa bai so ba, sai dai yadda Mama ta kafe da kuma hujjojin ta na amfanin da aikin zai mana yasa Baba amincewa, da wayar Baba Mama ta kira Hajja Anti take sanar da ita Baba ya amince, tace washegari zata zo suje wajen da Uwar ɗakin nata.

*_Page 23 to 24_*



*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻




Kamar yadda Hajja Anti tace Washegari da safe tazo ta ɗauki Mama da yake ranar weekend ne bamu da makaranta hakan yasa Mama kama hannuna muka tafi tare, bana mantawa a lokacin ina da shekara goma sha ɗaya ne a duniya aji na biyar a primary school dake nan unguwar Ora, duk da ƙarancin shekaru na ganin gidan da muka shiga sai da ya burgeni, sosai yamin kyau tunda muka shiga na fara tsalle ina cirar flower ɗin da sukayi ado dashi a gidan, Mama ce ta kamo hannuna tana zaro min ido tace.
    "Kul ka ƙara cira musu flower Babana sojaji zasu kamaka su maka bulala ka gansu can suna kallon mu ka bari kaji Babana ba kyau yiwa mutane ɓarna."
  Dubana na kai ga sojojin dake zaune bakin get ɗin, dama kuwa ni gwanine wajen tsoron soja, cikin sauri na ƙanƙame Mama har muka shiga part ɗin nasu muka shige babban falon gidan ban kuma taɓa wani abun ba, a falon muka zauna har lokacin ina maƙale jikin Mama, bamu juma da zama ba Ummi ta sauƙo daga sama, kana kallon matar zaka fahimci matar mutunci ne domin kuwa fuskarta a sake take, a kujera ta zauna tare da duban Hajja Anti tace.
    "Hajja Anti yau kece a gidan nawa, wata sabon gani, sannu baiwar Allah."
   Ummi tayi Maganar tana kallon Mama, cikin girmamawa Mama ta gaishe ta kafin Hajja Anti tayi dariya tana cewa.
    "Aunty Ramata kwana biyu sai a hankali, amma wallahi ina da niyyar zuwa na gaishe ki, ranan munyi magana da Aunty Luba take cemin kina son ƙarin ƴar aiki, to shine na samo miki wannan baiwar Allah, nasan zata iya abinda kike buƙata, na kira Aunty Luba take cemin na kawota kawai darect wajen ki idan ta miki sai a ɗauketa."
  Murmushi Ummi ta saki tace.
    "Eh tabbas munyi magana da ita ina bukatar mai goge goge da sharan falon nan, idan har kun yaba da halinta da tsaftar ta, to ai ni bani da matsala sai ta fara aiki kawai."
   "Ehh gaskiya bata da matsala ko kaɗan tana da tsafta sosai."
   Duban Mama Ummi tayi tace.
   "Baiwar Allah na ɗauke ki aiki dan Allah ki riƙe min amana, menene sunan ki."?
   Kanta Mama ta ɗago tace.
   "Insha Allah zaki sameni mai riƙe amana sunana Fatima amma ana kirana da Maman Nameer."
  Murmushi Ummi tayi ta kalleni hannu ta miƙo min tace.
    "Ayya wannan shine Nameer ɗin ko, zo kaji yarona."
   Sakin Ummi nayi na tashi naje wajenta akan cinyar ta ta ijiye ni da gudu na hango wani ƙaramin yaro ya fito daga wani room ya faɗa a jikin Ummi ko da ganin yaron nasan zan girmesa da wajen shekara biyu Ummi ne ta dubesa tace.
   "Kai HISHAM ina ka shige tun ɗazu lami na jiranka zata sauƙe ka a gidan Mamabisola."?
  Ransa Hisham ya haɗa cikin ƙunƙuni yana tura baki yace.
    "Ummi ni bazani ba babu inda zanje ni ball zanyi."
  Yayi Maganar yana kama hannuna yace.
   "Kazo muje muyi Ball, Abbana ya kawo min sabuwar Ball daga Indonesiya zo muje mu buga."
  Murmushi Ummi tayi cikin mamaki tace.
   "Ikon Allah lallai Nameer da Sa'a ka shigo gidan nan yau har Hisham ne zaiga baƙo bai masa hali ba yace suzo suyi wasa, to kaga Nameer kuje kuyi wasan kaji banda faɗa, kai kuma karka kuskura ka masa wani abun nasan halinka kaga ɗan uwanka ne, sunan sa Nameer."
  Dariya Hisham yasa yana tsalle yace.
   "Ummi ina sonsa bazan dakesa ba Ball zamuyi, mu tafi Nameer."
  Yayi Maganar yana jan hannun Nameer suka fita, da kallo Ummi ta bisu tana murmushi kafin tace.
   "To Maman Nameer zaki fara aiki yau falon nan da can ɗakin baƙi zaki dinga mopping sai wanke wanke kina iya farawa zamu baki dubu goma a wata 10k."
   Cike da farin ciki Mama  tayi dariya tace.
   "Na gode sosai Hajiya kin taimakeni na gode."
   Murmushi Ummi tayi tare da tashi ta haura sama bata juma ba ta fito hannunta riƙe da kuɗi ta bawa Hajja Anti, anan Hajja Anti tabar mu ta tafi.
   Ƙarfe shabiyu na rana Mama ta gama aikin, a ɗan wannan wuni har sabo ya shiga tsakanina da Hisham tunda nazo bamu rabu ba, har sai da Mama tazo tafiya, sosai Hisham ya haɗa ransa yace sai ya bimu, da ƙyar da lallashi Ummi ta rarrashesa kafin muka koma gida.



  Ko da muka koma bayan Baba ya dawo Mama ta ke sanar dashi yadda sukayi a gidan aikin da albashin da za'a dinga bata sosai hakan ya yiwa Baba daɗi yasan komai yaya wata wahalar zata ragu.
 


   
 ************



Haka rayuwa ta dinga tafiya kullum sai Mama taje gidan aikinta yayin da shaƙuwa mai ƙarfi ta shiga tsakanina da Hisham, duk kuɗi da ikon su Hisham hakan baya hana wata rana ya biyo Mama ya kwana a gidan mu, haka duk weekend dani Mama take zuwa, sai ya zama Mama da Ummi suma suka shaƙu sosai, Ummi tana kyautatawa Mama dai-dai gwargwado, bana mantawa akwai ranar da mai gadin su Ummi na part ɗin su ya ijiye aikin, hakan yasa Mama neman alfarmar Ummi akan a ɗauki Baba ya maye gurbin mai gadin, babu musu Ummi ta sanar da Abba yace a kira Baba yazo suyi magana, ko da saƙon ya isawa Baba baiyi ƙasa a gwiwwa ba yazo a ranar da yazo shima Baba a ranar ya fara aiki, hakan tasa Abba bawa Baba wani ƙaramin part dake cikin gidan yace mu dawo da zama tunda aikin Baba na kwana ne, baiyi musu ba Baba duba ga Irin kyautatawar da suke mana.


Numfashi Nameer ya saki tare da juyowa ya kalli jidda yace.
 "wannan shine dalilin da yasa muka baro wannan gidan muka komo gidan ALHAJI IYABO da rayuwa, ba iya nan labarin ya tsaya ba Ahmad.

Ni sawa Ahmad yayi cike da mamaki yace.
    "Na matsu naji ƙarshen labarin ahalinka Nameer."
  Kanta Jidda ta kaɗa itama tace.
   "Tabbas rayuwar ka abar tausayi ce Nameer dole ka sakawa Alhaji IYABO da alkairi menene ya faru bayan dawowarku gidan Alhaji Iyabo.?"
   
    Numfashi Nameer ya saki ya cigaba da cewa.
    "Bayan mun dawo gabaki ɗaya Hisham sai ya tare a Wajen mu babu abinda yake mayar dashi part ɗin su sai har idan tare dani zamu shiga, Abba hakan sosai ya masa daɗi domin kuwa a yanda suka san Hisham bai taɓa aboki ba, asali ma duk yaron da yazo gidan korarsa yake amma sai gashi lokaci ɗaya Hisham yayi sabo dani, wannan dalilin yasa Abba yamin transfer daga Makarantar gomnati da nake ciki zuwa privet inda Hisham ke karatun aka ijiye mu waje guda, duk abinda zai taɓani Hisham baya so haka nima bana yadda wani ya ɗaga masu hannu, a haka rayuwar mu ta tafi har muka girma muka shiga jami'a komai namu tare yake tafiya duk wanda yasan Hisham to yasan ni, haka kuma ake min kallon ɗan Alhaji Iyabo domin kuwa ya maidani ɗansa komai tare yake mana haka Ummi duk abinda zata yiwa Hisham sai ta min wani lokacin takan hana Hisham ni ta bani amma hakan bazai sa Hisham yaji haushi na, ranar da bazan taɓa mantawa da ita ba a tarihin rayuwata itace ranar da muka wayi gari da mummunan tashin hankali ranar talata, ni da Hisham bamu kwana a part ɗin mu ba kasancewar Abba yace mu kuma wajen su, cikin dare wuta ta tashi a part ɗin su Mamana, wajen misalin ƙarfe 2 na dare, kwalala da ihun sojojin gidan shine ya tashi kowa dake cikin gidan, yayin da ake ta watsawa part ɗin ruwa sai dai ina tamkar ƙara hura wutar ake, har abin kashe gobara da Abba ya ijiye anyi amfani dashi amma a banza, hankalina sosai ya tashi bansan sanda hawaye ke zubowa daga idanuna ba, fire service aka kira ko da sukazo sukayi nasarar kashe wutar da sauri aka shiga part ɗin sai dai kash ƙaddara ta riga fata domin kuwa su Mama sun ƙone rai yayi halinsa nayi kuka tamkar zan bisu haka shima Hisham yaji mutuwar, ko wanka ba'a samu an musu ba kasancewar sun ƙone sai ruwa kawai aka yayyafa musu aka musu sallah, ranar babu wanda hankalin sa bai tashi ba da mutuwar su Baba, bayan anyi sadakar Uku, ne Abba da Ummi suka ijiye ni suka min nasiha sosai da nuna min mahimmancin haƙuri inda Abba yace min.
    (Baka rasa iyaye ba NAMEER kai mai iyaye biyu ne, muma iyayenka ne baka da banbanci da Hisham a wajen mu, bazamu bari kayi maraici ba duk gatan da uba zai nunawa ɗan da ya haifa zan nuna maka shi NAMEER ka cigaba da yiwa iyayenka addu'a Allah ya musu rahama.)
   Wannan Maganar ta Abba sosai tamin daɗi domin a lokacin da iyayena suka rasu ina da shekara ashirin ne a aduniya tun daga wannan ranar kuwa na cigaba da rayuwata a wajen Abba da Ummi da zuri'arsu domin kuwa dangin mahaifina babu wanda ya kuma nemana har sai da sukaga na kawo ƙarfi sannan suke nemana saboda ina da abinda zan basu, wannan shine tarihina.


 Kallon Jidda yayi ya cigaba da cewa.
     "Ki faɗa min Jidda ta yaya za'ace dukkan masu kuɗi azzalumai ne, Abba Mutum ne na gari kuma na kwarai."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki.
    "Ƙwarai kuwa na yadda da maganar ka Nameer, kayi haƙuri bansan cewa Hajara sharri ta maka ba sai jiya da daddare Dpo ya faɗa min."
    "Ba komai Jidda nasan yanzu haka Umma tana can hankalin ta a tashe muje na sauƙe ku."
    "Nameer ina tsoron komawa Umma a wannan lokacin da wani baki zan faɗa mata cewa na rasa aikina, dashi muka dogara nasan dole hankalinta zai tashi."
   Murmushi Nameer yayi tare da cewa.
    "Karki damu Jidda Umma mutum ce mai tawakkali a kallo ɗaya da na mata, na kuma gama hukunci tunda Hisham yasa kikayi asarar aikinki ni zan baki babban matsayi a COMPANYN MU *_HISHAM RICE MILL LIMITED_* zaki samu salary sama da wanda kike ɗauka a aikinki na baya."
   Zaro idanunta Jidda tayi a firgice tace.
    "Whattttttt! Hisham rice mill limited, hmmm! Sorry Nameer bazan taɓa iya haɗa inuwa ɗaya da Hisham ba domin kuwa nayi rantsuwa da Allah sai na masa rashin mutuncin da bai taɓa cin karo dashi ba a rayuwarsa alkawari na ɗauka."

"Ashsha haba Jidda banji daɗin wannan maganar taki ba, wannan faɗan naku ya kamata ya tsaya haka meye amfanin rigimar, fitina fa a kwance take meyasa zaku ta da ta, halinku yazo ɗaya da Hisham shiyasa bazaku taɓa daidaitawa ba, muje na sauƙeki."
    Ba tare da jidda tayi Magana ba ta shige gaba, bayanta sukabi motar Nameer ɗin suka shiga, a hanya Ahmad ya sauƙa shi kuma Nameer ya shige da Jidda har ƙofar gidan su yayi parking tare suka shiga cikin gidan da Nameer, Basmah ce kawai a tsakar gidan tana shara, ganin Jidda yasa Basmah tahowa da gudu ta rungumeta.
    "Alhamdulillah! Sannu Jidda."
   Murmushi Jidda ta saki tana kama hannun Basmah tace.
    "Ina Umman take.?"
 "Umma ta fita makwabta can gidan su Indo."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki ta dubi NAMEER.
    "Umman bata nan, na gode Nameer, ka fitar dani daga cikin cell bazan manta da hakan ba."
   "Bakomai idan ta dawo kwa gaishe ta."
  Basmah ce tace.
      "Zataji insha Allah Nameer mun gode."
Kansa Nameer yasa ya fice zama Jidda tayi bakin ƙofa Basmah ta ijiye tsintsiyar ta taho kusa da jidda ta zauna."
    "Har kunyar kallon idanunki nake, na miki laifi nine sanadiyyar faɗawa halin da kika shiga gashi kin rasa aikin ki, wallahi banyi da nufi ba, nayi ne saboda neman mafita na ciwon Abba da nasan tarko Hisham ya ɗana miki wallahi da bazan fara kuskuren saki ki amshi kuɗin ba."
   Murmushi jidda ta saki tace.
   "Karki damu Basmah ni nasan bazaki taɓa cutar da rayuwata ba, hmm! Abba mahaifi nima nake kallon sa zan iya komai domin ganin ya samu lafiya dan haka karki saka a ranki naji haushinki, ko ɗaya sai ma sonki da na ƙara ji a raina ƙawata ki kalli idona ki cigaba da faɗa min abinda ya dace dani a rayuwata, Basmah da Jidda abu ɗaya ne. Jidda bazata taɓa jin kunyar Basmah ba dan haka Basmah ma karta kuskura taji kunyar Jidda."
  Dariya Basmah tasa tare da rungume Jidda, tace.
   "Dama na sani Jidda bazata taɓa jin haushin Basmah ba, za'a yiwa Abba aiki."
  Cike da farin ciki Jidda.
     "Kai haba a ina aka samu kuɗin.?"
    "Nameer jiya da yazo wajen Umma ya bata 50k ni kuma 30k shine Umma ta cika kuɗin aikin tun jiya da daddare na kai kuɗin yanzu su muke jira su saka ranar da zasuyi aikin, Jidda gaskiya da alamu wannan Nameer ɗin yana da kirki yafi wannan Hisham ɗin."
   "Hmmm! Haka naga alamu amma har yanzu zuciyata bata amince dashi ba saboda mayaudara ne, dan haka karki kuskura kiyi saurin sakin jiki dashi, aikina kuma dana rasa karki damu haka Allah ya hukunta, shi kuma Hisham dani yake zancen zan bambance masa tsakanin aya da tsakuwa."
   "A'a Jidda dan Allah ki fita a harkarsa babu ruwanki dashi wannan fa ɗan tasha ne bazaiji kunyar wulaƙanta ki ba."
    "Mubar maganar Basmah domin kuwa bazan hanu ba, bari naje na watsa ruwa kafin Umma ta dawo."
    "Shikenan nima idan kin fito zanje asibiti na dubo Abba."
   "Shikenan bara na fito ɗin.''




************



Lumshe idanunsa Hisham yayi yana zugar sigarin sa hankali kwance, shi kuwa Sadiq shisha ma yake zuƙa, yasar da karan sigarin yayi ya takesa, tare da sanya hannu ya ɗauki coffee ɗin dake gabansa a hankali yake kurɓar coffee ɗin.
    "Hisham da zan baka shawara da kaci nasara a yaƙinka tsakaninka da wannan yarinyar nasan maganar da ta faɗa maka a police station har yanzu shine yake damunka a ranka, duk nasan babu abinda ta isa ta maka, amma yana da kyau ka koyawa rayuwarta darasi."
    Ijiye coffee ɗin Hisham yayi tare da mai da kallonsa ga Sadiq yace. 
    "Wacce irin shawara zaka bani ina jinka."
    "Hanya ce mai sauƙi Hisham, shi yaƙi ɗan zamba ne ba ɗan fito na fito ba, ba'a taɓa cin nasara ga maƙiyi ɓaro ɓaro, yaƙin sunƙuru yafi aika saƙo ga maƙiyi, yanzu fa zamani ya sanda a da ana yaƙi ne da takwabi amma yanzu zamani ya kawo bindiga idan har bindigar ma ta gagara, sai kawai a tashi bom."
    "Kasan bana fahimtar irin wadannan murɗaɗɗun kalmomin tafi kawai kai tsaye ka faɗa min abinda yake ranka."
    "Hisham ka yaudari yarinyar nan da zummar Soyayya ka tusa mata sonka a cikin ranta ta yadda zataji bazata iya rayuwa ba tare da kai ba, ina son Soyayyar ta kusa kaiku ga Aure ma'ana har rana asaka randa za'a ɗaura Auren kace ka fasa aurenta ka fito ka nunawa duniya cewa tana da cutar da ke karya garkuwar jiki, sannan ka saye dukkan likitocin garin nan ta yadda Maganar ka zata Tabbata, idan ka mata haka to ba dukan zuciyar ta kayi ba kasheta kayi, domin kuwa ba ita ba hatta ƴan uwanta sai baƙin ciki ya kusa kashe su."
   Waro idanunsa Hisham yayi tare da saki dariya har yana dukan teburin gabansa
    "Nice idea Sadiq tabbas wannan ce kaɗai hanyar da zan gama mata wulaƙancin duniya, bansan meye Soyayya ba amma wannan karon zan buga wasa da ita domin kuwa idan wasa akan maƙiyi ne ba ƙaramin daɗi yake min ba, ka zuba idanunka ka kalli yadda wasan zai fara."
     "Nasan zaka iya shiyasa na kawo maka wannan shawarar amma karka yadda kowa yasan Game kake bugawa a Soyayyar ta, ka nuna musu Soyayyar gaskiya ne, hatta Nameer dasu Ummi su ɗauka son na gaskiya ne, amma karka sanar da kowa har sai ta amshi tayinka."
   "Kana da basira Sadiq shiyasa nake son saka cikin lamurana, an gama."
  Yayi Maganar yana mikewa tare da ɗiban wayoyinsa yace.
   "Bari na shige Company sai mun haɗu da daddare."
    "Okay sai mun haɗun."
 Har Hisham zai fita Sadiq yace.
    "Sorry ya maganar yarinyar nan ne Hajara ya kamata ka bawa Dpo ita fa ta horu ai 5days tana rufe cikin duhu."
    "Anjuma insha Allah idan muka haɗu zamuje a fitar da ita wannan ya isheta darasi."
   "Okay limamin zamani mai hukunci da zamani."
   Sadiq ya masa kirarin yana dariya m
 Shima Hisham fita yayi yana dariya yabar Sadiq yana zuƙar shishirsa.



  Nameer daga gidan su Jidda gida ya dawo da yaso ya shige Company sai yaji bazai iya zuwa Companyn ba, babu kowa a falon Tutu ta tafi school Ummi kuma sun fita unguwa da drever, bedroom ɗin su ya shige tare da zama bakin bed ɗin ya dafe kansa, tun lokacin da sukaje wani program a  Companyn Zeen global concept ya ga jidda tun lokacin hankalin sa ya koma kanta a ko da yaushe tunaninta yake yayi bincike sosai akanta ya tabbatar da a Aminci take, sati biyu da haɗuwar su a Zeen global concept, tazo gidansu da Police akayi arresting ɗin sa, numfashi ya saki tare da furta.
     "Wannan lokacin ya kamata na furta mata soyayyata itace macen da ya kamata na aura zuciyata ita take so, ya zamo wajibi na shiga jikin mahaifiyarta hakan zaisa Jidda ta soni."
   Ya furta yana lumshe idanunsa, wayarsa ce tayi ringing, Hisham ya gani hannu yasa ya ɗauki wayar.
     "Ya akayi."?
Daga can Hisham yace.
    "Kana ina ne yazo office ɗin ka sakatariyar ka tace baka shigo ba kuma naga tun safe ka fita."?
    "Gani a gida na dawo kaina ke ciwo bazan iya shigowa ba, ɗazun station naje na amso belin jidda."
   "Whatt! Belin Jidda kuma, well shikenan ka samu kasha magani sai na dawo."
  Hisham yayi Maganar daga can yana taɓe bakinsa tare da katse kiran duban sakatariyar yayi yace.
    "Bazai samu shigowa ba, duk wanda yazo ganinsa ki turo sa office ɗina, ina da abu mai muhimmanci da zanje nayi yanzu."
   Sakatariyar ce tace.
"Sir akwai wanda ya kawo file ɗin sa yace a bawa Oga Nameer idan ya shigo."
   "File kuma okay let me See."
   "Okay Sir."
Sakatariya tayi Maganar tana ɗauko file ɗin ta miƙawa Hisham amsa yayi tare da buɗe file ɗin, idanunsa ya ɗago ya kalli sakatariya yace.
   "Yaushe aka baki File ɗin, shi Tunde da kansa ya kawo file ɗin ko wani ya aiko."?
    "Yes Sir da kansa ya kawo ɗazu kafin ka shigo nace ya kai maka office ɗin ka, yace a'a na riƙe kawai na bawa Oga."
  Murmushi Hisham ya saki yace.
    "Okay ba damuwa ki cigaba da aikinki zanje da file ɗin."


 Shigowar Tutu gidan babu kowa hakan ya sata haurawa sama har zata buɗe bedroom ɗin Ummi taji motsin mutum a bedroom ɗin su yaya NAMEER, fasa shiga bedroom ɗin Ummin tayi ta nufi na su Nameer nocking tayi Nameer dake kwance yana aiki da system ɗinsa yace.
    "Yes come."
 Shigowa Tutu tayi ganin Nameer ne kawai ya sata zama bakin bed ɗin.. 
   "Ina yaya Hisham ɗin na ganka kai ɗaya.?"
   "Yana Company ya akayi har kun tashi daga school ɗin kenan."?
   "Eh, Yaya Nameer am.......
   Katseta yayi da cewa.
       "Um um, kinga I love you, kinsan Nameer bashi da wata mata bayan Tutu, wai ma na tambayeki naga sai ƙara kyau kike meye sirrin ne."
  Murmushi Tutu ta saki cike da ƙaunar Nameer tace.
   "Waye sirrina bayan kai, Yaya Nameer ji nake nafi ko wacce mace Sa'a domin kuwa babu wanda ya kaini dacen samun jarumin saurayi."
  Murmushi kawai Nameer ya saki yace.
    "To naji sarauniyata, time ɗin dawowar Hisham yayi zamu fita da yamma ki shirya."
   "Okay I love you."
Ta furta tana murmushi.
   "I love You too."
Shima Nameer ya furta yana kanne mata ido, fita Tutu tayi cike da farin ciki domin kuwa a duk sanda ta kalli Nameer farin ciki ne yake gauraye zuciyar ta, bata juma da fita ba kuwa Hisham ya shigo ko takalmin sa ba cire ba ya zauna tare da miƙawa Nameer file ɗin Tunde.
    Amsa Nameer yayi tare da buɗe file ɗin sanda ya karanta tukunna kafin ya dubi Hisham.
    "Amma ai ko kai zaka iya Sumit ɗin file ɗin idan kasa hannu ai shikenan ka basa office ɗin da ya kamata yayi aiki ba sai ni ba."
    "Okay na fahimta, kuma nasan hakan sai dai karka manta ni ya fara kawowa file ɗin office ɗina, kuma na yanke hukuncin bazamu ɗauki ɓarawo aiki ba, dan haka baza'a ɗaukesa ba."
Murmushi Nameer yayi yace.
   "Amma karka manta Abba ne ya bada umarnin mu ɗaukesa dan haka ya zamo wajibi Tunde yayi aiki a Companyn mu, bayan shima zan ɗauki Jidda itama aiki a cikin Companyn saboda kaine ka mata asarar aikinta dan haka ya zamo wajibi ni kuma na biya bashin da ka ɗauka rigimar ta ƙare."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki yana sunkuyawa ya zare takalmin ƙafarsa bai cewa Nameer komai ba, ganin yayi murmushi yayi shuru yasa Nameer cewa.
   "Nayi magana kamin shuru baka faɗa min ra'ayinka akan hakan ba.?"

 "Au wai Hisham banza kayi dani nayi maganar kayi min shuru."
   Agogon hanunsa da ya cire ya ijiye tare da juyowa yace.
     "Meyasa zaka damu dole sai kaji ra'ayina ba dai kai ka gama yanke naka hukuncin ba to meye nawa a ciki."
    Tashi Nameer yayi ya zauna tare da cewa.
    "Ina son jin ra'ayinka ne saboda bana son rigima bana son su fara aiki ka dinga ɗaga mana hankali a Company, nasan halinka sarai Hisham."
   Dariya maganar ta bawa Hisham tare da juyowa yace.
     "Oh saboda ni ne masifaffe zan ɗaga muku hankali ko, to ai abinda baka sani ba Nameer saboda ka ɗauki Jidda aiki ni wannan ba matsala ta bace abu ɗaya ne dai bazan yadda ba dole sai anyi kafin a ɗauke ta aiki shine cike dukkan wani doka na Company dole sai an tantance ta, bayan duba takaddunta ana bukatar sai an mata interview kasan wannan wajibi ne a cikin Company, sannan shi Tunde da kake magana a kansa kamar yadda ka faɗa bazanyi jayayya da maganar Abba ba, to shima sai ya cike ƙa'idojin Company idan har sun cancanta za'a gani."
   "Hmmm! Haba Hisham nifa ba yaro bane, kawai kace zaka koresu a wajen interview tunda kaine mai interview a cikin Company kaga kace sun faɗi ai ba wani abu mai wahala bane a wajenka, musamman Jidda da kake jin kamar ka kasheta."
   Sosai Hisham yayi dariya cikin zuciyarsa ya furta.
    _Nameer baka san shirina akan Jidda bane shiyasa kake kallon kamar bazan bari tayi aiki a Companyn mu ba, wannan dama ce mai kyau ka kawo wanda burina zai cika cikin sauƙi muddun Jidda tayi aiki a Companyn mu._
    Numfashi ya saki har yanzu dariyar yake yace.
    "Nameer ka fahimce ni, ni fa yanzu bani da matsala tsakanina da Jidda, bashin gaba ce kuma an fanshe, babu bashi tsakanin mu yanzu, ta kulleka na kulleta Final, karka damu ka shigo ayi interview tare da kai tunda kana min kallon maƙaryaci."
   "Ba kallon maƙaryaci nake maka ba Hisham sanin hali ne yafi sanin kai nafi kowa sanin waye kai."
   "Okay shikenan babu kuma abinda zance maka yanzu."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana ɗaukar towel ya shige tollet, tashi Nameer yayi ya fita falo, Ummi ya gani yanzu, murmushi ya saki yace.
   "Ummi yaushe kika dawo."
   "Kuna ƙuryar ɗaki Nameer yaushe zaku san dawowata."
  Zama yayi yana murmushi yace.
   "Shigowa ta babu kowa shiyasa ma na shige bedroom."
   "Nameer wai ya Maganar Aure ne, na fara gajiya fa da ganinku haka just 35yrs ai ya kamata ace zuwa yanzu kunyi haihuwar fari."?
   Dariya Nameer ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Kai Ummi nawa shekara 35 take da har za'a gaji damu, insha Allah Ummi Auren nan yana zuwa nan kusa kusa amma fa nawa bansan na Hisham ba, dan shi ko zancen Soyayyar ma baya so."
    "Ƙyaleni da ɗan ƙwal uba, Auren nan dai sai yayisa ko na masa Auren dole."
    "Haba dai Auren dole ai yanzu an shige wannan zamanin da ake yiwa mata Auren dole bare kuma maza."
   "Naji to kai surukar tawa a ina take.?"
   Murmushi Nameer yayi yana jin Jidda cikin ransa yace.
   "Ummi na dai ganota amma ban furta mata ra'ayina ba, amma Ummi ina sonta sosai so beautiful ce yarinyar."
   "To marar kunya ko kunyata baka ji, to dai kayi gaggawar sanar da ita karka tsaya kallon ruwa ƙaton kwaɗo ya maka ƙafa, amma dan Allah Nameer ku nemi Aure a gidan mutunci saboda mace ta gari itace ƙashin bayan al'umma daga gareta ake samun ƴaƴa na gari, yana da kyau ku kula."
   Numfashi Nameer ya saki yana jinjina kansa yace.
    "Hmmm! Hakane Ummi insha Allah bazamu ɗauko macen da za'a kuka da ita ba."
   "To Allah yayi jagora ya muku albarka."
  Tayi Maganar dai-dai Hisham na sauƙowa, kusa da ita ya zauna yana cewa.
    "Ummi jiya Abba yake cemin zai tafi Istanbul by tomorrow, kuma zai kwana biyu it's true."?
    "Eh insha Allah gobe zai tafi suna da meeting da zasuyi acan so shima tafiyar ta taso masa ne kawai lokaci guda, bayan haka ai ya juma baije Istanbul ba."
   "To Allah ya kaimu, maganar wannan Yarinyar Hajara da na rufe a gidan gonar Abba ya za'ayi da ita na miƙawa hukuma itane ko kawai na ƙyaleta tayi tafiyar ta.?"
    "Subahanallah! Hisham dama baka saki yarinyar nan ba, ni Hisham mai yake damunka ne, kana da hankali kuwa ka ɗauki yarinya mace ka je ka ɓoyeta saboda ubanka ya ɗaure maka gindi, bana fa jin daɗin abinda kake Hisham ka dinga kiyayan ɓacin raina, yanzu dan ubanka idan Tutu aka yiwa abinda ka yiwa wannan yarinyar zakaji daɗi, tashi ka tashi kaje ka ɗauki ƴar mutane ka mayar da ita gidan ubanta."
  Idanunsa Hisham ya runtse yana jin faɗan Ummi, a rayuwarsa ya tsani faɗa da tsawa.
    "But Ummi........
"Kul kamin wata magana cewa nayi ka tashi kaje ka saki ƴar mutane."
   Nameer ne ya miƙe tsaye tare da cewa.
    "Allah ya huci zuciyarki Ummi kiyi haƙuri ni bara naje da kaina na maida ta gidan su, a wani room ka rufeta.?"
    Ɗago idanunsa Hisham yayi ya kalli Nameer kafin cikin ɓacin rai yace.
    "Ka tambayi mai gadi zai nuna maka key ɗin room ɗin yana hannunsa."
  Yayi Maganar yana tashi yabar wajen, harara Ummi ta watsa masa tana cewa.
    "Kai dai Allah ya shiryeka, ina sane da baka daina shan sigarin nan ba ai dan uwarka shasha wanda baisan ciwon kansa ba, kana ganin ɗan uwanka yanda yake tafiyar da rayuwar sa cikin nutsuwa da albarkar kowa amma kai Banda kai sai zafin ran tsiya da bazai amfanar da kai komai ba ga shegen taurin kai."
   Shi dai Hisham baiyi magana ba ya shige a zuciye.
    "Ummi dan Allah kiyi haƙuri ki daina masa faɗa haka, kinsani Hisham haka Allah ya hallice sa a haka ya taso bazai sauya ba, dan Allah Ummi ki daina wallahi raina suya yake min idan kina zaginsa."
   "Umhum ai kune masu ƙara tunzura sa dole ya cigaba da ganin abinda yakeyi dai-dai ne, zaka shige ne ko kaima sai na ɓata maka rai."
   "Allah ya huci zuciyarki Ummi."
  Yayi Maganar cikin girmamawa yana fita, tsuka Ummi taja cikin ɓacin rai, tana jin haushi da takaicin wannan sigari da Hisham ke sha tayi faɗan tayi zagin amma abu ya gagara, ga sigari illace ga kayan cikin Mutum musamman Huhu tana saurin lalata sa.
     _Allah ka shirya min Hisham, Allah ka fitar masa da wannan sigarin a ransa._
   Tayi zancen cikin zuciyarta tana sakin ijiyar zuciya.



 A zama yayi tare da dafe kai cikin matsanancin ɓacin rai, tebur ya buga tare da cewa.
     "Rabon da Ummi ta zageni ko ta min faɗa harna manta a rayuwata, amma yau Ummi ke zagina, kuma duk wannan matsiyaciyar yarinyar ce sanadi, JIDDA na tsaneki na tsaneki I hate you!."
  Yayi Maganar da ƙarfi yana runtse idanunsa.
  Ya juma sosai yana huci har Nameer ya dawo sai kusan isha kafin ya ɗan saki har suka ci abinci, Hisham fuskarsa tana ɗaure ita dai Ummi bata kulasa ba, ko Nameer bai yiwa sallama ba ya yi ficewarsa, shima Nameer wanka yayi ya ɗauki Tutu suka fita.




 ************


 A bakin railway ya ɗauki Sadiq sunyi nisa da tafiya Sadiq yace.
    "Ina zamuje ne, can gidan gonar Abba ne.?"
  Girgiza kansa Hisham yayi Yace.
    "No ba can zamuje ba Nameer yaje yayi handling ɗin wannan Case ɗin."
   "To ina kenan zamuje."?
"Kai Sadiq ka cika yawan tambaya kasan dai duk iskancina bazan je na sayar da kai ba ko, Ora zamuje, gani nayi ya kamata mu fara buga Game ɗin nan yau."
  Dariya Sadiq ya saka tare da cewa.
    "Au har ka gama playing ɗin yadda game ɗin zai kasance kenan, to  menene tsarin."?
    Kansa Hisham ya girgiza tare da sakin murmushi yace.
    "Ka zuba idanunka zaka gani, Hisham ne fa ko ka manta ne.?"
   "Ina kuwa zan manta Hisham, ai nasan saƙar ka shiyasa wani lokacin nake tsoron ka, amma dai ka taka a hankali gudun zamewa kasan wani sa'in bama fahimtar akwai santsi a waje har sai mun jimu a ƙasa, ya kamata mu dinga tafiya da waiwaye."
    "Karka damu Sadiq duk santsi wajen akwai takalmin da ya fi ƙarfinsa muje zuwa zakaga ƙarshen wasan."
   Dariya Sadiq yayi basu kuma magana ba har sai da suka yi parking a ƙofar gidan su Jidda, duban Hisham Sadiq yayi yace.
     "Hisham wai kiranta za'ayi ne.?"
   "No shiga gidan zamuyi da kanmu."
    "Shiga kuma Hisham ba tare da neman izini ba."
    "Karka damu biyoni kawai."
  Kansa Sadiq ya kaɗa tare da fitowa daga motar key Hisham ya yiwa motar kafin suka nufi gidan su Jidda kansa tsaye Hisham ya shiga da sallama Umma ne ta amsa Jidda kanta na sunkuye tana ɗaurawa Abdu karatu, Basmah ma tana gefe, Umma cike da mamakin su sun shigo musu gida kai tsaye duk da Umma bata san Hisham ba, kamar wanda akace Basmah ta ɗago kanta taga Hisham na doso su a zabure Basmah tace.
    "Hisham."
Ai Jidda saurin ɗago kanta tayi cikin mungun mamaki taga Hisham miƙewa tayi tsaye tayi tace .
    "Hisham meya shigo da kai gidan mu.?"

*_Page 29 to 30_*




*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻


Murmushi kawai Hisham yayi bai kuma tsaya ba ya cigaba da tahowa, cikin azama Jidda ta taho zata tare gabansa Umma ta daka mata tsawa.
   "Karki kuskura! ki dawo ki zauna a inda kike.''
    "Hisham ne fa Umma ta yaya zan barsa ya shigo mana gida ya cima mutunci, kinsan kuwa yanda na tsani ganin wannan mutumin, wallahi Umma da ganin wannan gara nayi karo da mungun maciji, shine fa mutumin daya tozarta ni shine mutumin da baya ƙaunar yaga asirin mu ya ruhu shiya rabani da aikina Umma saboda kawai yaga mun tozarta, wannan maƙiyin Allah ne."
  Kanta Umma ta jinjina domin kuwa ta fahimci yadda Jidda take jin zafin Hisham a cikin ranta.
    "Na sani Jidda, dan haka ki koma ki zauna haka na ce miki."
  Shuru Jidda tayi tana cije bakinta zuciyarta na tafasa ta koma ta zauna, Basmah ma daga inda take ta zauna Umma kuwa nuni ta yiwa Hisham da tabarma tace.
   "Ga waje ku zauna."
Zama sukayi Hisham ya dubi umma tare da cewa.
   "Barka da dare."
"Barka yaro kaine Hisham ko, Allah yasa ba wani kuskuren muka sake maka ba."?
    "Ko ɗaya Umma babu wani kuskure da kuka min ni ne ma na muku kuskure, Umma ba tashin hankali bane ya kawo ni, nazo ne na baki haƙuri a matsayin ki na uwa na tabbata zaki fahimce ni, nasan ba lallai Jidda ta yadda dani ba, saboda tana kallon na yaudareta a baya da nufin na saduda wanda hakan yasa ta faɗa tarkona har na samu hujjar da na rufeta a police station, Umma a yanzu ba yaudara ba ce ta kuma kawoni gidan ku ke uwace kuma kin girme mu idan har yaudara ce ta kuma kawoni na tabbata ke zaki fahimci hakan, Umma jiya mun zauna da Nameer munyi magana ya nuna kuskurena akan abinda na yiwa Jidda, kuma na fahimta na gane cewa ita bata da laifi gashi kuma na cutar da ita da yawa, jiya banyi bacci ba tunani da damuwar zaluncin da na mata su suka dameni da tunanin anya kuwa Jidda zata yafe min, ban tunkaro Jidda nazo gareki saboda ke uwace zaki gane zafin da nakeji a raina ita Jidda ba lallai ta fahimce ni ba, dan Allah ku yafe min, maganar aikinta kuma da ta rasa munyi magana da Nameer zata kawo takaddunta mu duba insha Allah zamu ɗauketa aiki, Umma zafi da ciwon abinda Jidda tasa aka yiwa ɗan uwana shine yasa idanuna rufewa harna mata haka, bana iya riƙe kaina muddun aka taɓa Nameer, amma yanzu na fahimci kuskure na aikata ga Jidda kuyi haƙuri, wannan shi ya kawo ni."
    Shuru Umma tayi tana sauraren Hisham sosai Maganar sa ta shiga jikinta kuma bata hango yaudara ko rashin gaskiya a tattare dashi ba, tsantsar gaskiya da nadama ta hango a idanunsa, numfashi ta saki kafin tace.
    "Na saurari maganganun ka Hisham, ni kaina bazan ɓoye maka da farko naji zafin ka, amma tun sanda ɗan uwanka yazo gidan nan yake bani haƙuri ya kuma tabbatar min kai ba mutumin banza ba ne na yarda dashi, saboda Mutum ne na ƙwarai shi, sai kuma yanzu da kazo na tabbatar da hakan wannan akasi ne daya faru kuma Allah ya ƙaddara sai anyi hakan ni na fahimce ka, ita kanta Jidda tun farko da nace ta janye jikinta a cikin Case ɗin da taji maganata dama hakan bata faru ba, ni na yafe maka kuma ita ma jidda ta yafe maka."
   "Wallahi ban yafe masa ba Umma kuma bazan yafe masa ba har abada! Da wannan mungun maƙaryacin zaki yadda macijin sari ka noƙe, da kake zancen zaku ɗauke ni aiki a Companyn ku, ce maka akayi mahaukaciya ce ta yayeni da zan yi aiki a ƙarƙashin ku, Allah ya tsari gatari noma ko kwarya ta fashe wallahi akwai sauran zane, kuma ka sani ina nan akan bakata sai ka girbi abinda ka min."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana huci, Umma da kallo ta bita shi kansa Hisham dama yasan bazai ci nasara cikin sauƙi ba, miƙewa yayi tare da cewa.
    "Umma ranta ne a ɓace har yanzu tana jin haushi na bata gama hucewa, kiyi haƙuri, zan cigaba da baki haƙuri har sai kin haƙura, muje Sadiq Umma na barki lafiya."
    "Ba komai na gode Allah yayi muku albarka, ka gaida ɗan uwan naka."
   "Zaiji insha Allah."
Hisham ya amsa suna ficewa, Umma kallon Jidda da sai cika take tana batsewa tace.
   "Jidda meyasa zuciyar ki bata da kyau ne wani lokacin Jidda idan babu haƙuri duniyar zata zauna ƙalau ne, ita kanta duniyar ma fa ƙalilan ce ko yaushe ina nuna miki mahimmancin haƙuri Jidda, da kike cewa bazaki amshi aikin su ba,  so kike mu koma halin da muka shiga a baya ne Kafin ki samu aiki, idan bakiyi danni ba, ko dan Abdu zakiyi saboda kuɗin makarantar sa, kin sani bani da wani yaro Babba Namiji kece jigona dalilin kenan da yasa na dage kikayi karatu, duk rintsi da wahala sai da na tabbatar kin samu ingantaccen ilimi da ingantaccen kwali, kema kin sani babu irin wahalar da bansha ba a rayuwata."
    Numfashi Jidda ta saki idanunta na ciko da hawaye tace.
   "Na sani Umma kuma bazan taɓa mantawa ba, amma Umma ki sani Hisham yaci mutunci na a rayuwa, Umma mayaudari ne zai iya fakewa da wannan hanyar saboda ya ƙara cutar dani, baki san waye Hisham bane shiyasa kika yarda dashi cikin sauri amma ni bazan taɓa yarda dashi ba, idan har kince dole sai nayi aiki a ƙarƙashin su zan miki biyayya Umma amma sai dai ni nasan dole sai wani mummunan abun ya faru, duk wanda ya haɗa hanyar sa da Hisham dole sai ya taka ƙaya."
  Jidda tayi maganar hawayen yana biyo idanunta tare da tashi tabar wajen Umma duban Basmah tayi tace.
    "Basmah kinfi kowa sanin waye Jidda dan haka ina son ki bani gudummawa wajen ganin Jidda ta amshi wannan aikin, bamu wata madogara da ta wuce amsar aikin muddun muna son mu cigaba da rayuwa cikin rufin asiri kema shaida ce kan wahalar da muka sha a rayuwa."
    "Na sani Umma kuma insha Allah zanyi iya bakin ƙoƙarina domin ganin Jidda ta amshi aikin bara naje na sameta."
    "Shikenan Basmah na gode."
  Umma tayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki, kafin Basmah ta tashi tabi bayan Jidda.




   Sunyi nisa da tafiya HISHAM yace.
    "Sadiq meka fahimta a zuwan mu gidan nan shin ka hango nasara kuwa.?"
    "Eh to kusan haka amma dai akwai ƙura, kaima tunda muka fito naga kamar ka sare, ko da zakayi nasara akanta to zata baka wahala da alamu tana da taurin kai yarinyar sannan ƙwaƙwalwarta yana ja sosai sai ka taka a hankali."
   Iska Hisham ya fesar daga bakinsa kafin yace.
    "Naga alamar haka, Sadiq kasan kuwa yadda nake jin zuciyata kamar ta fashe tsabar baƙin ciki sanda na ƙasƙantar da kaina a gabansu, matsiyacita wadanda basu wuce bayina ba, Sadiq lallai duk wani baƙin ciki da zuciyata ta ƙunsa ya zamo BASHIN GABA tabbas zansa Jidda cikin JALALAR da sai ta gwammaci mutuwa fiye da rayuwa, sai tace meyasa ma tazo duniya, ban taɓa tsanar mutum irin yadda na tsani Jidda ba, kamar yadda tace da ganina gara ganin baƙin maciji da ni a gareta to kuwa na gwammaci ganin mutuwata sama da ita, hmmm! Sadiq wannan yarinyar ta ɓallo ruwan da bazata iya taresa ba, kuma nayi alƙawarin sai ta soni sai nayi wasa da zuciyar ta kamar yadda sarakuna suke wasa da Doki sai ni tsalle a cikin zuciyarta ina tafe ina zaneta kamar yadda ake yiwa bayi, zan kunna mata wutar da bazata iya kasheta ba a zuciyarta, na rantse sai na koya mata soyayyata, ko da kuwa zansha wahala kafin hakan ta kasance."
   Numfashi Sadiq ya saki yana sakin murmushi yace.
    "Na godewa Allah da yasa abinda ta maka baisa ka zuciya ba, ka sani sai ansha wahala ake samun nasara ba'a taɓa cin galba akan masoyi cikin sauƙi ka sauƙe ni anan zanje unguwa."
      "Unguwa kuma to na kaika mana sai na shige, nima gidan Aunty Halima zanje."
    "Karka damu Hisham, ka sauƙe ni kawai."
   Kansa Hisham ya kaɗa tare da yin parking Sadiq ya sauƙa, yana fita yaja motar yabar wajen murmushi Sadiq ya saki yana bin motar da kallo sanda yayi nisa wata farar mota tayi parking a gaban sa, buɗe motar yayi ya shiga, yana shiga ya ɗaga kai ya kalli Tunde sai suka saki murmushi Tunde ya ɗagawa Sadiq babbar yatsa alamun jinjina👍🏼
     "Banyi zaton zaka iya buga wannan wasan haka ba, sanda Mama ta kiraka baka wannan kogilar cikin tsoro da tunanin kai Aboki ne a wajen Hisham wanda ya yadda da kai, sai gashi ga baro ƙasar da kake saboda amsar wannan kogilar, a yayin da kasa Hisham wasa da zuciyar wata kai kuma bai san wasa kake da rayuwar sa ba, da alamu dai zamu ci Nasara akan ahalin Iyabo ta dalilinka, hmm tabbas zamu kauda hankalinsa da wannan yarinyar akan mu, Hisham wawa ne na ajin farko marar lissafi."

*_Page 31 to 32_*





*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻




Murmushi Sadiq ya saki yana duban Tunde kafin ya girgiza kansa yace.
   "Idan har Hisham wawa ne to kuwa tabbas kowa ma dake wannan duniyar wawa ne, Hisham fa ba gaɓo bane kamar yadda kake tunani komai da lissafi yake yi, tabbas ni zan iya wofintar da tunaninsa saboda yadda bazai taɓa ankara zan cutar dashi ba, yarda ce zata sa na iya kai Hisham ƙasa ba wauta ko rashin lissafi ba, idan wayo ne da kuma lissafi Hisham ya fini nesa ba kusa ba dan haka ko yanzu karka ɗauka cewa Hisham ba'a ankare yake ba, takun mu ya kamata mu iya."
   "Hakane na fahimta, Mama tana son zatayi Magana da kai."
   Kansa Sadiq ya girgiza.
 "No Bazanyi Magana da ita ba ba kuma zan haɗu da ita ba, ina yin komai cikin tsaro ne, bana bukatar wani ya fara fahimtar akwai alaqa tsakanina da ku, kaima kanka ina haɗuwa ne kawai da kai ba tare da motar gidan ku ba, dan haka duk abinda take son tattaunawa da ni ta sanar dakai kai ka zamo ɗan saƙo."
   "But ko waya bazaku iya kuyi ba, ko da baku haɗun ba.?"
   "A wannan lokacin hanyar sadarwa ma ba cikakken tsaro bane da ita saboda abu mai sauƙi ne a bibiyi da wadanda kake waya dan haka bazanyi waya da ita ba, sannan ka jaddada mata cewa muddun muka kammala wannan mission ɗin ta cire HISHAM RICE MILL LIMITED a cikin lissafin ta domin nine wanda zai mallakesa kamar yadda na faɗa muku zamu kawo ƙarshen gaba ɗaya wadannan mutum huɗun, Hisham Ummi Abba da kuma Tutu saboda sune suke da gadon IYABO, to tabbas zan iya tsayar da numfashin su, ka tabbatar mata Company nawa ne, idan kuma aka ci amanata to tabbas kuma bazan barku ba sai na kawo ƙarshen ku, da lissafi nake komai cikin nazari nasan tunda kukaci amanar Alhaji Iyabo to abu ne mai sauƙi a wajen ku kuce zakuci amanata, to ina ankare cin amanata gareku tamkar kun ɗebi tafasasshen ruwa ne kun watsa a jikin ku, magana ta da kai a yanzu tazo ƙarshe."
   Sadiq yayi Maganar yana buɗe motar ya fita numfashi Tunde ya saki tare da jan motar yabar wajen, ko da ya koma gida yadda sukayi da Sadiq ya sanarwa Mamabisola, murmushi ta saki Tunde ne yace.
   "Mama wannan mutumin bashi da hankali idan har muka basa Companyn ai shikenan mu mun tashi famfam saboda Company shine fa ƙarfin arzikin su, shiya kawo musu dukkan kuɗin da suke alfahari dashi yanzu gaskiya Mama bazai yiwu mu basa wannan Companyn ba."
    "Tunde kenan kai yaro ne shiyasa ba komai zaka fahimta ba, Tabbas HISHAM RICE MILL LIMITED shine babban Companyn su amma ka sani suna da ƙananun Company a jahohi daban-daban, sannan akwai wani babban Companyn nasu a Indonesiya NAMEER COTTON LIMITED ana ji dashi Company ne na AUDUGA suna kuma sarrafashi ta hanyoyi daban daban, suna yin atampopi da sauransu, dan haka mu barsa da wannan Companyn domin kuwa Sadiq shine garkuwar da zai iya kai Hisham ƙasa mu kuma dama Hisham shine matsalar mu domin kuwa idanunsa suna kammu."
    "Okay to shikenan Mama, yace bazaiyi magana da ke ba kuma bazaiyi waya da ke ba, saboda tsaro duk saƙon da kike son isar masa yace ki faɗa min ni na isar masa idan muka haɗu."
  Dariya Mamabisola tayi sosai kafin tace.
    "Tabbas kansa yana ja, yana tafiya da lissafi na fahimci karatun nasa da alamu kuma zai kawo hadda, ba damuwa ka zamo ɗan saƙo."
    "Okay ya maganar zuwa ƙauyen da zasuyi da Wamimo."?
   "Tunde zan tsagaita tukunna naga takun Sadiq idan akwai nasara Bama bukatar tsafi, mu jira muga iya tseren sa tukunna itama Wamimo na sanar da ita hakan."
   "Shikenan Allah yasa tseren yayi gudu dan ina bukatar ganin ƙarshen su cikin gaggawa."
  Yana rufe bakinsa wayarsa tayi ringing dubansa ya kai ga wayar Nameer, Mamabisola ya kalla.
   "Nameer ke kira."
"Ka ɗauka kaji abinda zai ce maka."
   Wayar ya ɗauka yayi sallama sun ɗan juma suna waya kafin Tunde ya katse yana kallon Mamabisola yace.
   "Kinji wai na kawo takadduna gobe za'a min interview."
   "Okay ba damuwa kaje ɗin, bara na je na kwanta."
   Mamabisola tayi Maganar tana tashi tabar falon.




***********


Dariya Aunty Halima tasa tana duban Hisham tace.
     "Wai da gaske kake Hisham."
   "Allah kuwa Aunty Halima, da gaske nake ina son haɗa Sadiq da Tutu Aure, saboda amanarsa da kuma hankalinsa, Sadiq yana bani gudummawa sosai a cikin al'amari na, amma ke ya kika gani."
   Numfashi Aunty Halima ta saki tace.
   "To wani hanzari ba gudu ba, Kamar Tutu tana da wanda take so domin kuwa ni ta taɓa cemin tana da wanda take so, kaga kuwa bazamu mata Auren dole ba, wanda take so shi zamu ɗauka mu bata."
  Cike da mamaki Hisham yace.
   "What Aunty akwai wanda Tutu take so ya akayi mu bamu sani ba daga ni har Nameer babu wanda ta sanar hakan kuma bana tunanin ta sanar da Ummi dan da Ummi ta sani zata sanar damu, amma Aunty waye tace miki wanda take so ɗin."?
   "No bata sanar dani waye bane gaskiya ta dai cemin tana da wanda take so, amma kafin kayi magana da Sadiq ka fara zama da Tutu tukunna."
   "Okay shikenan hakan zanyi zan samu lokaci nayi magana da ita, dare yayi zan koma gida Aunty sai da safe."
   "Allah ya tashe mu lafiya ka gaishe da Ummin naku, ka cewa Nameer ina neman sa."



 Tunda Hisham ya dawo gida har yayi wanka ya kwanta tare da jawo system ɗinsa ya hau aiki, ƙarfe 10:30 har zuwa wannan lokacin Nameer bai dawo ba, ture system ɗin Hisham yayi tare da miƙewa ya fito falo Ummi da mai aikinta kawai Hisham ya gani suna kallo zama yayi yana Zama mai aikin ta tashi tabar wajen.
   "Ummi ina Tutu.?"
"Tutu kuma ba sun fita da Nameer ba wai sunje zatayi shopping."
   Shuru Hisham yayi tare da kallon agogo yace.
    "Amma ya kamata ace zuwa yanzu sun dawo 10:35 fa Ummi, Hmm mtsss bai dace Nameer ya kai har wannan lokacin da Tutu ba."
   "Hisham meye damuwarka ba dai da ɗan uwanta ta fita ba, bana son shirmen banza."
   "Allah ya huci zuciyarki Ummi, bawai wata fassara dabam nayi ba, yauwa Ummi wai nikam Tutu ta sanar da ke akwai wanda take so ne ma'ana zata aura.?
  "Tutu kuma to ai bamma taɓa jin tace min ta fita zance ba, bani da labari gaskiya amma me ka gani."?
    "No Ummi ba komai kawai na tambaya ne."
   "Hisham."
  "Na'am Ummi."
"Hisham kana ɓata min raina abinda kake min Hisham bana jin daɗinsa."
   "Ummi ni kuma me nake miki ummi.''
  Yayi Maganar cikin tashin hankali, numfasawa Ummi tayi tace.
    "Sigari, sigari da kake sha Hisham shine yake ƙona min raina na ce maka bana so amma Hisham kaƙi bari kafi so ko yaushe raina ya dinga ɓaci ko.?"
   "Kai Ummi kin razana ni, nifa sigarin nan idan ban shata ba ji nake tamkar bani da lafiya musamman ma idan raina yana ɓace sai nasha nake samun sukuni, kawai ki cigaba da min addu'a idan da rabon zan daina sha is okay, kuma idan banda ke ma Ummi bafa sigarin da zata fito a jikina na matsiyacita nake sha ba, babu wanda ya isa yace ina shan sigari sai wanda ya sani,sigarin ma ai kala-kala ce."
   "Hmmm! Ikon Allah ina nuna maka gabar kana kai yamma zakayi, yanzu Hisham idan bata fito a jikinka ba ai dai bazata fasa cutar da lafiyar ka ba ko, Hisham ka sani ina maka tsananin ƙaunar da bazan iya jure rashinka ba, dan Hisham ka daina shan sigarin nan she is dangerous to why zaka nace da shan abinda kasan cutarwa ce ga rayuwarka, ka bari bana so."
   "Shikenan Ummi kiyi haƙuri insha Allah zanyi ƙoƙarin naga na rage, idan bansha bane Ummi bana jin daɗi, musamman idan ina cikin damuwa amma insha Allah zan rage."
   "Allahu yasha Hisham."
       "Ameeen Ummi Abba fa ya kwanta ne.?"
   "Ehh ya kwanta tun ɗazu kasan gobe sammako zaiyi zuwa Indonesiya, na Companyn Nameer."
   "Eh ya faɗa min, Ummi dai kina ji da Nameer baki cewa Companyn mu sai Company Nameer."
    "Ya za'ayi bazanji dashi ba, shi da baya ɓata min rai duk abinda na hanasa hanuwa yake, amma kai fa Hisham ba ka jin maganata."
  Tayi Maganar dai-dai Tutu na shigowa da sallama kusa da Hisham ta zauna tana cewa.
   "Ummi mun dawo."
 "Ta ganki ai, nayi zaton ai kwana zakuyi acan."
   "Kai yaya Hisham daɗinta ma dai da Yayana na fita, ba da wani ba."
    "Ki fita da wani mana dan ubanki idan ban karya miki ƙafa ba."
  Daga nesa Nameer yace.
   "Umhum ai nasan za'a rina, kai dai a duniya babu wanda kake sauƙe fushinka a kansa sai Tutu."
   Dariya Hisham yayi yace.
   "Au dama haka kake ɗauka, na ma kusa na aurar da ita na huta."
  Ummi dariya tasa tana kallon Hisham tace.
   "Ikon Allah mutumin da bai aurar da kansa bane yake tunanin aurar da wata yo Hisham gaka ƙaton tuzuru bakayi Aure ba sai auta na zaka takurawa ai kuwa baka isa ba, ka fara auren tukunna kafin ka saka wani yayi."
  Dariya Nameer tare da ƙarisowa ya zauna yace.
   "Yauwa Ummi faɗa masa dai, Malam ka fara fito da mata tukunna."
   "Au wai taron dangi zaku min akan nace wannan ƙaramar alhakin tayi Aure, to kai mai bakin banga taka matar ba."
   "To idan ba tsoro ba, nan da wata biyu kowa ya fito da tashi matar da zai aura mana."
   Cewar Nameer, dariya Hisham yayi yace.
    "Na kuwa yarda Ummi ki zamo shaida, alƙawari kuma mukayi, da dai bani da niyyar Aure amma tunda yanzu ka hasala ni zan kuma baka mamaki."
   "Wannan alƙawarin kuwa zan kasance mai tuna muku."
  Itama Ummi tayi Maganar, dukkan su dariya sukayi, Hisham ya juya ya kalli Tutu yace.
    "Naje gidan Aunty Halima ɗazu muke tattauna magana akanki take cemin kince mata akwai wanda kike so zaki aura, waye ne.?"
   Da sauri Nameer ya juya ya kalli Tutu, itama shi ta kalla tare da sakin murmushi ta juya ga Hisham.

*Page 33 to 34*



*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_*





 "Yaya Hisham Yaushe Aunty Halima ta faɗa maka hakan.?"
   Tayi Maganar tana kallon Nameer girgiza mata kai Nameer yayi alamun karta sanar da Hisham, girarta ta ɗaga masa alamun meyesa, Muryar Hisham taji yace.
   "Malama ina ruwanki da ranar da ta faɗa min tambayar ki kawai nayi sai ki bani amsa."
  "Okay sorry, amma yaya Hisham nifa cikin wasa na yiwa aunty Halima wannan maganar, bansan ta ɗaukesa serious ba, ni bani da wani saurayi, da kuma da Ummi ta sani."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Wa yaga sarkin baƙin jini yarinya mai shekara ashirin wai bata da manemi, to ai shikenan ni hakan ma yamin daɗi domin kuwa zan sadakar da ke cikin sauƙi."
   "Yaya Nameer kana jinsa ko wai ni zai sadakar, wallahi nafi ƙarfin ayi sadaka dani ina nan Allah zai kawo min miji na gari.''
   Dariya Nameer yayi tare da miƙewa yana cewa.
    "Wannan faɗan ni yafi ƙarfina, kunga ma shigewata dan wallahi bacci nakeji."
   Ummi ne tace.
"Kana da gaskiya Nameer nima tashi zanyi naje na kwanta kwa ƙarita ku biyu."
   Ummi tayi Magana itama tana tashi tayi bedroom ɗin ta shima Nameer shigewa yayi Tutu tana tura bakinta itama ta tashi bakinsa Hisham ya taɓe tare da jawo wayarsa ya kira Sadiq Ringing ɗaya ya ɗaga.
   "Ya akayi Hisham.?"
  "Mtsss! Kai dai Sadiq barni, kasan wata cacar da muka sanya da Nameer.?"
    "A'a sai ka faɗa min."
Numfasawa Hisham yayi yace.
    "Musu muke akan maganar Aure cikin haka har mukayi muƙabalar fitar da matar Aure cikin wata biyu, Ummi itace alƙali, anya kuwa ban ɗauko dala babu gammo ba."
  Daga can Sadiq yasa dariya tare da cewa.
    "Haba Hisham babu wani dala daka kinkima, she is gentleman so beautiful, kaga kuwa kana da damar sace zuciyar Jidda cikin sauƙi, sai dai inda gizo yake saƙar babu Auren Jidda cikin mission ɗin ka."
   "Wannan ba damuwa ba ne, Sadiq wannan itace damar da Hisham zai gwada ƙarfin sa'ar sa, dan haka ka zuba ido kaga yadda zan ɗauko zuciyar shegiyar a tafin hannuna 3week komai ready insha Allah."
   "Okay hakan ma yayi, sai mun haɗu gobe."
   "Allah ya kaimu."
Hisham yayi Maganar yana katse kiran murmushi ya sanya tare da furta.
    "Ina zan samu phone number ɗinta, hmmm! Okay Idi maye shi zan bawa wannan aikin zuwa gobe zai antayo min numbern nata."
  Murmushi ya kuma saki tare da kiran idi maye aikin ya yi basa da adress ɗin Jidda sannan ya katse kiran.


 Can kuwa Nameer tunda ya shiga yake kai kawo ya na tunanin ta ina zai fara aika saƙon sa ga Jidda iya nazarin da yayi akan rayuwarta macece mai kafiya da kuma ji da kai, numfashi ya saki tare da zama yayi tagumi, yana jinta a ransa fiye da kima baya jin cewa akwai macen da ya taɓa kaunarta kamarta, tabbas rasa Jidda gareshi tamkar tarwatsa rayuwarsa ne a ko yaushe zuciyar sa tana harbawa ne da sunanta, jigo ce ita a cikin rayuwarsa, a fili ya furta.
    "Bazan taɓa yarda na rasaki ba Jidda ko ta halin yaya sai na mallakeki ke tawace zanyi yaƙi da kowa a kanki, komai tsanani komai wahala Jidda bazaki kaucewa rayuwata ba ke tawa ce nima kuma naki ne."
  Yayi Maganar yana ɗaukar wayarsa ya kirata bata ɗaga ba, wajen sau biyu, sai kawai ya kira ummanta yana kira kuwa cikin Sa'a Umma ta ɗauka cikin girmamawa Nameer yace.
     "Umma Barka da dare, kiyi haƙuri na kira a wannan lokacin."
  Daga can Umma tace.
    "Ba komai Nameer komai a kamawa take, Allah dai yasa lafiya."
   "Lafiya alhamdulillah Umma, dama na kira numbern Jidda ne bata amsa kiran ba, maybe bata san number na bane, dama zance mata ne gobe ta taho da takaddunta na makaranta Companyn mu za'a mata interview, muna saka ran ɗaukarta aiki insha Allah."
  Cikin farin ciki umma tace.
    "Alhamdulillah! Gaskiya naji daɗin wannan kiran naka Nameer ɗazu kuwa ɗan uwanka ma Hisham yazo ya bamu hkr yace ka nuna masa abinda yayi bai kyauta ba, mun gode da gudumowarka garemu insha zatazo ta Allah ya maka albarka."
   "Ameeen Umma na gode sai goben."
    "To Allah ya tashe mu lafiya na gode."
  Katse kiran Nameer yayi cike da mamaki ya furta.
   "Hisham kuma yaje gidan su Jidda, kai anya kuwa Hisham zaije ya basu haƙuri kai mtsss ba Hisham bane gaskiya."
  Yayi Maganar Hisham na shigowa.
    "Kayi mamaki ne Nameer saboda Hisham yana da ɗagawa ko bazai iya bawa talaka haƙuri ba, to kayi kuskuren fahimta domin kuwa a wannan lokacin Hisham ya yadda yayi kuskure, ni ne naje na basu haƙuri ka daina mamaki."
   "But Hisham lallai kuwa idan har da gaske ne Hisham ya fara sauyawa."
    "Haka kuke so ku ganni ai shiyasa na sauya saboda kuyi farin ciki." 
    "Hakane na kira Tunde gobe zaizo interview itama Jidda na kirata zata zo gobe Company."
   "Allah ya kaimu goben."
Hisham yayi Maganar a takaice.




***********



Washegari bayan sunyi breakfast suka raka Abba har airport sanda jirgin su ya tashi tukunna suka shige Company, da misalin sha ɗaya da rabi, Tunde ya shigo, a office ɗin Nameer ya zauna kasancewar yasan halin Hisham, bai wani juma da zuwa ba Jidda ta kira Nameer cewa ta shigo Companyn, security ya kira yace ya shigo masa da Jidda office ɗin sa, tare suka shigo da Basmah Jidda tasha doguwar riga marun color tayi rolling ɗin gyalen a kanta duk da Jidda ba gwanar makeup bane sosai tayi kyau, da sallama suka shigo tunda suka shigo Nameer ya tsurawa JIDDA ido tamkar zai cinyeta sosai ta masa kyau kujera ya nuna mata tare da cewa.
    "Basmah sannunku da zuwa Bismillah ku zauna."
   Zama sukayi, Jidda ba tare da ta kalli Nameer ba tace.
   "Sannu aiki, jiya Umma ta isar min da saƙon ka, ga takaddun nawa."
   "A'a bani zan amshi takaddun ba Hisham ne, wannan bangaren sa ne shike tace ma'aikata."
    "Hisham kuma, to amma namee........
  Hannu Nameer ya ɗagawa Jidda tare da cewa.
    "Hisham ya sauya ba yadda kika sanshi a baya bane, karki yanke hukunci ki jira har yazo tukunna."
  Nameer yayi Maganar yana ɗaga waya ya kira Hisham yace yazo office ya samesa su jidda sunzo, kafin ya ijiye wayar, jefa jifa Nameer suna taɗi da Basmah ita dai Jidda tayi shuru, kusan 30minute babu Hisham baizo ba, har aka doshi awa ɗaya, Jidda sosai ranta ya sosu domin kuwa ita a rayuwarta babu abinda ta tsana sama da jira cike da hasala ta miƙe tace.
    "Tashi mu tafi Basmah."
   "Ta tashi ku tafi kuma meyasa."
  Kallon Nameer tayi kai tsaye tace.
   "Saboda banzo dan a wulaƙanta ni ba."
   "Haka zalika kuma babu mai wulaƙanta ki."
  Sukaji muryar Hisham ta doki kunnen su gaba daya juyowa sukayi suka kallesa murmushi ya saki ya ƙariso yana cewa.
    "Sorry kun gaji da jirana ko, akwai waɗanda suka juma kafin ku su na tsaya na sallama amma har kin ƙosa da yawa."
   Shuru Jidda tayi tare da komawa ta zauna dariya Hisham yayi tare da dafa dest ɗin da take zaune ya kanne mata ido yace.
    "Kamar dai har yanzu haushina kikeji ko, we'll da ma kinyi murmushi domin ko yaya kika juya yanayinki kyau kike ƙarawa, zan iya ganin takaddun naki."
  Yayi Maganar yana ɗaga mata gira, idanunta jidda ta runtse cike da jin haushin sa tace.
   "Bani na fara zuwa ba, sai ka fara sallamarsa tukunna."
  Murmushi Hisham yayi tare da juyawa ya kalli Tunde.
      "Au wai dama wannan shagiri girbau ɗin yana nan ne shima, hmm! Wai Tunde baka da zuciya a ƙirjinka ne, da ace ni ne kai ko inda Companyn nan yake bazan kalla sai dai ina kare ya cinye zuciyar, sai dai fa mu anan bama ƙyale ɓarawo muddun ya saci abummu, hannu muke cire masa, miƙo min takaddun naka ɗan uwana Tunde."
   Yayi Maganar yana sakin murmushi wani irin ɗaci Tunde yaji a zuciyarsa sosai ransa ya ɓaci ji yake tamkar ya tashi ya cakwamo Hisham, idanunsa ya runtse.
