Murmushi Hisham ya kuma saki ganin ran Tunde yayi mungun ɓaci nauyin jiki ya sa ya kasa miƙo masa takaddun nasa.
    "Hannunka yayi nauyi ne bazaka iya miƙo min takaddun ba, au ko nazo na ɗauka da kaina ne."
   Teburin gabanta Jidda ta daka da ƙarfi tare da miƙewa a hasale, gaba ɗaya juyawa sukayi suna kallonta har Hisham ɗin, gaban Tunde ta ƙarisa ta ɗauki takaddun nasa ta matso kusa da Hisham.
    "A rayuwata na juma banga mutum irinka ba, nayi tsammanin cewa ni ne kaɗai kake wa wannan wulaƙancin ashe kowa baifi ƙarfin wulaƙanci a wajen ka ba, ta yaya zai iya miƙo maka bayan ka ci masa mutunci, ka gyara rayuwarka Hisham domin kuwa baka kan turba mai kyau, saboda yazo neman aiki wajenka bashi zai baka lasisin wulaƙanta sa ba, ga takaddar ni na miƙo maka idan ma hannu ɗaya bazai baka sau biyu ni na haƙura da nawa shi dai ya samu."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki yana kallon Jidda.
    "Haka zalika ke ma ɗaukar damuwar wani ki ɗaura a kanki baya baki wahala, shi wannan ɗin da zaki ari faɗansa to ɗan uwana wannan faɗan cikin gida ne yana da kyau ki kawar da kanki, amm me ma kikace idan ma hannu biyu bazai bani na karɓa ba ke kin haƙura da naki, Hmm! Ko sau dubu hannunki ya miƙo min ni mai amsa ne a gareki saboda ina neman afuwarki, gaskiya ke macece ta musamman ƙwarin zuciyar ki na burgeni."
  Yayi Maganar yana amsar takaddun Tunde yace masa.
     "Interview room idan na gama dashi zan kiraki.''
   Yayi Maganar yana fita NAMEER ne ya dubi Tunde yace.
     "Ka tashi kaje kayi haƙuri kasan halin Hisham muma haƙuri muke dashi."
   Kansa Tunde ya haɗa yana dariya zuci domin kuwa shi a yanzu kallon biri yake yiwa Hisham, tarkon da suka ɗana masa zaiyi wuya ya tsallake sa tashi yayi yabi bayansa Jidda ne tace wa Nameer.
    "Nameer wai shi Hisham haka rayuwarsa take, ya kamata ku dinga masa burki bai kamata ku barsa ya tafi a haka ba, da zan samu dama wallahi da zan saisaita rayuwarsa."
    Numfashi Nameer ya saki, yana kallon Jidda yayin da yakejin ƙaunarta na nunkuwa a zuciyarsa, kafin yace.
      "Zaiyi wahala ki iya gyara Hisham tunda har muka gagara sanyaya masa zuciyar sa to babu wanda zai iya, kuma baku fahimci Hisham bane, shifa baya taɓa shiga harkar wani, idan kinga Hisham ya shiga harkarki to tabbas kece kika taka masa gonarsa baki san waye Tunde bane da bazakiyi gaggawan shiga faɗansu ba, su ɗin ƴan uwane na kusa iyayensu gindinsu ɗaya."
    "Kai ai kullum a cikin kare Hisham kake, Nameer halin ka mai kyau ne da ace Hisham kamarka yake to da tabbas sai yafi kowa jin daɗin rayuwarsa, kasan meye ina tausayawa matar da zata auri Hisham domin kuwa zata sha wahala kafin ta fuskanci waye shi."
  Murmushi Nameer ya saki yace.
    "To ni kuma tawa matar wani irin tausaya mata akeyi."?
   Murmushi itama Jidda tayi tace.
     "Ai kai Nameer duk matar da ta aureka to tabbas tayi sa'ar miji na gari, za kuma taji daɗin rayuwarta."
   Dariya Nameer yayi yace.
    "Gashi kuma wacce nake so ita bata san ina yi ba, ina sonta sosai soyayya ta haƙiƙa Soyayyar gaskiya ji nake tamkar bazan iya rayuwa ba tare da ita ba, ita ɗin rayuwata ne."
   "To amma meyasa bazaka sanar da ita Soyayyar da kake mata ba, kana da kyawawan halin da Mace bazata ƙika ba, ko wacce Mace ka furtawa kalmar so na tabbata bazata furta maka kalmar ƙiyayya ba."
    "Shikenan lokaci shine alƙali, Allah yasa maganar ki ta tabbata, ta soni Kamar yadda nake sonta."
    Jidda batayi magana ba Hisham ya kira waya yace Jidda ta ƙariso, kallonta Nameer yayi yace.
    "Hisham yace ki ƙarisa, kije sakatariya zata saka a kaiki."
    "Okay to shikenan Basmah ki jirani ina zuwa."
   Tayi Maganar tana tashi ta fita, kafin ta fita ma Nameer ya kira sakatariya tana fita ta samu security ya nuna mata, inda zatayi, tana zuwa ta shiga room ɗin babban room ne ma'aikata ta samu sosai a wajen, hakan ya nuna mata ba room ɗaya bane a ciki, wani ɗan korido ta bi, kanta ta ɗaga taga wani room an rubuta BUA INTERVIEW ROOM, rasa inda zata samu Hisham ya sakata sakin numfashi ta tura room ɗin wani mutumi ta samu zaune cikin office ɗin sallama tayi ya amsa.
    "I'm sorry, Hisham nake nema."
  Murmushi ya saki tare da tashi yaje ya rufe room ɗin yana murmushi ya juyo tare da doso Jidda yana cewa.
    "Wow she is beautiful, ƴan ba sai kin sayarwa Hisham ba, idan aiki kike nema kawai ki bani hadin kai kin samu just 11minute ma is okay.''
  Yayi Maganar yana matsota Jidda ganin da gaske neman rungumeta yake ya sata yin baya cikin tashin hankali, take baya tana cewa.
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Auzubillah! Allah ya tsare ni daga sharrinka Hisham fa nake nema dan Allah ka buɗe min ƙofa na fita ni ba Yar iska bane ka matsa min na shige."
   Dariya ya sanya yace.
     "Gaki kamar Mace mai wayo ashe wawiya ce, just 11minute fa kawai ya isa."
  


  Hisham jin Jidda shuru bata shigo ba ya sashi kiran Nameer yana tambayar sa ina jidda cike da mamaki Nameer yace ai tun ɗazu security ta kawo ta, ijiye wayar Hisham yayi cike da mamaki ya fito, room ɗin da yake cikin yana kallon wanda Jidda ta shiga, har zai shige sai ya jiyo tamkar Muryar mace tana kuka cikin room ɗin khabeer alkanawi, bakin room ɗin yaje ya tsaya tare da sanya hannu yayi nocking jin nocking yasa khabeer saurin juyawa sai kuma ya juyo ya kalli Jidda da har ya ɓarka mata rigarta, a zatonsa security ne daga inda yake tsaye yace.
    "Waye ne.?"
"Hisham."
Amon Muryar Hisham ta doki kunnen sa, cikin ɗaga murya a gigice Jidda tana kuka tace.
    "Hisham! Ka taimake ni! Zai keta min mutunci na."
    Cike da mamaki HISHAM ya toshe bakinsa tare da furta.
    "What Jidda, khabeer buɗe ƙofar nan."
  Bakinsa khabeer ya toshe tare da ɗaura hannunsa a ka, cikin mungun tsoro, da gudu Jidda ta buɗe ƙofar, har tana bige Hisham wajen fitowa ta ƙanƙame jikinta sakamakon yagewar da rigarta yayi, kallon Jidda Hisham yayi tare da juyawa ya kalli khabeer, ƙafarsa yasa zai shiga room ɗin sai kuma ya tsaya cikin zuciyarsa ya furta.
     _me zaisa na taimaka mata, to wai ma meya aikata mata anya kuwa ba fyaɗe ya mata ba._
  Yayi Maganar yana juyawa ya kalli Jidda rigarta daya yayyage ya ke kallo rabin ƙirjinta duk a waje idanunsa ya runtse tare da mai da kansa gefe, Rigarsa ta sama ya cire tare da miƙa mata ta amsa cikin sauri, da wani irin mungun kallo yake bin khabeer idan akwai abinda ya tsana a rayuwarsa shine yaga ana ketawa mace haddi.............

*_Page 37 to 38_*





Idanunsa ya kuma runtsewa tare da ware su akan khabeer da yayi tsuru_tsuru yana kallon sa a mungun fusace Hisham yayi kan khabeer tare da shaƙo wuyansa wani irin naushi ya kaimasa a cikinsa tare da jidesa da ƙafarsa ya faɗi warwas a ƙasa, ƙara kai masa naushi yayi a bakinsa, wani irin ƙara khabeer ya danna tare da yunƙurowa zai tashi Hisham yasa ƙafa ya kwashesa, yayin da Jidda ta ke gefe takure hawaye na cigaba da biyo idanunta tabbas tayi danasanin zuwa wannan ma'aikatar da ace tasan haka zata faru da ita da babu abinda zai kawota, shi kuwa HISHAM ya dage sai dukan khabeer yake, ya gagara ramawa domin kuwa yasan yana wani yunkuri zaiyi asarar aikinsa, jin ihunsa yasa ma'aikatan wajen zuwa suka taru a kansa, ƙoƙarin ƙwacesa suke amma sun gagara, da ƙyar suka kwacesa daga hannun Hisham, wani irin huci Hisham yake tamkar mayunwacin zaki, Nameer da Basmah a gigice suka taho wajen sanda security suka sanar dashi, Gaban Hisham ya tsaya tare da cewa.
    "Hisham meyake faruwa, da girman ka Hisham kasa hannu a jikin ma'aikacin ka why.?"
  Idanunsa da suka kaɗa sukayi jawur Hisham ya ɗaga tare da nuna masa JIDDA da hannun sa gabaki ɗaya juyawa sukayi suka kalli Jidda itama idanunta sunyi jajur da sauri Basmah taje ta riketa tana cewa.
    "Innalillahi! Jidda meya sameki, ke da..........
  Bata ƙarisa maganar ba idanunta suka sauƙa akan rigarta da ya yage sai yanzu ta kula da rigar Hisham dake jikinta, a fusace Basmah tazo ta tsaya a gaban Hisham.
   "Me kuka aikata mata, me ka mata Hisham, dama kun kirata da sunan bata aiki ne saboda ku wulaƙanta ta, macece fa ita, Meyasa baka yafiya ne Hisham."
   "Ya isheki haka, ba dai Hisham ya aikata wannan mungun aikin ba, duk tsiyata ban taɓa tunanin ketawa Mace haddinta ba, ki juya ki yiwa wannan ɗan bunsurun wannan tambayar da kike min."
   Hisham yayi Maganar cikin tsawa yana ɗaukar wayarsa Police ya kira yace suzo su tafi da khabeer Idanunsa shima Nameer ya runtse wani irin ɗaci da baƙin kishi ne yaji ya sarƙafe masa wuya, a fusace idanunsa a rufe yasa hannu ya finciko khabeer da ƙarfi tare da haɗasa da jikin bango, take goshin khabeer ya fashe jini ya biyo fuskarsa, wuyansa ya kuma shaƙowa wani irin gurnani khabeer keyi idanunsa duk sun firfito waje alamun wahala, shi kuwa Hisham kansa ya dafe tare da neman waje ya zauna yana jin zuciyarsa na hura, yama kasa yiwa NAMEER magana, Basmah tana ƙanƙame da Jidda duk hankalinsu na tashe, ƙarar motar Police da sukaji shiya ƙara ɗagawa Khabeer hankali, duk alokacin yana jin a ransa cewa kashesa Nameer zaiyi, jin motar Police yasa Hisham tashi da sauri ya wani irin kwashe khabeer rigib yayi a ƙasa, ƙafarsa Hisham yasa ya taka ƙafar khabeer da ƙarfin gaske ji kake ƙafar tayi ƙas, ya ɗaga ƙafarsa zai kuma takesa Police ya yi saurin rike Hisham ƙoƙarin ɗaga khabeer sukayi ya tsaya da ƙafarsa abu ya gagara, hakan ya tabbatar da cewa Hisham ya karya khabeer, Police ɗin ne yace.
    "What Hisham ka karyasa fa, duba kaga wannan ƙafar tasa bata tsayuwa."
   "karaya huɗu naso yayi ba ɗaya ba, duka hannayensa da ƙafofin naso su karye, ɗan matsiyaciya bunsurun banza! Wannan tsinanne ne la'anannen Ubangiji, ku fice da shege daga wajen nan, dan uwarsa an faɗa maka cewa nan Companyn tsinannune irinka kwarton banza kaji kunya kuma kayi asara, ɗan shegiya, tauren banza."
   Hisham yayi Maganar cikin tsawa, dubansa Police ɗin yayi yace.
   "Kabi a hankali mana Hisham meyasa kai ka fiye zafin zuciya ne, komai sannu muke binsa dole sai mun san abinda ke faruwa kafin mu tafi dashi."
   "Ba zafin rai kawai nake da shi ba harda zafin hannu, bayan zafin hannu ma harda na ƙashi na rantse da Allah idan baku fita dashi ba zan illata sa yadda ko likitocin duniyar nan zasu taro a kansa bazasu iya gyarasa ba, dan haka ku fice da tsinanne, tunda ba Companyn ubansa bane."
   Hisham ya kuma maganar yana huci, Nameer ne ya girgiza kansa yace.
   "Sargent Bala ku shige dashi can office ɗin naku zan biyo bayan ku, sannan karku sassauta masa fyaɗe yaso aikata mana a cikin Company juya kaga yadda yaci zarafinta."
  Kansa Sargent Bala ya kaɗa tare da juyawa ya kalli Jidda keyar khabeer ya kaɗa sukayi gaba, Police ɗin suna fita Hisham ya dubi ma'aikatan dake tsaye a wajen da yawa idanunsu yana kan Jidda baiyi magana ba yaja hannun Jidda ya shige da ita room ɗin daya fito yana huci, tunda Hisham ya riƙe mata hannu Jidda taji tsikar jikinta ya zuba wani abu ya taso mata daga ƙafarta har cikin ƙwaƙwalwarta, bai jira su Nameer sun shigo ba ya rufe ƙofar da ƙarfi idanunsa sunyi wani irin kaɗawa har abun tsoro idan ya ɗago ya kalleki.
    "Ki faɗa min, ya miki fyaɗe ne karki ɓoye min meya miki."?
   Kanta Jidda ta ɗago hawaye na tsiyaya ta kallesa kuka ne yaci ƙarfinta madadin ta masa magana sai ta fashe da kuka, kansa Hisham ya dafe yana cije bakinsa, ya bigi teburin gabanta.
   "Oh my god, hmmm! Ya lalata miki rayuwa ko, ki faɗa min gaskiya mana, ya miki fyade ko."?
   Ita dai Jidda taƙi magana sai kukanta take ta fama, cike da jin haushi ya daki bango gefensa cikin ɓacin rai ya furta.
    "Kuka! Kuka! Kuka! Wannan wanne irin munafurci ne, uban meye ya kaiki office ɗinsa nan aka kiraki ne, kin sani a gaba sai kukan munafurci kike min, bayan ya gama lalata rayuwarki duk macen da ta rasa budurcinta to ki sani ta rasa kimarta da mutuncinta har ma da kimar iyayenta, gashi kema kin shiga wannan jerin waɗannan Mutanen, khabeer ya hallaka miki rayuwa ya cuc.............
   "Ya isa haka Hisham! Ka barni da abinda ya dameni, ka barni nayi kukana a duk sanda na haɗa hanya da kai masifa nake gani duk da kayi ƙoƙarin ƙwatata a hannun wannan azzalumin, amma babu alkairi a tarayyata da kai Hisham, bana bukatar aikin na haƙura zanje na riƙe talaucina Allah bazai hanani yadda zan kula da ahalina ba, da kake kiran fyaɗe! Fyaɗe! Waye yace maka ya rabani da darajata, kana ina akayi da zaka yanke hukunci bisa son ranka ko ka yarda ko kar ka yarda ni ban rasa mutunci na ba, babu abinda ya min Allah ya kwace ni a hannunsa, akwai CCTV Camera kaje ka duba ka gani idan yamin fyaɗe, ka daina yanke hukunci da son ranka ka buɗe min ƙofa nabar wannan Companyn."
   Iska ya fesar daga bakinsa yace.
   "Babu Alkairi a tare da ni kakace, Hmm! Ƙarya kike duk inda na shige alkairi ne ke bin bayana, ki sani babu sharri a tare dani, da kuma akwai sharri a tare dani da banzo na ceceki ba kije ki kwanta kiyi tunani, kije mun ɗaukeki aiki."
  Hisham yayi Maganar a zuciye yana ɗaukar takaddun ta ya fita dasu, numfashi Jidda ta saki hawaye na biyo idanunta.


  Nameer ganin Hisham ya buɗe ƙofar ya fito a fusace ya sashi shiga da sauri Basmah na biye dashi, Jidda Basmah ta riƙe har yanzu itama ranta na ɓace, Nameer ne yace.
   "Kiyi haƙuri Jidda nasan Khabeer bai miki fyaɗe ba, ya dai kuskureki, amma na miki alkawari sai ya gane kuskuren sa, muje na kaiki gida da kaina."
  Kanta Jidda ta girgiza tana jan majina tare da sanya hannu ta share hawayen ta, tace.
    "Bazan koma gida a haka ba Umma bazata ji daɗi ba, zanje kawai gidan su Basmah na sauya kaya."
   "Okay to muje sai na sauƙeki a gidan su Basmah."
  Kanta ta girgiza tare da tashi suka fita.


 Hisham tunda ya fita daga interview room ɗin motarsa ya shiga tare da wurga takaddun Jidda a bayan motar, darect Joint ya nufa, baiyi wani parking ɗin arziki ba ya fito, ko motar bai rufe ba, sai security ne ya taho da sauri ya rufe masa motar, teburin da babu kowa ya samu ya zauna tare da runtse idanunsa, abubuwan dake faruwa ne suke dawo masa a cikin idanunsa.
     _Aduk sanda na haɗa hanya da kai masifa nake gani, babu Alkairi tarayyata da kai_
   Da ƙarfi Hisham ya furta.
    "Kaga ƴar shegiya, bazaki san tsakanina da ke babu Alkairi ba sai sanda na karya miki zuciya a ranar zaki tabbatar da haɗuwata da ke masifa ce, zan tabbatar miki da wannan furucin naki sai na sanyaki cikin masifar da bakiyi zato ba, tabbas zan ɗaukeki aiki domin shine damata da zan jawoki jikina."
  Yayi Maganar yana zarar sigari, leta ya ɗauka a gefen table ya kunna sigarin, zuƙa yayi tare da lumshe idanunsa, sai kuma ya saki murmushi.
     "The game is ready, wasan yanzu za'a fara."
  Hisham yayi Maganar bakinsa ɗauke da murmushi, Sadiq ya dannawa kira 4 miss Call bai ɗaga ba ya juma sosai yana shan sigarinsa ko ruwa bai sha ba a wajen kafin ya tafi.



************


Tsakiyar dare wajajen 2 na dare idanun Jidda suka buɗe bacci ya gagareta zullumi da tsoron duniya suka shiga zuciyarta rayuwa ta lalace mutane duk sun zamo marassa imani, an maida fyaɗe ruwan dare, ko dan saboda ba'a hukunta masu aikata wannan laifin ne shiyasa laifin ya yawaita an maida mutunci mace ba komai ba, numfashi Jidda ta saki tana tuno abinda ya faru, _yanzu da Hisham baizo ya ceceni ba da yaya kenan da yanzu na rasa mutunci na_ tayi Maganar zucin tana sakin numfashi tare da furta.
    "Duk munin halin Hisham ya taimakeni domin kuwa da badan shi ba da yanzu rayuwata tana cikin tasku, lallai ya taimakeni, ashe dama Hisham zai iya taimakon wani, lallai yadda na ɗaukesa ba haka yake ba, meyasa ma na faɗa masa magana marar daɗi bayan shine ya taimake ni, bai dace na kirasa da masifa, wannan kalmar bata dace ga mutumin daya taimaki rayuwar ka ba, nayi kuskuren faɗa masa wannan kalmar ya zamo lallai na basa hakuri, mtsss! To amma Hisham yana da jiji da kai da isa muddun na basa haƙuri to zai rainani ne, amma ai durƙusawa wada ba gajiyawa bane, kai ina anya zan iya bawa Hisham haƙuri kuwa."?
  Ta yiwa kanta tambayar tare da yin shuru tana nazari ringing ɗin wayarta ta ji, duban wayar tayi taga bakuwar numbern hannun ta sa ta ɗauki wayar tare da cewa.
    "Hello da wa nake magana."?
    Daga can taji ance.
 "Hisham."
        "Hisham kuma."
Jidda ta furta cike da mamakin ina ya samu numbern ta.
    "Eh Hisham kinyi mamaki ne.?"
    "Ƙwarai kuwa gani nayi babu haɗin danga da garahunu."
  Daga can Hisham yayi murmushi tare da cewa.
    "Ayya to ai muddun ɓera zai shiga turken mage ki sani dole wannan magen tazo neman wannan ɓeran wataƙila sata ɓeran yayi kinsan ba'a raba ɓera da sata."
    "Au ni ce ma ɓerar, amma dai kaci mutunci na, ni zaka kira da ɓera, me na sata a Companyn naku Banda bala'in da na gani."
   Dariya sosai Hisham yayi kafin yace.
   "Ayya Malama ɓera meye na jin haushi saboda na kiraki ɓera, shifa ɓarawo dole ne ya amsa sunan ɓera."
   Cike da hasala JIDDA tace.
    "Malam wai me kake nufi da ni ne, ka kirani tsakar dare kana neman faɗa min baƙar magana, idan Mutum ɗan mutunci ne zai kira Mace cikin wannan daren ne."
   Dariya ya kuma mata har tana jiyo sautin dariyar tasa yace.
    "Haka kuma idan Macen yar mutunci ne ko da an kirata tsakiyar dare bazata ɗaga ba, kinga kuwa marar mutunci ne ya haɗu da ƴar Uwarsa marar mutunci."
   Kanta Jidda ta riƙe cike da jin haushi taja doguwar tsuka tare da katse kiran tana furta.
    "Banza."

*_Page 39 to 40_*




*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻



Tunda Hisham ya amshi takaddun Jidda kusan 14days babu wani sako sai dai abinda ya bawa Jidda mamaki yadda lokaci ɗaya shaƙuwa ta shiga tsakanin Umma da Hisham domin kuwa a kai akai yake zuwa gidan nasu ko Nameer bai shaƙu da Umma ba yadda Hisham ya shaƙu da ita kayan Abinci da wasu abubuwan buƙata na gida haka Hisham ke kawo wa su Umma, harta Basmah yanzu mutunci ne sosai ke tsakanin ta da Hisham yanda Hisham ke yiwa Umma biyayya kamar mutumin kirki ke kyace itace ta haifesa, maganar Khabeer kuwa tuni su Nameer suka Kaisa kotu, zama ɗaya aka yankewa Khabeer shekara bakwai a gidan yari na yunkurin fyaɗe da ya yiwa Jidda da taran Naira dubu ɗari wannan abu ba ƙaramin daɗi ya yiwa Jidda ba, sai dai har yanzu akwai guntun faɗa tsakanin Jidda da Hisham, Soyayyar Jidda kuwa ko yaushe ninkuwa yake a zuciyar Nameer yayin da ya kasa sanar da ita ko nuna mata alamun yana sonta.
      


 Yau Jidda tana zaune ita da Basmah tace wa Basmah.
     "Basmah wai yaushe ne zaki koma gidan ku, kinga tunda aka yiwa Abba aiki suka dawo gida ya kamata ace kin koma, nasan Mama idanu kawai ta zuba miki taga gudun ruwanki bazata ji daɗi ba."
    "Au korata kike wai Jidda."
    "Ko ɗaya Basmah gidanku gidan mu ne kamar yadda kike kallon gidan mu a naku, nasan dai Mama bazata ji daɗi bane da zamanki anan dole zata buƙace ki."
   "To gobe INSHA ALLAH sai na koma."
  Da Allah ya kaimu Jidda ta amsa dai-dai Nameer na sallama a cikin gidan nasu fuskarta a sake Jidda ta amsa kafin ya shigo, kujera ƴar tsugunne Jidda ta basa ya zauna.
    "Sannu da zuwa Yaya Nameer."
  Cewar Jidda murmushi Nameer ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Yauwa sannu Jidda ke da Basmah ne.?"
   "Eh wallahi yaya Nameer, ya aikin jiya da daddare ai na kiraka sai Hisham ya ɗaga wayar yace baka kusa."
   "Eh tabbas bana kusa Basmah, ya akayi da wani labarin ne."
   Murmushi Basmah tayi tace.
   "A'a ba komai kawai dama gaisuwa ce, kuma na jika shuru kwana biyu shine Umma tace na kiraka naji ko lafiya."
   "Ayya Allah sarki Umma na, bana gari kwana biyu naje Abuja."
  Jidda ne tace.
      "Gashi kuma Umman bata nan taje unguwa."
   "Ba wajenta dama nazo ba, takaddun ki na dawo miki dasu, gobe zaki fara aiki, a ƙarƙashin Hisham, office ɗin Khabeer da ya bari wannan gurbin muka baki kece zaki tace ma'aikata na fannin goge shinkafa, ina fatan zaki iya aikin."?
  Numfashi Jidda ta saki tare da cewa.
    "Zan iya mana sai dai banso ace an ajeni a ƙarƙashin Hisham ba."
    "Shine yake da alhakin tace ma'aikata, kuma shine ya baki wannan gurbin bani da ikon da zance masa meyasa domin aikinsa da ya juma yana yi."
  Basmah cewa tayi.
"Ke dai kawai Jidda kice Allah yasa hakan shi yafi alkairi."
    "Wannan shine gaskiya Basmah."
  Cewar Nameer, kanta Jidda ta girgiza tace.
   "To Allah yasa hakan yafi alkairi, jiya Abdu ya dawo yace yaje zai biya kudin makaranta sai suka ce masa, ai an biya masa wani yaje last week ya biya, nasan kaine ka biya kudin."
   Cike da mamaki Nameer yace.
     "A'a gaskiya bani bane infact ma bansan lokacin biyan school payment ɗin nasu yayi ba, to amma waye ne zai biya masa har 39k.?"
   Cikin mamaki sosai Jidda tace.
   "Ba kai bane to waye ne ikon Allah."
   "Sai Allah amma dai ko waye ne wata rana zai bayyana, ni bara naje muna da meeting yanzu."
   "To shikenan mun gode yaya Nameer gobe insha Allah zan fito da wuri."
   "To Allah ya kaimu kya gaishe da Umman idan ta dawo."
   Yayi Maganar yana fita, Jidda cike da mamaki ta dubi Basmah tace.
    "To waye ya biyawa Abdu kuɗin makaranta.?"
  Shuru Basmah tayi tana tunani kafin tace.
   "Anya ba Hisham bane, kin fa san yanzu ya sauya, daga yadda kika sanshi."
   "Gaskiya ne ya sauya tun sanda ya taimake ni lokacin da khabeer yaso keta min mutunci na, daga ƙwayar idanunsa yadda ransa ya ɓaci na tabbatar da ya sauya, da Hisham yazo har gidan nan yace nayi haƙuri da abinda yamin na yarda ya sauya sai dai ba'a yabon ɗan kuturu sai ya shekara goma da yatsa."
    "Hakane yanzu ni bara naje nayi wanka sai nazo muje gidan ko."
   "To shikenan sai kin fito."
   Tayi Maganar tana jingina da jikin bango.




**********



"Wai Hisham ya kuke ciki ne da Jidda har yanzu zuciyarta bata fara yarda da kai bane, ya kamata zuwa yanzu ta fara yarda da kai, ka sani alƙawarin ku da Nameer na fitar da matar Aure sati biyu ya rage karka yadda ka kasa cika alkawari."?
     "Sadiq komai taurin zuciyar ta na tabbata zuwa yanzu tana jina a ranta kasan meye dalilina."?
  Kansa Sadiq ya girgiza yace.
    "No sai ka faɗa."
"Dalilina shine komai bushewar zuciyar ta, tunda har na sunkuya a gabanta da mahaifiyarta na bata hakuri dole jikinta yayi sanyi, domin kuwa tasan abune mawuyaci wanda zaisa ni Hisham na bata hakuri, sai kuma Khabeer da ya saukaka min aiki, domin kuwa cetonta da nayi a hannunsa wannan kaɗai ya isa ta yarda da ni, uwa uba kuma tallafawa uwarta da nakeyi da wannan nakasasshen ƙanin nata, abu ɗaya ya rage min kuma shima sa ido kayi kallo, na tabbata nan da sati ɗaya zan bijiro mata da soyayyata kuma ina tabbatar maka ko shawarar wani bazata nema ba zata amince sani, ina tabbatar maka sai nasa rayuwarta a cikin taskun masifar da bata taɓa zato ba."
   "Shege abokina gaskiya ƙwaƙwalwarka naja, kayi aiki mai kyau wasa yazo tsakiya."
Cewar Sadiq, sai kuma yace.
    "Hisham ni wai Company nawa kuke dashi ne."?
   Ɗago idanunsa Hisham yayi cikin mamaki domin kuwa ta yaya Sadiq zai masa wannan tambayar akan kadarorinsu kafin yace.
   "Meyasa kamin wannan tambayar Sadiq, me hakan zai amfanar dakai."
   Murmushi Sadiq ya saki _wato Hisham ya zargi wani abu a ransa kenan, ina bazaka taɓa gane santsin da nake ƙoƙarin yarfawa ƙafarka ba sai randa ka buɗi idanunka ka ganka a ƙasa warwas a ranar zaka fahimci kai mahaukacin kare ne_ Sadiq yayi Maganar cikin zuciyarsa kafin yace.
   "Me kuwa zai amfaneni dashi Hisham, jiya ne ina zaune a wani Joint ni da Abba naji wani yana cewa, kuna da Company biyar a ƙasar Rasha, to shine abin ya bani mamaki domin ni bansan kuna da Company a Rasha ba, yadda nake da kusanci da kai ya kamata ace na sani, amma kuma sai naga kamar tambayar bata maka daɗi ba."
   Murmushi Hisham yayi yace.
    "Bawai banji daɗi bane abunne ya bani mamaki da ka min wannan tambayar domin kuwa a sanina bamu da Company a rasha, infact ma idan muna dashi,ai bai zamo lallai sai na sanar da kai ba komai kusancin dake tsakanin mu, Sadiq asirin ciki sai hanji sanin sirrin dukiyar mu, wannan tsakani na da ahalina ne, dan haka babu wannan layin tsakanina da kai Sadiq mu tsaya iya abotar mu."
   Numfashi Sadiq ya saki domin kuwa ba haka yaso ba, yaso ace ya bigi cikin Hisham amma ba komai lallai wata rana sai na sani kafin na aika ka lahira murmushi ya saki tare da cewa.
   "To shikenan Allah ya baka haƙuri, yanzu ya za'ayi ina son zan shige Abuja gobe."
   "Okay kana buƙatar kuɗi kenan, muje POS sai na baka, nima zan shige ne muna da  meeting."
   Tashi sukayi suka shiga mota, a POS ɗin suka tsaya Hisham ya cire 50k ya bawa Sadiq kafin ya saukesa a ƙofar gidan su ya shige Companyn nasu .




**********



Washegari da safe Nameer da Tutu ne kawai suke breakfast Ummi tana azumi bata fito daining ɗin ba, shima Hisham da azumi ya tashi, yana can bedroom ɗin su bai fito ba.
    "Yaya Nameer kullum ji nake tamkar na jawo sati huɗin da kuka yi alkawarin fitar da mata kai da yaya Hisham, gashi har mun cinye sati biyu."
   Spoon ɗin da ke hannun Nameer ya ijiye tare da xubawa Tutu ido yace.
   "Ban fahimta ba, meyasa kikeji tamkar ki jawo wannan watan yazo da wuri.?"
   "Hmmm! Saboda a wannan lokacin ne zaka fitar dani a matsayin matar da zaka Aura, duniya zata san Soyayyar dake tsakanina da kai, ina kwadayin wannan ranar shiyasa nake gaggawar tazo ta, i love You so much Yaya Nameer, bansan Irin Soyayyar da nake maka ba, ina burin ganina a ƙarƙashin inuwar aurenka."
  Numfashi Nameer ya saki, tare da runtse idanunsa.
    _Ta kasa fahimtar bata ra'ayina, ta kasa gane bana jinta a raina yanzu, ya kamata zuwa yanzu ta fahimci haka ba sai na faɗa mata da bakina ba, Jidda itace zaɓina itace muradina, kuma ya zamo wajibi na aureta komai rintsi komai wahala sai na mallaketa kiyi haƙuri Tutu bazan iya aurenki ba, bazan yiwa Soyayyar Jidda kishiya ba, itace muradina ba ke ba._
  Nameer yayi Maganar zucin idanunsa na runtse, plat ɗin abincin ya ture daga gabansa tare da miƙewa zai bar wajen ba tare da ya yiwa Tutu magana ba, kallon sa tayi tace.
    "Ya ka tashi baka ce komai ba, ko akwai wata matsala ne.?"
  Murmushi Nameer yayi yace.
   "Matsala kuma Tutu, Allah ya rabamu da ita, lokacin aiki ne yayi, Allah ya nuna mana lokacin."
  Yayi Maganar a takaice yana barin wajen numfashi Tutu ta saki tana sakin murmushi, ko kaɗan bata fahimci Nameer baya sonta ba.

*_Page 41 to 42_*




*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻






Hisham ya riga Nameer fita Company, ko da yaje ya samu tuni har Jidda ta isa, a bakin office ɗinsa ya tarar da ita zaune murmushi Hisham ya saki ko kaɗan Jidda bata kula da shigowar sa wajen ba, sai ji tayi mutum ya sunkuyo kanta har tana jiyo hucin numfashin sa idanunta ta runtse tsikar jikinta ya zuba, ƙamshin turarensa ne ya tabbatar mata da Hisham ne, ganin ta runtse idanunta yasa Hisham sakin murmushi yana ƙarewa ƙaramin bakinta kallo, murmushi ya saki tare da cewa.
     "ke meye na runtse idon ko ance miki sunbatarki zanyi dallah Malama buɗe idanunki."
   Jidda da idanunta ke runtse ne tace.
    "Ka matsa kusa dani malam akan me to zaka ranƙwafo kaina kamar wanda zai bani magani."
  Kansa Hisham ya ɗaga har yanzu fuskarsa ɗauke da murmushi ya zauna a gefen ta, mutane tsirarun da suke wajen ne suka bi Hisham da kallon mamaki domin kuwa tunda suke dashi basu taba ganin yayiwa wata murmushi ba ko ya tsaya kusa da wata sai yau Hisham duban Jidda yayi yana dariya yace.
    "Kinsan me kuwa Jidda, gaskiya ke  kyakkyawa ce babu ƙarya duk wanda yace miki mummuna sai dai maƙiyi, matsalar ki ɗaya."
   Hisham yayi Maganar yana taɓe bakinsa, dubansa Jidda tayi tare da sakin murmushi domin kuwa maganar tasa dariya ta bata."
   "Hmmm! Ikon Allah to meye kuma matsalar tawa yanzu ni ɗin har na fika matsala.?"
    "Ƙwarai kuwa kin fiye izza da ji da kai ga shegen taurin kai, sai dai fa ahakan ma kina burgeni."
  Ɗan zaro idanunta Jidda tayi cike da mamaki ta kalli Hisham.
    "Ni ce nake burgeka Hisham ni ɗin."?
   Jidda ta masa maganar cike da mamaki, dariya Hisham ya sanya har dimple ɗinsa na loɓawa yace.
   "Yeah meye Kuma abun mamaki just wanted you to impress me, so why should be surprised? or do you want me to tell you I love You."
  Zaro idanunta Sosai Jidda tayi tana mamakin anya Hisham ne, kafin tace.
   "To ikon Allah gani nayi kamar ba Hisham ɗin da na sani ba, dole nayi mamaki, cewa kayi fa ina burgeki, so Hisham.........
   "Ya isa haka Jidda karki ce komai tabbas rashin jituwa ya faru tsakanin mu a baya amma hakan bashi yake nufin bani da hali mai kyau ba, zan ƙara baki haƙuri a karo na biyu Jidda be patient and forget what happened in the past and take that Hisham that you know now is not a new one."
  Wani irin sanyi taji jikinta yayi tunda ta ke da Hisham bai taɓa burgeta ba irin na yau lallai yau ta yarda da maganar Nameer Hisham mutumin kirki ne mune bamu fahimce sa yadda ya kamata ba, murmushi Jidda ta saki tace.
    "Hisham ko Ubangijin da ya hallice mu muna masa laifi kuma ya yafe mana to meyasa mu bazamu yafewa junanmu ba, Hisham daga yau na manta da abinda ya faru a baya, a yanzu na ɗaukeka sabon abokina, komai ya wuce."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki cikin zuciyarsa ya furta _lallai ko da kin manta abinda ya faru a baya, to tabbas tarihi zai maimaita kansa da kaina zan tuna miki da wannan mantuwar da kikayi yayin da zaki tafi da ciwon zuciya na bakin ciki, wata kila ma hakan ya zamo ajalinki wannan alƙawarin na ne._ kafin yayi murmushi yace.
   " Naji daɗi da ya kasance kin manta komai Jidda, tabbas kin samu sabon abokin da zai tare miki wani bangon, zamu ciza kuma zamu hura tare barka da samun sabon aboki."
   Dariya Jidda tayi kafin kuma ta ɗan haɗa ranta cikin zolaya tace.
   "Amma tsaya Malam na tambayeka, ta yaya zaka iya abota da mai izza, jiji da kai, shegen taurin kai, ta yaya zaka iya answer me idan har kana son nayi aiki a Companyn nan yau."?
   Dariya sosai Hisham yayi, kafin yace.
   "Yess! Shiyasa nace kina burgeni ai, taken mace kenan izza da ji dakai wannan na mace mai aji ne, taurin kai ɗin ne dai Hisham zai sauya miki shi."
  Murmushi Jidda tayi ta girgiza kanta ganin yau wai Hisham ke dariya abun kamar almara, bakinta ta taɓe tare da mikewa tsaye tana cewa.
   "Kafin Hisham ya gyara Jidda sau ɗaya na tabbata ita ta gyarasa sau goma, muje ka kaini office ɗina na fara sanin abubuwan da suke gabana."
   Murmushi yayi tare da cewa.
   "Dole dai sai kin fara shiga office ɗina na gama sanar dake ayyukan ki sannan na haɗaki da wanda zasu nuna miki yadda aikin mu yake tafiya dole sai kin zagaya Kinga yadda muke harkokin mu, dan haka muje office."
    "Okay."
Jidda tayi maganar tana tashi, tare suka shiga office ɗin, suna zance kamar abokan da sukayi shekara da sabawa.


 Nameer bai shigo office da wuri ba har wajen 12:30 tunda yazo ya shiga office ɗin sa bai fito ba, tare da sanar da sakatariyar sa duk wanda yazo neman sa tace bazai ga kowa ba yau, shuru yayi cike da tunani da zullumin yanda zaiyi ya rabu da Tutu, idanunsa ya runtse tare da miƙewa tsaye kaiwa da komowa yake a office ɗin sa duk damuwa ta ishesa. _meyasa Kan Tutu baya ja ne, meyasa bazata fahimci baya ra'ayinta ba, duk wannan kuskure na ne da na fara sonki Tutu, Hmm Jidda itace muradina, ina sonta fiye da komai a rayuwata, meyasa na kasa sanar da ke soyayyata Jidda ya kamata ki fahimci ina sonki fiye da yadda uwa ta ke son ɗanta, kece alƙibilata Jidda, I love you so much, i love You so much Jidda, Hmmm! Mtsss ya zamo wajibi na bayyana miki soyayyata idan ba haka ba ciwon sonki zai illata zuciyata, yaya zanyi da soyayyar Tutu ya zanyi na rabu da ita cikin sauƙi, Hmm! Lallai ya zamo wajibi ki cire Soyayyata a ranki a baya Tabbas na soki amma a yanzu babu ɗigon sonki a zuciyata, na yadda da kalmar hausawa da suke cewa so tsuntsu ne yakan tashi daga wannan bishiyar ya koma wata._ dafe kansa yayi tare da neman waje ya zauna, wayarsa ce tayi ringing duban wayar yayi yaga Dear murmushi ya saki tare da ɗaukar wayar ya kara a kunnensa.
    "Hello Jidda."
Daga can Jidda tace.
    "Gani a bakin office ɗin ka, sakatariya ta hanani shigowa, kace mata wai baka bukatar ganin kowa yau meyasa.?"
   Numfashi ya saki yace.
       "Bawa Sakatariyar wayar."
   Miƙa mata wayar Jidda tayi ta amsa umarni ya bata da tabar Jidda ta shigo, bawa Jidda wayar tayi tace zata iya shiga shiga Jidda tayi da sallama, Nameer yana zaune ya dafe kansa kusan bakinta Uku tana sallama baiji ba ya lula duniyar tunani, zama Jidda tayi tare da buga teburin dake gabansa a ɗan razane Nameer ya ɗago kansa ganin Jidda ya sashi sakin numfashi tare da cewa.
   "Har kin shigo."?
"Eh tunanin me kake haka ina ta sallama bakaji ba, da alamu Dai yau kana cikin damuwa."?
   "No babu wata damuwar da nake da, kawai yau stress ɗina ne haka, ya akayi.?"
   "Wannan file ɗin ne Hisham yace na kawo maka ka duba."
  Amsa Nameer yayi ya buɗe file ɗin, kafin yace.
   "Zanyi aiki a kansa kafin na dawo miki dashi sai ki nemi mai file ɗin idan ya dace da muyi aiki dashi."
   "Okay to shikenan bara na koma office."
  "Da wuri haka, tun bamuyi hira ba, dama fa neman ki nake akwai Magana mai mahimmanci da nake son mu tattauna, amma yanzu nasan lokacin aiki ne, mayi maganar wani lokacin."
   "To shikenan ba damuwa bara naje ni."
  Kansa Nameer ya haɗa kafin Jidda ta fita.





**********


Inda suka saba haɗuwa yauma dai nan suka tsaya, Tunde ne ya dubi Sadiq yace.
   "Mama tace har yanzu taji shuru babu wani motsi, babu wani harin da ka aikawa Hisham meyasa."?
   Murmushi Sadiq ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Ka ce mata yaƙin sunƙuru nake bana bukatar kai hari cikin gaggawa, ka sanar da ita cewa ta yadda dani ne yasa ta bani wannan aikin dan haka ta saka ido kawai tayi kallo, ta yaya kuke tunanin na kai masa hari, wato na fargar dashi cewa ana neman rayuwarsu ne, ina bazan taɓa barin ya fahimci hakan ba nafi bukatar na kaisa ƙasa kafin ya ankare."

Dariya Tunde ya saka kafin yace.
   "Hakane amma kasan bana bukatar ɓata lokaci, kusan kullum sai na faɗa maka wannan kalmar, kawai ka aika masa makamanka."
   "Okay tunda haka kuke bukata kun matsa zan jaraba, amma duk abinda ya biyo baya ku kuka da kanku domin kuwa ni zan iya zillewa, zan saka aiki tunda naji next week ne zaka fara aiki, zan nemo guba, da za'a sawa Nameer a jus ko copee ta kansa zamu fara, zan sanar da kai yadda aikin zai kasance, guba ce mai haɗarin gaske dole sai ka kula."
   "Takan Hisham ya kamata mu fara ba Nameer ba domin kuwa nameer bashi da gadon IYABO Hisham shine jininsa dan haka shine zai zamo abin harin mu na farko da jinin Iyabo ya kamata mu fara yaƙi ba ɗan riƙo ba."
  Tunde yayi Maganar cikin rashin gamsuwa da maganar Sadiq tsawa Sadiq ya dokawa Tunde tare da cewa.
   "Karka kuma bani umarni domin kuwa ba sayata kukayi ba, idan har ka kuma bani umarni to kuwa zan ijiye muku aikin ku sannan kuma na zamo Mutum na farko da zai tarwatsa aniyarku, kunsan waye Sadiq yana da kyau ku kula, da ƙwaƙwalwata zanyi aiki ba da daƙiƙiyar ƙwaƙwalwarka ba, zan kama gabana yanzu idan kaje ka sanar da tsohuwar kakar taka yadda mukayi yanzu, tabbas Nameer shine first target ɗin mu."
  Sadiq yayi Maganar yana buɗe murfin motar da sauri ya fice ya tokare fuskarsa da kula posingcap, numfashi Tunde ya saki tare da jan motar ya nufi gida, Wamimo ya tarar a falon nasu babu Mamabisola zama yayi gefen kujera mai biyu cikin harshen yarbanci su gaisa da Wamimo kafin yace.
   "Mo ni Mama?"
Ya tambayi Wamimo cikin harshen yaruba, kitchen Wamimo ta nuna masa tace.
    "O wa ni ibi idana ounjẹ bayi nduro fun ọ"
   "Okay bara naje na sameta kawai."
Tunde yayi Maganar yana tashi ya shiga kitchen ɗin tana wajen gas tana tafasa ruwan Lipton, Tunde ya tsaya gefenta ransa a haɗe.
  "Na ga ka haɗa ranka meye Sadiq ɗin ya maka.?"
    "Babu abinda yamin Mama sai dai da alamu bamu samu abokin aiki ba, domin kuwa baya amsar umarni daga garemu wanda a wannan tsarin mune muka ɗaukesa aiki kuma ya zamo wajibi ya ɗauki umarninmu."
  "Ban tari numfashinka ba Tunde, amma ka sani aikin kai da kai muke da Sadiq bani gishiri na baka Manda ne dan haka nemosa mukayi akan gaɓar da ta dace, ƙwaƙwalwarsa na ja sosai dan haka muje dashi a yadda yake so mu bukatar mu burin mu ya cika, da zarar burinmu ya cika to ƙofar rago zamu masa."
   "Okay sai yanzu na fahimci karatun, yace da Nameer ya kamata a fara bansan dalilinsa na hakan ba, amma koma menene ke na fahimce ki."
   "Tunda ka fahimce, kaje kawai mu barsa da ƙudirinsa."
   "Shikenan Mama ko part ɗina ban shige ba nazo nan bara naje."
  Tunde yayi Maganar yana sakin murmushi, itama Mamabisola murmushin tayi kafin Tunde ya bar kitchen ɗin.




***********

Jidda ganin ta tashi aiki da wuri ya sata biyawa gidan su Basmah, dan ta sanar da ita ranar zata koma gida, ta samu kuwa ta koma, Abban Basmah ta shiga ta gaisar kafin ta shige ɗakin Basmah tana zaune tayi shuru da alamu tunani take ga wayarta kuma a gefe ana kira bata lura ba zama Jidda tayi tare da dafa kafaɗar ta tace.
    "Ke tunanin me kike haka ga wayarki kuma majnunun naki yana kira."?
  Tsuka Basmah taja tana kallon wayar tata tare da jan doguwar tsuka cike da mamaki Jidda tace.
    "Basmah Ahmad ne fa yake kiranki kika ja tsuka lafiya ko kunyi faɗa ne."?
Kanta Basmah ta girgiza tare da cewa.
     "Faɗa kuma kema ai kin san bamu taɓa faɗa da Ahmad ba, kawai dai wayar ce yau bana son yi har kun tashi daga wajen aikin ne."?
   Kanta Jidda ta ɗagawa Basmah tace.
    "Eh na dawo 5:00pm suke tashi daga aiki ga ko wanne ma'aikaci."
   "Jidda ina son na ijiye aikina na jarida nazo na nemi aiki a Companyn ku nima, saboda bana jin daɗin aikin babu ke."
   Ɗan zaro idanunta Jidda tayi tace.
   "Basmah kinyi shaye-shaye ne da kike faɗar wannan maganar."
  "Jidda sai nayi shaye-shaye ne zanbar aiki a Aminci, just ce miki nayi na gaji bana son aiki dasu, ina bukatar sauyi, fine babu wani laifi tunda ba Companyn ubana bane da zan tabbata anan ke ko Companyn ubanane idan na gaji zan iya sauya waje bare wannan da bani da alaka ta kusa ko ta nesa dashi."
   "Amma ai komai yaya ne bai kamata ki bar aiki a ƙarƙashin Aminci ba, aikin jarida yana da matukar muhimmanci, ko nima ba'a son raina nabar aiki dasu ba, amma idan kina ganin hakan yafi miki shikenan sai ki fara tuntubar Nameer kiji."
  Numfashi Basmah ta saki tare da cewa.
     "Ba Nameer zan tuntuba ba, Hisham zan tuntuba saboda shi mutumin kirki ne, infact ma hankalina yafi kwanciya dashi."
    Dariya Jidda ta saki tace.
    "Au har Hisham ne yafi Nameer kirki, impossible kawai dai son ranki kike faɗa Malama."
  "Ke nifa Hisham burgeni yake wallahi, gayen yayi arayuwa kina ganin handsome guy, kinga kamar fa yadda Nameer ɗin ya faɗa ne a baya HISHAM mutumin kirki ne kawai rashin fahimta yasa muke masa kallon wani bahagon mutum amma ni yanzu na fahimci waye Hisham."
   Taɓe bakinta Jidda tayi tace.
   "Hmmm! Hakane nima na fahimci hakan amma duk da Hisham bazai kai Nameer kirki ba, ke Kinga ni wannan chapter ta isheni haka dama jikin Abba nazo gani, kuma tunda jikin da sauƙi tashi ki rakani nayi gida."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana tashi, tashi itama Basmah tayi Jidda ta shiga ta yiwa Mama sai da safe kafin Basmah ta rakata har waje.




**********


Da daddare Jidda tana zaune suna hira da Umma taji wayarta tayi ringing dubanta ta kai ga wayar taga Hisham, murmushi ta saki tare tashi ta koma ɗaki tana ɗaga kiran.
   "Hello."
Jidda tayi maganar tare da yin shuru daga can Hisham yace.
   "Hi ƙawata ya akayi kin koma gida lafiya."?
   Murmushi Jidda tayi tace.
   "Na koma lafiya, ya naka gajiyar aikin."?
  Murmushi Hisham yayi yace.
    "Gajiya kuma sai kace mace, no ni ban gaji ba, amma ina fatan kinji daɗin aiki da Companyn mu ko.?"
   "Eh to da dai da ne sai nace aiki da Hisham babu daɗi amma da Hisham ya sauya lokaci guda aiki dashi yafi komai daɗi, naji daɗin fara aiki a ƙarƙashin ka.''
   Ɗan dariya Hisham ya saki yace.
    "Masha Allah kice Hisham ya ciri tuta, kin san meye Jidda.?"
   "A'a sai ka faɗa."
Gauron Numfashi ya saki tare da ɗan hura iska a Speaker wayar, yace.
     "Ke macece ta musamman, kina abubuwa da yawa wanda ba ko wacce mace ta ke dashi ba, ke ta dabance da sauran mata, kina burgeni sosai Jidda, gaki da kyau ga ado ga diri ga kyawun sura ga ilimin addini dana zamani, bakina yayi kaɗan ya lissafo ingancinki shiyasa nace ke macece ta musamman Jidda."
    Murmushi Jidda ta saki sosai taji daɗin maganganun Hisham.
   "Duka wannan yabon ni na cancanci amin sai na ke ga kamar bani da wannan abubuwan da ka lissafo."
    "Bazaki fahimci hakan bane ke, amma ni na hango abinda ke baki hango ba, bara na barki haka dama na kira naji kin isa lafiya ne, ki gaishe da Umma."
   "Shikenan zataji na gode."
   "Okay bye."
Hisham yayi Maganar yana katse kiran murmushi Jidda ta saki tare da lumshe idanunta ta furta..  
    _Hmmm! Hisham kai mutum ne na musamman wanda ya zauna da kai shine kawai zai fahimci hakan._
  Tayi Maganar tana sakin numfashi tare da tashi ta dawo wajen Umma.
    "Ke dawa kike waya ne.?"
  Cewar Umma Jidda zatayi Magana yaro ya shigo yace.
   "Wai Jidda tazo inji Abdulra'uf."
   "Abdulra'uf kuma na masa meye jeka ce ina aiki bazan samu fitowa ba."?
 Umma ne tace.
     "Kai yaro kace tana zuwa."
  Da sauri Jidda ta kalli Umma cikin mamaki, Umma cewa tayi.
    "Kalleni da kyau ja'ira yo meye laifin Abdulra'uf da zaki dinga nuna masa ƙiyayya gani nayi wanda yace yana sonka ya fi maƙiyinka komai lalacewarsa, maza kije ki sauraresa baki san inda alkairinki yake ba, yaron arziki da yake mutunta naki ke kice zaki nuna masa ƙiyayya, wannan ba tarbiyya ta bace."
  Bakinta Jidda ta tura tace.
   "Ni fa Umma bana sonsa, dan haka banga dalilin da zaisa na dinga tsayuwa dashi ba muna ɓatawa juna lokaci, kawai Umma ke babu ruwanki a tsakanin mu."
  Abdu ne yace.
    "Amma fa Adda gani nayi Abdulra'uf bashi da laifi yana da sana'ar sa, kuma mutumin kirki ne, yana da cikakken ahali, ina ga wannan ake bukata a neman Aure."
  Harara Jidda ta watsawa Abdu tace.
   "Kai kuma wa ya saka bakinka a cikin wannan maganar, ko Maganar ne.?"
   "Ya fiki gaskiya ki daina masa tsawa, ki tashi kije kiransa maza."
  Miƙewa Jidda tayi ranta a ɓace ta yayiɓi Hijab ɗinta zata fita taji Umma tace.
   "Au saboda zaki wajen maƙiyinki shiyasa bazaki kimtsa ba, zaki fita haka to maza ki dawo ko ɗan powder ki goga a fuskar ki."
  "Kanta Jidda tasa ta fice ba tare da ta juyawa ba kanta Umma ta girgiza ta cewa Abdu.
   "Rabu da ita sai dai ta fita ko bata so, ni banga laifin yaron nan ba da tabi ta ɗaura masa tsana, yaron da tun yana ƙanƙaninsa ubansa ke tahowa dashi a hannu ƙofar gidan ga, nasabar sa duk an sani amma tsabar iya shege irin na jiddatul kair tabi ta ɗaura masa karan tsana, na rasa dalilin da yasa bata sauraron samari duk wanda yazo zance wajenta sai tasan yadda tayi ta koresa, ni wannan lamari harya fara bani tsoro, shuru kawai nayi ina kallonta ta mai sheni shasha bansan sonta shi Abdulra'uf yake ba."
    "Hmmm! Umma ni fa na rasa me yake damun Adda, ko fa zancen Aure bata so a mata."
  Abdu yayi Maganar yana kallon Umma.
   "Barni da ita zan daidaita ta ne, ka tashi kaje ka karɓo min kamu a wajen Iya."
  Amsawa da to Abdu yayi yana tashi ya fita.



 Ko da Jidda ta fita ta samu Abdulra'uf tsaye jikin gidan Alhaji Barau da ke manne jikin gidan nasu, tsayawa Jidda tayi tare da sallama ta kawar da kanta gefe fuskarta a haɗe daga wannan sallamar Jidda bata kuma magana ba, ko arzikin gaisuwa bai samu ba, numfashi ya saki ganin Jidda bata da niyyar magana yasa shi nisawa yace.
   "Da ganin fuskar ki baki so kika fito, kiyi haƙuri idan na takuraki ba laifina bane laifin zuciyata ne, na kasa cireki a cikin zuciyata, ina sonki, amma na rasa dalilin da yasa kike nuna min ƙiyayya, Meyasa haka Jidda.?"
   Sai yanzu Jidda ta ɗago kanta ta kalli Abdulra'uf tace.
    "Naji daɗi da kamin wannan tambayar, ba ƙiyayya nake nuna maka ba, bana son na yaudareka saboda bana jin zan iya Auren ka, kayi haƙuri ba wai ina faɗa maka wannan maganar ba ne saboda kaji zafi a ranka bane, a'a ina faɗa maka gaskiyar abinda yake raina ne, bana jin sonka na Aure a cikin zuciyata karmu yaudari juna dan Allah kayi haƙuri ka nemi wata Please."
      "Hakane na juma da fahimtar bakya sona sai dai idan na zauna ina yawaita tunanin cewa ko wani mungun hali nake dashi wanda bazaki iya aurena ba saboda shi, kokuwa saboda ni talaka ne shiyasa, ki amsa min wannan tambayar abinda ya kawo ni ƙofar gidan ku kenan yanzu."?
   "A'a ko ɗaya Abdulra'uf baka da wani mungun halin da za'a ƙika saboda shi halinka mai kyau ne, haka kuma ni ba yar kowa bace dan haka bazan ce sai mai arziki zan aura ba, sonka ne kawai banayi ka fahimta kayi haƙuri kaje ka nemi wata zaka samu wacce ta fini idan har bakaji daɗin maganata ba, dan Allah kayi haƙuri."
     "Ba sai kin bani hakuri ba Jidda ba komai Allah ya haɗa kowa da rabonsa, amma ki sani ina jinki a cikin zuciyata bazan taɓa daina sonki ba Jidda Allah ya baki miji na gari wanda zai riƙeki bisa gaskiya da amana, ni zan tafi ki gaishe da Umma."
   "Na gode da ka fahimce ni kaima Allah ya baka wacce ta fini."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana komawa ciki.



**************


Bayan sati guda


Damuwa da soyayyar Jidda ta hana Nameer sakat duk sanda ya kasance shi ɗaya babu abinda yake sai tunaninta, zuciyarsa ta gaza samun nutsuwa, sai yayi yunkurin sanar da ita Soyayyar sa sai yaji ya kasa hakan, yayin da tsananin shaƙuwa ta shiga tsakanin JIDDA da HISHAM ya zamo tamkar abokinta na yarinta, zuciyarta ɗaya take jin Hisham sai dai shi kuwa har yanzu cikin zuciyarsa tsanar JIDDA ce a cikin ta, yana sake mata ne kawai saboda burin samun cikar ƙudirinsa akanta.



Yau takama asabar ranar weekend yana zaune shi kaɗai a falo ya kira JIDDA, da sanar da ita zasu haɗu ya fitar da abinda ke ransa ko zai samu sukuni, ringing ɗaya ta dauka, daga can tace.
    "Barka da safiya Nameer."
   "Barka Jidda ina fatan yau kina free ina son zamu haɗu ne da ke a inter room garden."
   Numfashi Jidda ta saki kafin tace.
   "Okay ba damuwa amma lafiya kuwa sannan zan iya zuwa da Basmah."?
  Murmushi NAMEER ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Zaki iya zuwa da Basmah, wannan kiran alkairi ne Jidda fatana dai mu haɗu yau ɗin."
    "Okay da misalin ƙarfe nawa zamu haɗu."?
    "By 4:30pm."
Nameer ya faɗa a takaice, murmushi Jidda tayi tace.
    "To shikenan sai mun haɗun ka gaishe da ummin ku."
    Da zataji Nameer ya amsa kafin ya katse kiran dai-dai Hisham na sauƙowa ya zauna gefensa yana cewa.
     "Ya kake zaune kai ɗaya, ina Ummi da Tutun suke.?"
    "Tutu ai tana da school yau, Ummi kuma bata juma da shiga bedroom ɗin ta ba, ya akayi alkawarin mu fa ya kusa Malam kuma har yanzu banji motsinka ba."
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Karka damu da motsina ka jira yau zakaji kyakkyawan labari domin kuwa a yaune zan kiraka kaga matar da zan Aura."
   "Serious Hisham."?
Nameer ya tambayesa cike da mamaki.
    "Yeah serious kasan dai ban taɓa ɗaukar alƙawari ban cika ba ko, duk da  soyayya bata gabana haka kuma har yanzu banga kalar matar da tamin ba, ko wannan da zan fitar manage zanyi da ita."
   "Hisham ni fa kana bani mamaki ta yaya zaka auri abinda baka so, wacce Yarinyar ce zatayi gangancin Auren mijin da baya sonta, karka zalunci Yarinyar mutane Hisham, idan baka sonta Please karka yaudareta tukunna ma a ina ka samu yarinyar tell me.?"
  Dariya Hisham yayi yace.
    "Tell me! Tell me! Hmmm! Nameer koma wacece ka jira zaka ganta, sannan maganar Soyayya ko yaudara da kake, ita ta gani kuma zata iya ɗauka, dan haka babu ruwanka da rayuwata a wannan sabgar kowa ya iya ruwa zai fito ko teku ya faɗa."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana tashi zai bar wajen, magana Nameer yake masa amma Hisham bai ma tsaya ya sauraresa ba ya fice, numfashi Nameer ya saki tare da furta.
    "Wacce yarinya ce Hisham ke shirin kai rayuwarta ya baro, mtss! Allah ya rufa asiri, to ni kuma ya zanyi da matsalar Tutu ya zataji idan na fitar da wata a matsayin matar aurena ba ita ba, anya zuciyar ta zata iya ɗaukar wannan ƙaddarar."?
  Ya tambayi kansa yana dafe da kansa, ya juma sosai a falon yana saƙar zuci kafin yabar falon.





**********


Jidda cikin shiri ta fito da la'asar dai-dai lokacin da sukayi alkawarin haɗuwa da Nameer yayin da ya rigata zuwa wajen yana zaman jiranta, tare da Basmah suka fito, cike da mamaki Jidda ke kallon Hisham da ta gansa tsaye a ƙofar gidan su jikin motarsa.
    "Hisham kaine kazo babu kira.?"
   Hannunsa Hisham ya harɗe tare da cewa.
   "Au dama doka ce idan zanzo wajenki sai na kiraki a waya."?
    "Ko ɗaya babu wata doka kawai naga ka saba kirana ne idan zakazo."
    "Okay yau kuma ban kira ba, Umma tana ciki ne ina son zan sanar da ita zamu fita unguwa da ke.?"
      "Unguwa kuma yanzu Hisham baka ga fita zamuyi da Basmah ba ne ko baka ganta bane, ina da alƙawari da Nam.........
  Bata kai ƙarshen maganar ba Hisham ya katseta da cewa.
   "Listen to me, I told you, we will go out or I will not go out with you tell me."?
   Kanta Jidda ta girgiza tare da cewa.
   "Ni fa bance baka kai ka fita unguwa dani ba cewa nayi akwai wanda yake jirana yanzu haka.''
  Fuskar sa Hisham ya haɗe tamau tare da kallon Basmah yace.
   "Ki koma gida Basmah ki cewa Umma na fita tare da Jidda plaza zata tayani sayayya kiyi haƙuri Basmah bazamu daɗe ba yanzu zamu dawo."
  Yayi Maganar yana buɗewa Jidda gaban mota kallon Basmah Jidda tayi da ta ke kaɗawa Hisham kai kamar ƙadangare kafin ta buɗe motar ta shiga ita kuma Basmah ta koma cikin gida duk abinda ke faruwa ABDULWASI'U yana ƙofar shagonsa yana kallon su, domin kuwa shi gani yake Jidda irin su Hisham take harin Aure ba irinsa ba talaka shima Hisham motar ya shiga ya ja suka ɗauki hanya, sun ɗanyi nisa da tafiya wayar Jidda tayi ringing hannu tasa cikin jakarta ta ɗauko wayar zata amsa kiran HISHAM yayi saurin kwace wayar daga hannunta ya kashe ba tare da ya duba mai kiran ba cikin aljihun sa ya jefa wayar, Jidda ko da bata ga mai kiran ba tasa Nameer ne yana can yana jiranta yaji shuru.
    "Hisham why are you turning off my phone when you are calling me?"
Kansa Hisham ya girgiza.
    "I do not need to know, Jidda I want you to give me peace of mind when I need you ."
  "Ka fiye rigima Hisham, muje na ga inda zaka kaini."
   "Kinyi kyau fa sosai tamkar ɗanyen gold haka kika fito."
  Murmushi Jidda ta saki.
    "Ka iya tsara zance Hisham, yanzu ina muka nufa."
 Murmushi Hisham ya saki yace.
   "Karki damu zaki gani yanzu."
  Yayi Maganar yana kashe mata ido, kafaɗarta Jidda ta ɗaga alamun to, suna zance jefi jefi har suka shiga babban Mall ɗin Alhassan Ɗan Iya da ke titin Darasawa, parking Hisham yayi tare da fitowa itama Jidda fita tayi tabi bayan Hisham, wani office ya shiga tana tsaye a bakin office ɗin bai juma ba ya fito suka shige ciki tunda Jidda tasa ƙafarta a wannan wajen wani irin sanyi ya ratsata idanunta ta zaro tana kallon abun mamaki yanda aka ƙawata wajen da kyawawan furanni, mai launin ja da kore, ga wasu irin wuta da suke bada hasken ja da kore.
    "Wow." Jidda ta furta a fili, murmushi HISHAM yayi Jidda bata ankare ba taga Mutum ya durƙusa a gabanta tare da buɗe akwatin zobe da ke hannunsa zoben azurfa ne mai kyawun gaske an masa ado da zanen love.
    "I LOVE YOU JIDDA I WANT YOU TO MARRY ME, I REALLY LOVE YOU, HELP ME RESPOND TO MY LOVE, I LOVE YOU."
  Tamkar saukar aradu Jidda taji maganar idanunta ta zaro tana bin Hisham da zoben dake hannunsa kallon mamaki.
    "Hisham, are you in love with me?."
    "Yeah Jidda i really love you."
   "No Hisham you are dreaming, I do not have a position to loving me, bani da wannan matsayin ko wacce kwarya da abokiyar burminta."
    Kansa Hisham ya kaɗa cikin nuna tsantsar damuwa yace.
    "Ki taimakeni ki amshi soyayyata muddun ba so kike rayuwata ta shiga cikin haɗari ba, babu ruwan so da sani matsayi ko iko Jidda, ki taimakeni ki soni dan Allah."
       "No Hisham babu soyayya tsakanin m........
    Katseta yayi da cewa.
"Please JIDDA ki taimakeni ki amshi soyayyata wallahi ina sonki na juma da soyayyarki a cikin raina please kiyi seving ɗin rayuwata I love You Jidda."
    Runtse idanunta Jidda tayi ta sani babu Macen da Hisham zaice yana so ta ƙisa, itama idan tace bata sonsa a yanzu wallahi tayi ƙarya girgiza Kanta tayi tare da miƙo masa yatsarta zoben Hisham ya sanya mata a yatsar kafin ta ɗago yatsar tana kallon zoben idanunta na runtse ta furta.
    "I accept your Love Hisham, I love You too. "
   Miƙewa daga tsugunnen da yake Hisham yayi yana sauƙe ijiyar zuciya mai nauyi a fili ya furta.
    "Yes! Jidda thank I love you so much."
Ya furta cikin farin ciki tare da zagowa gabanta ya ciro wayarsa selfie ya ɗaukesu dukkan su suna dariya ita dashi suna kallon juna, kana kallon picture ɗin kasan dukkan su suna cikin farin ciki, nuni Hisham ya yiwa Jidda da kujerar dake gaban ƙaramin table ɗin da ke ɗauke da kayan alatu na ci dasha ya mata alamun ta zauna, zuwa Jidda tayi ta zauna har yanzu murmushi ta ke.

 Shi kuwa Hisham picture ɗin ya turawa Nameer ta Whatsapp tare da rubuta masa.
    _surprise she is my wife to be, My first Love JIDDA._
  Yayi sending yana sakin murmushi, tare da tura masa text ta message yace ka buɗe Whatsapp ɗinka na aiko maka da saƙo mai mahimmanci.


Nameer dake can yana zaman jiran JIDDA a cikin inter room garden, sai duba agogo yake har 5:30pm babu Jidda wayarta kuma ya kira yafi sau goma switch off ya kira Basmah ma wayarta baya tafiya, jin shigar text cikin wayarsa ya sashi duba sakon Hisham ya gani, data ya buɗe cikin minti goma sai sakon nasa na Whatsapp ɗin ya shiga, Nameer kansa tsaye ya shiga kan saƙon picture ya gani rubutun ƙasan ya fara karantawa kafin ya buɗe picture ɗin.
     _Surprise She is my wife to be, My first Love Jidda._
   Murmushi Nameer ya saki ko a zuciyarsa bai kawo Jiddar sa bace ya furta.
    "Wai dama da gaske Hisham yake ya fitar da matar aure, cap waye ce tayi gangancin shiga rayuwar Hisham."
  Yayi Maganar yana saka hannu ya buɗe picture ɗin, a wani irin firgice ya miƙe tsaye ganin Hisham tsaye da Jiddar sa da yake zaman jira yanzu cikin nishaɗi suna dariya.
    "Whatt! Jidda no impossible ba Jiddana bace."
  Nameer yayi Maganar cikin tashin hankali yana goge idanunsa wai ko sune suke masa karya.

*_Page 45 to 46_*





*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻



"Ta yaya hakan zata faru soyayya tsakanin wuta da auduga, no bazai taba yiwuwa ba domin kuwa babu ta yadda wuta zata jitu da Auduga a waje guda, na tabbata dole sai wannan wutar ta ƙoneta, ya akayi haka Hisham da Jidda soyayya."
  Ya furta maganar a kiɗime cikin munguwar mamaki.
    _yeah serious kasan dai ban taɓa ɗaukar alƙawari ban cika ba ko, duk da soyayya bata gabana haka kuma har yanzu banga kalar matar da tamin ba, ko wannan da zan fitar manage zanyi da ita_
  Ya maimaita maganar Hisham ta ɗazu a cikin zuciyarsa yana neman warware kalaman nasa amma ƙwaƙwalwarsa ta gaza fassara kalaman Hisham, ko cikin mafarki kai ko a zahiri aka ce masa yau HISHAM da Jidda zasuyi jituwar da soyayya zata shiga tsakanin su wallahi sai ya ƙaryata, runtse idanunsa da suka kaɗa sukayi jawur yayi tare da buɗesu ya dafe kansa dake sara masa ji yake tamkar ya kurma ihu da ƙarfi ko nauyin da zuciyar sa ta masa zata ragu.
    "Meyasa ƙaddara zaki min haka, meyasa kika haɗa soyayyar mace ɗaya a zukatan ƴan uwa masu ƙaunar junan su, wannan wacce irin mummunar ƙaddara ce, I love Jidda, bazan iya rayuwa ba tare da ita ba, why Hisham zaka rabani da farin cikin rayuwata why, bazan iya jure rayuwa ba tare da ita ba, HISHAM! Why.!?"
  Yayi Maganar cikin ƙaraji yana dunƙule hannunsa ya daki teburin gabansa idanunsa ya runtse tare da cije lebensa idanunsa sun dawo tamkar gauta wato Jidda shanyasa tayi ta tafi wajen Hisham ta ma manta da yana jiranta, yayi Maganar zucin zuciyar sa tamkar zata fashe, sakon Hisham ya ƙara dubawa bai san sanda hawaye ya biyo idanunsa ba, hannunsa na karkarwa zuciyar sa na ƙuna ya yiwa Nameer reply da
    _congratulation Bro na tayaka murnar samun Mace ta gari, ko cikin mafarki ban taɓa zaton soyayya zata shiga tsakanin Hisham da abokiyar faɗansa JIDDA ba, ashe Alkairi ne tsakanin ku, naji daɗin haɗuwar ku Allah ya sanya muku alkairi._
  Ya tura saƙon zuciyar sa na harbawa da ƙarfi hannunsa ke tura saƙon amma zuciyar sa tamkar zata fashe haka yakeji, numfashi ya saki tare da kifa kansa jikin kujerar da yake zaune hawaye ya cigaba da zirarowa daga idanun sa, ga wani kwallon da ya tsaya masa na baƙin ciki ya tokare masa ƙirji ya juma sosai kansa yana kife kafin ya ɗago ya share hawayen sa miƙewa tsaye yayi tare da kwasan wayoyinsa ya fito daga wajen motarsa ya shiga yaja da ƙarfi cikin matsanancin tashin hankali ga kansa dake mungun sara masa.


  

 ********

Zama yayi a gabanta ya fuskance ta tare da dafe kumatunsa ya zuba mata idanu yana lumshe idanunsa.
    "Kallon yayi yawa idan dai ba haɗiye ni zakayi ba."
  Jidda tayi maganar tana sakin murmushi, murmushi shima Hisham ya saki yace.
   "Kinsan meye kuwa."?
"Ban sani ba har sai ka faɗa."
   Numfashi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Banyi zaton zaki amshi soyayyata cikin sauƙi ba Jidda, hmm! Jidda tun sanda na fahimci cewa ke macece mai adalci da riƙon amana da kuma tallafo damuwar wani daga ranar na fara jin Soyayyar ki a raina da farko banyi zaton cewa soyayya bace na ɗauka cewa kawai burgeni kike, sai da zuciyata ta fara matsa min da tunaninki da kuma yawan kallonki a cikin idanuna da kuma mafarkin ki da nakeyi ko yaushe yasa na fara gaskata zuciyata sonki take, Soyayya kuma ba ta wasa ba, i love You so much Jidda, hmmm! Na miki abubuwa da yawa na rashin kyautawa hakan yasa nake jin zaiyi wahala ki amince da soyayyata, meyasa kikayi saurin amincewa dani Jidda?."
   Tunda ya fara Maganar ta tsura masa idanu tana kallon sa har sai da ya kai ƙarshe kafin tayi murmushi tace.
   "Hisham babu ruwan so da ɗabia dan haka ka daina tambayata dalilin Soyayyarka a gareni abinda dai na sani ina sonka a koma yaya kake ni zan iya rayuwa da kai a haka."
    "Hmmn! Hakane Jidda na sani babu ruwan so da ɗabia amma kuma ki sani akwai ɗabiar da so ke gujewa, musamman ni da............
  Saurin dakatar da Hisham Jidda tayi da cewa.
     "A'a Hisham karka tuna baya mu kalli gaban mu da kuma tunanin yaya zamu tafiyar da goben mu, ina sonka Hisham soyayya ta gaskiya."
    Murmushi Hisham ya saki sun juma sosai a wajen suna fira cikin nishaɗi har sai 5:59 sannan Hisham ya dawo da Jidda gida bayan ya gama mata sayayya.

 Jidda na shiga gida Hisham ya saki numfashi tare da sakin murmushi wayarsa ya ɗauka ya kira Sadiq yana ɗagawa yace su haɗu a gida kafin ya katse kiran ya ja motar.



***********


Tutu tana zaune a falo taga shigewar Nameer kamar mai tangaɗi yana riƙe da kansa da sauri Tutu ta miƙe tsaye tare da cewa.
    "Subahanallah! Yaya Nameer meya sameka.?"
   Bai ko kulata ba ya haura sama da ƙyar bayansa Tutu tabi da sauri, kusan tare suka shiga bedroom ɗin nasa, a saman bed ya zube yana runtse idanunsa har yanzu ƙirjinsa zafi yake masa ga ɗaci da yakejin zuciyar tasa na masa, idan ya runtse idanunsa sai ya ga hoton Hisham da Jidda da sauri ya buɗe idanun nasa a firgice tare da furta "Ni!." Da ƙarfi cikin ƙaraji har sai da tutu ta tsorata da sauri tace.
    "Yaya Nameer meya sameka, hankalina ya tashi Yaya Nameer please meke faruwa."?
   Idanunsa da suka gama rinewa ya ɗago ya kalli Tutu wani irin haushinta yaji bakinsa ya cije bai kulata ba ya juyar da kansa gefe, cikin tashin hankali Tutu ta kuma fuskantar Nameer ta sunkuya a gabansa tace.
     "Yaya Nameer please meyake damunka, dan ka faɗa min hankalina ya na tashe."?
  Cike da jin haushin ta Nameer ya daka mata tsawa yace.
   "Get out Tutu."
"Yaya Nameer na fita kace, kana cikin damuwa zaka ce na fita na baka waje, ta yaya zan iya barinka cikin wannan damuwar, idan kana cikin damuwa zuciyata bazata taɓa samun sukuni ba, bazan bar nan ba har sai ka faɗa min abinda ya sameka."
   Cikin tsawa ya kuma ce mata.
    "I told you to get out of this room! And don't make me worry more, Get out."
   "Nameer...........
        "Please! I say get out."
     Yayi Maganar cikin tsawa yana yunƙurowa tamkar zai kai mata duka, a firgice Tutu tayi baya tare da tashi ta fice da sauri tana kuka, Nameer kansa ya dafe.
    "I love you so much Jidda, how can I own you? why Hisham you will come between me and my happiness, why."?
   Ya furta hawayen na kuma biyo idanunsa.

 Tutu tana fita falo ta zauna a saman kujera tana rera kukanta, cikin munguwar damuwa da tashin hankali ta furta.
    "Why are you expelling me from you Yaya Nameer, what is happening to you? Your concern lifts my spirits, I love You so much Yaya Nameer, i love You."
  Ta furta cikin tashin hankali tana cigaba da kukanta, tana cikin kukan Hisham ya shigo tsayawa yayi cak yana kallon Tutu tare da furta.
    "Ke kukan uban me kike yi me aka miki.?"
      Jin muryar Hisham yasa Tutu miƙewa da sauri ta faɗa jikinsa tare da kuma sakin kuka mai sauti.
   "Tutu tambayar ki fa nake wht happening.?"
    Cikin kukan tace.
 "Yaya Nameer yaya Nameer ne yaya Hisham yana can."
    "Nameer kuma meye Nameer ɗin ya miki dukanki yayi."?
    Hisham ya mata tambayar a takaice.
   Hawayen ta ta share tana sauke ijiyar zuciya tace.
   "A'a ni ba dukana yayi ba, shine dai yana can ya shigo cikin damuwa har hawaye yake idanunsa kuma sunyi jajur da na bisa bedroom ɗinku ina tambayar sa meya samesa shine yamin tsawa wai na fita na basa waje, hankalina ya tashi Yaya Hisham saboda ban taɓa ganinsa a cikin irin wannan mummunan yanayin ba."
   Numfashi Hisham ya saki cike da mamaki yace.
    "Nameer kuma yake cikin damuwar da har zata sanya sa kuka, kuma ban sani ba kai mtsss bara dai na je na gani."
  Yayi Maganar yana cire Tutu daga jikinsa ya haura bedroom ɗin da sauri, Tutu numfashi ta saki har yanzu tana cikin tashin hankali wayarta ta ɗauka ta kira kawarta Samira tace tazo zasuyi magana kafin ta katse kiran tana zama ta zuba tagumi.


    A firgice ya tura bedroom ɗin nasu ko sallama baiyi ba turus ya tsaya ganin Nameer cikin yanayin da Tutu ta sanar dashi.
    _Da gasken kuwa hawayen yake_
   Hisham ya furta cikin zuciyarsa kafin ya ƙaraso kusa dashi da sauri ya zauna yana dafa kafadarsa jin an da fa kafadarsa yasa Nameer saurin ɗago rinannun idanunsa ya zuba a cikin na Hisham da baisan ma shigowarsa ba.
    "Nameer dama ashe zaka shiga damuwa ka shigo ɗaki kayi kuka kai ɗaya, ni bazaka sanar dani ba yaushe ka fara ɗaukar damuwarka taka kai ɗaya, ba raba damuwar junan mu muke ba why zaka ɓoye min why Nameer.?"
   Hawayen dake fuskarsa Nameer ya sa hannu ya goge tare da girgizawa Hisham kai yace.
    "No Hisham babu wata damuwa da take damuna, kawai kaina ke ciwo sosai jin kan nake tamkar zai rabe gida biyu, sai kuma tsinkewar zuciya da ke damuna jima nake kamar na tattara kayana na gudu."
  Numfashi Hisham ya saki tare da cewa.
   "A'a Nameer wannan damuwar taka ba ta ciwon kai ba ne, tsinkewar zuciya kuma meye ma'anar sa, amma ka tashi muje asibiti a maka gwajin bp."
    "Gwajin BP kuma Hisham me zai haɗani da Bp ni da ba wata damuwa bace gareni, please Hisham ka ƙyaleni da wannan maganar na samu nutsuwa tukunna idan kaina na ciwo kafi kowa sanin bana son damuwa please dan Allah."
      "Nameer ni fa nace maka wannan damuwar ba ta ciwon kai bane akwai dai abinda yake damunka wanda ni kake ɓoye min, ciwon kai bazai saka tsinkewar zuciya ba Nameer, kuma ɗazu munyi waya da Hassan abokinka yace min ya shigo gari ya nemi layinka baya tafiya sai ka taɓa sa."
   Cike da fara gajiya da maganganun Hisham ga shi haushin sa yakeji yau yace.
      "Hisham na ce maka kaina ne yake ciwo Final babu abinda zan ɓoye maka a wannan duniyar, Please kabar ni haka."
    "Okay shikenan amma dai kasha magani, ni fita ma zanyi na bar maka bedroom ɗin, zanje garden zamuyi magana da Sadiq gacan ƙanwarka a falo ka sanyata kuka."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana tashi ya fita, da kallo Nameer ya bisa zuciyarsa na masa ƙunci komawa yayi ya kwanta tare da runtse idanunsa da ƙarfi.
     _Hassan ya dawo tabbas ya dawo akan gabar da nake buƙatar sa, shine zai warware min wannan matsalar da na ke ciki na MAHAUKACIN SO, ita kuma Tutun kuka ta ke hmm! Ya zataji kenan idan ta fahimci cewa bazan iya Auren ta ba, me zai faru idan har su Abba suka fahimci akwai alaqar Soyayya tsakanina da Tutu kuma suji cewa bazan aureta ba, wanne irin hukunci Abba zai yanke min?._
   Nameer ya furta cikin zuciyarsa yana jin jikinsa duk babu daɗi shi kansa bai so ace ya rabu da Tutu ba sai dai babu yadda ya iya da zuciyar sa JIDDA itace kaddararsa da ke biye dashi tamkar Inuwa bayajin zai iya haƙura da Jidda.





***********


Sadiq na zaune cikin mota shi da Tunde kiran Hisham ya kuma shigo masa waya daga kiran yayi tare da ce masa ya basa 10minute gashi nan karasowa kafin ya ijiye wayar yana duban Tunde yace.
    "Na faɗa maka kayi hankali da gubar nan domin kuwa tana da hadarin gaske, ka tabbatar yarinyar da zakuyi aikin tare mai basira ce, muddun Nameer yasha wannan gubar babu makawa bazai ƙara minti biyar ba a duniya, idan muka kashe Nameer to mun kashe ƙarfin Hisham da hasken sa, domin kuwa cikin duhu da tunani zai fara rayuwa yayin da hankalinsa zai rabu biyu gefe guda na ga ciwon rashin Nameer gefe guda kuma na ga tunanin ɗaukar fansa akan Jidda ina fatan ka fahimta, idan kuma har ka yadda wata matsala ta samu to ka sani babu ruwan Sadiq akanka damuwar zata tsaya domin kuwa na faɗa maka zan iya zillewa."
  Kansa Tunde ya kaɗa yace.
    "Yes na fahimta, insha Allah gobe komai zai kammala, zanyi aiki cikin basira da kwarewa babu wata matsalar da zata samu."
    ''okay fatan Nasara."
Sadiq yayi Maganar yana fita daga cikin motar ya shiga nasa tare da jan motar yabar arean wajen.



 A parking space na ciki part ɗin su Hisham yayi parking ɗin motarsa tare da fitowa yana kiran Hisham, garden ɗin ya nufa bayan Hisham ya sanar dashi yana garden ɗin, yana zaune a ƙasa kan darduma, fuskarsa cike da fara'a kana ganinsa zaka fahimci yana cikin farin ciki zama Sadiq yayi gefen sa, ganin Hisham na murmushi ya sashi cewa.
    "Daga ganinka kana cikin Happy, me ka samo mana, Allah yasa wata hajace mai nauyi."?
   Dariya Hisham ya sanya yana cewa.
   "Wa ya ce maka samuwa ce, wannan ba samuwa ba ce waken gizo ne dai zaisha wuta harma ya babbake, domin kuwa Allah ya kai damo ga harawa, kaga kuwa ɓarna dole ne, burina ya cika akan wannan bahamagiyar yarinyar domin kuwa ta faɗa tarkona, na kusa biyan bashin gaba, kasan shi bashi tamkar adashe yake muddun kaci to sai ka biya wallahi, ko kafi uwarka kokari, tabbas ta faɗa tarkon da zai zamo tabon baƙin cikin da bazata taɓa mantawa dashi ba har karshen rayuwarta."
  Dariya sosai Sadiq ya sanya ko da Hisham bai fayyace masa inda ya nufa ba, kalama ya basa a dunƙule, ya fahimci Jidda ce ta faɗa tarkon sa, Sadiq duban Hisham yayi yace.
    "Ni dama nasan dole zata faɗa tarkonka, domin kuwa babu Macen da zata kalleka tace bata so, amma mubar wannan maganar tukunna mu dawo kan lamarin Mamabisola ina son muyi magana akan Tunde."
  Dago idanunsa Hisham yayi ya dubi Sadiq cike da mamaki yace.
    "Mamabisola kuma Sadiq, meyasa zaka min magana a kansu why SADIQ kana da Alaqa da su ne wanda har zai sa su baka wani saƙo, ko ka fara shiga sabgar su ne."?
   A ɗan firgice Sadiq ya ɗago ya kalli Hisham _kar fa nayi wasa Hisham ya gano wani abu na shiga Uku, ko da yake ina bazai taɓa fahimtar komai ba idan kasan wata ai baka san wata ba Hisham na fika iya buga wasa._
   Sadiq yayi Maganar zucin yana sakin murmushi kafin ya ɗago ya kalli Hisham yace 
   "Kana zargina da wata alaqa dasu ne saboda kawai zanyi magana da kai akansu.?"
    "Ko ɗaya Sadiq bana zargin ka kuma bazan taɓa zargin ka ba domin kuwa nasan bazaka cutar da ni ba.?"
  Numfashi Sadiq ya saki yace.
       "Kasan wani abun mamaki kuwa Hisham.?"
  Kansa Hisham ya girgiza yace.
     "A'a babu abinda zan sani har sai idan ka sanar dani sai dai nayi mamaki sosai da kai da bakinka kace zamuyi magana da kai akan Mamabisola."

*_PAGE 47 TO 48_*



"Ba fa wata magana ba ce ta ɗaga hankali Hisham mamaki na ke yadda Mamabisola ta lalata rayuwar Tunde ta barsa ya ke abinda ya ga dama a rayuwarsa, ina wannan yaron A'lameen ɗan gidan Alhaji Dage yaron da ya kashe wannan almajirin har aka kaisa prison na Abuja kafin mahaifinsa ya shiga ya fita aka fito dashi."?
  Shuru Hisham yayi yana tunani kafin yace.
   "Okay na gane Dagash zaka cemin sai nafi ganewa na ganesa meya faru.?"
   Numfashi Sadiq ya sauƙe kafin yace.
   "Tare na gansa shi da Tunde wajen sau uku ina ganinsu tare nan a ShopRite na gansu nan kuma a Sudan Markey sai kuma yau na gansu tare a mota kasan abokin ɓarawo ɓarawo ne Hisham, ni fa wannan lamari ya fara bani tsoro kuma tashin hankali na yanzu Tunde ya shigo Companyn ku."
   Tsuka Hisham yaja yace.
    "Mtsss ni nayi zaton wani abun zaka faɗan to Sadiq ni ina ruwana dashi idan yaga dama yaje ya shiga cikin Yan shan jini ma dan Allah mubar wannan maganar mu dawo kan maganar wannan jakar yarinyar."
   "Jakar yarinya kuma Hisham JIDDA itace jakar."?
    "Kwarai kuwa Jaka ma ta bugawa a jarida yo idan Banda mahaukaciya yanzu ita idan akace zanso yar matsiyata irinta sai ta yarda, ban taɓa raina basirarta ba sai yau wallahi."
  Dariya sosai Sadiq ya saki tare da cewa.
   "Shege abokina wato dai yanzu kai murna kake ka samu karenka ya tsotsi ƙashi karfa reshe yayi ƙoƙarin juyewa da mujiya dan itama yarinyar naga so beautiful, ka dai taka sannu Hisham."
   Tsuka Hisham yaja yace.
    "Mtsss! Tsiyata da kai kenan wani sa'in ba'a tadin arziki da kai wallahi, yo ni ko baki Ammi tamin zanso wannan Yar talakawa ne, da dai  nayi rayuwa da wannan Yarinyar ta aure na gwammaci na mutu gauro yanzu dai ba wannan ba, ta faɗa tarkona meye next takun da zanyi mata kasan fa wani sa'in kaine madubina a wannan harkar saboda ba wai shiga sabgar mata nake ba ka sani."
    "To naji abinda ya kamata kayi yanzu shine ka ƙare dagewa wajen ganin ka kama zuciyar ta ma'ana ya zamana cewa bata jin ko wanne ɗa namiji a ranta sama da kai taji bazata taɓa iya rayuwar Aure da wani ba idan ba kai ba, ka ƙara kwantar da kanka gareta da nuna mata kulawa Hisham zakaga yadda zata mace a akanka idan har ta kai wannan matakin a Soyayyar ka to da shine zaka iya yiwa rayuwarta illa."
   Duban Sadiq Hisham yayi tare da cewa.
    "Kana nufin kenan har yanzu ban gama samun makaman da zan yaki JIDDA dasu ba."?
   "Kwarai kuwa Hisham makami ɗaya kawai ka riƙe da sauran rina a kaba tukunna yanzu ta fara sonki yana da kyau ka riƙe zuciyar ta."
   "Na fahimta Sadiq insha Allah kuma sai na baka mamaki domin kuwa bazan taɓa daina jin ciwon tozarta Nameer da JIDDA tayi ba har sai na wulaƙanta rayuwarta sannan ne zan huce zuciyata taji cewa da gaske Hisham bazai taɓa bari a wulaƙanta Nameer ba."
   "Hakan ma yayi domin kuwa ana tauna ƙasa ne saboda aya taji tsoro."
  Sadiq yayi Maganar yana kallon Hisham sun juma sosai a wajen da Hisham kafin Sadiq ya tafi.


  Da daddare bayan sunyi deaner Ummi ta koma tsakiyar falo shi kuwa Hisham bedroom ɗin su ya shige saboda yana da aikin da zaiyi ya rage daga Nameer sai Tutu a daining ɗin, idanunta Tutu ta ɗaga ta kalli NAMEER da take lura dashi tunda aka fara cin abincin shi bai wani ci ba yafi mai da hankalinsa ga tunani da alamu yana cikin matukar damuwa.
    "Kayi haƙuri yaya Nameer dazo nazo na takuraka da tambaya kana cikin yanayin da baka bukatata a lokacin kayi haƙuri zuciyata ce ta gaza samun nutsuwa da halin da na ganka a ciki a zatona ni abokiyar shawararka ce babu abinda zaka iya ɓoye min a matsayina na matar da zaka Aura."
  Kallonta yayi sai kuma tausayinta ya kamasa sosai _tabbas da tasan abinda yake zuciyata bazata so na furta mata shi ba, tabbas RASHIN SANI YAFI DARE DUHU_ Nameer yayi Maganar zucin har zuwa lokacin idanunsa na kanta, numfashi ya saki tare da cewa.
    "Ba ɓoye miki damuwata nake ba,  kawai ina cikin yanayi marar daɗi ne yau shi yasa bana so kowa ya kasance kusa dani nafi bukatar kadaici a wannan lokacin karki damu ba wata damuwa ce ta ɗaga hankali ba infact ma komai ya shige."
    "Amma yaya Nameer zaman mu cin abinci fa ina lura da kai baka wani ci abinci ba, sai ma tunani da kake yi wannan kaɗai ya isa ya nuna damuwar da kake ciki mai girma ce, Please farin ciki na tell me."
   Kansa ya girgiza yace.
"Tunda na ce miki kibar maganar nan ta wuce to ki barta ba gashi ba yanzu kallonki ya san yani cikin nishaɗi, ke ta musamman ce Tutu i love You so much."
  Murmushi Tutu ta saki sosai taji daɗin maganganun NAMEER a gareta kafin tace.
     "Shikenan na bari amma ka sani bana son sake ganinka cikin damuwar nan, domin farin cikin ka shine nawa, kasan dai irin matukar Soyayyar da nake maka numfashi ma wuya zai bani muddun babu kai cikin rayuwata domin kuwa rayuwar duhu zata min irin duhun da ko tafin hannu baza'a iya kallo ba, yau idan na tuna cewa gobe ne alkawarin ku na neman matar Aure da kuka yiwa Ammi zai cika sai naji wani irin farin ciki domin kuwa gobe ne kowa zai ji labarin Soyayyar mu harta Abba da baya ƙasar nan sai mun kirasa mun sanar masa."
  Dariya Nameer ya saka tare da cewa.
    "Saboda baki da kunya Jidda sai ki sanar da Abba Soyayyar ki."
   Nameer yayi Maganar cikin dariya Tutu idanunta ta zaro cike da mamaki tace.
   "What JIDDA kuma yaya Nameer wacece kuma JIDDA da har zakayi sha'afan da zaka kirani da sunanta wani irin mahimmanci ta ke dashi a rayuwarka."?
  Tayi Maganar tana haɗa ranta tamau.
  Kansa Nameer ya dafe bai san ma ya ambaci Tutu da sunan Jidda ba, tsabar Jidda ce kaɗai take yawo a cikin ransa numfashi ya ɗan saki tare da cewa.
   "Sorry Farin ciki na, babu wani mahimmanci da take dashi a rayuwata kawai dai sunan ne yazo bakina, Kinga mubar wannan maganar yanzu ina son zan haura akwai aikin da zanyi."
  Kanta Tutu ta kaɗa tare da cewa.
    "Okay shikenan amma dai hankalina bai kwanta da wannan Jiddar ba dan harma naji na tsaneta tun kafin nasan waye ita."
  Murmushi kawai Nameer ya saki tare da tashi yace.
    "I love You."
Yayi Maganar yana haurawa sama murmushi Tutu ta saki ta bisa da kallo cike da so da kauna kafin ta tashi tayi wajen Ummi.



   Ko da Nameer ya shiga ya samu Hisham zaune a saman kujera mai biyu dake tsakiyar bedroom ɗin, tollet Nameer ya shige yayi wanka ya fito tare da sanya boxer da vest ya zauna yana ƙoƙarin jawo system ɗinsa yaji muryar Hisham.
     "Nameer wai bakaga sakon da na turo maka bane ta Whatsapp."?
  Kansa Nameer ya ɗago ya kalli Hisham tare da cewa.
    "Bakaga sakon da na maido maka ba kenan halan baka buɗe data bane."
   "Eh ban buɗe ba gaskiya, kayi mamaki ko da kaga Jidda na fitar a matsayin matar da zan aura."?
  Numfashi NAMEER ya saki yana jin wani ɗaci na taso masa a zuciyarsa kafin yayi murmushin ƙarfin hali yace.
    "Babu abun mamaki ai Hisham shi Allah ba yadda baya lamarinsa naji daɗi da ya kasance Jidda ka fitar domin kuwa ko shakka bana yi kayi dacen mace ta gari, amma Hisham Soyayyar gaskiya kuwa kake yiwa Jidda anya babu wani manufa a ƙasan ranka karfa ka zalunci Yarinyar mutane."?
   Murmushi Hisham ya saki kafin yace.
    "Nameer kana ganin zan cutar da Jidda ne meyasa zakayi wannan tunanin, hmm! Nameer har cikin zuciyata nake jin Soyayyar Jidda ina sonta sosai."
    "Ban yarda ba Hisham ko ka manta abinda ka faɗa min ko matar da zaka fitar bawai kana sonta bane manage kawai zakayi da ita ko ba haka ka faɗa min ba."
  Dariya sosai Hisham yayi yana ture system ɗin sa gefe yace.
    "Malam ni fa wasa nake maka wai dama serious ka ɗauki maganar gaskiya she is funny."
  Numfashi Nameer ya saki yace.
    "Okay Allah ya sanya alkairi nima insha zakaga tawa."
   "Allahu yasha" Hisham ya furta yana maida kansa ga system ɗin sa.






**********


Cike da farin ciki Jidda ta shiga gidan nasu babu kowa tsakar gidan hakan ne ya bata damar faɗawa ɗakin ta domin kuwa tasan Basmah na ciki tana zaune tana ninke kaya Jidda ta faɗa jikinta tare da rungumeta ta baya tana dariya juyowa Basmah tayi tare da cewa.
    "Lallai yau da alama ta samu wannan farin ciki haka."
   Dariya Jidda ta kuma saki tare da sakin Basmah ta zauna a tsakiyar katifar tace.
    "Basmah yau dai nima nayi tsuntuwa domin kuwa burina ya cika na samu irin namijin da nake burin aura, zancen da nake miki harma ya furta min kalmar I love You."
   "Ban fahimta ba naga da Hisham kuka fita a can ɗin kika gamu da saurayin."?
  Kanta Jidda ta girgiza tare da cewa.
   "A'a shi ɗin dai Hisham."
  Da ƙarfi zuciyar Basmah ta buga tare da dafe ƙirjinta idanunta suka firfito waje da karfi ta furta.
    "What! JIDDA Hisham shine yace yana sonki.?"
  Ta jefa mata tambayar ƙirjinta na harbawa da ƙarfi, dariya Jidda tayi tace.
    "Ƙwarai kuwa Hisham ɗin, Basmah bazan ɓoye miki ba nima fa na kwana biyu ina jin sonsa a raina kawai ina dannewa ne saboda ban san me gobe zata haifar ba, nayi mamaki da baki fahimci hakan ba keda kikafi kowa sanin waye ni."
  Gumine ya ketowa Basmah tamkar zata danna ihu haka taji zuciyar ta, jikinta duk ya mata nauyi hankalinta ya tashi.
