*_WRITING ON 01/03/2022_* ✍🏻




     *FREE BOOK ONE 📚*





*_Page 49 to 50_*





Jidda ganin Basmah ta kwabe fuska har tana haɗa gumi ya sata cewa.
   "Basmah meye haka kin wani haɗa rai sai kace wanda na faɗa miki ranar mutuwarki, har kina haɗa gumi akwai matsala ne."?
  Saurin girgiza kanta Basmah tayi tace.
   "Yo Jidda ba dole na haɗa gumi ba naji kinzo min da wata magana tamkar almara, meye zai haɗaki soyayya da Hisham, har kin manta halinsa kin manta waye Hisham kin manta kalar rashin mutuncin sa, da zakiyi gangancin shiga rayuwar sa."?
  Murmushi Jidda ta saki tare da cewa.
   "Hmmm! Basmah kenan meye aibun Hisham, ke fa da bakinki kike cemin ashe Hisham mutumin kirki ne mune bamu fahimce sa ba, yanzu kuma kice zaki tuno da baya, we'll munin halinsa ba abun gudu bane domin kuwa irinsu suna bukatar macen da zata nuna masu rayuwa shiyasa banji tsoron shiga rayuwar sa ba, ina son Hisham Basmah bazan iya rabuwa dashi ba kiyi haƙuri mace ma tana gyara miji ki fahimta, ina da yakinin Hisham bazai juyawa rayuwata baya ba, na kawo miki labari mai daɗi shine zaki sa gwiwwata ta sare madadin ki karfafa min gwiwwa akan abinda nake muradi."
   Numfashi Basmah ta saki zuciyarta na hura har yanzu hankalinta a tashe yake _Wannan wacce irin masifa ce yaya Hisham zai min haka ni da nafi kowa muradinsa ni da nake da shirin shiga Companyn su domin samun kusanci dashi, innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un Jidda meyasa zaki min haka_ Basmah tayi Maganar zucin cikin matsanancin tashin hankali kanta ta girgiza tace.
    "Shikenan Jidda Allah yasa haka yafi Zama Alkairi, yanzu ya kukayi dashi kin tabbatar Soyayyar gaskiya yake gwada miki.?"
   "Ƙwarai kuwa Basmah na tabbatar ɗari bisa dari, ina labarin Ahmad ne kwana biyu bamu haɗu ba."
  Bakinta Basmah ta taɓe
    "Yana nan lafiya jiya ma sai da ya tambayeki."
   "Ayya dan Allah idan kunyi waya kice masa na gode da mantawa da yayi dani, bara naje wajen Umma bata san na dawo ba."
  Kanta Basmah ta girgiza kafin Jidda ta fita tana fita Basmah ta mike tsaye.
   "Na shiga Uku ni Basmah duk adana soyayyar Hisham da nake amma sai da aka min ƙofar rago, Hmm why Jidda, mtsss bazan iya haƙura da Hisham ba kiyi haƙuri Jidda dole sai na kwaci soyayyata."
  Tayi Maganar cikin huci tana riƙe kunkuminta.





**********


        After two days


Companyn cike yake da ma'aikata kasancewar yau take ranar aiki Monday kowa yana harkokin gabansa, Yau lokaci a kure Hisham ya shigo Companyn, darect office ɗin Jidda ya nufa yaga ko tazo, ya samu ta shigo amma tayi office ɗin mr John hakan ya sashi juyawa ya koma nasa office ɗin, Tunde kuwa tun shigowarsa ya nufi Wajen Zainab ya sameta zaune ita ɗaya a wajen da suke goge shinkafa, gefenta Tunde ya zauna.
    "Ya akayi Tunde ka shiryawa aikin ne."?
    "Kwarai kuwa na shirya shiyasa ma kika ganni anan Zainab bana son matsala a aikin nan duk yadda za'ayi ki samu ki zuba wannan gubar a tea ɗin da Nameer zaisha bana son kuskure."
   "Kuskure Hmm kaima fa Tunde idan har baka yarda dani ba bazaka bani wannan aikin ba dan haka karka damu aiki zai tafi yadda kake bukata."
   "Shikenan ga nan gubar ki kiyaye."
  Tunde yayi Maganar yana miƙa mata kwalbar gubar amsa Zainab tayi kafin tace.
   "Nan da 30 minute ina jiran alart ɗina yana shigowa zan aiwatar da aikin."
   "Karki damu zakiji" Tunde yayi Maganar yana tashi yabar wajen murmushi Zainab ta saki tare da furta "akan kuɗi ba abinda bazan iya aikatawa ba, kayi haƙuri Nameer kuɗi yafi min komai a rayuwa."


   Kansa tsaye Sadiq ya shigo office ɗin dubansa Hisham yayi yana sakin murmushi yace.
   "Nayi zaton bazaka zo ba ai Bismillah ka zauna."
   Zama Sadiq yayi yace
      "Ai kasan muddun ka kirani zanzo, Allah dai yasa lafiya ka kirani da sanyin safiya kasan ko breakfast banyi ba."
   "Mtsss! Kiran dole ya kama Sadiq, bara na kira sakatariya tasa a kawo maka tea ko da bread ne ka fara kafin muyi maganar ko."
   Kansa Sadiq ya girgiza yace.
   "Bar tea ɗin tukunna sai mun gama tattaunawa."
    "Okay Sadiq ni har yanzu akwai Yarinyar da ka fitar a matsayin zaka Aura.?"
    "Wannan wacce irin tambaya ce Hisham da ina da budurwa ai da ka sani bani da ita, amma meyasa kamin wannan tambayar."?
    Numfashi Hisham ya saki yana gyara zamansa yace.
    "Saboda ina son ka nemi ƙanwata Tutu, amma fa sai idan kaji ta maka babu dole."
  Murmushi Sadiq ya saki yace.
   "Kai ka isa kamin dole ne Hisham, ka sanni sarai bana ɓoye maita ta, Tutu ba Macen da za'a ce ba'a so bane Hisham sai dai ka gane Soyayya tana buƙatar haɗuwar jini, amma ita ka tuntubeta ne."?
     "A'a ban tuntunbeta ba tukunna nafi bukatar na fara ji daga gareka, na tabbata ita bazan samu matsala da ita ba, Sadiq kai Mutum ne na ƙwarai da kowa zaiso haɗa zuri'a da kai shiyasa na yiwa ƙanwata kwaɗayin samun ka a matsayin mijin aurenta."
   "Karka damu Hisham kaje ka tuntunbeta idan har ta amince ni bani da matsala, waye zaiki haɗa zuri'a daku Hisham sai dai marar rabo."
  Dariya Hisham ya saki yana ɗaukar sigarinsa ya saka a baki tare da kunna mata wuta kafin yace.
    "Kai kuma bazaka zamo cikin marassa rabo ba ko, ya labarin wannan shegen ne Dawud."?
   "Kai dalla bar zancen ɗan iskan nan ka sanya a kawo min tea ɗin na samu na bawa hanjina hakkinsa, sigari dai ta zamo maka ɗabia Hisham."
  Dariya yayi yana ɗaga waya ya kira sakatariya yace tasa a kawo tea da bread kafin ya dubi Sadiq yace.
     "Ba ɗabia ta zame min ba rayuwa ta zame min musamman idan ina cikin damuwa nafi jin daɗin ta."
   "Kuma kafi kowa sanin illarta ba."
   Yayi Maganar dai-dai Sakatariya na shigowa da tea ta ijiye a tebur ɗin kafin ta juyawa, jawo tea ɗin Sadiq yayi tare da bread ɗin a hankali ya fara sha suna hira da Hisham har ya gama shan tea ɗin yana ijiye cup ɗin cikinsa yayi wani irin juyawa idanunsa ne suka firfito waje ya fara wani irin gurnani a ɗan gigice Hisham ya miƙe yana yasar da sigarin hannunsa ya take tare da  furta.
    "Subahanallah! Sadiq meke faruwa, me yake damunka what kumfa a bakinka Sadiq!''?
  Hisham yayi Maganar da ƙarfi yana zagowa ya tallafo Sadiq cikin tashin hankali magana ma ta gagari Sadiq saboda shakewar da wuyansa yayi, idanunsa ya zazzaro numfashin sa sai sama sama yake yana neman daukewa, security Hisham ya dannawa kira cikin gaggawa suka shigo office ɗin kafin kace me zance ya karade Companyn Jidda da Nameer kusan tare suka shigo Nameer shiya kira Police ya sanar dasu, cikin gaggawa kuwa sukazo akayi asibiti da Sadiq yayin da sai shidewa yakeyi kowa da ke cikin Companyn ya shiga cikin tashin hankali, Tunde ma ya shigo sai hankalinsa yayi matukar tashi kar fa ace gubar nan Sadiq yasha, tare suka dunguma zuwa asibitin Nameer da Hisham har Jidda, Take likitocin suka ruhu a kansa, Hisham sosai hankalin sa ya tashi, sai kaiwa da komowa yake ya gagara samun nutsuwa, likitocin sun juma sosai kafin suka fito, doctor yace su Hisham su samesa a office. 
   Koda sukaje likita ya tabbatar musu guba yasha mai haɗarin gaske kuma shine last abincin da ya ci tare da cewa suna bukatar suga last abinda yaci, a firgice Hisham ya miƙe tare da furta.
    "What! Last abinda Sadiq yaci shine tea kuma a office ɗina yasha, ta yaya hakan zata faru, kaga Doctor tukunna a wani hali Sadiq yake ciki.?"
   Doctor ne yace.
     "Muna bukatar sauran tea ɗin ko cup ɗin da aka sha tea ɗin domin mu gwada muga irin gubar da akayi anfani dashi a yanzu dai haka Sadiq yana halin rayuwa ko mutuwa gaskiya saboda dole sai mun san kalar gubar gubar kafin mu san treatment ɗin da zamu masa na ainihi duk yanzu ma munyi iya bakin ƙoƙarin mu, amma bamu muku alƙawarin zai rayu ba gaskiya."
   Numfashi Hisham ya saki tare da dafe kansa Nameer ma sosai hankalinsa ya tashi guba a cikin Companyn su waye yake ƙoƙarin kaiwa rayuwarsu farmaki, wato Hisham aka so kashewa lamarin ya sauƙa akan Sadiq waye ne.
     "Hisham ya akayi haka guba kuma a Companyn mu, Hmm! Lamarin ya bani mamaki sosai, ya kamata Police suje suyi bincike."
    "Ni kaina mamakin da nake kenan Nameer sakatariya ce ta kawo tea ɗin itace first target ɗin da za'a zarga, Inspector Lawan yana can Companyn zaiyi duk abinda ya dace ni aka so kashewa ba Sadiq ba, doctor muje da nurse ta ɗauko kuma cup ɗin."
  Hisham yayi Maganar yana miƙewa jikinsa a sanyaye cike da mamakin yadda hakan ta faru.

_Wannan littafin ba free bane na kuɗi ne Book one shine Free kuma mun kusa zuwa karshen Free zamu shiga Book two inda zaku cigaba da karatun wannan littafin akan 200 kacal ban tsawwala ba farashin da zaku iya biya na sanya dan Allah idan har kina son wannan littafin kina bibiyar sa kizo ki biya kudin ki, ki cigaba da karatun sa akan farashi ƙalilan, karki yadda ki kasance cikin ɗaya daga cikin masu cin hakkin wani 08147537180._




*_Page 51 to 52_*




Numfashi Nameer ya saki cike da tunani gabaki ɗaya Companyn suka koma sun sami Police suna ta famar bincike, inspector Lawan da kansa ya dage wajen binciken, Zainab sosai ta shiga cikin tashin hankali domin kuwa ta rasa ya akayi akayi sauyin cup ɗin har Sadiq yasha gubar duk da bata san akwai alaqa tsakanin Tunde da Sadiq ba, cikin tashin hankali Tunde ya nufi Zainab ganin an tsawaita bincike tana nan tsaye itama cikin tashin hankali ya iske ta.
    "Bana faɗa miki cewa kar a samu kuskure ba, meyasa zaki min wannan shirmen kinsan rayuwar waye kuwa kika sanya cikin hadari, yana da matukar muhimmanci a rayuwata, kin riga kin aikata kuskure amma ki sani karki kuskura a san cewa kece kika sanya wannan gubar idan har akayi ƙoƙarin ganoki babu shakka kafin a iso gareki zan ɗauke numfashin ki."
  Yayi Maganar yana nuna mata yatsa tare da barin wajen cikin hanzari, numfashi Zainab ta sauƙe tana huci ta furta.
     "Babu wanda ya ganni da zan aikata haka kuma babu wanda ya isa ya gano ni ko shatin yatsata bai taɓa cup ɗin ba bare nayi tunanin tashin hankalin rayuwa."
  Tayi Maganar tana sakin murmushi, can kuwa nurse da police ɗaya suka koma da cup ɗin tea ɗin asibiti inda darect sukayi lap da shi domin gwada launin gubar, Duk binciken Police ɗin ya ta'allaka ne akan sakatariya da Esther wacce itace ta haɗa tea ɗin.
     Sosai sakatariya ta shiga tashin hankali, Inspector Lawan ne ya sasu su biyun a gaba sukayi asibitin za'a gwada zanen yatsunsu, shi kansa Hisham lamarin ba ƙaramin ɗaga masa hankali yayi ba wunin ranar haka sukayi shi cikin bincike da tashin hankali sai Nameer bai yadda an sanar da Abba abinda ke faruwa ba.

   Dukkan su babu wanda ya samu damar barin cikin Companyn har sai da suka kai 6:30pm kusan kiran magaruba dan har sai da Police suka dawo da sakatariya da Esther tukunna, Hisham Jidda ya ɗauka ya Kaita gida.





********



Ƙafafuwan sa ya tankwashe a gabanta yayi shuru, yayin da ranta ke ɓace cikin hasala tace.
     "Wace shegiya daƙiƙiyar ka bawa wannan aikin, idan har Sadiq ya rayu ba lallai ya ƙyale mu ba, domin kuwa cewa zaiyi gangancin mu ne, koma yace da gangan muka aikata masa hakan saboda ya nemi wani abu daga cikin dukiyar da muke hari, ya kamata yarinyar nan ta fuskanci hukunci, a wannan karon idan har Hisham ya gano akwai hannun mu cikin sanya wannan gubar wallahi sai ya tarwatsa rayuwarmu."
   Kansa Tunde ya girgiza tare da cewa.
    "Mtsss! Mama ban taɓa zaton cewa Zainab zata min ganganci ba, sai dai ki sani bazata taɓa bari a gane itace ta aikata ba, asirin mu bazai tonu ba, baƙin ciki na ɗaya ne Mama na tarwatsewar mission ɗin nan banso ace Nameer ya tsallake wannan tarkon ba, naji baƙin ciki sosai, Mama karki damu wannan gubar bazata bar Sadiq ya rayu ba bare ya zamo barazana a rayuwar mu shine ya tabbatar min da ƙarfin gubar duk wanda yasha yace bazai rayu ba dan haka karki damu Sadiq tasa yazo karshe."
    "Amma Sadiq yana da mahimmanci a aikin mu rashin sa wata matsalar ce fa, waye zamu samu na jikin Hisham wanda zai iya mana wannan aikin shine babban tashin hankali na, domin Sadiq shine jigon aikin mu."
    "Hmmm! Na sani Mama amma ki sani bazamu rasa ba, gari da yawa maye bazai ci kansa ba, ke dai ki cigaba da addu'a, sannan Mama dole fa sai kunje kauyen nan da Wamimo domin kuwa bazamu iya kaisu ƙasa ba dole sai mun haɗa da asiri."
   Shuru Mamabisola tayi tana tunani kafin ta ɗaga kanta tace.
    "Shikenan, zan nemi Wamimo zamu san yadda za'ayi kaje ka ci abincin ka."
  Kansa Tunde ya girgiza tare da tashi yabar wajen, bayan ya fita ne Mamabisola ta miƙe tsaye cike da tunanin yadda zata bullowa lamarin domin kuwa taga lamarin yana shirin fin ƙarfinta muddun tayi wasa kwabarta zata mata ruwa.
     "Lallai dole Wamimo tazo muje ƙauyen nan na Agunjin."
   Tayi Maganar tana sakin numfashi cike da damuwa wayarta ta ɗauka ta kira Wamimo sukayi magana akan tafiyar sun juma suna waya kafin sukayi sallama.




**********


 "Meyasa zaka sawa kanka damuwa haka bayan Police suna nan suna bincike, dole kasan zasu gano wanda ya aikata wannan aikin bazai tsira ba tunda akwai CCTV Camera."?
   Kansa Hisham ya girgiza yana kallon Jidda cike da damuwa yace.
   "Har yanzu baki fahimci damuwata ba Jidda, damuwata rayuwar Sadiq ne idan Sadiq ya mutu ta wannan dalilin bazan yafewa kaina ba domin silata hakan ta faru dashi, Sadiq wani tsagi ne na rayuwata Jidda, mutumin kirki ne da babu ruwansa bashi kyashi ko mugunta bare kuma hassada, gashi can a tsakanin rayuwa ko mutuwa babu tabbacin zai tashi, da ace zansan wanda yayi wannan aikin babu makawa sai na kashe sa kafin aje ga hukuma, ina jin zafin halin da Sadiq ya shiga da wani ido zan kalli mahaifinsa tun ɗazu na gagara komawa asibitin da akace Alhaji Sagir mahaifinsa yaje."
     "Insha Allah ina da yakinin babu abinda zai samu Rayuwar Sadiq insha Allah zai tashi, rayuwarsa tana hannun Ubangiji ne ba na likita ba faɗa ne kawai nasu, amma ainihin sanin gaibun yana hannun Ubangiji, ka daina ɗagawa kanka hankali insha Allah komai zai tafi normal."
  Bakinsa Hisham ya cije tare da cewa.
     "Hmmm! Jidda rayuwata aka nema bata Sadiq ba domin kuwa ni nace akawo min tea ba tare da sanin waye zai sha ba kinga kuwa ni aka so kashewa ya faɗa kan Sadiq."
    "Karka ce haka waye kake dashi maƙiyi a wannan Companyn da zai nemi rayuwarka naga zaman lafiya kuke da kowa cikin mutuntawa da ƙaunar juna tunda na fara aiki ban taɓa ganin Company mai haɗin kai daya burgeni irin Companyn ku ba."
   Murmushin takaici Hisham yayi yace.
    "Kin manta kenan da fyaden da Khabeer yaso miki kuma a cikin Companyn namu, a hakan ne kike yabon mutanen cikin ta, ɗan adam ba abin yadda ba ne Jidda, ina da makiyin da zai iya neman rayuwata."
   "Ban fahimta ba waye wanda zai iya neman rayuwar ka a cikin Companyn.?"
   "Tunde.?"
"What!?"
Jidda ta furta tana zaro idanunta cike da mamaki.
    "Ƙwarai kuwa, meyasa zakiyi mamaki ko kin manta abinda ya faru ne a baya na Case ɗin Hajara ko kin manta da shine ya sanya Hajara ta yiwa NAMEER sharrin fyade duk wanda zai maka sharrin fyaɗe to tabbas zai iya kasheka."
    "A'a ban manta ba, amma wannan bashi zai baka hujjar cewa Tunde zai nemi rayuwarka ba, zato zunubi ne ko da ya kasance gaskiya, idan baya ta wuce babu amfanin a tuno sa, amma naga kai kamar baka manta ba, alkairin baya ake tunawa ba sharrinsa ba."
   "Kina da saurin manta maƙiyi Jidda da wannan zuciyar taki wata rana za'a cutar da ke, ki shiga gida ana kiran sallah nima zan shige."
  Yayi Maganar yana buɗe mata murfin motar, kanta Jidda ta girgiza tace.
     "Dan Allah ka samu nutsuwa insha Allah babu abinda zai faru sai Alkairi."
  Tayi Maganar itama tana fita daga cikin Motar da kallo Hisham ya bita ba tare da yace komai ba har ta shiga gida, kafin yaja motar sa yabar wajen ransa cunkushe da damuwa.


  Ko da Hisham ya koma gida ma ya samu Nameer dasu Ummi a falo a zaune suna ta jajanta maganar, inda Nameer ya hana Ummi sanar da Abba maganar gudun kar a ɗaga masa hankali baya ƙasar, ranar haka suka kwana cikin yanayin rashin jin daɗi musamman Hisham da yakejin zafin abinda ya faru da Sadiq shi Nameer tashin hankalin ma biyu ya zame masa ga na zullumin rashin Jidda ga kuma na abinda ya faru a Companyn nasu, haka dai suka kwana ba daɗi washegari sammako Hisham yayi zuwa asibitin sai dai zuwan sa yayi bala'in ɗaga masa hankali domin kuwa ya tarar da mahaifiyar Sadiq da ƙanwarsa sai ihu suke Allah ya yiwa Sadiq rasuwa Hisham jingina bayansa yayi da jikin bango ya runtse idanunsa bai san sanda hawaye ya biyo fuskarsa ba, gagara magana yayi idanunsa na lumshe hawaye na cigaba da biyo idanunsa, Nameer a haka ya ƙariso ya samu Hisham gaban Safiya ƙanwar Sadiq ya ƙarisa tare da cewa. 
    "Me yake faruwa karki cemin Sadiq ya rasu.?"
  Kanta safiya ta ɗaga tana kara sakin kuka cikin rawar murya tace.
    "Yaya Sadiq ya rasu, mun shiga Uku."
  Tayi Maganar cikin matsanancin kuka kansa Nameer ya dafe tare da furta.
     "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Baku shiga Uku ba safiya akwai Allah ki daina kukan."
  Yayi Maganar yana tashi yaje ya tsugunna a gaban Alhaji Sagir, yace.
   "Alhaji.........
    Hannu Alhaji Sagir ya ɗaga wa Nameer tare da tashi yabar wajen, shuru Nameer yayi tare da tashi ya nufi Hisham.
      "Hisham ka yi haƙuri Please ga police sun shigo."
 Idanunsa Hisham ya ɗago ya kalli Nameer tare da juyawa ya kalli Police ɗin, room ɗin da gawar Sadiq yake suka nufa ganin Alhaji Sagir yana huci yasa Hisham bai tunkari inda yake ba.
    Inspector Lawan sun juma sosai cikin ROOM kafin suka fito da umarnin za'a iya ɗaukar gawar Sadiq, Hisham inspector Lawan ya nufa tare da dafa kafadarsa yace.
    "Munyi duk wani binciken da zamuyi bamu samu wata hujjar da ta nuna wanda yayi amfani da gubar nan ba, guba ce mai haɗarin gaske wanda duk wanda ya shata rayuwarsa bazata tsira ba, sakatariya da Esther babu hannunsu cikin aikata wannan masha'ar, sannan mun koma Companyn naku da daddare mun ƙara bincike har CCTV Camera dake Companyn sai da muka duba amma bamu ga komai ba, bamu da wata hujjar da zamu kama wani da laifi amma zamu cigaba da binciken ma'aikatan ku ɗaya bayan ɗaya.''
  Kansa HISHAM ya girgiza yace.
    "Na gode Inspector Lawan, tabbas duk wanda yayi wannan aikin babu shakka ya juma yana harin mu yayi komai ne cikin dabarar da baza'a iya gano sa ba, kuyi kokarin gano wanene zan baku duk wani haɗin kan da kuke bukata."
   Hannu inspector Lawan ya bawa Sadiq kafin yabar wajen, gawar Sadiq aka fito da ita a motar asibitin akayi gida dashi, shima Hisham yabi bayansu tare dashi akayi sallar jana'izar Sadiq bayan sun dawo ne daga makabarta Hisham ya sunkuyawa gaban Alhaji Sagir yana basa hakurin abinda ya faru, a wannan lokacin ya saurari Hisham harma suka zauna waje daya aka cigaba da karɓar gaisuwa.
     Tun da Tunde yaji labarin mutuwar Sadiq hankalinsa ya tashi sai dai ko da wasa baije wajen amsar gaisuwar ba gudun kar Hisham ya zargesa.




***********


Dariya ta sanya da jin labarin mutuwar Sadiq madadin tayi jimami, cike da mamaki Wamimo take kallonta ita da Tunde kafin Wamimo ta nisa tace.
     "Mamabisola meyasa kike dariya madadin kiyi alhinin rasa abokin aiki, wannan kisar fa kune kuka yisa."?
  Dariyar ta kuma saki tace.
   "Ba dai mu ba, shi dai ya kashe kansa waye ya kawo gubar idan ba shi ba, shine ya bada ajalinsa da hannunsa, dan ni tunda nazo duniya ban taɓa sanin akwai wannan gubar ba, duk makirci na."
    "To amma Mama meyasa kike dariya.?"
  Tunde ya jefa mata tambayar, Zama tayi tare da kallon sa tace.
    "Ina dariya ne saboda na samu hanyar durƙusar da IYABO, da wannan mutuwar da Sadiq yayi a Companyn su zanyi amfani wajen ganin na durkusar da Companyn su, duk wani bincike sunyi basu samu hujjar wanda yayi wannan kisan ba, da Wannan zan tunzura Alhaji Sagir ya shigar da Companyn ƙara kotu domin bin kadun jinin ɗansa, zan nuna meyasa meyasa aka rasa duk wata hujja, hakan na nuna kenan anyi shirin kashe masa dane shiyasa aka goge hujjar da zata nuna wanda ya aikata kisan ni nasan yadda zan tunzura zuciyarsa harya hasala ya maka *Hisham rice mill limited* a kotu, yayin da su kuma basu da wata hujjar da zasu kare kansu."
  Tayi Maganar tana sakin murmushi, gabaki ɗayansu dariya suka sanya tare da kallon juna suna jinjinawa Mamabisola.

*_Page 55 to 56_*




Kallon Hisham Abba yayi ya kalli takaddar sammacin kansa ya girgiza tare da cewa ɗan sakon.
     "Okay kana iya tafiya Allah ya kaimu Monday."
   Kafin ya dubi HISHAM da yayi Matukar kaduwa yana gigice har yanzu hawayen yake.
    "Hisham baka da hankali, yanzu Hisham ayi kisan kai a cikin Companyn mu baku sanar da ni ba saboda rashin hankali, yanzu wannan gangancin da kukayi kasan irin asarar da zai haifarwa Company kuwa.?"
    Kansa Hisham ya kaɗa sai yanzu ya samu damar fashewa da kuka cikin kukan yace.
    "Bashi bane damuwata Abba, na amince nayi asarar komai a rayuwata muddun ɗan uwana zai kasance tare dani har abada, Abba lafiyar Nameer shine damuwata Abba mu nema masa lafiya kana jin abinda likita yace ɗan uwana bazai wuce wata biyu ba a duniya, bazan iya kirga wannan kwanakin ba Abba kace min mafarki ne, kace kunnena karya yake min Abba."
    Yayi Maganar cikin wani irin kuka mai ɗagawa mai sauraro hankali.
  Tausayin sa ne ya kama Abba, ya jawo sa jikinsa tare da rungumesa Ummi ma kukan ta kuma sanyawa tana neman waje ta zauna.
    "Rayuwa zata mana tsanani Hisham rashin Nameer a rayuwar mu tamkar rasa jigon jikin mu ne, bazan iya jurewa rashinsa na kwana goma ba bare kuma na tsawon rayuwa, Abban Hisham mu ɗauke Nameer daga wannan asibitin mu fita dashi waje watakila su zasu iya ceto mana rayuwarsa na shiga Uku idan na rasa Nameer."
  Ummi tana kuka take wannan maganar, a wannan lokacin duk wanda ya zo ya wuce yaga wannan ahalin sai ya tausaya musu, kusan gigita Abba sukayi suka kuma tayar masa da hankali, ƙasa yayi tare da Hisham suka zauna cikin sanyin murya Abba yace.
    "Ya ya kuke son nayi da rayuwata da wanne zanji da tashin hankalin ku ne ko da ciwon Nameer ko kuwa da fargabar wannan takaddar da tazo gareni, so kuke ku haukata ƙwaƙwalwata, Ramata da girmanki kike abu tamkar yarinya karama madadin ki rarrashi Hisham shine zaki sanya kuka kamar shi kina kin shiga Uku, ku sani babu yadda muka iya da ƙaddara a yau Ubangiji yaga dama zai ɗauki Nameer kuma babu yadda zamuyi dole muyi haƙuri dan Allah ku kwantar da hankalinku mubi komai a sannu ko nima zan samu nutsuwar da zan san Abin yi."
   Kanta Ummi ta ɗaga tana cigaba da kukan Abba zame Hisham yayi daga jikinsa ya tashi ya koma wajen Doctor.


   Ko da ya shiga sun tattauna sosai akan matsalar NAMEER tare da neman shawarar Doctor ko akwai inda yake ganin idan sunje zasu samu Nasara sai dai ina Doctor ya tabbatarwa da Abba sai dai suyi haƙuri domin kuwa duk inda zasuje wannan result ɗin za'a basu, haka Abba ya fito cikin sanyin jiki daga office ɗin, wajen Nameer ɗin suka koma dukkan su Abba ya zauna gefensa, ya zuba masa idanu cike da tausayin sa, ya taso tun yana yaro a hannunsa bai taɓa ɓata masa rai ba komai cikin ladabi da girmamawa yake masa, ko yaushe tunaninsa da burinsa akan yadda zai kare masa ahalinsa, yadda yake kula dashi ko Hisham ɗan da ya haifa a cikin sa baya masa wannan kulawar, da ace akwai inda za'a bawa Nameer rayuwa tabbas da komai nisan wajen sai yaje ko da dukiyarsa ne gaba daya zai iya kashewa domin ceto rayuwar Nameer, numfashi ya saki shi kaɗai yake zancen zucin Ummi kuwa tana rike da hannunsa.

   Itama Tutu a gigice ta shigo asibitin ko da Ummi ta sanar da ita abinda ke faruwa da Nameer ihu Tutu ta sanya sosai hankalinta ya tashi Abba ne ya mata nasiha sosai kafin ta risina tabar kukan amma har yanzu tana ijiyar zuciya, Nameer bai farfaɗo ba har wajen karfe biyar na yamma, tunda ya buɗe idanunsa yake ƙarewa ɗakin kallo, akan Hisham da ya takure a gefe yayi shuru Nameer ya sauƙe Idanunsa.
    "Hisham."
Ya kira sunansa ɗago idanunsa Hisham yayi tare da tashi da sauri ya nufesa kusa dashi ya zauna sai kuma yaji kuka yazo masa sai yanzu Hisham ya samu damar sakin kuka mai sauti yana kifa kansa a jikin Nameer idanunsa Nameer ya runtse cikin sanyin jiki yayi Magana.
    "Tabbas kukan ɗan uwana gareni ya tabbatar da cewa mummunan abu na faruwa ga rayuwata, Abbana ka faɗa min me likita yace yake damuna karka ɓoye min Abba ka faɗa min zan rungumi kaddara tunda ni musulmi ne nasan bazan kaucewa kaddarata ba."
  Wani irin tausayinsa ne ya kuma shiga zuciyar Abba shima hawayen ne ya ciko a idanunsa saurin mayar dasu Abba yayi gudun karya karyawa Nameer zuciya, Abba cikin tashin hankali da yanayin damuwa yace.
   "Ba wata matsala bace fa Nameer likita yace zaka samu lafiya......
   Saurin ɗago kansa Hisham yayi tare da katse Abba da cewa.
    "A'a Abba karka ɓoye masa cutarsa kasa ya sanya rai ga abinda ba lallai bane ya tabbata, yana da kyau yasan cutarsa, domin ko ba komai zai san tanadin da zai yiwa goben sa, Nameer ba kai kake cikin damuwa ba mune, cutar a jikinka take amma mune muke jin zafin ciwon, mune muke cikin tashin hankali da masifa domin kuwa rasaka garemu masifa ce Nameer babbar masifa ma, zan shiga cikin kuncin rayuwa komai zai ƙwace min muddun na rasaka, kaine fitilar rayuwata ta yaya zan koyi rayuwa ba tare da kai ba, likita yace kana ɗauke da *KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA* yace bazaka wuce wata biyu ba a duniya zaka mutu, amma nasan karya yake zaka rayu, zamu cigaba da rayuwa tare bazaka mutu ka barni ba sai dai ni na mutu na barka."
  Yayi Maganar yana kuka har da majina idanunsa sunyi jajur, idanunsa Nameer ya runtse madadin ya shiga cikin tashin hankali sai murmushi ya saki tare da kamo hannun Hisham, gaba ɗaya da mamaki suka kallesa ganin murmushi a fuskar sa madadin tashin hankali.
   "Wai kuka kake Hisham saboda Yar wannan maganar, ni fa mai rai ne dole ne wata rana zan mutu Meyasa zaku damu akan abinda yake kan kowa, dukkan mu zaman jiran mutuwar muke, dan haka ku daina damuwa, idan na mutu ba ga ka nan ba Hisham, zaka kula mana da Abba da Ummi da Tutu bazaka bari suyi kukan rashina ba, ku daina damuwa Ubangiji ya gama tsara lamarinsa a kaina karkuyi fushi da ikon Ubangiji muyi haƙuri sai mu ci jarabawar mu, ummina Abbana ku daina kuka muddun kuna son na cigaba da dariya kafin lokacin mutuwata."
   Hawayen fuskarta Ummi ta goge tare da kamo hannunsa tace.
    "Hakane Nameer insha Allah ma zaka rayu, zamu cigaba da nema maka inda zaka samu lafiya."
   Abba shima nasiha ya dinga yiwa Nameer yayin da sai murmushi yake yana jansu da tadi har Hisham ya ɗan sake, Tutu ma duk kukan da take sai da Nameer yasa tayi dariya.
   Ranar Hisham ne ya kwana tare da shi a asibiti, washegari da safe Ummi ta taho da breakfast ita da Tutu, wajen sha biyu na rana  Mamabisola tazo asibitin, bata samu Abba ba sai Ummi da Hisham, cikin girmamawa Ummi ta bata waje.

     "Sannu da zuwa Mama."
  Ummi tayi Maganar cikin girmamawa, da yauwa Mamabisola ta amsa tare da cewa.
     "Yanzu nake jin labarin abinda ke faruwa da Nameer, Iyabo ya kiran yake sanar dani, Allah ya basa lafiya."
   Da ameeen Ummi ta amsa kafin Mamabisola ta kuma cewa.
     "Ramata garin yaya wannan mahaukacin ɗan naki yayi gangancin sakewa ma'aikata har akayi kisan kai a cikin ma'aikata, hmm! Iyabo yace min mahaifin yaron da aka kashe ya shigar da Companyn ku ƙara kuma wannan duk gangancin y'ay'anki ne, zaku samin Iyabo cikin masifa dan yanzu ma gashi hankalin sa ya tashi."
   Nameer dake kwance ne ya ɗan zaro idanunsa yana kallon Hisham, kansa Hisham ya girgizawa Nameer yana hucin maganganun Mamabisola, Ummi ce mata tayi.
   "Bamu da hakkinsu insha Allah babu abinda zai faru, gaskiya zatayi halinta."
   Murmushi Mamabisola ta saki tare da cewa.
    "Abinda zai ceci wannan Company hujja ce kawai kuma ance babu ita ku dai kun jawowa IYABO masifa, to dama Ramata ya ya za'ayi abinda kika haifa yayi albarka shima Iyabo ai yasan abinda ya debowa kansa, ni kinga tafiyata."
  Tayi Maganar tana tashi ta fita daga room ɗin, numfashi Ummi ta saki tare da kallon Hisham da ke famar huci, zaiyi magana ta ɗaga masa hannu.
    "Duk abinda yake cikinka ka mayar dashi, Mamabisola Tana da ikon da zata dakeni ma bare duka, uwa na ɗauketa dan haka bana son naji kayi wata maganar a kanta."
   Shuru yayi tare da mayar da kansa gefe Nameer ne yace.
    "Alhaji Sagir ne ya shigar da Company ƙara.?"
   "Eh Nameer nayi mamaki kwarai domin kuwa shi da bakinsa yace ya yafe."
    Shuru Nameer yayi ya kasa magana domin kuwa maganar ta ɗaga masa hankali.
   Hisham ne ya tashi ganin kiran Jidda yasan ta jisa shuru ne shiyasa ta nemesa bata san abinda ke faruwa da Nameer ba, fita waje yayi ya amsa wayarsa, Abba bai dawo asibitin ba har wajen la'asar lis ko da Ummi ta tambayesa inda yaje yake sanar da ita yaje sun gana da Chief judge ne akan karar da Alhaji Sagir ya shigar ya ce har Alhaji Sagir yaje ya samesa amma yana nan kafe akan lallai sai an shiga kotu, kanta Ummi ta jinjina tare da tambayar Abba yaya za'ayi.
    "Hmmm! Wallahi ban sani ba Ramata sai an shiga kotun dai musan abinda zai faru wadannan yaran ne suka min shirme da tun fari sun sanar dani da na yiwa tufkar hanci tun wuri, kai Nameer yaya jikin naka.?"
   "Da sauƙi Abba, kayi haƙuri Abba ba laifin Hisham ba ne, ni ne na hana a sanar da kai abinda ke faruwa gudun kar mu ɗaga maka hankali bamuyi zaton lamarin zai rincabe haka ba, dan har munyi magana da Alhaji Sagir ɗin ya tabbatar mana da ya yafe, yaushe sukace za'a shiga kotun."?
   "Akan Monday sukace amma na samu Chief judge na roki alfarmar a daga shari'ar zuwa ka samu lafiya, yanzu sai last this month zamu shiga kotun, maybe shawara ya sauya gani yake bazai iya haƙuri ba, Doctor ya bamu sallama zamu iya komawa gida yanzu."
  "To Abba maganar Hisham da Jidda fa."?
   "Yau da daddare Alhaji Bawa zai kawo sakamakon binciken da yayi, mu jira sa tukunna."
   Kansa NAMEER ya kaɗa shi dai Hisham yana ji baiyi magana ba Ummi kuwa haɗa ranta tayi cike da haushi basu fi minti sha biyar ba, suka haɗa komai suka taho gida.
   Hisham bayan ya sauƙe su ne ya juya da kan motarsa zuwa gidan su Jidda a ƙofar gida ya tsaya ya kirata a waya kusan minti goma da kiranta ta fito sanye da Hijab har ƙasa motar ta bude ta shiga tana duban Hisham.
    "Zuciyata har ta fara dukan Uku Uku ɗazu da naji shurun yayi yawa, gashi weekend ba aiki bare nasan halin da kake ciki, ya jikin yaya Nameer ɗin, meke damunsa.?"
   Numfashi Hisham ya saki yana jingina kansa da site ɗin motar kafin cikin damuwa yace.
    "Ina cikin tashin hankali Jidda makiya sun sako rayuwata a gaba, ga tashin hankalin Nameer na fara saduda da rayuwa Jidda, babu makawa makiya sunci galba a kanmu, domin kuwa ina ji a jikina za'a rufe Companyn mu, ga kuma Nameer yana tsakiyar rayuwa da mutuwa, bashi da lafiya Doctor ya tabbatar mana da cewa KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA ke damunsa yace Nameer bazai haura wata biyu ba a duniya zai mutu, rayuwar nan babu komai a cikin ta Jidda sai tarin bakin ciki."
    Wani irin razana Jidda tayi tare da furta.
     "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un, Hisham me kake faɗa min, Yaya Nameer ɗin ne yake cikin wannan halin, kai ina faɗar likita ai ba faɗar Ubangiji bane, a dai sauya wani asibitin aji su kuma me zasu ce.?"
    "Hmmm! JIDDA kenan lokacin da likita yace Sadiq yana halin rayuwa ko mutuwa ai haka kikace kuma yazo ya mutun, kawai dai Jidda ina cikin tashin hankali, mtsss Hmm! Nazo na ganki ne kawai na tafi kije kawai kaina yana ciwo zanje na kwanta."
   "Amma Hisham ina bukatar lokacinka fa, daga zuwa sai tafiya."
    "Please JIDDA ki barni na tafi, wallahi jiri nakeji na juma anan bazan iya kai kaina gida ba, nazo ne na ganki ko zan samu sukuni."
  Kanta Jidda ta kaɗa tace.
     "Shikenan ka gaishe da yaya Nameer ɗin insha Allah gobe zanzo na gaishe sa."
  Da zaiji Hisham ya amsa sanda Jidda ta shiga gida kafin ya tafi.



******


Ko da daddare Nameer yana kwance a gefen Abba Shi kuma Hisham yana zaune kan kujera Alhaji Bawa ya shigo shida Alhaji Ahmed, tashi Nameer yayi ya zauna Abba duban Hisham yayi yace yaje ya kira Ummi tashi yayi yaje ya kirata suka dawo tare itama gefen Abban ta zauna tana cewa su Alhaji Bawa.
   "Barkan ku da zuwa Alhaji Bawa Alhaji Ahmed Barka."
   Cikin fara'a suka amsawa Ummi Nameer da Hisham ma gaishe su sukayi har suna tambayar NAMEER jikinsa, Abba ne yace.
    "Alhaji Bawa tun dazu na ke sauraron zuwan ka, Allah dai yasa binciken da kukayi akan Yarinyar an samu sakamako mai kyau."
  Shuru Alhaji Bawa yayi sai Alhaji Ahmed ne ya nisa yace.
     "To Alhaji maganar gaskiya dai anje anyi bincike da farko yaron wajena Kabiru na aika unguwar su Yarinyar ya min bincike, sanda ya kawo min sakamakon ban daddara ba na koma da kaina na sake bincike shima Alhaji Bawa da kansa yaje yayi binciken abun mamaki gabaki ɗaya sakamako ɗaya muka samu babu wani sauyi, da farko dai ita Yarinyar bata da wani aibu kowa Alkairi yake faɗa a kanta tana da riƙon addini ga girmama manya, mahaifin yarinyar bahaushe ne haka kuma mahaifiyarta bahaushiya ce mahaifiyarta ne yaruba, Alhaji IYABO, akwai babbar matsala gaskiya, dangin mahaifiyar Yarinyar duk mijin da ya auresu farko sai ya rasa rayuwarsa, gaba daya haka suke, ita na mahaifiyar Yarinyar yafi tsananta domin kuwa maza huɗu ta aura duk basa wuce sati da Auren suke mutuwa, shine dalilin da yasa aka kyamace ta tabar cikin Yan uwanta ta dawo gefe ta zauna, wannan itace matsalar Alhaji Iyabo, duk wanda ya auresu da fari mutuwa yake, sannan kakar Yarinyar uwar mahaifiyarta kenan ance cikakkiyar ƴar bariki ce, amma ita yarinyar bata da matsala, wannan shine abinda muka binciko kuma mun tabbatar da gaskiyar lamarin haka yake."
    Kansa Nameer ya dafe,  Abba numfashi ya saki kafin ya kalli Hisham da yake kallonsu hankali tashe domin kuwa a yanzu yana jin Soyayyar Jidda sama da komai a rayuwarsa yana jin cewa zai iya rayuwa da ita a ko wani hali, ya ijiye duk wata ɗaukar fansa da yayi niyya a gefe, a yanzu da gaske yake jin Soyayyar ta, ƙirjinsa ya dafe dake dukan Uku Uku Muryar Abba yaji yana cewa.
    "Subahanallah! Alhaji Ahmed, gaskiya wannan ba irin da za'a aura ba ne, bazai yiwu Hisham ya auri wannan Yarinyar ba, menene amfanin auren da babu jin daɗin rayuwa, sati ɗaya miji ya mutu, no Hisham kayi haƙuri da wannan Yarinyar ka nemi wata ka dai ji da kunnen ka, Nameer ka basa shawara ya nemi wata, Ramata kema ki faɗawa danki babu Aure tsakanin sa da wannan Yarinyar, Alhaji Bawa na gode sosai."

*_Page 57 to 58_*



Idanunsa Hisham ya kuma runtsewa tare da cije lebensa yana jin maganar Abba tamkar yana zuba masa ruwan zafi a jikinsa, Muryar Ummi ne ya kuma dukan kunnen Hisham.
    "Dama ni ai Abban Hisham da ka saurareni tun fari baza'a kai zuwa wannan lokacin ba zamu rabasa da yarinyar amma tunda yanzu yaji da bakinsa zai haƙura dole ai."
    Numfashi Alhaji Ahmed ya saki tare da tashi suka yiwa Abba sallama, shima Abba tashi yayi ya rakasu har ya dawo Hisham bai ce komai ba, Abba ne yace masa.
     "Muna magana Hisham kayi shuru tamkar baka wajen, bakaji abinda ake magana bane.?"
  Abba ya jefa masa tambayar sai yanzu Hisham ya ɗago idanunsa da sukayi jajur ya kallesu tare da cewa.
    "Ina jin ku Abba, to me zance muku, ni na amince ko wacce irin mummunar kaddara Jidda ta ke ɗauke dashi ni na amince zanyi rayuwa da ita a haka, Abba Ƙaddara rayuwa bata nufin karshen mafarkin Rayuwa, Jidda kaddarata ce ko iya soyayyar ta da nakeji a raina ya nuna min cewa Jidda kaddarata ce, da farko ba son gaskiya nake yiwa Jidda ba Abba a  nufina na kona zuciyarta da soyayya domin ɗaukar fansar abinda ta yiwa ɗan uwana Nameer sai Ubangiji ya juya lamarin sa ya sanya min Soyayyata na haƙiƙa a raina bazan iya jagorantar rayuwata ba dole sai da ita Abba, ba dole ace kaddararta da ta ahalinta ɗaya ba ne, kayi haƙuri bazan iya bin umarninka ba akan rabuwa da Jidda, ina son Jidda na amince idan na aureta na mutu ko ba komai zan mutu cikin farin ciki da cikar muradina, *KO BAKU AURA MIN ITA BA DOLE SAI NA AURE TA, BA ZAN TAƁA RABUWA DA ITA BA KO MAI ZAI FARU KO DA HAKAN YANA NUFIN KARSHEN NUMFASHI NA NE.*
   Yayi Maganar a zuciye yana tashi ba tare da tsayawa sauraren abinda Abba zaice ba zai bar wajen, Muryar Nameer ya dakatar da shi.
     "Tabbas ƙaddarar rayuwa bata nufin karshen mafarkin rayuwa Hisham na yarda da wannan maganar taka, sai yanzu na fahimci ƙaddarar ciwo na, da alamu Ubangiji yana nufin ni ne zan fanshi rayuwar ɗan uwana, bani da doguwar rayuwa a wannan duniyar, wata biyu ne ya rage min zanyi amfani da wannan kwanakin domin ceto Soyayyar ɗan uwana, zan kasance mijin Jidda na farko domin bawa Hisham farin ciki na tsawon rayuwa, zan auri Jidda idan na mutu ka samu damar kasancewa da ita a matsayin mata ba tare da wani tsoron mutuwa ba, Abba ni zan auri Jidda idan na mutu Hisham ya kasance da ita na har abada."
  Tunda suka fara Maganar Abba ke bin Hisham da kallon mamaki duk da yasan zuciyar Hisham amma bai taɓa tsammanin zai iya kallon idanunsa ya ce zai juya masa baya akan mace ba, kafin Abba yayi Magana Ummi tace.
    "Akan wannan matsiyaciyar Yarinyar zaka sadaukar da rayuwarka Nameer wa ya ce maka bazaka rayu ba, zaka samu lafiya Nameer a wannan asibitin kawai mukaji furucin su bamu ji na wasu asibitin ba, zamu je asibitin Doctor Barde shima zai ƙara gwadawa bazamu tsaya ba har sai ka samu lafiya, kai kuma Hisham a wannan karon bazan ɗauki iskancinka ba, tabbas zan koya maka hankali, dole ka rabu da ita dan ubanka ai ba tsoronka mukeji ba bare ka juya mu yadda ranka yake so shasha."
   Ummi tayi Maganar cikin ɓacin rai, shi dai Hisham bai ce komai ba bayan wannan maganar da yayi, Nameer zaiyi magana Abba ya ɗaga masa hannu tare da cewa.
    "Ya isa haka, duk ku tafi ku bani waje, ya maka kyau Hisham na gode iyakata ka nuna min kaje kayi abinda kake so kaje maza ka nemi wasu iyayen da kake dasu bayan mu su nema maka Auren mu ai bamu isa da kai ba ."
   Wani irin sanyi jikin Hisham yayi, kansa ya girgizawa Abba tare da cewa.
      "Kayi haƙuri Abba wallahi kun isa dani, ka fahimce ni Abba ina son Jidda wallahi bazan iya rabuwa da ita ba Please Abba ka taimakeni ka Aura min ita ko da ita ko babu ita bazan taɓa tsallake wa'adin da Ubangiji ya min ba a duniya mu yarda da ƙaddara nas...............
      "Ka shige ka bani waje Hisham!."
  Abba ya dakatar dashi a zafafe cikin tsawa, idanunsa Hisham ya runtse tare da barin wajen cikin kunar rai, bayansa Nameer yabi da sauri Ummi Abba ta duba tare da cewa.
     "Nasha faɗa maka cewa mu daina biyewa Hisham muna masa duk abinda yake so amma kaƙi saurarana, duk abinda Hisham yayi a wajen ka daidai ne yau ga irinta nan ya nuna maka bazai yadda ya rasa abinda yake so ba, saboda ka saba masa da samun duk abinda ya nema."
   "Ramata karki ɓata min rai da maganar banza, ko yanzu ma ba hanasa abinda yake so nayi ba, rayuwarsa nake son karewa da ace Yarinyar nan bata da wannan tabon mutuwar to da tabbas zan aura masa ita, sannan ko yanzu idan har aka samu ƙaddarar da zata sauya wannan tabon mutuwar to tabbas zan aura masa yarinyar."
   Cike da mamaki Ummi ta ke kallon Abba.
    "Abban Hisham har akwai wata kaddarar da zata sauya mutuwa, oh na fahimta saboda muradin danka ya cika ka yarda ya aureta ko da zai rasa rayuwarsa, hakan Soyayya ce, kai fa kace ya haƙura da yarinyar yanzu kuma ka sauya maganar me yaja hankalinka haka.?"
    "Ni ne Ummi ni ne naja hankalinsa, da ku rasa mu gabaki ɗaya gwara a rasa ɗaya dole dai ni mutuwa zanyi, ki yarda da ƙaddara Ummi, Doctor Sabo bazai faɗi result ɗin karya ba akan ciwo na, ko da asibiti ɗari zamuje amsar dai ɗaya ce waye kika ga mai KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA ya yi doguwar rayuwa ya kamata mu yiwa kanmu hisabi idan rai ya ɓaci mu kai zuciya nesa Ummi Hisham yana son Yarinyar nan ya kamata ki barsa ya aureta, Ummi auran Jidda da zanyi shine kawai zai ceto Hisham daga mutuwa, ban taɓa neman alfarma a wajenki ba Ummi, amma yau zan nema, idan har kika min haka Ummi shine zai tabbatar min da kinfi kowa ƙaunata a duniya, kuma zan mutu ina farin cikin wannan alfarmar da kika min dan Allah Ummi."
  Hawaye ne ya zubo daga idanun Ummi, tare da takawa taje gaban Nameer ta tsaya.
    "nasan alfarmar da zaka nema a wajena Nameer bazan iya ba, bazan iya kallo kasa rayuwarka a cikin haɗari ba, duk da yanzun ma Rayuwar taka acikin haɗari take, Nameer ina ji a jikina zaka samu lafiya."
    "A'a Ummi bazan samu lafiya ba kin sani na sani, kawai kimin wannan alfarmar."
    Kanta Ummi ta kaɗa tace.
    "Shikenan zan maka zan bar Hisham ya auri yarinyar nan ne kawai saboda kai Nameer amma Allah ya gani har cikin zuciyata bana kaunar ta, amma da sharadi ɗaya har sai mun koma asibiti an tabbatar da cewa bazaka rayu ba, sannan ne zan iya barinka ka ɗauki wannan kasadar Nameer."
  Ummi tana faɗin haka ta juya da sauri tabar wajen tana kuka, Nameer wajen Hisham ya koma.


  Yana zaune yayi shuru.
      "Hisham na faɗa maka wannan gardamar zan rabata ni zan auri Jidda tunda dama ai ba jumawa zanyi a duniyar ba, kaga idan na mutu shikenan ka samu damar zama da ita na tsawon rayuwa."
     "Hassan tun ɗazu yake kiran wayarka."
  Hisham ya masa maganar tamkar baiji mai yace ba.
    "Magana fa nake maka Hisham ba damuwata kiran Hassan ba."
  Idanunsa ya ɗaga ya kalli Nameer kafin ya girgiza kansa.
     "Hmmm! Maganar taka ce bata da daɗin ji Nameer, wa yace maka nidin mai tsawon rayuwa ne, kasan ƙaddarar mutuwa tace, kaga Nameer idan Allah yasa kana da tsawon kwana duk wahala da ciwo sai ka tashi kayi Rayuwar da Ubangiji ya diba maka, ka barni na ɗauki kaddara ta Jidda kaddarata ce kuma ni zan ɗauki kaddararta."
    "Hisham kenan kaga mun gama magana da Ummi muddun kana son Auren Jidda to dole ne sai na fara rabaka da kaddarar da take cikin ahalin Jidda."
   "Ka tashi muje gidan su Jidda na tabbatar da abinda kunnena yaji."
   Kansa Nameer ya kaɗa yace.
    "Shikenan zamu iya zuwa Hisham hankalina ya kasa kwanciya akan karar da Alhaji Sagir ya shigar da Company, watakila a gama shari'ar bana raye, ko mai zai faru Hisham karka bari Companyn mu ya wulakanta, ka kankaro mutuncin Abba, kayi ƙoƙarin ganin ka kare mana Company."
    "Nameer shi kansa Abba bazai bari Companyn sa ya wulakanta ba, yafi Alhaji Sagir karfi da iko a cikin ƙasar nan, nasan a wannan lokacin Abba zai tauna tsakuwa dan aya taji tsoro wallahi Nameer bana jin tsoron wannan kotun da za'a shiga, ni nasan wanda ya je ya saka Alhaji Sagir ya shigar da Company ƙara, ba kowa bane face Mamabisola, tar na ke kallonta da idanuna, burinta ɗaya mu rasa komai a rayuwa bazan taɓa tona mata asiri ba, nafi so shi Abba ya fahimta da kansa."
   "Me kake nufi Hisham waya faɗa maka cewa itace tasa Alhaji Sagir ya shigar da ƙara kotu."?
    Miƙewa tsaye Hisham yayi tare da goya hannunsa a baya yace.
     " *MAI GADIN GIDAN TA* Shine ya sayar min da wannan jaridar akan kuɗi Naira dubu ɗari b."
  A razane shima Nameer ya miƙe yace.
     "What! To amma meyasa zai fitar mata da sirri muddun gaskiya yake fadi baya tsoron abinda zaije ya dawo, kai mtsss to meye tsakanin ta da wannan kisan tasan wanda yayi kisan ne da har zataje ta zuga Alhaji Sagir.?"
    "A'a bata sani ba kawai dai ta shiga ciki ne saboda ta samu taga mun wulaƙanta, shine ita burinta zai cika."
    "Kai lamarin da mamaki Hisham, to Allah ya kyauta, ka tashi muje gidan su Jiddan."

*_Page 59 to 60_*




Bakinsa HISHAM ya taɓe ransa duk a cunkushe ya ɗauki wayarsa tare da cewa Nameer suje.

    Abba bedroom yabi Ummi ya dinga rarrashinta da ƙyar ya samu Ummi tayi shuru.


 Ko da sukaje unguwar su Jiddan Hisham kansu tsaye suka shiga, Umma ce kawai da Basmah a tsakar gidan Jidda tana dakin ta, darduma Umma ta shimfida musu Basmah hankalinta duk yana kan Hisham tana jin baƙin ciki da kunar rasashi duk da a ranta ta saka sai ta ƙwaci Soyayyarta, bayan sun gaisa ne Umma ta fahimci kamar suna cikin damuwa, nisawa tayi tace.
    "Nameer kamar akwai abinda yake faruwa ko, naga kamar duk jikinku yayi sanyi.?"
   Numfashi Nameer ya saki tare da kallon Hisham kafin yace.
    "Umma tabbas akwai abinda ya kawo mu sai dai fa Umma ban san ta yaya zamu fara miki maganar nan ba ina jin nauyin maganar."
   "Nauyi kuma Nameer ni ai uwa ce, ku faɗa min damuwarku babu komai."
  Ganin Nameer na wala wala yana jin nauyin tambayar yasa Hisham cewa.
     "Umma magana ce akan neman aurena da Jidda, Abba yasa anyi bincike akanku, kuma ankawo masa sakamako marar daɗi, har hakan yasa iyayena suna son min katanga da Jidda, Umma da gaske ne duk wanda ya auri zuri'arku Auren farko sai ya mutu? Umma ance ke maza huɗu kika aura duk basa fin sati da Auren suke mutuwa, ance dalilin da yasa kenan kika bar cikin Yan uwanki kika dawo gefe, sannan kuma ance mahaifiyarki ƴar bari............
    "Ya isa haka Hisham wadannan tambayoyin ma kaɗai sun isa, domin sune matsalar mu ba sauran ba."
  Tunda Hisham ya fara magana Jidda ta fito take jinsa har Nameer ya dakatar dashi hankalinta ne ya tashi gumi ya keto mata bata san sanda hawaye ya biyo idanunta ba, umma kuwa innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un ta hau furtawa a cikin zuciyarta shikenan ranar da take tsoron zuwanta tazo numfashi Umma ta sauke kafin tace.
   "Hisham idan har iyayenka sun maka katanga da Jidda to kabi umarninsu domin kuwa bazasu cutar da kai ba saboda duk wanda zai soka yaso farin cikin ka a duniyar nan bayan sune, *_Wannan ba ƙarya aka faɗi akanmu ba tabbas ƙaddarar da ke cikin zuri'ar mu ne sai dai bani da tabbas idan Jidda tana ɗauke da wannan kaddarar saboda ita jinin gidan ubanta ne ba gidan mu ba, duk da acikin ya'yan mata da ke gidan mu da yawa sun gajemu dole a guje mu Hisham, tabbas mazan aurena huɗu suka rasa ransu babu za'ida aciki, Hmm! Ance muku goggona tana rainon ƴan bariki ko shine tambayar da Nameer ya dakatar da kai ga yinta gareni ko, shima gaskiya ne babu ƙarya a ciki, kaje ka yiwa iyayenka biyayya ka rabu da Jidda zata iya rayuwa ko ba Aure, ko da ta rasa mijin Aure, ai shi Aure Sunna ce ba farilla ba, farilla ce dole ba Sunna ba, manyan malaman Musulunci sunce idan mace batayi Aure ba har iya tsawon rayuwarta ba haramun bane kuma Allah bazai tambayeta akan meyasa batayi Aure ba, na gode da KULAWARKU da soyayyar da kuka nunawa yata Hisham da kuma ɗawainiyar da kukayi gareni, kaje ka yiwa iyayenka biyayya Allah ya muku albarka ya baku mata na gari_*."
  Umma tayi Maganar cikin sanyin jiki, Wani irin kuka Jidda ta sanya sautin kukan ta shiya dawo da hankalin su Hisham gareta, dubanta Hisham yake cike da tausayi Muryar Nameer sukaji yace.
     "Umma dama munzo mu tabbatar da gaskiyar maganar ne, bani da lafiya umma, munje asibiti sun tabbatar da cewa ina da Cutar KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA sunce bazan haura wata biyu ba a duniya zan mutu, nayi magana da mahaifiyar mu cewa ni zan ɗauki kasadar Auren Jidda, tunda dama ni mutuwa zanyi dan haka zan aureta idan na mutu Hisham ya auri matar sa, a ina dangin mahaifin Jidda suke Umma zamuje mu nemi aurenta."
   "Subahanallah! Nameer abinda ke faruwa da kai kenan innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un! Amma Nameer wannan ba ɗaukar kasada ba ce zakayi faɗar likita ai ba faɗar Ubangiji ba ne Nameer insha Allah zaka samu lafiya."
   "A'a Umma karki hanani, babu wanda ya taɓa kamuwa da cutar nan ya rayu, ki duba kiga yadda take kuka ko hakan ya isa ya nuna miki ƙarfin soyayyar da suke yiwa junansu ina da halin taimakawa ɗan uwana bazan barsa ya shiga wani hali ba, ki mana addu'a da fatan Alkairi kawai."
   Tunda Nameer ya fara Maganar Hisham yaji wani kishi yazo masa ya tsaya a wuyansa, idanunsa ya runtse, yana ji Umma tace.
    "To shikenan Nameer Allah yasa haka yafi Zama Alkairi, amma fa ka sani bazan waiwaici bayana ba, bana son tuna abinda ya wuce, akwai baffan ta a cikin garin Gombe sai dai kuje wajensa ya baku Auren Jidda amma bazan iya faɗa muku inda Ahalinta suke da zama ba saboda babu Alkairi a tsakanina dasu."
  Kansa Nameer ya girgiza cikin zuciyarsa ya furta _tunda har aka samu baffan ta to tabbas za'a samu cikakken ahalinta._
    "Shikenan ba damuwa idan mun gama magana da Abba zamuzo sai a bamu numbern wayar baffan nata mu zamu koma."
  Nameer yayi Maganar yana tashi shima Hisham tashi yayi sukayi sallama da Umma jidda da Basmah bayan su HISHAM sukabi suna tsaye jikin Mota har yanzu Jidda hawaye take sun juma suna tattaunawa akan lamarin, Nameer ya tabbatar da cewa shine zai raba wannan gardamar sai dai ita Jiddan har cikin zuciyarta bata amince da lamarin ba, cikin jimami suka rabu da Jidda.



**********


Acikin kwana shida da maganar Abba da Hisham da kuma Nameer sunje asibiti huɗu da Nameer manyan asibitin da suka fi dukkan asibitin da ke cikin garin har result ɗin sa aka tura India suka ƙara bincike amsar dai ɗaya ce bazai rayu ba bazai gaza wata biyu a duniya ba, Hisham da Umma sosai suka kuma shiga cikin tashin hankali sai dai babu yadda suka iya da kaddara dole suka haƙura, sai a wannan lokacin Abba da Ummi suka amince da Nameer ya auri Jidda duba da yadda Hisham ya shiga tashin hankali akan Jiddar, shima Hisham da kyar ya amince Nameer ya auri Jidda dan har cikin ransa yake jin kishin hakan duk da Nameer ɗan uwansa ne, Abba yace gara ayi auren cikin gaggawa kafin wata biyun ta cika.

  A cikin wannan satin Abba ya tura su Alhaji Bawa sukaje har gida suka samu Umma ta musu kwatancen inda zasuje su sami Baffan Jidda Malam Aminu da ke zaune cikin jihar Gombe tare da basu numbern wayarsa, ko da suka dawo Abba da kansa ya kira Malam Aminu ya masa bayanin abinda ke faruwa, cikin mutuntawa da kulawa Malam Aminu yace Allah ya kawo su lafiya, Abba Nameer yasa yaje ya yankawa su Alhaji Bawa ticket ɗin jirgin Gombe washegari su tafi.
  Haka kuma akayi washegari suka tafi Gombe harda Nameer, wuni guda suka gama abinda ya kaisu suka dawo, inda Malam Aminu ya bawa Nameer Auren Jidda sai dai shi baya da masaniya akan neman Jidda da Hisham dan Abba yace kar a sanar da Malam Aminu a nuna masa kawai Nameer shine yake neman auren nata, ansa ranar auren wata ɗaya tal, inda Malam Aminu yace shima zaizo Kwaran a ɗaura Auren kawai anan, ko da suka dawo suka sanar da Abba yace hakan yayi sai dai Umma ita bata ƙaunar shigowar Jidda cikin ahalinta a haka aka ijiye maganar auren.



**********


Sosai Basmah ta ke dariya ita kaɗai a cikin daki, shuru tayi na wasu mintinan sai kuma ta kuma sakin dariya tare da furta.
     "Allah mai iko rana ɗaya sai ya baka dama tabbas Hisham zai zamo nawa."
  Tayi Maganar tana dariya text ne ya shigo wayarta hakanne yasa Basmah Daga wayar ta buɗe sakon zama tayi ta fara karanta sakon.
    _Bana son kusan ko ni waye shiyasa na ɓoye number ta na turo miki wannan text ɗin, nasan kece aminiyar Jidda ta haƙiƙa, wacce kike ɗaukar damuwarta amatsayin naki, to shawara zan baki ki bawa Jidda akan Auren da take niyyar yi da Nameer nan ba da jumawa ba babban kuskure ta ke kokarin tafkawa rayuwarta, Nameer ƙarya yake bashi da wata cutar KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA, wannan shirinsa shi da likitotin daya saya saboda bukatar ya mallaki Jidda, bashi da wannan cutar dan haka idan har ta auresa to ta tabbatar ta rasa Hisham na har abada NAMEER MUNGUN MACIJI NE WANDA BASHI DA ALKIBILA, dan Allah ki sanar da Jidda ku san yadda zaku dakatar da wannan Auren ni musulmi ne wallahi na miki rantsuwa da Allah Nameer bashi da wata cuta ƙarya ne, idan kuma wannan maganar da na faɗa miki ƙarya ne to na yadda bala'in Ubangiji ya sauƙa a kaina, shida abokinsa Doctor Hassan suka kulla wannan makircin saboda cikar ƙudirinsa na mallakar Jidda ya gama bincike akan Mahaifiyar Jidda shiyasa ya ɓullo da wannan makircin saboda yasan ta nan ne kawai burinsa zai cika._
  Miƙewa tsaye Basmah tayi a firgice  da ƙarfi ta furta.
    "What!!! Nameer ɗin."

*END OF BOOK ONE LAST FREE PAGE 📚*

*_Page 61 to 62_*





Cike da tsantsar mamaki Basmah ta nemi waje ta zauna _ta yaya za'ace NAMEER zai ci amanar Hisham wannan ba maganar da hankali zai ɗauka bane, amma kuma ai ana iya komai akan Soyayya, to tunda nima zan iya cin amanar Jidda meyasa shi Nameer zan kasa yarda da zai ci amanar Hisham, ko da yake kusancin dake tsakanin Hisham da Nameer yafi ƙarfin kusancin dake tsakanina da Jidda_ numfashi Basmah ta saki a fili ta furta.
    "Hmmm! To ma wai waye ne ya turo min wannan sakon a idan gaskiya ne a ina ya samu wannan sirrin waye Hassan ya kamata nasan waye shi Hassan domin sanin dabiun sa shine zai tabbatar min da waye Nameer a zahiri dai Nameer mutumin kirki ne to amma ta yaya cikin sa zai kasance baƙi, to ma meyasa zan faɗawa Jidda wannan saƙon ni ai wannan babbar dama na samu bazan taɓa bari Jidda tasan wannan maganar ba, domin kuwa Auren Nameer da JIDDA shine zai bani damar samun Hisham a matsayin mijin aurena, lallai wannan babbar dama ce gareni."
  Tayi Maganar tana sakin murmushi tare da jingina kanta a jikin bango.



  Tun ɗazu ta ke kuka tamkar ranta zai fita taki tayi shuru idanunta yayi jajur, Ummi tayi tambayar duniya ta faɗa mata abinda yake damunta amma taƙi sai kuka kawai take tayi rarrashin duniya kuma taki tayi shuru, Hisham ne ya shigo yana waya shima duk fuskar sa ta faɗa dan tunda aka saka ranar auren Nameer da Jidda zuciyar sa take tsinkewa ga fargar mutuwar da aka Nameer zaiyi nan da wata biyu, ganin Tutu nata kuka ya sashi katse wayar tare da nufar su yana cewa.
   "Ummi meke faruwa kuma, meya sameta."?
  Numfashi Ummi ta saki dai-dai Abba na shigowa shi da Hisham tace.
    "Umm ka ganta tun ɗazu take wannan kukan nayi tambayar duniya ta faɗa min abinda ke faruwa taƙi, nayi rarrashin ma kuma taƙi tayi shuru bansan meke damunta ba."
   Zama gefenta Hisham yayi tare da dafa kafadarta.
   "Tutu kalli idona ki faɗa min meke damunki ko Allah zaisa na iya share miki hawayenki, faɗa min."?
   Kuka ta kuma fashewa dashi jikinta na rawa daƙyar ta buɗe bakinta tace.
    "Yaya Hisham da gaske zaka share min hawayena."?
   "Eh zanyi maganin damuwarki idan har bata fi ƙarfi na ba, faɗa min menene matsalar."?
   Idanunta ta ɗaga ta kalli Abba kafin ta sunkuyar da kanta.
    "Yaya Hisham idan har ana son na cigaba da farin ciki a cikin gidan nan to a fasa Auren Yaya Nameer da Jidda, ni ya Aure Ni."
   A firgice Hisham ya ɗago kansa ya kalli Ummi ya kalli Abba da Nameer da suma suka zaro idanunsu suna kallon Tutu.
   "Tir ƙashi."
Hisham ya furta yana mai da kallonsa ga Tutu kafin ya miƙe tsaye yace.
    "Tutu kinsan abinda kike faɗi kuwa, kin haukace ne, Nameer ba matsayin uwa ɗaya uba ɗaya ya kamata ki ɗaukesa ba, ya fasa auren Jidda ya aureki ke gaki uwarsa kin basa umarni ko, to yaƙi bin umarnin naki uwarsa tsine masa albarka ya shiga duniya, dan Allah Ummi kiji wani rashin hankali wai bazata ƙara farin ciki ba muddun bai aureta ba, to sai ki mutu da baƙin ciki wawiya."
  Hisham yayi Maganar a fusace tamkar zai kai mata duka, Nameer kuwa wani irin tsinkewa zuciyar sa yayi ko da wasa baiyi zaton Tutu zata birkice haka ba, numfashi ya saki tare da jinginuwa da bango ya kasa magana Ummi ce tace.
    "Wai dama wannan uban kukan da kike duk na wannan shashancin ne Tutu, kin manta da lalular da Nameer yake ɗauke dashi ne bai kai wata biyu ba ya rage masa a wannan duniyar bama da wani kwanciyar hankalin farin ciki daga mu harshi Nameer ko wani wayewar gari da fargabar mutuwar sa muke tashi, ita kanta Jiddar da zai Aura kin sani Tutu ba wai da san ransa zaiyi Auren ba, kinsan dalilin aurenta da zaiyi, amma kuma ke yanzu sai ki ɓullo mana da wannan shashancin, karna kuma jin kinyi wannan Maganar."
   Kukan Tutu ta kuma fashewa dashi cikin tashin hankali ta hau cewa.
    "Ni fa Ummi na yarda na auresa a haka ko da kwana ɗaya nayi tare dashi, zanyi farin cikin ganin wannan ranar na tsawon rayuwata, Ummi wallahi ina son yaya Nameer, shima fa Ummi yana sona ku tambayesa mana kuji, mun fa juma muna soyayya Ummi karku hana mu farin ciki Ummi wallahi muna son junan mu."
  Kansa Nameer ya dafe jin ta fasa masa taya amma da ke namijin duniya ne sai yayi fuska tamkar baiji zancen ba, Hisham da Abba ido suka Daga suka kalli Nameer.
    "Nameer kana jin abinda Tutu ta ke faɗa, da gaske ta ke kuna son junanku kai da ita.?"
  Ɗago kansa yayi ya kalli Hisham shima tare da nuna kansa da yatsa cikin yanayin mamaki yace.
    "Wai ni ɗin ne nake soyayya da Tutu, wannan wacce irin magana ce kike haka, nifa kallon ƙanwar da muka fito ciki ɗaya na ke miki Tutu, anya kuwa yau kina cikin hankalin ki, Hisham da ace ina soyayya da Tutu kaine mutum na farko da zan fara sanarwa ko da ban sanar dasu Abba ba, kaima ka sani, ina ga yau dai Tutu bata cikin hankalin ta akwai abinda ke damunta."
  Kansa Hisham ya gyada tare da cewa.
   "Tabbas haka ne Nameer da bazaka ɓoye min ba, barni da ita idan kin haukace ne to ni kuma zan kaiki asibitin mahaukata ko tausayin halin da yake ciki bakya yi, yanzu na taka miki wuya."
   Da wani irin mungun mamaki Tutu ta ke bin Nameer da kallo tana matsowa garesa hawaye na daɗa gudu a fuskarta tamkar saukar aradu taji maganar tasa, sanda tazo daf dashi cikin kuka da wani irin sautin Muryar da ya fara dashewa itama ta nuna kanta da yatsa sai kuma ta fashe da kuka.
      "Yaya Nameer ƙarya nake! Karya nake kace meyasa meyasa zaka min wannan wasan Please yaya Nameer yanzu ba lokacin wasa bane loka.....
  Ba ta kai ƙarshe ba Hisham ya ɗauketa da mari sai da taga duhu ya gifta ta cikin idanunta har sai da ta shiɗe, da yatsa ya nunata.
   "Idan kika ƙara wata magana sai na ballaki wallahi shige kibar falon nan."
  Yayi Maganar yana nuna mata hanya da gudu Tutu ta haura sama tana kuka tamkar ranta zai fita, ɓoyayyen numfashi Nameer ya saki cikin zuciyarsa yana godewa Allah da babu wanda ya fahimci komai a cikin su Hisham haƙuri ya bawa Nameer yana ta famar faɗa Ummi ita kasa magana ma tayi shima Abba kansa ya kaɗa tare da furta Allah ya sawwaka ya shige bedroom ɗinsa.



A saman bed ɗinta ta saki kuka mai ban tausayi ita kaɗai tasan irin matsanancin soyayyar da ta ke yiwa Nameer ashe dama soyayya kan dawowa ƙarya, meyasa Nameer zai juya mata baya ya ƙaryata soyayyar ta _kin faɗa min gaskiya naki na ɗauka Samira kullum kina faɗa min ni ne na ke son yaya Nameer shi bana gabansa amma naki yarda, a zatona ko da dukkan mazan duniya zasu juyawa mace baya yaya Nameer a matsayin sa na babban wana da nakejin zai iya tare min komai a duniya shi bazai min haka ba wannan wacce irin munguwar ƙaddara ce_ tayi Maganar zucin hawaye na cigaba da zuba daga idanunta ƙirjinta kuma tamkar zai fashe haka take ji tana jin Ummi na murda handle ɗin kofar bedroom ɗin nata tana kiranta tayi shuru tamkar bata ji ba domin haushin kowa takeji a cikin gidan karar shigowar saƙo ta ji a wayarta, hawayen idanunta ta goge tare da jawo wayar ta buɗe sakon a hankali ta hau karantawa sanda ta gama karantawa ta miƙe a firgice tare da mutstsika idanunta wai ko bata karanta bane ƙara maimaita karantawa tayi, sakon dai da aka turowa Basmah ne sai dai ita ana mata gargadi da karta kuskura tabari ayi Auren gumi ne ya yankowa Jidda hawayen dake zuba a idanunta suka kafe zama tayi dabas a tayels tare da furta.
   "What! Yaya Nameer ɗin lamarin nasa har ya kai haka.?"
    idanunta ta ɗaga ta kalli agogo karfe tara na dare numfashi ta saki tare da dafe kanta tana jin wani irin jiri na kwasarta, wayar ta ta ɗaga cikin nauyin jiki ta kira Nameer ɗin kusan miss Call 8 bai ɗaga ba, saƙo ta tura masa.
     _Na sani bazaka ɗaga wayata ba yaya Nameer, ashe bani kaɗai ka cuta kaci amana ba, harda mutumin da ya baka yardarsa fiye da kowa a wannan duniyar, Hmm! Soyayya mai juya tunanin marar lissafi, ashe soyayyar Jiddan har ta kai ga kaci amanar mutumin da yafi kowa yadda da kai a wannan duniyar labari ya iso domin kuwa Ubangiji baya bacci baya bari a zalunci bawansa dan haka Allah ya toni asirinka ashe dama baka da Cutar KANSAR ƘWAƘWALWA ka daurawa kanka masifa saboda neman biyan buƙatar kanka, muddun ina numfashi to wallahi sai na tona maka asiri bazan taɓa bari ayi wannan Auren ba._
   Ta tura saƙon tana kuma sakin kuka mai sauti tare da tsanar rayuwa ace ka yadda da mutum ya cuceka.



 ********

Yana ganin kiran nata yayi banza yaƙi ɗauka, Hisham ya na can gefe da system a gabansa saƙon ya shigo wayar Nameer bakinsa ya taɓe tare da ɗaukar wayar ya buɗe saƙon, yana gama karantawa ya miƙe a firgice tare da furta.
    "What! Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraji'un!."
  Ya furta a firgice, Hisham ɗago kansa yayi da sauri ya dubi Nameer tare da cewa.
     "Lafiya Nameer meya faru kake wannan salatin.?"

Shin da gaske ne Nameer baya ɗauke da wata cuta, idan har haka ne meyasa zai ci amanar Ahalin IYABO da suka mai dashi mutum duniya ta sanshi, sannan yaya zaiyi da kaddarar ahalin Jidda na mutuwar mijin da ya Auresu na farko, sannan yaya Hisham zaiji idan har ta tabbata Nameer yaci amanar sa ya zumuncin su zai kaya."
